Apotheosis – Ascension to Godhood
Vol.10

Chapter 901
When they heard about the Black Star Pickaxe, the people from the Demi-Immortal Tribe finally understood.
Although the Black Star Pickaxe was not on the Weapon List in the Upper Realm, that was because it was just a pickaxe and was not suitable to be used as a weapon.
However, as a Supreme Artifact, the Black Star Pickaxe was very useful at certain times, so it was definitely famous.
The middle-aged man who looked like a scholar nodded. "No wonder. I was wondering why someone from the Lower Realm could break my enchantment!"
This Qing Feng had also obtained a title in the "Illusory Tower". Although it was a bronze title, the title was "Asura"! He was one of the best among all the bronze titled battle pet warriors! It was enough to make him very proud!
As far as he knew, it was impossible for the ants from the Lower Realm to break his enchantment. In his enchantment, there was a Karma Law of "relative defense". Among the natives from the Lower Realm, who knew how to use the Karma Law?
In fact, although Qing Feng's idea was not wrong, he had underestimated the warriors from the Lower Realm. For example, Cui Xie in the Central Domain used the Nameless Buddhist Commandment Blade to use the Karma Law. However, Cui Xie did not master the Karma Law. He only attracted the Wind of Life and Death from the Underworld River.
Luo Zheng's situation was roughly the same as Cui Xie's. He did not understand his own Karma Law. He only relied on the Karma Law of the Black Star Pickaxe to break Qing Feng's enchantment.
"Don't talk nonsense. Let's take the Ancestral Dragon first. Although it has hatched, the imprint can't be reversed. Let's go back to the tribe and think of other ways," the burly man said. He also felt a little strange that Gu Peier and Yu Ze did not take the Ancestral Dragon, but brought it over.
Boss Mo was the core of their group, and his brain was still better than the burly man. Seeing Gu Pei 'er's expression, Boss Mo asked: "Pei' er, did you discover something?"
Gu Peier nodded and said, "Yes! I found something important! "After thinking for a while, Gu Peier smiled and said," Very important! "
Hearing Gu Peier's serious emphasis, the other six people suddenly became curious and focused their eyes on Gu Peier.
"What important thing?" Boss Mo continued to ask.
Yu Ze chuckled, and spoke in front of Gu Pei 'er: "Little Senior Sister has discovered the whereabouts of the Twinklegem Pagoda! The Exquisite Pagoda should be in the Great Thousand World! "
"What? Exquisite Pagoda! "
"One of the three Training Towers, the Exquisite Tower? Hasn't it been lost for many years? "
"Pei 'er, are you serious?"
The others asked Gu Pei Er for confirmation.
Boss Mo paused for a moment before asking, "Pei 'er, is the Exquisite Pagoda really in the Great Wide World?"
Gu Pei 'er nodded, "Yes, it is almost certain that the Exquisite Pagoda was lost in this lower realm."
"Heavens, Boss Mo, our luck this time is indeed very good! It was just that this Evil Dragon was difficult to deal with! But this Exquisite Pagoda's value is immeasurable! "The burly man said with a laugh.
Qing Feng also nodded, "The Illusory Pagoda and the Exquisite Jade Pagoda are currently overcrowded. Two Training Towers are far from enough. If we can find the Exquisite Pagoda, it will definitely be a great achievement. Pei 'er, how do you know the whereabouts of the Exquisite Pagoda?"
Gu Pei 'er pointed at Luo Zheng and said, "I got this information from this person."
"Kacha!"
The burly man waved the giant scythe in his hand, and it was embedded in the ground. He walked towards Luo Zheng and said coldly: "You, tell me the whereabouts of the Exquisite Pagoda!"
Luo Zheng stared at the burly man indifferently and did not answer his question.
Seeing that the "woman" in front of him was ignoring him, the burly man took two steps and walked towards Luo Zheng. "Kid, I will ask you another question. If you know what's good for you …"
The burly man's threat was only halfway through, but Gu Pei 'er continued: "The important thing I said is not the whereabouts of the Exquisite Pagoda!"
The Exquisite Pagoda was extremely precious to the Demi-Immortal Clan. For the seven people in front of them, it was absolutely incomparably important. However, Gu Pei 'er said that the Exquisite Pagoda was not important. This made the other six people puzzled. Qing Feng and the others looked at Gu Pei' er strangely.
Boss Mo said indifferently: "The Exquisite Pagoda is not important? Is there anything more important than that? "
"She …" Gu Pei 'er pointed at Luo Zheng and continued: "She said that she went to the Exquisite Pagoda and took the test of the Talent Stele. She even saw my father's name on it!"
"Senior Gu?" Boss Mo asked.
Gu Heche's talent could only be listed in the first row. He did not get a title in the Exquisite Pagoda. However, talent was only one aspect that determined the height of a warrior's achievements. Even if he did not get a title, Gu Heche had a good position in the Demi-Immortal Clan. Therefore, Boss Mo had to respectfully call him senior.
Among the seven people present, only Gu Pei 'er and the burly man did not get a title on the Talent Stele. Among the other five people, Boss Mo got a silver title, and the remaining four had a bronze title.
Strictly speaking, Gu Pei 'er was not qualified to form a team with them. The reason why Gu Pei' er could participate in this operation was mainly because of her father, Gu Heche.
"Yes," Gu Pei 'er nodded, "This cannot be faked, so I believe that he did enter the Exquisite Pagoda!"
"Then what is more important than the Exquisite Pagoda? Can you tell us the key points? "The burly man continued to ask.
"It's still her!" Gu Pei 'er said again.
This time, Gu Pei 'er's words had confused the other six people. What did she mean?
The woman in front of them was indeed special. In the universe, there were not many creatures with golden pupils. It was possible that some of the descendants of the Dragon Clan had such pupils. However, just a descendant of the Dragon Clan was not enough to interest them too much.
"She said that she got a title in the Talent Stele, so she can be considered a member of the Demi-Immortal Clan," Gu Pei 'er said calmly.
Hearing this, Qing Feng smiled faintly, "Got a title, hehe, someone in the Lower Realm can get a title in the Exquisite Pagoda. This is strange! What is the color of the title, and what is the title? "
Among the warriors present, except for Boss Mo, Qing Feng's title was the most advanced. He was indeed very concerned about this issue.
"The title issue still needs to be verified," Gu Pei 'er thought for a while, then said, "She said she got a gold title."
When Gu Pei 'er's words just came out, the other six people's faces were extremely interesting!
The one with the most intense reaction was Qing Feng. He smiled proudly, then said, "Pei 'er, is there a bug in your head? Are you stupid? Do you know what you are talking about? "
Even Boss Mo shook his head slightly, "Pei 'er, you are still lacking some experience. How can you believe this kind of words?"
"Hahaha … Pei 'er, don't make me laugh to death! What nonsense are you talking about! "The burly man also laughed.
When Gu Pei 'er's words came out, they did not believe it at all.
In fact, Gu Pei 'er was also skeptical of Luo Zheng's words. After all, it was just his empty words, and there was no proof.
However, Gu Pei 'er trusted Luo Zheng based on her own judgment. She said, "Boss Mo, the Linglong Pagoda was lost a long time ago. As far as I know, before it was lost, the Linglong Pagoda had only been opened for a short period of time. Hence, no one had obtained a title from the pagoda. The golden Heaven rank title was said by her. If she did not see the title, how could she say it? There can't be such a coincidence in the world, right? "
Qing Feng snorted. "What if this kid knows something about the Demi-Immortal Clan? Or maybe someone else has obtained the title of Golden Demi-Immortal?"
"Others … there is no such possibility," Boss Mo shook his head.

Chapter 902
Boss Mo was very sure of this point.
It was not that the warriors from the Upper World were arrogant and looked down on the creatures of the Lower World.
The number of living beings in the Boundless Thousand Worlds was indeed extremely large. It was almost impossible to count them. Many of the cultivators who had ascended to the Upper Realm also displayed extremely powerful talent and an extremely tenacious heart of martial arts!
Many of the creatures from the Lower World had achieved great things after ascending. Some of them were even conferred the title of Realm Lord, Celestial Lord …
But overall, there was still a considerable gap between the warriors from the Lower World and the Upper World. This gap was already limited at birth! And this limitation was almost everywhere.
For example, the vitality of the Upper World was much more violent than that of the Lower World. The creatures born from the vitality of the Upper World were naturally stronger than those from the Lower World.
In addition, there were also problems with resources, inheritance, and so on.
So when Gu Pei 'er suddenly said this, how could they believe it?
"Pei 'er, don't forget the most important point. This woman said that she was recognized by the Demi-Immortal Clan, but I didn't feel any mark of the Demi-Immortal Clan on her body. What kind of recognition is this?" Qing Feng said again.
Everyone's eyes flashed. Indeed, because Gu Pei 'er's words were too shocking, they had forgotten this point.
To be recognized by the Demi-Immortal Clan, one only needed to climb to the second level of the Talent Stele. At that time, the Talent Stele would quietly hide the mark of the Demi-Immortal Clan on the subject's body.
They could not feel any mark on Luo Zheng's body. This meant that Luo Zheng was either making things up, or he had indeed entered the Talent Stele and taken the test. But he was not even qualified to go to the second row …
Gu Peier nodded. "That's right. I didn't feel it either. However, this physical body of hers was not hers in the first place. She had taken it over …" Seeing the crowd continuously refuting her judgment, Gu Peier was also shaken. Although her previous logic supported Gu Peier's guess, now that she thought about it, this golden heavenly rank title was indeed somewhat inconceivable. She began to doubt it again.
"That's easy," the burly man said with a smile. "You just have to find her real body. What's so hard about that? Trying to impersonate a Demi-Immortal isn't easy at all!"
From the beginning to the end, Luo Zheng had not said a single word. It was very unrealistic for him to fend off the seven of them by himself. Not to mention that Luo Zheng was only occupying Feng Nianyun's body. Even if Feng Nianyun's soul wasn't sealed, she probably wouldn't be able to fend off the seven of them even if she used her full strength.
An expert of this realm was an existence that could completely crush the entire Great Wide World.
At this time, Luo Zheng also understood that if he wanted to take this Ancestral Dragon away safely, Luo Zheng could only prove himself … Fortunately, he didn't lie.
Therefore, Luo Zheng said, "Yes, my physical body is outside the Heavenly Stellar Holy Land. If you don't believe me, you can come with me!"
Boss Mo pondered for a moment and turned to look at the dust-covered Evil Dragon. After thinking for a moment, he said, "The Evil Dragon is already covered in dust. I guess we'll have to invite someone else to remove the dust. It's not impossible …"
The burly man sneered, "It'll only take a moment to verify the truth. However, if you're lying and waste my time … Hehe!"
Luo Zheng nodded calmly. He looked at the group of Divine Sea Realm experts and said, "Please take them with you!"
The Divine Sea Realm experts of the four Divine Kingdoms were like wooden blocks. They stood there in a daze, listening to the conversation between Luo Zheng and these people.
They didn't understand the topic of the Demi-Immortal Tribe at all. They only vaguely understood that Luo Zheng wanted to prove something to them. No matter what, these people didn't directly attack Luo Zheng. Instead, they believed Luo Zheng's words. This brought them a ray of hope!
After all, a group of Divine Sea Realm experts, even if they didn't occupy the bodies, couldn't fight against seven Ultimate Divine Realm experts in their peak state.
Now that Luo Zheng asked them to take them out, a glimmer of hope rose in the hearts of the warriors of the four Divine Kingdoms.
Unexpectedly, the burly man glanced at these "wooden blocks" indifferently and said, "They're just a bunch of rats. Just kill them. It's too troublesome to take them out!"
When these words came out, the hearts of the warriors of the four Divine Kingdoms sank.
Their lives were worthless in front of these Ultimate Divine Realm experts.
"Hey, Yuan Bai is right. It's too troublesome to take them out. Just kill them." After speaking, Yu Ze walked towards Zhou Huang and the others with the black knife in his hand. Unable to move, he only needed one knife to completely obliterate the souls of the warriors of the four Divine Kingdoms.
Seeing this, Luo Zheng's golden pupils suddenly flashed with a touch of fierceness. "Stop!"
Yu Ze didn't listen to Luo Zheng. He raised the black knife in his hand. Black knife energy gradually spread along the back of the knife. It seemed that the knife was about to be chopped out.
At this moment, the warriors of the four Divine Kingdoms were wailing in their hearts. They never thought that they would die in this situation. How could they be willing to be killed so carelessly when they couldn't resist?
However, although they were wailing in their hearts, their bodies were completely out of control. Except for the resentment and hatred in their eyes, they couldn't even say a word.
Luo Zheng said coldly, "If we find the Exquisite Pagoda and prove that I really have the title of Golden Celestial Realm, then what?"
Hearing this, Yu Ze really stopped the black knife in his hand. He saw that Luo Zheng's tone was full of confidence. The corners of his mouth twitched and he mocked, "Oh? It seems that you're really sure that you have the title of Golden Celestial Realm? "
"Yes." Luo Zheng's eyes were calm as he stared at Yu Ze.
"What if you don't?" Yu Ze continued to ask with a smile.
"In this Vast Thousand Domains, no one can be your enemy. At that time, you can do whatever you want to me … and us. It won't be difficult!" Luo Zheng said.
At this moment, Gu Pei 'er frowned and said, "Yu Ze, stop. It's not too late to verify it first."
Yu Ze's smile turned cold. At this moment, the black saber in his hand suddenly slashed out. However, this slash wasn't aimed at those Divine Sea Realm warriors. Instead, he tilted the edge of the black saber. A thick saber energy suddenly extended out, pulling out a black saber energy that was dozens of feet long. The end of the saber energy sank into the ground, shooting out into the distance!
"Whoosh …"
If one were to look down from above the Heavenly Stellar Holy Land, they would see a thin line from the center of the Heavenly Stellar Holy Land all the way to the edge of the Holy Land.
"Okay, I'll listen to Senior Sister!" Yu Ze smiled. For him, these rats couldn't be killed, so they didn't have much meaning.
Then, the flower in Gu Pei 'er's right eye spun gently and removed the binding force on the Heavenly Wind War Emperor and the others. At this moment, all the corpses regained their freedom.
Even after regaining the ability to move, none of the Divine Sea Realm experts chose to run away.
These corpses couldn't use True Yuan. They couldn't even fly with their physical bodies, let alone run fast. Moreover, they had no power to resist the seven Ultimate Divine Realm experts. Choosing to run away now was no different than courting death.
"Let's go. Your physical body shouldn't be too far from the Heavenly Stellar Holy Land, right?" Qing Feng said calmly.
"How far can it be? The Great Thousand World itself isn't big. Hmph. "The burly man sneered.
Luo Zheng didn't say anything. Instead, he said to the Heavenly Wind War Emperor and the others, "Everyone, let's go out together!"
The warriors of the four Divine Kingdoms nodded silently and followed behind Luo Zheng. The seven Ultimate Divine Realm Almighties followed at the very back of the group.
After leaving the Heavenly Stellar Holy Land, it took them an incense stick's worth of time to repair the Teleportation Array. Everyone entered the Heavenly Feather Sacred Ocean again and returned along the Saint's Road.
However, when they returned to the Saint's Road, the Divine Sea Realm experts took out the giant shields in their hands. They wanted to use the shields to block the blue arrows.

Chapter 903
Those blue arrows were powerful, but they couldn't penetrate Qing Feng's barrier.
Under the protection of this barrier, they easily passed through the Path of the Saint and entered the Divine Emperor Secret Realm.
"It turns out that this place is connected to a huge graveyard. No wonder you all went into the corpses. These corpses must have come from this graveyard, right?" Boss Mo asked.
Although the seven from the Demi-Immortal Tribe looked down on the strength of the warriors from the lower world, they admired them for coming up with this method to avoid the power of the blood sacrifice …
"Yes!" Luo Zheng nodded gently.
"So, where are your corpses?" Boss Mo asked again.
"On the other side of the Divine Emperor Island," Luo Zheng answered.
Generally speaking, when returning to the graveyard, the warriors of the four Divine Kingdoms would place the corpses in their original places. For so many years, they had been doing this.
After all, there weren't many corpses that were so well-preserved, especially the corpses of the Divine Sea Realm. Every one they used was one less.
If the corpses were thrown away after each use, there wouldn't be many corpses left in the graveyard.
This time, the Divine Sea Realm warriors didn't do that. Instead, they directly controlled the corpses to go through the graveyard and to the seaside of the Divine Emperor Island.
There were still some Spirit Sea Realm warriors guarding the seaside of the Divine Emperor Island. After all, everyone's physical bodies were left at the seaside. If they didn't send anyone to stay here, it would be troublesome if their physical bodies were all destroyed in case something happened.
In addition, there were White Brow Daoist and Bai Yuerong. They didn't enter the Heavenly Stellar Holy Land.
Many shrines were lined up by the sea, and many corporeal bodies were sitting cross-legged on the ground, meditating.
"They're back. They're back!" A Divine Sea Realm warrior who stayed behind shouted.
Bai Yuerong and White Brow Daoist's eyes flashed. They stared at the corpses with strange expressions. "What happened? They directly controlled the corpses to come out? "Bai Yuerong asked in surprise.
As for Daoist White Brow, his eyes were fixed on the girl at the very front! In this young girl's arms, there was a furry little thing! Although its appearance was very strange and didn't look like a dragon, the aura that leaked out of it made White Brow Daoist very sure!
"Ancestral Dragon? They got it! " White Brow Daoist's face lit up with joy. He was about to fly over, but at this moment, he felt several powerful pressures!
There was no expert in the Great Thousand World who could make Daoist White Brow feel pressured!
Even Luo Zheng couldn't give White Brow Daoist such pressure. After all, although Luo Zheng had displayed powerful skills, his cultivation base was only at the Life and Death Realm. As for his strength … Putting aside the fact that he had merged with the will of the world, he was probably no different from an ordinary Divine Sea Realm warrior.
"Seven people! ..........................................
After a long journey, the Tianfeng War Emperor and the others finally returned to the Divine Emperor Island. Their expressions relaxed. No one was willing to put their soul into a corpse. It didn't feel good. But to avoid the power of blood sacrifice, they could only use such a clumsy method.
"Everyone, let's return to our bodies!" The Tianfeng War Emperor and the others had just escaped death. They couldn't be bothered with White Brow Daoist. Their souls left their corpses and flew back to their bodies!
After the corpses lost their souls, they fell to the ground one by one and scrambled to return to their bodies.
As for Luo Zheng, he wouldn't treat Feng Nianyun's body like that. Feng Nianyun wasn't dead. If he treated her body like that, she would probably go berserk.
Therefore, he found an open space and sat down. Then, his soul left his body and flew back to his body.
Luo Zheng slowly opened his eyes. At this moment, he saw the newly born Ancestral Dragon running towards him!
"Eh?"
Luo Zheng's face revealed a puzzled expression. Didn't the Ancestral Dragon have imprints? He was occupying Feng Nianyun's body. Logically speaking, he should see Feng Nianyun as his mother. Why was this little dragon running towards him?
As soon as this question popped up in his mind, Qinglong's faint voice drifted over. "The Ancestral Dragon does have imprints. However, its imprints aren't differentiated by appearance. They are differentiated by soul … But thank you, Luo Zheng. You did it!"
Previously, Luo Zheng's soul had left his body and distanced himself from Qinglong. Although the two maintained a connection, Qinglong couldn't observe the surroundings. Now that Luo Zheng had his body back, this problem naturally didn't exist!
Qinglong was naturally happy to see Luo Zheng bring the Ancestral Dragon with him.
"Ah, this guy is a man …"
Not far away, Gu Pei 'er saw a soul leave Feng Nianyun's body and enter the body of a man with delicate features. She couldn't help but cry out, and then a look of disappointment appeared on her face.
Beside her, Boss Mo looked at Luo Zheng indifferently and nodded slightly. "He does have the imprint of my Demi-Immortal Clan on him … This guy has been to the Twinklegem Pagoda."
"Even if he has the imprint, it doesn't mean he has a title. The second row of the Talent Stele is enough to leave the imprint," Qingfeng said lightly.
Boss Mo nodded and continued, "It doesn't matter. We can continue to verify. After all, we have to find the Twinklegem Pagoda eventually."
"Man, this guy is a man …" Gu Peier was now very conflicted. As for whether Luo Zheng was recognized by her Demi-Immortal Clan, she didn't care.
The newly born Ancestral Dragon didn't have the slightest sense of alienation between Luo Zheng and it jumped into Luo Zheng's arms and kept licking Luo Zheng. At this moment, White Brow Daoist walked towards Luo Zheng with a smile on his face and said, "Luo Tianxing, thank you for bringing the Ancestral Dragon out of the Heavenly Stellar Holy Land!"
Holding the Ancestral Dragon in his arms, Luo Zheng glanced at White Brow Daoist and asked coldly, "Why are you thanking me?"
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, White Brow Daoist frowned and said, "We've been searching for this Ancestral Dragon egg for so long and haven't taken it out. Now that you've brought it out, I naturally have to thank you."
"Haha … You don't have to thank him, because the Ancestral Dragon doesn't belong to you." At this time, Yu Ze walked over slowly with the black saber in his hand.
They were all Divine Realm experts and both sides noticed each other in an instant. However, the seven people of the Demi-Immortal Clan didn't put White Brow Daoist in their eyes. White Brow Daoist thought that he was alone and the Ancestral Dragon was in Luo Zheng's hands, so he didn't pay attention to the seven people.
Hearing Yu Ze's words, White Brow Daoist's face suddenly darkened. "Where did you come from? I'm the subordinate of Thunder Punishing Skylord … "
White Brow Daoist knew that he was no match for the seven people in front of him. Fortunately, the force behind him was well-known in the world. Facing this group of experts of the same level as him, White Brow Daoist could only use the force behind him.
However, before he could finish his sentence, the burly man laughed and said, "You're using the Celestial Venerables to scare people. Haha, you can use this trick to swindle people in the lower realms, but since we're all from the Great Realm, there's no need to use this trick!"
"Swindle people?" White Brow Daoist narrowed his eyes.
At this time, Boss Mo said, "The weakest of the three forces of the Human Clan is Thunder Punishing Skylord. However, we can't comment on Skylord level experts!"
"It's good that you know!" White Brow Daoist smiled faintly. He could see that the man wearing the silver mask was the strongest. He might be able to fight the other six people one-on-one. However, he had no confidence in facing this man wearing the silver mask. Therefore, White Brow Daoist restrained himself.
"Putting aside Skylord experts, this Ancestral Dragon must belong to our Demi-Immortal Clan. It's impossible for other clans to get it," Boss Mo continued.
"Demi-Immortal Clan!" White Brow Daoist's eyelids suddenly trembled when he heard Boss Mo's words.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 904
Just like Feng Nianyun's reaction, White Brow Daoist was extremely conflicted when he heard that the seven of them were from the Demi-Immortal Tribe!
The Demi-Immortal Tribe didn't have a good reputation in the Upper Realm. This tribe had mixed origins and was few in number. However, almost every one of them was an elite.
The biggest problem was that these guys were a bunch of lunatics from head to toe!
Originally, White Brow Daoist was hoping to announce the name of the force behind him. Although the Thunder Punishing Skylord was the weakest of the three major forces of the human race, it still occupied two thousand great worlds in the Upper Realm. It wasn't inferior to any of the other tribes.
If he had known that this group of people were from the Demi-Immortal Tribe, White Brow Daoist wouldn't have even bothered to speak!
The problem was this Ancestral Dragon …
The Divine Sea Realm experts of the four Divine Kingdoms all retreated to the side. The Tianfeng War Emperor, Zhou Huang, and the others didn't say anything. After all, this Ancestral Dragon wouldn't fall into their hands no matter how much they fought over it. If the Supervisor wanted to boast about his strength, he could snatch it from the hands of this group of Divine Realm experts.
White Brow Daoist rolled his eyes and said, "So you are from the Demi-Immortal Tribe. However, this Ancestral Dragon, I've already …"
"No matter how much effort you've spent, it's impossible for these natives to enter the Heavenly Stellar Holy Land and obtain the Ancestral Dragon egg." Boss Mo rudely interrupted White Brow Daoist's words. "This Ancestral Dragon was originally hatched by us. That's why we took it out of the Heavenly Stellar Holy Land!"
Yu Ze and the burly man fiddled with the weapons in their hands. From time to time, they would stare at White Brow Daoist and grin. It seemed that they were looking forward to a battle with White Brow Daoist.
White Brow Daoist continued, "The Demi-Immortal Tribe can't be so unreasonable. I …"
Who knew that before White Brow Daoist could finish his words, Yu Ze and the burly man rushed towards White Brow Daoist!
They attacked without hesitation!
How terrifying was the speed of Divine Realm experts?
Under the watchful eyes of the crowd, before they could even blink, the two men had already launched an aggressive attack on White Brow Daoist.
"Hu …"
However, White Brow Daoist's reaction was extremely fast. When the two men attacked, he also quickly retreated several hundred feet away. At the same time, he shouted, "Stop! Let's talk things over … "After saying that, a small shield in his hand expanded in the wind and transformed into rays of light, blocking in front of him.
Yu Ze and the burly man revealed a regretful expression. At the same time, they cursed and slowly retreated back to Boss Mo's side.
These White Brow Daoist. White brow, White Brow, White Brow Daoist. White Brow Daoist.
White Brow Daoist knew that he had no chance of winning. It seemed that he could only find a way to ask for help through the Reverse Spirit Channel. He glanced at Luo Zheng, who was holding the Ancestral Dragon. He felt strange. If the Demi-Immortal Tribe didn't lie and the Ancestral Dragon was hatched by them, why was the Ancestral Dragon following Luo Zheng?
Boss Mo didn't care about White Brow Daoist's thoughts. Their purpose was simple. They wanted to verify whether Luo Zheng really had a title. As for the Ancestral Dragon following Luo Zheng, they wouldn't forcibly take it back for the time being.
"Since you really have the mark of the Demi-Immortal Tribe, the next step is to find the Exquisite Pagoda." Boss Mo walked towards Luo Zheng. His tone had softened a lot. Because he felt the mark of the Demi-Immortal Tribe on Luo Zheng, he could indeed treat Luo Zheng as one of his own.
Luo Zheng nodded. He would never get involved in the dispute between White Brow Daoist and the Demi-Immortal Tribe. So Luo Zheng nodded. "Of course. I will take you to the Exquisite Pagoda."
After that, Luo Zheng's gaze focused on Feng Nianyun's body. At the same time, he asked Qinglong in his mind, "What … what about Feng Nianyun?"
"Sigh, she is under the Soul Seal Technique. Although her body is alive, it is no different from being dead. Only after finding someone to undo the Soul Seal Technique will she be able to recover. However, the Soul Seal Technique is a secret technique of the Soul Tribe. There are too few creatures who are good at it …" Qinglong sighed. The nine True Dragons had a close relationship with Feng Nianyun. Qinglong was naturally very concerned about her.
Since he had brought Feng Nianyun out this time, Qinglong naturally wanted to help Feng Nianyun.
However, at this moment, he saw Gu Peier walking towards Feng Nianyun with a face full of disappointment …
"Is this just an empty shell? A body without a soul … "Gu Peier muttered as she stared at Feng Nianyun. At the same time, she reached out to touch Feng Nianyun's face. She was interested in Feng Nianyun, but she had no interest in Luo Zheng.
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng was a little speechless. Gu Peier was indeed a weirdo. He guessed that Feng Nianyun was starting to get angry in her inner world.
Sure enough, at this moment, Feng Nianyun's consciousness suddenly transmitted. "You are the body without a soul!"
"Oh! There is another soul? "Gu Peier's face suddenly revealed a look of pleasant surprise.
At this moment, Luo Zheng said to Gu Peier, "Her soul is sealed in her inner world."
"Sealed in her inner world?" Gu Peier's eyes suddenly flashed. Then she said, "The Soul Seal Technique?"
Although it was a secret technique of the Soul Clan, the Soul Seal Technique was even more famous. Moreover, Gu Peier was originally proficient in soul cultivation techniques. When she heard that Feng Nianyun's soul was sealed in her inner world, Gu Peier instantly guessed it.
Luo Zheng nodded. He had an idea in his mind. Since Qinglong couldn't undo the Soul Seal Technique, could he borrow the silver-haired woman's hand? Weren't there many capable people in the Demi-Immortal Clan?
"You … can you help her undo the Soul Seal Technique?" Luo Zheng asked.
Gu Peier's expression immediately turned serious. Only those who understood the Great Soul Sealing Technique would understand how difficult it was to undo it. Gu Peier thought for a moment, and seemed to have made up her mind about something. She then said, "I can undo it, but …"
There was hope. Luo Zheng's heart was slightly happy, but he quickly asked, "But what?"
Gu Peier said, "But there is a condition!"
"What condition?" This time, before Luo Zheng could ask, Feng Nianyun in her inner world spoke first.
In the past hundred thousand years, because of the Soul Seal Technique, she had obtained a lifespan that far exceeded that of ordinary warriors. Her body had even stopped aging, and the Deva's Decay hadn't come for a long time. In fact, the Soul Seal Technique was one of the methods that some Upper Realm warriors used to extend their lifespan.
The problem was that in the past hundred thousand years, she could only be alone in her inner world, slowly building a world that belonged to her …
Even in her dreams, Feng Nianyun hoped that someone could undo her Soul Seal Technique.
Now that Gu Peier said that she could undo it, even if she hated Gu Peier, Feng Nianyun was extremely excited at this moment.
Gu Peier chuckled and temporarily forgot about Luo Zheng. She returned to her cheerful nature and said with a smile, "But you have to marry me. Is that okay?"
"Cough cough …" Hearing this, Luo Zheng couldn't help but cough violently.
When the others of the Demi-Immortal Clan heard this, they couldn't help but shake their heads. They knew Gu Peier's personality and even more so her preferences.
Feng Nianyun was silent in her inner world. At this moment, she didn't know what to say.
She was different from Gu Peier. She couldn't fall in love with a woman no matter what. But in comparison, the Soul Sealing Technique brought Feng Nianyun much more torment.
Seeing that Feng Nianyun wasn't responding to her, Gu Peier blinked and said, "You know that it's quite troublesome to undo your Soul Seal Technique. I can ask someone to help me, but I'll have to pay a hefty price! This condition isn't too much, right? "
Luo Zheng shook his head slightly. He hugged the Ancestral Gragon in his arms and didn't say anything. The relationship between these two women didn't have much to do with him.
Now that he had left the Tianchen Sacred Land, he had temporarily brought the Ancestral Gragon with him. He had also obtained the life crystal to save Ning Yudie. This time, he would first return to the Central Domain to wake Ning Yudie up, then take this group of people to the Sea God Continent.
Feng Nianyun and Gu Peier talked about something, but Gu Peier finally revealed a satisfied smile. It seemed that the two of them had come to an agreement.

Chapter 905
The seven of them couldn't solve the matter of the Evil Dragon's Dust Seal, so they could only ask the Demi-Immortal Clan to send other almighty experts down.
In comparison, the Exquisite Pagoda was more important.
Although Boss Mo and the others didn't believe that Luo Zheng really had the title of Gold Demi-Immortal, they really wanted to see how Luo Zheng was recognized by the Talent Stele. Was his talent ranked in the second row or the first row? Or did he get a Bronze Title?
They needed to verify all these.
However, Luo Zheng replied indifferently, "I will take you to the Exquisite Pagoda, but before that, I have something important to do."
Initially, after Luo Zheng obtained the Life Crystal, he planned to return to the Central Domain as soon as possible. However, he promised Qinglong that he would enter the Heavenly Star Secret Realm to obtain the Ancestral Dragon egg, so there was a slight delay.
Now, this Ancestral Dragon was following him, but it was hard to say where it belonged.
Even if this Ancestral Dragon had his mark, the Demi-Immortal Clan would never give up. Moreover, White Brow Daoist was eyeing him covetously. It seemed that White Brow Daoist wouldn't give up either.
At present, Luo Zheng was the least anxious. Whether it was White Brow Daoist's side or the Demi-Immortal Clan's side, they didn't show any absolute malice towards him. Moreover, he had indeed obtained the title of Gold Demi-Immortal in the Exquisite Pagoda. What should he do next, Luo Zheng just needed to cooperate. However, before that, he had to return to the Central Domain to save Ning Yudie's life!
"Important matter? Don't tell me you don't dare to go? " Yuze said coldly. Even if Luo Zheng had the mark of the Demi-Immortal Clan, it was only recognized by the Demi-Immortal Clan. Yuze felt that Luo Zheng was bragging and didn't dare to verify it with them.
Hearing Yuze's question, Luo Zheng said seriously, "I'm not from the Divine Kingdom Continent. I only came to the Divine Kingdom Continent to find medicine and save people! If you don't believe me, you can slowly search in the Great Wide World. The Great Wide World isn't that big for you, right? "
The Great Thousand World wasn't that big, but it was still difficult to pinpoint the exact location of the Seagod Continent. The four Divine Kingdoms were relatively close to the Central Domain, but the Seagod Continent was very far away. It wasn't that the Demi-Immortals couldn't do it, it was just that Luo Zheng didn't provide them with the coordinates and directions, so the Demi-Immortals couldn't find the Exquisite Pagoda in a short time.
"Hmph, isn't that simple? Directly searching your soul will do. Little Senior Apprentice Sister is most proficient in this kind of method, right? "Yu Ze continued to say with a cold smile.
Luo Zheng's expression darkened. But then Gu Pei 'er said, "He's been acknowledged by the Demi-Immortal Clan. If he joins us in the future, we'll all be colleagues. Yuze, can you not speak like that?"
Yu Ze snickered and continued, "Just a Lower Realm martial artist. Being fortunate enough to be able to get on the Talent Stele's list, at best, it's just leaving his name on the second row! Colleagues? Is this guy qualified? "
Gu Pei 'er shook her head. This Yu Ze sometimes spoke without thinking. Compared to other people, she still had a faint glimmer of hope. After all, Luo Zheng really didn't seem to be lying! Then Gu Pei 'er said, "If he's telling the truth, if he really has a Gold Demi-Immortal title, what should we do?"
If it was really confirmed that Luo Zheng's talent was worthy of a Gold Demi-Immortal title, it would be a big deal for the entire Demi-Immortal Clan!
"Haha," Yu Ze laughed and said, "Little Senior Apprentice Sister, you've always been astute. I can't believe you actually believe this kid's words. I …"
Beside them, Qing Feng, the burly man, and the others also laughed. If Luo Zheng had lied about having a Silver Demi-Immortal title or a Bronze Demi-Immortal title, they might have believed it. Unfortunately, Luo Zheng's title sounded too unbelievable. If they hadn't seen it with their own eyes, they wouldn't have believed it even if they were beaten to death.
"If it's true, what do you plan to do?" Gu Pei 'er looked serious and stared at Yu Ze. She didn't know why, but this time, Gu Pei' er chose to believe Luo Zheng. Maybe it was purely her sixth sense, but she really had a hunch that this matter wasn't fake!
"Xiu, xiu, xiu …"
Yu Ze gently turned the black saber in his hand and placed it in front of him. He said indifferently, "If it's true, I'll swallow the Onyx Saber!"
"Really?" Gu Pei 'er blinked her eyes and smiled.
Yu Ze nodded hard, "Really!"
Looking at the two people betting like this, Luo Zheng's face revealed a strange expression. This Onyx Saber was unusually sharp. It was at least a Supreme Artifact. This guy was going to swallow it. Let's see if he could swallow it when the time came!
"You two, stop fooling around." Boss Mo said to Luo Zheng, "Since you said you want to go back to save people, then leave immediately. We can take you there. It won't take much time!"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng laughed instead!
Right now, there should be a terrible storm in the Chaotic Astral Ocean. Even a Divine Sea Realm expert would find it difficult to pass through. Even though Luo Zheng was a Life and Death Realm warrior, if he forced his way through the Chaotic Astral Ocean, there was a possibility that he would die!
The magical powers of these Ultimate God Realm warriors were almost unimaginable. If they could take him back to the Central Domain, it would be the best thing for him.
"In that case, we'll leave immediately!" After saying that, Boss Mo waved his hand, and a blood-red rune appeared in his hand. He threw it casually, and the blood-red rune formed a huge red cross. Then, the cross was firmly engraved on the ground.
"What is that?" Some warriors of the four Divine Kingdoms asked in a low voice.
"Hmph, it's just a locator talisman used by the Demi-Immortal Clan! Through special means, they can come back here at any time! " Daoist White Eyebrows said coldly. His face was still very ugly. Daoist White Eyebrows had heard the conversation between Luo Zheng and them clearly. It seemed that Luo Zheng had been tested in the Trial Tower of the Demi-Immortal Tribe?
The Demi-Immortal Tribe didn't have a good reputation in the universe, but it was very famous. Daoist White Eyebrows also knew about the three Trial Towers of the Demi-Immortal Tribe. It was said that they had lost one Trial Tower? When did Luo Tianxing go through the Trial Tower's test?
When Luo Zheng was crossing the tribulation, Daoist White Eyebrows already knew that this kid had opened the Mahayana Lotus! At that time, Daoist White Eyebrows was overjoyed. For him, the Ancestral Dragon egg was an important matter, but Luo Zheng was even more important.
Just now, when he saw Luo Zheng holding the Ancestral Dragon on the Divine Emperor Island, he was overjoyed. He didn't expect the Demi-Immortal Tribe to come in. Now, it seemed that Luo Zheng had been tested in the Trial Tower and was marked with the mark of the Demi-Immortal Tribe …
Therefore, at this moment, Daoist White Eyebrows felt a trace of despair in his heart, and his face became very gloomy.
After setting up the positioning talisman, Boss Mo raised his crutch again and waved it gently toward the void. Then, he chopped down, and an irregular space crack appeared out of thin air.
"Qing Feng, open it," Boss Mo immediately ordered.
After Qing Feng nodded, he walked toward the space crack that was ten feet high. He stretched out his hand, and a boundary shot out from his hand. Then, the boundary began to expand rapidly. There was a burst of crackling sounds in the passage, and the space crack was opened by Qing Feng's boundary, forming a small "space hole".
At this time, the martial artist named Ha Peng took out an eight trigrams wheel that was several feet wide. This eight trigrams wheel expanded in the wind. It seemed that this was their group's flying magic weapon.
After Boss Mo, Yun Ze, and the others jumped on it, Gu Peier looked at Luo Zheng and said, "Okay, let's go!"
Luo Zheng nodded, but his eyes were fixed on Huo Yuner, King Yan, and the others. Although he didn't stay in the Divine Kingdom Continent for long, he made many friends!
Huo Yuner, King Yan, and the others all had different thoughts. Previously, Luo Zheng had saved them several times. This time, in the Tianchen Mystery Land, if it wasn't for Luo Zheng, they wouldn't have been able to come back. Not only them, but even their fathers, all the Divine Sea Realm experts in the four Divine Kingdoms, probably wouldn't have been able to come back!
Therefore, besides being grateful to Luo Zheng, King Yan and the others were also reluctant to part with him.
The current situation didn't allow them to communicate too much. Even the four Monarchs chose to remain silent in front of this group of Divine Realm experts.
Under their gazes, Luo Zheng just smiled and said, "I will be back!"

Chapter 906
Seeing his daughter suddenly rush out, the Tianfeng War Emperor's expression changed.
Although he was a monarch of the Divine Kingdom Continent and was definitely one of the top figures in the lower realm, he was nothing in front of this group of Divine Realm experts!
What was worse was that these seven people had been acting a little crazy since the beginning. He was afraid that his daughter's actions would provoke these people! The Tianfeng War Emperor wanted to stop her, but his daughter rushed out like a sharp arrow. He couldn't stop her in time.
The Tianfeng War Emperor was most concerned about Huo Yuner, so he knew her character very well. He had long seen that his daughter liked Luo Zheng, but she had always been gentle and reserved. He didn't expect her to be so impulsive.
Huo Yuner rushed out and finally stopped in front of Luo Zheng. She stared at Luo Zheng with her beautiful eyes. There was no smile on her face, but her expression was very serious.
Huo Yuner bit her lips and asked softly, "Are you really coming back?"
Seeing Huo Yuner's expression, Luo Zheng smiled and nodded seriously. "Of course I will come back."
She looked like she wanted to say something, but it was just a moment of impulse. Now that she realized that everyone was looking at her, she blushed shyly. However, this hesitation only lasted for a breath's time. For a young girl, some things were indeed difficult, but she made up her mind. She took the necklace from her chest, walked forward, and put it on Luo Zheng's neck.
The necklace wasn't expensive, but it was a keepsake for her.
Huo Yuner then said, "I'll wait for you to come back!" She forced a smile and turned to leave.
Wearing the necklace, Luo Zheng looked at Huo Yuner's back with a complicated expression. He nodded silently and got on the Wheel of Eight Trigrams. As for Yuze and the burly man's faint smile, Luo Zheng chose to ignore them.
In addition to Luo Zheng and the seven members of the Demi-Immortal Clan, Feng Nianyun was also brought into the Eight Trigrams Wheel.
As the Eight Trigrams Wheel rotated slowly, it carried everyone into the Space Hole. After a while, the Eight Trigrams Wheel disappeared before the eyes of the people from the Divine Kingdom. After Qing Feng removed the boundary that propped up the Space Crack, the Space Crack quickly recovered. The Space Crack was actually a "wound" of the entire Vast Thousand World. However, the Vast Thousand World originally had a certain "self-healing" ability, so the Space Crack was just an insignificant wound to the entire world. The Space Crack quickly recovered and gradually dissipated. However, if a warrior who was proficient in the Law of Space was able to see the shadow in that area, that was the weakness of the space.
In the eyes of the warriors of the four Divine Kingdoms, the Demi-Immortal Tribe was like a plague. As soon as they disappeared, the Divine Sea Realm warriors felt relieved.
Only Daoist White Eyebrows had a dark face.
At this moment, Bai Yuerong said, "Supervisor, do we still need to report Luo Zheng's information?"
"Humph, report! Why didn't he report it? Today, we will open the Reverse Spirit Channel! The Demi-Immortal Tribe's hands are too long! "Daoist White Eyebrows could no longer suppress the anger in his heart. He almost roared out these words.
In Daoist White Eyebrows' view, whether it was Luo Zheng or the Ancestral Gragon, they must be discovered by him! If the Demi-Immortal Tribe wanted to get their hands on them, they would have to see if they had the ability to do so!
As for Daoist White Eyebrows' anger, the Divine Kings turned a blind eye to it. They were lucky to be able to keep their lives thanks to Luo Zheng. After tidying up, they set off and left the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea. The power of the blood sacrifice had slowly begun to recover. They had to leave this place before the power of the blood sacrifice fully recovered …
In the darkness, the Eight Trigrams Wheel was shuttling back and forth rapidly.
There was no light in the Space Crack, and nothing existed. On the other side of the Vast Thousand World was a void.
After Luo Zheng pointed out the direction, Ha Peng controlled the Eight Trigrams Wheel to move forward, and its speed became faster and faster. This time, after passing through the Chaotic Astral Ocean and walking on the other side of the world, they would not be threatened by the deadly storm at all.
Previously, Luo Zheng and Yun Longtao had fought in the void, but only for a very short time. This time, however, they were going to travel a long distance in the void.
At first, Luo Zheng was somewhat curious. However, the surroundings were pitch-black, as if nothing existed. Luo Zheng withdrew his gaze and sat on the Eight Trigrams Wheel. After thinking for a while, he asked, "Is there nothing in the void?"
The seven people of the Demi-Immortal Tribe were also sitting cross-legged on the Eight Trigrams Wheel. When Yuze heard Luo Zheng's question, he said sarcastically, "Nonsense, why else would it be called the void?"
Luo Zheng rolled his eyes. Among these people, except for Boss Mo and Gu Peier, the others were not easy to get along with. They were either very arrogant, or like this guy, they spoke very unpleasant words. Therefore, Luo Zheng simply did not speak. He closed his eyes and sat cross-legged in meditation.
However, at this time, Luo Zheng suddenly felt a gloomy and cold aura sweeping towards him!
At the same time, the seven people of the Demi-Immortal Tribe opened their eyes, especially Boss Mo, who suddenly stood up and frowned.
It seemed that it was not only Luo Zheng who felt the gloomy and cold aura, but everyone felt it. Gu Peier frowned and asked, "What's that?"
Boss Mo's expression was very cold, "Bad luck, it seems that we have encountered a shadow creature!"
"What the hell is a shadow creature?" Yuze shouted.
Qing Feng opened his mouth and said, "It's a creature that lives on the other side of the world. Therefore, I have to correct you. There is something in the void! Although the number of shadow creatures is rare, they are very strong. We are in trouble! "
"Why is there such a thing …" Yuze's expression was unnatural. He had just used this to teach Luo Zheng a lesson. In the end, this shadow creature appeared, which was equivalent to reality slapping him hard in the face.
As for Luo Zheng, he rolled his eyes. He never liked to argue with idiots.
"Huhuhu …"
In the darkness, even these Divine Realm warriors could not see clearly what was going on in the distance. Moreover, the Eight Trigrams Wheel was still flying at high speed. When they heard this sound, everyone immediately became nervous. Everyone began to look around, but they could not find the shadow creature!
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed, and a wisp of emerald green light flashed in his pupils. After the Clear Eyes Spirit Pupil was activated, the darkness in the void slowly disappeared and was replaced by a green world. After he looked around, his gaze fell on the back. At the same time, his face revealed a look of horror, and he shouted, "What is that!"
Tens of thousands of feet behind the Eight Trigrams Wheel, there was a gigantic head that was following it at a high speed!
That head looked like a human's head, but its size was comparable to a huge mountain. Behind this head hung a thick python, which seemed to be the head's hair!
Using the Clear Eyes Spirit Pupil, Luo Zheng saw the huge head in the distance, but the others could not see it.
"What did you see?" Boss Mo asked nervously. Any shadow creature was not easy to deal with, and this was the reason why he was nervous. As for how Luo Zheng saw the shadow creature, although Boss Mo was curious, he did not ask.
Luo Zheng stared at the approaching head, and then he replied, "A head, a huge head!"
"A head?"
Boss Mo pondered for a moment, and his eyes suddenly revealed a look of fear. Then he said, "So unlucky? In the shadows of the Great Thousand World, there is such a thing? "
Qing Feng's face was also very ugly. At this moment, even his voice was trembling, "Oh no, quickly, quickly leave this void!"
"Leave the void? But the outside world should be the sky of the Chaotic Astral Ocean! This Star Sea Storm is also … "Luo Zheng said.
Boss Mo snorted, "Compared to that thing, the Star Sea Storm is just a child's play! Qing Feng, get ready! " After that, Boss Mo waved the crutch in his hand forward again. It could be seen that this time, Boss Mo almost exerted all his strength, and the crutch seemed to be mixed with a great force.
"Rip!"
Because the force was too great, the space exploded together, and it sounded extremely harsh, as if tens of thousands of people were tearing a huge piece of cloth at the same time!
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 907
The huge crack in front of them was suddenly torn open. Qing Feng clapped his hands together forcefully. Then, his hands separated and intertwined, and a boundary instantly opened up the crack in the space.
"Huff, huff, huff …"
At this moment, the thing behind them was getting closer and closer.
Even if he did not use his Clear Eyes Spirit Eyes to observe, he could see a huge silhouette with his naked eyes. It was indeed a head, and that head had already opened its huge mouth, wanting to swallow the Eight Trigrams Wheel in one gulp!
The big mouth was like a huge cave. As for the Wheel of Eight Trigrams, it was less than five feet wide. Compared to the big mouth, it was as small as a mosquito.
When everyone looked back, they could even see that there were many shadow creatures hiding in the big mouth. Because the light was too dim, they couldn't distinguish the shapes of those shadow creatures. They could only vaguely see their horrifying silhouettes.
The shadow creature was drifting at an extremely fast speed. The distance between it and Luo Zheng and the others was rapidly closing. Looking at the momentum, they were afraid that they would be swallowed by the head in one gulp before they could pass through the crack!
At this moment, Ha Peng gritted his teeth and suddenly took out a sharp weapon. He ruthlessly cut his wrist and slapped his palm on the Wheel of Eight Trigrams!
"Puchi!"
A stream of golden blood suddenly splashed out. As soon as it splashed on the Wheel of Eight Trigrams, it was absorbed by the Wheel of Eight Trigrams.
"Let's go!"
"Whoosh …"
After absorbing Ha Peng's blood, the Wheel of Eight Trigrams suddenly emitted a bright light around it. Its speed instantly increased by more than two times!
With the help of the flickering light of the Wheel of Eight Trigrams, everyone could see the creature in the big mouth. Luo Zheng, Gu Pei 'er, the burly man, and the others all took a deep breath.
As for Qing Feng and Boss Mo, although they looked nervous, they seemed to have guessed what it was. Therefore, they didn't have such a shocked expression.
After the speed increased, the Wheel of Eight Trigrams drilled into the crack in an instant!
However, the shadow creature only emitted a cold and gloomy aura. That aura covered the Wheel of Eight Trigrams, and everyone couldn't help but shiver.
This cold and gloomy aura contained the negative will of killing, blood, cruelty, and at the same time, a trace of unwillingness!
"Hmph! That thing can only survive in the void. Once it leaves the void, it's like a fish out of water. It can't survive and it doesn't dare to chase after us … "Boss Mo snorted coldly and said after rushing out of the void. However, his face was still ghastly pale.
However, as soon as his voice fell, the sound of wind howled in everyone's ears. Millions of sharp wind blades circled above everyone's heads!
"What the f * ck is this!" Yu Ze screamed.
From the bottom of their hearts, the Paramount God experts of the Demi-Immortal Clan didn't think much of the lower realms. Little did they know that there were exceptions in the Greater Thousand Worlds.
The green Wind Blades contained the fourth level of the Laws of Wind, while the black Wind Blades were like quenched black blades that contained the fifth level of the Laws of Wind.
Previously, when they heard Luo Zheng's warning that the storm in the Chaotic Astral Sea was very powerful, they thought, how powerful could it be? Just like the power of blood sacrifice in the Heavenly Feather Holy Sea, in the eyes of the Divine Sea experts of the four Divine Kingdoms, it was an existence that they couldn't get close to. They could only enter through corpses. However, for the seven of them, it wasn't an obstacle at all.
Now, it seemed that they had indeed underestimated this vast storm!
"Dang, dang, dang …"
Seven or eight black Wind Blades shot toward them. At this moment, the giant scythe in the burly man's hand shook slightly. The fiery red giant scythe rolled up and cut the Wind Blades into pieces.
However, it was obvious that the burly man had used eighty to ninety percent of his strength! Under the huge counterforce, the entire Wheel of Eight Trigrams began to shake violently.
Perhaps they weren't afraid of a few Wind Blades, but the Wind Blades in this storm were almost innumerable …
After cutting down a few Wind Blades, there were tens of thousands of green Wind Blades in front of them. There were also thousands of black Wind Blades shooting toward them.
"Qing Feng! What are you waiting for? Hurry up and set up a barrier! "Yu Ze roared.
Qing Feng glared at Yu Ze. He clapped his hands, and a barrier enveloped everyone. Then, he said coldly, "My barrier can't stop the fifth level of the Laws of Wind!"
As soon as his voice fell, a violent knocking sound came from the barrier. Then, everyone saw Qing Feng's barrier suddenly shatter!
Qing Feng's barrier did contain the Laws of Causality, but the Laws of Causality he comprehended were of a lower level. Among the Laws of Heaven, although the Laws of Causality were superior, there were still differences between them. A lower level Law of Causality might not be able to resist a higher level Law of Origin! Moreover, there were so many Wind Blades!
"I can't count on you!" The Onyx Blade in Yu Ze's hand shook slightly. Suddenly, a black aura bloomed from the blade. Then, as he waved it crazily, hundreds of black blade energies shot forward.
"Puff, puff, puff …"
As the Saber Qi and the Wind Blades intertwined, a series of dull sounds erupted. Yu Ze's black Saber Qi was also extremely powerful, but it could only shatter those green Wind Blades. As for the black Wind Blades, they directly ate his Saber Qi!
"Whoosh!"
Several black Wind Blades pierced through Yu Ze's blade energies and rushed toward Luo Zheng at an extremely fast speed!
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng's eyebrows suddenly rose, and his expression suddenly changed! At this moment, everyone was using their own methods to resist the chaotic blade energies. Luo Zheng couldn't count on these Ultimate God Realm experts.
"Be careful!" Gu Pei 'er noticed this scene. However, she had just dodged three Wind Blades, so she couldn't help Luo Zheng.
Facing the black Wind Blades that were close at hand, Luo Zheng clenched his fists. The power of the dragon scales surged, and he suddenly punched those Wind Blades!
"Idiot, it will directly cut off your hand!" Yu Ze saw this scene and scolded. Even Ultimate God Realm experts like them didn't dare to meet the Wind Blades that had the power of Law at the fifth level. Even if he used the Onyx Blade in his hand, he was afraid that a crack would appear on it. How could Luo Zheng have the courage to meet it head-on?
"Bang bang bang bang bang …"
Luo Zheng didn't pay attention to Yu Ze. He waved his fists, and a huge force exploded above the Wind Blades. Immediately, the black Wind Blades were shattered!
However, Luo Zheng's body didn't suffer any damage.
Seeing this scene, Yu Ze was stunned. At this moment, he even forgot to resist the Wind Blades. He stared blankly at Luo Zheng, and felt that he couldn't understand this guy.
What was this guy's body made of? A Divine Artifact? A Supreme Artifact? Even an existence that surpassed a Supreme Artifact …
As for Gu Pei 'er, Qing Feng, Boss Mo, Ha Peng, and the others, when they were resisting the Wind Blades, they also noticed this scene from the corner of their eyes.
Because Luo Zheng was recognized by the Demi-Immortal Clan, they thought highly of Luo Zheng. However, before they saw whether Luo Zheng really had a title, in their eyes, Luo Zheng was just a talented warrior from the lower realm!
As for Luo Zheng's strength, they didn't even bother to test it …
After all, a 1st level Life and Death Realm warrior wasn't worth the attention of a group of Ultimate God Realm experts.
Not to mention Luo Zheng, even White Brow Daoist, a Ultimate God Realm warrior, wasn't put in their eyes by the seven Demi-Immortal Clan experts. In the Demi-Immortal Clan, every one of them was an elite. Although the human race was powerful, that was only in terms of overall strength. The gap between an ordinary Ultimate God Realm warrior and a Demi-Immortal Clan expert was huge.

Chapter 908
Of course, the seven of them didn't know that Luo Zheng had destroyed eight black wind blades!
About 1,500 miles away from them, on a mountain that was 300 to 400 feet above the sea surface, eight muffled noises were heard.
That mountain was colorful. Even though it had been swept by the terrible storm of the Chaotic Astral Sea for thousands of years, and millions of black wind blades had not left a single mark on it …
This mountain was the Divine Magnetic Mountain that was submerged in the Chaotic Astral Sea!
Most of the Divine Magnetic Mountain was buried under the sea surface, and only a part of it was exposed. No matter how the waves rolled and the wind blades slashed, this extremely sturdy mountain remained unmoved.
This time, Luo Zheng had learned his lesson. When he was fighting against the terrible power at the end of the Heavenly Tribulation, he had transferred all his power to the five million mountains in the Great Thousand World. Except for the Eastern Region, all the other mountains were destroyed!
Luo Zheng didn't want the whole Great Thousand World to be destroyed because of him. After thinking about it, he realized that he was a bit stupid. The Divine Magnetic Mountain was impervious to all things, so he should have transferred the terrible power to it. Unfortunately, the situation was so serious that he didn't think of it.
Now that Luo Zheng had learned his lesson, he wouldn't make the same mistake again.
"Puff, puff, puff, puff …"
The Eight Trigrams Wheel flew forward, and the seven people of the Demi-Immortal Tribe used their own methods to fight against the dense black wind blades.
Boss Mo waved his walking stick and said anxiously, "Qing Feng, we can't go on like this! Show me what you have! "
Hearing Boss Mo's words, Qing Feng gritted his teeth and showed a reluctant expression. "I've used my Seven Stars Plate five times … If I waste it again …"
Qing Feng did have a treasure called the Seven Stars Plate. This Seven Stars Plate was comparable to a Supreme Artifact, but it couldn't be used indefinitely. It could only be used seven times. Over the years, Qing Feng had relied on the Seven Stars Plate to survive several disasters. It could be said that the seven chances of using the Seven Stars Plate were equal to his seven lives!
He really didn't want to waste one of the chances here!
"This Chaotic Astral Ocean is very vast. We don't know how long it will take to cross it. Do you think we can hold on?" Boss Mo said coldly.
Qing Feng rolled his eyes and said reluctantly, "Boss, can't you use the Sacred Blood Secret Technique?"
"Hmph!" Hearing this, Boss Mo's face darkened and he glared at Qing Feng.
Qing Feng reluctantly took out a milky white plate from his Sumeru Space …
Hearing their conversation, Luo Zheng was quite speechless. Everyone was in a crisis, and they were in the same boat, but these guys were still hiding something.
However, this was only because they were skilled and daring. If it were the Spirit Sea Realm warriors from the four Divine Kingdoms, they would have been torn to pieces by these wind blades.
After activating the Seven Star Plate, the black pupils in Qing Feng's eyes emitted rays of dazzling starlight. Although it was broad daylight and there was a heavy storm above his head, there were still seven stars shining brightly in the sky!
Seeing this, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. The principle of the Seven Star Plate seemed to be similar to the "Star Battle Body" that Red Dragon taught him. However, Luo Zheng's "Star Battle Body" was connected to himself, while Qing Feng connected to the seven stars through the Seven Star Plate in his hand. Moreover, his Seven Star Plate seemed to have a limit on the number of times it could be used.
"Buzz, buzz, buzz …"
A milky white barrier appeared above the Seven Star Plate. After it appeared, the barrier expanded against the wind and quickly spread out in all directions.
In a short while, everyone was shrouded in it!
"Puff, puff, puff, puff, puff …"
Countless wind blades bombarded the milky white barrier, but the barrier was constantly dented. The barrier itself seemed to be very soft, but there was a strong toughness hidden in its softness!
"It's okay. The Seven Star Plate's isolation force is not something that these black wind blades can cut," Qing Feng said helplessly. Although his barrier had blocked these fatal wind blades, he was still very unwilling to use the Seven Star Plate once.
Seeing this, everyone heaved a deep sigh of relief. Whether it was Gu Peier, Yu Ze, or the others, they all showed a trace of relief on their faces.
Boss Mo shook his head and said, "No matter how you look at it, this storm is a little abnormal!"
Qing Feng muttered, "This Great Thousand World is very abnormal!"
Before the Demi-Immortal Clan set off, they had investigated this Great Thousand World. First of all, the ascension passage of this Great Thousand World was destroyed, and it was impossible to ascend to the Upper Realm by normal means. This was only a small problem. Among the millions of Great Thousand Worlds, there were more than one or two whose ascension passages had been destroyed.
Therefore, the seven people did not pay too much attention to it. However, just now, they were chased by the shadow creatures in the void. It was not easy for them to escape, and then they encountered a terrifying storm. No matter how they looked at it, it was abnormal.
Luo Zheng actually agreed with Boss Mo's words. The Chaotic Astral Ocean had existed since ancient times, and because of this storm, it had completely isolated the Central Region from the four Divine Kingdoms. Therefore, although the distance between the two continents wasn't particularly far, but for so many years, there hadn't been much interaction between the two continents. The question was, how did this storm come about? Perhaps no one knew the answer …
"Puff, puff …"
Withstanding the cutting wind blades, the Tai Chi Wheel flew forward steadily.
Boss Mo kept guessing the cause of this storm, but he soon denied it one by one. Finally, Qing Feng said, "No matter how it came about, a storm of this intensity should not appear in a small world!"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng gently shrugged. Until now, Qing Feng was still very arrogant and did not put the Lower Realm in his eyes.
Seeing Luo Zheng's small action, Qing Feng frowned slightly and remembered what had just happened! Compared to this storm, Luo Zheng resisted the black wind blades with his body, which was even more incredible!
"Luo Zheng … How did you break those wind blades? Those black wind blades contained the power of the fifth level of the Law of Wind! "Qing Feng stared at Luo Zheng and asked.
Qing Feng's words immediately reminded the others. Now that Yu Ze and Gu Peier had just calmed down, they all stared at Luo Zheng. Gu Peier also asked with a strange expression, "Yes, Luo Zheng! You just broke those wind blades with your fists, but you are not injured? "
After saying this, Gu Peier directly walked over and reached out to touch Luo Zheng's fists with a curious expression …
Luo Zheng really did not know how to answer this question. He just smiled and said, "It's just a small trick. It's a joke in front of you."
"Humph, if you don't want to say it, just say it," Gu Peier glared at Luo Zheng and said discontentedly.
Luo Zheng smiled and said, "It's really not convenient to say it."
They naturally knew that although Luo Zheng was being modest, he was actually hiding something. However, it was not good for them to interrogate Luo Zheng now. Everyone had their own secrets. Moreover, Luo Zheng was not an enemy to them now. If it was confirmed that Luo Zheng had really obtained a title, no matter what title it was, they would still be comrades in the trenches.
Through the boundary, the crowd looked at the dense wind blades around them and fell silent for a moment. Even Divine Realm experts felt small in front of the ferocious nature. At this moment, Luo Zheng opened his mouth and asked, "Boss Mo, the giant head that followed us in the void just now, what exactly is it?"
Although the windstorm in front of them was powerful, the group of Divine Realm warriors were not too afraid of it. Those wind blades were just a little troublesome, but after using some powerful techniques, they were able to easily block them. Based on the conversation between Qing Feng and Boss Mo, it seemed like they had other methods to deal with this windstorm.
However, when they were chased by that giant head in the void, Boss Mo's face was pale with fright. What in the lower realm could make him so scared?

Chapter 909
These seven people were all Divine Realm powerhouses. In particular, Boss Mo's strength was likely at the peak of the Divine Realm!

The Fish Dragon Sect was the easternmost continent of the Central Domain. It had remained unscathed during the several upheavals of the Central Domain. At this time, many martial artists of the Fish Dragon Sect were cultivating in front of the sect gate.

From far to near, from near to far, from near to far, from the moment the black dot appeared to the moment it disappeared, only two or three breaths of time had passed. It was truly a flash of light!

Ever since Luo Zheng killed Cui Xie, his prestige had reached the peak in the Central Domain. He had replaced Cui Xie as the number one person in the Central Domain. All kinds of stories about Luo Zheng were constantly spreading.

After Luo Zheng killed Cui Xie, Wu Zhanhe quickly fled and lived in seclusion again. Wu Zhanhe had hidden in the Yunmeng Swamp for hundreds of years. Perhaps he was tired of living in seclusion. Whoosh …

Chapter 910

Chapter 911
The person playing the zither was Xi Youqin.
Ever since Luo Zheng left, Xi Youqin had been immersed in her own thoughts. She didn't ask for a status from Luo Zheng, but staying by Luo Zheng's side was Xi Youqin's deepest obsession.
Even when the Void Spirit Sect was rebuilt, Xi Xiaojie who had disappeared for a long time came to Tianqi City, wanting to bring Xi Youqin back, she rejected him.
She wanted to stay here and wait for Luo Zheng. If Luo Zheng returned, at least she would be able to see Luo Zheng as soon as possible.
However, although Xi Youqin didn't return to the Void Spirit Sect, because of her, the World Business Alliance had put in a lot of effort to help rebuild the Void Spirit Sect.
Firstly, the relationship between the World Business Alliance and the Void Spirit Sect wasn't bad. Secondly, although Xi Youqin was a delicate girl, after awakening the Extreme Purple Yin Body, Xi Youqin's overall strength had skyrocketed!
Although she was still just a Divine Core warrior, with the help of the Extreme Purple Realm, Xi Youqin's overall strength was at least ranked in the top three in the Central Domain …
That day, Xi Youqin summoned countless huge swords and killed countless warriors from the Tian Xie Sect like a black cloud. The scene was still vivid in everyone's mind. In order to fight against the Tian Xie Sect, Luo Zheng was the key figure, and Xi Youqin was the second most important figure!
However, Xi Youqin wasn't famous, and most warriors didn't even know where those huge black swords came from. Therefore, apart from a few mighty warriors, most people didn't know that there was such a woman in the Central Domain.
She stayed in the Heavenly Palace and rarely went out. She was able to endure such peaceful days, but recently, her longing for Luo Zheng had been increasing day by day.
Sometimes, when she was in the mood, she would instruct the servant girls to bring out the unconscious Ning Yudie to bask in the sun. She would then concentrate on playing the zither. Perhaps she would be able to comfort Ning Yudie.
At this time, Xi Youqin heard footsteps behind her. She originally thought it was her maidservant Xiao Qing, so she ordered her to bring the flower tea. However, after she finished speaking, she didn't hear any movement. She slightly strummed the zither strings in her hand and frowned slightly. It was impossible for her Eldest Miss temper to disappear. It was only in front of Luo Zheng that she didn't dare to show it in the slightest. This time, when she didn't hear Xiao Qing's response, she was naturally unhappy.
"Little Blue?" Xi Youqin's voice deepened. She strummed the strings of the zither in her hands. The sound of the strings revealed a hint of irritation. She had been feeling irritable recently because she missed him.

Luo Zheng had a smile on his face. He stared at Xi Youqin.
Xi Youqin pursed her lips. She suddenly took a few steps forward. Those few steps went from slow to fast. She turned into a warm and fragrant jade and threw herself into Luo Zheng's arms.
Half a year of separation was just a snap of a finger for a cultivator. However, this longing had been tormenting her day and night.
However, she didn't close her eyes to enjoy Luo Zheng's broad chest. Her eyes were fixed on Gu Pei 'er behind her.
Xi Youqin was a scheming woman. However, for the sake of Luo Zheng, she was willing to change everything about herself. No matter how many women Luo Zheng had, she wouldn't show any dissatisfaction. However, just because she didn't show any dissatisfaction, it didn't mean she didn't have any thoughts about it. She threw her glance at Gu Pei 'er.
Xi Youqin was only a Divine Core warrior, but she could sense a faint aura emanating from Gu Pei 'er's body. It wasn't an aura that came from the soul, it was an aura unique to some super mighty warriors! She didn't know what kind of heaven-defying strength Gu Pei 'er possessed, but she knew she was very strong.
Whether Gu Pei 'er was strong or not, it had nothing to do with Xi Youqin. She was only willing to be Luo Zheng's little woman.
But this woman in front of her … was she from the four Divine Kingdoms?
She followed him here from the four Divine Kingdoms? Was she Luo Zheng's new lover?
But, why was she looking at Gu Pei 'er like this?
Xi Youqin laid her head on Luo Zheng's shoulder, and stared into Gu Pei 'er's eyes. There was no such thing as a generous woman in this world. Even though Xi Youqin had taken the initiative to take care of Ning Yudie, she still felt jealous of Ning Yudie from time to time. She would rather be in a deep sleep like her, while Luo Zheng was running around for her …
But, the way Gu Pei 'er looked at her was a strange one, as if a man was looking at a beautiful woman. At this moment, Xi Youqin was confused. What was the meaning of this?
Xi Youqin didn't know that Gu Pei 'er's sexual orientation was problematic. When she looked at Xi Youqin, she was actually looking at Xi Youqin from a man's point of view …
Being stared at like this by Gu Pei 'er, Xi Youqin suddenly felt uncomfortable. She glared fiercely at Gu Pei' er, then brushed her hair to the side. She breathed out a warm breath at Luo Zheng, and hugged Luo Zheng tightly.

Gu Pei 'er laughed mischievously, "Nothing, I just think that your woman is very interesting."

"En!" Xi Youqin nodded, then stepped aside. Luo Zheng then walked towards Ning Yudie.

Luo Zheng nodded.
