It hasn't completely dried up, only a little remains. However, this kind of phenomenon would only appear when one's life force is nearing its end. However, this woman's age isn't very old … It's very strange!"
A person was like a bowl, and the bowl was filled to the brim with life force. As a person matured, the life force would constantly be consumed, and at the same time, it would also be slowly replenished.
However, after the Five Decay of the Heavenly Being, the life force would begin to be consumed continuously, and at the same time, it could not be replenished. Even through the use of life crystals, it could not be replenished, because the bowl was already broken …
Ning Yudie had not entered the Divine Sea Realm, so she would not experience the Five Decay of the Heavenly Being. However, the life force in the bowl would only dry up when an ordinary warrior's life force was nearing its end. For this to happen at such a young age, Gu Pei 'er was indeed puzzled.
Hearing her words, Luo Zheng said, "Her life force was taken away by the Wind of Life and Death on the Yellow Springs River."
"Wind of Life and Death?" Gu Pei 'er blinked her eyes, then said with a smile, "Are you kidding? The Wind of Life and Death contains the law of causality, and indeed, it directly erodes life force. It's almost certain death. How could this woman preserve her life? And … in your realm, you can even get the Wind of Life and Death from the Yellow Springs! "
"It's a secret," Ning Yudie was able to survive because of the Heavenly Dao Fragment, which contained the law of causality in the Wind of Life and Death. Of course, Luo Zheng would not explain too much to Gu Pei 'er. At this time, he took out a life crystal in his hand!
This life crystal was almost completely condensed from life force, and was almost completely different from the life origin stone!
Seeing the life origin stone in Luo Zheng's hand, Gu Pei 'er's eyes suddenly lit up, "You have such a good thing!"
Not only Gu Pei 'er, when Luo Zheng took out the life crystal, everyone felt a strong life force, including the three alliance leaders waiting outside the Heavenly Palace, and all the maidservants in the Heavenly Palace.
Even in the Upper Realm, this life crystal was a relatively rare thing. However, Gu Pei 'er originally wanted to say that it was too wasteful to use it like this, but thinking that it was Luo Zheng who got it himself, she did not have the right to say anything.
It was as if what Luo Zheng held in his hand was not a piece of ore, but a little guy full of life force, releasing a strong life force in all directions.
He put the life crystal on Ning Yudie's face, and gently pinched it with his hand. The surface of the life crystal gently shattered, and then Luo Zheng's hand withdrew like lightning. This life force was like a living thing, and would drill into the creatures closest to it. Luo Zheng was afraid that the life force would drill into his hand.
A strong life force gently floated above Ning Yudie, twisting into various irregular shapes.
After about a breath's time, this strong life force suddenly soaked into Ning Yudie's thin lips.

Chapter 912
After the life force was drained, although Ning Yudie's appearance did not change, anyone who had seen Ning Yudie could feel that her aura was very weak. The remaining life force was only enough to keep her in a deep sleep.
As the life force continued to soak in, Ning Yudie's originally haggard and pale complexion began to change.
The paleness began to gradually fade away, and dark red blood appeared on his face. His cheeks, in particular, were gradually dyed with a layer of red. It was as if he had drunk wine, or as if he had applied rouge. At a glance, he looked extremely beautiful.
Xi Youqin and Gu Peier could also feel that the life force in Ning Yudie's body was rapidly filling up. It was like a piece of wood that was about to wither suddenly burst with a strong life force. New buds began to sprout, and roots began to spread, giving people a feeling of vitality!
After using up a life crystal, Luo Zheng stared at Ning Yudie, his eyes full of nervousness.
"She hasn't woken up?"
Luo Zheng's eyes gradually sank. Luo Zheng's biggest motive for going to Divine Kingdom was to save Ning Yudie, and he had pinned all his hopes on this life force.
Looking at it, the life force had definitely taken effect, so why hadn't Ning Yudie woken up yet?
"Luo Zheng …"
Seeing Luo Zheng's ugly complexion, Xi Youqin called out from the side. Luo Zheng was like an iron statue, standing motionless in place.
Gu Peier also revealed a slightly surprised expression. She had previously investigated this woman. Her life force had only been emptied by the wind of life and death, and her body had then activated its self-protection mechanism, forcing herself to fall into a deep sleep. Only in this way could she save the remaining trace of life force to the greatest extent.
The flower in her right eye turned slightly, and an invisible perception spread out. Soon, a smile appeared on her face. Her Innate Skill was the flower in her right eye, and her perception of the soul was far more sensitive than other cultivators of the same level. Earlier on, when she had investigated, Gu Pei 'er had already sensed that the woman lying on the bed had already awoken …
'This fellow's women are quite interesting,' Gu Pei 'er commented in her heart. Then, the corners of her mouth gently moved, and she sent a true essence sound transmission to Luo Zheng, "Hey, your woman has woken up."
"What?" Hearing Ning Yudie's words, Luo Zheng's heart suddenly surged. But when he saw that Luo Zheng was lying motionless with her eyes closed, pretending to be asleep, he felt both angry and amused. Ning Yudie's temperament was very changeable. When she was pretending to be the Temple Master, she was solemn and determined, but most of the time, she was like a child.
After Luo Zheng's eyes turned, he waved his hand and sighed, "She hasn't woken up yet, let's go out first!"
Xi Youqin didn't know what was going on, so she looked at Luo Zheng with a puzzled expression. Gu Pei 'er smiled faintly and patted Xi Youqin's shoulder, "You're right, let's go out first!"
But when Xi Youqin was patted by Gu Pei 'er, it was as if she had been bitten by a scorpion. She immediately moved a few steps away, maintaining a considerable distance from Gu Pei' er, and glared at her fiercely.
Gu Pei 'er didn't care at all, and just chuckled twice. Then, the three of them left the courtyard.
Just after Luo Zheng and the others left, Ning Yudie's fingers trembled slightly. If an ordinary mortal slept like this for several months, their bodily functions would be affected, and they wouldn't be able to walk for a while. Because their eyes would be closed for a long time, they wouldn't be able to withstand the sunlight and wouldn't be able to see anything.
But for Ning Yudie, who had already stepped into the Life and Death Realm, it wasn't a big deal. After all, it was common for cultivators to cultivate in seclusion for eight to ten years. After cultivating to this stage, the structure and functions of one's body had long been pulled apart from that of ordinary people.
After her fingers trembled, her figure floated to the side. Her figure was originally very petite, and she was wearing an ice blue palace dress. The ribbon of the dress fluttered with her figure, like a blue ice flower dancing in the cold wind.
After her feet touched the ground, a sly smile appeared on her face, and she carefully walked towards the Heavenly Stellar Hall. But before she could enter the Heavenly Stellar Hall, a figure suddenly drilled out, and firmly embraced her like an iron hoop!
"Ah!"
Ning Yudie let out a scream. She didn't release her perception, otherwise, Luo Zheng, who was hiding behind a pillar, would have been discovered by her. Luo Zheng's sudden appearance gave her a fright.
The two hugged each other tightly, completely forgetting everything around them. Luo Zheng didn't say anything, tilted his head, and kissed her!
Ning Yudie was shorter than Luo Zheng by more than a head. Her petite figure was lifted up by Luo Zheng, and it was difficult for her feet to even touch the ground. Under such a kiss, she was unwilling to circulate her True Qi to support her body. Her white and delicate feet could only be stretched out like a bow, and the tips of her feet touched the cold ground. For ordinary people, maintaining this posture for a few breaths would cause their feet to ache unbearably. Of course, Ning Yudie was a Life and Death Realm master.
Thus, her feet were tightly stretched, and she maintained this posture for half an incense stick's worth of time …
Xi Youqin and Gu Pei 'er, who were standing in the Heavenly Stellar Hall, quietly watched the scene outside.
Xi Youqin's mood was as if all kinds of spices had been smashed, and she had mixed feelings. During this period of time, she had repeatedly reminded herself of her status in Luo Zheng's eyes. She was very clear that in Luo Zheng's heart, Ning Yudie's weight was much higher than her own. Being jealous of Ning Yudie was undoubtedly an extremely unclear choice. Thus, she had calmed down and stayed in the Heavenly Stellar Hall to take care of Ning Yudie with the other maidservants.
Under such an adjustment, Xi Youqin felt that her state of mind was already very relaxed. She had treated Ning Yudie as her elder sister, and they had taken care of Luo Zheng together.
But now, Xi Youqin discovered how weak and laughable her way of thinking was. For a woman to overcome her jealousy, it should be more difficult than for a man to overcome his lust …
Gu Pei 'er could feel Xi Youqin's emotional fluctuations. All of her emotions were emitted from the fluctuations of her soul. Through the flower in her right eye, everyone's soul seemed to be naked, and was displayed in front of Gu Pei' er's eyes.
She turned her head and glanced at Xi Youqin. With a smile on her face, she said, "Men are all like this. Not a single one of them is good, right?"
Xi Youqin glanced at Gu Pei 'er. Although Gu Pei' er was also a living beauty, and ignoring the strange flower in her right eye, her appearance and temperament were definitely not below hers, she still had a natural resistance towards Gu Pei 'er.
"Hmph, not a single man is good. Luo Zheng is an exception," Xi Youqin retorted.
Gu Pei 'er chuckled and said, "Indeed, this brat is indeed very outstanding. However, in the end, he is still unreliable. Why not …" Gu Pei' er looked at Xi Youqin again.
"Why not what?" Xi Youqin was slightly stunned.
"You might as well follow me!" Gu Pei 'er chuckled.
When Xi Youqin heard this, she fiercely glared at Gu Pei 'er, but for the first time, she scolded, "Crazy!"
Luo Zheng and Ning Yudie kissed like this for a long time before separating. After separating, Ning Yudie stared at Luo Zheng, who was right in front of her, with a faint smile on her face. However, she quickly thought of something, and her face revealed a worried expression. She then asked, "Then Cui Xie …"
After being hit by the Wind of Life and Death, Ning Yudie was in a state of coma. She was completely unaware of how long she had been unconscious, and the situation with the Tian Xie Sect. Although she saw that Luo Zheng was standing in front of her, she still couldn't help but worry.
"Dead. I killed him!" Luo Zheng immediately replied.
Ning Yudie nodded. Since Luo Zheng was standing in front of her, the possibility of Cui Xie's defeat was very high. However, she couldn't figure out how Luo Zheng defeated Cui Xie and how he killed Cui Xie.
Luo Zheng gave a brief account of what happened after Ning Yudie fell asleep. As for the matter of him going to God's Kingdom to look for the Origin Stone of Life, he only briefly mentioned it.
Although Luo Zheng concealed the various hardships in God's Kingdom, how could Ning Yudie not guess? She reclined on Luo Zheng's chest, and from time to time asked for details. Luo Zheng could only answer truthfully. She used her finger to gently draw circles on Luo Zheng's chest, and no one knew what she was thinking.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 913
When they talked about the dangers, Ning Yudie's slender fingers flickered with True Essence. Streaks of ice-blue cold air directly penetrated Luo Zheng's clothes and hit his chest.
This kind of cold air naturally couldn't hurt Luo Zheng, but it was cold and piercing. Luo Zheng didn't use True Essence to resist it, but he could still feel the bone-piercing cold. She seemed to be using this method to remind Luo Zheng that he shouldn't take such a risk …
The two of them completely forgot about themselves as they talked like this. It was as if they had forgotten about time, and an hour quietly passed.
At this moment, the sound of "Caw! Caw! Caw caw caw! "A furry figure descended from the sky at lightning speed.
This little thing was born with the ability to fly. It could even ignore the restrictions of the fifth layer's spatial laws and directly jump out of the "refracted" space.
It was just that it had only been born for a very short period of time. Although it was born with the ability to fly, it didn't have a good grasp of the distance. Therefore, it didn't have a good grasp of the speed of its descent. Like a meteorite, it directly smashed into the ground, creating a not small pit.
Ning Yudie frowned. After being suddenly disturbed, she naturally wasn't happy. She angrily looked at the little thing in the pit. "What is this?"
"Caw! Caw!"
This Ancestral Dragon was originally placed on the Eight Trigrams Wheel. Probably because it was separated from Luo Zheng for too long, it became unhappy and jumped down from the Eight Trigrams Wheel.
It shook its head vigorously, and the mud on its body immediately disappeared. Then, it pounced towards Luo Zheng!
At this time, Luo Zheng, who was originally half-hugging Ning Yudie, directly pushed Ning Yudie to the side. Its mouth even made "caw! Caw! Caw!" sounds, as if it was protesting against Ning Yudie!
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng was both angry and amused. Although he had obtained this little dragon, he didn't have much time to communicate with this little guy on the way back from the Heavenly Holy Land to the Central Domain. He didn't expect it to be jealous …
This Ancestral Dragon's body was already imprinted with Luo Zheng's imprint. It regarded Luo Zheng as its only relative. In its mind, it naturally wouldn't allow others to get close to Luo Zheng. Only it could.
However, Ning Yudie didn't seem to be convinced that she was disturbed by this guy. The Ancestral Dragon's appearance was very strange. It looked more like a seahorse than anything else. Ning Yudie didn't recognize that it was a dragon, so she reached out to push it aside.
However, the moment Ning Yudie reached out her hand, the little guy finally got angry.
"Caw!"
The little guy's voice suddenly became sharp. A very unique power suddenly erupted from its body.
Even Ning Yudie retreated several steps under the pressure of this power. This little guy's hostility caused Ning Yudie to feel a sense of fear and trepidation!
It wasn't just Ning Yudie. Xi Youqin, who was standing not far away, and the people waiting outside the Heavenly Holy Hall, also felt a sharp aura. This aura didn't seem to come from a human, but it felt like it belonged to a ruler!
Only Gu Pei Er's expression remained indifferent. Although the Ancestral Dragon's dragon aura was powerful, this Ancestral Dragon had just hatched. Moreover, Gu Pei Er was proficient in soul techniques. It didn't affect her in the slightest.
Seeing Ning Yudie and Xi Youqin's faces turn pale, the flower in Gu Pei Er's right eye turned slightly. She reached out her hand, and a faint halo floated out. It enveloped the Ancestral Dragon. The faint halo directly imprisoned the Ancestral Dragon's aura. At this time, Xi Youqin and Ning Yudie felt a little better.
Gu Pei Er said, "Although this dragon has just been born, it's still very dangerous. Especially since its intelligence hasn't fully awakened. Right now, it only has the intelligence of a three year old child. Don't provoke this guy."
Luo Zheng hugged the Ancestral Dragon in his hands. He glared fiercely at this little guy. He still hadn't thought of how to deal with this little guy. He'd wait until everything was over, then he'd see how Qinglong would deal with it.
"Dragon? You're saying this is a dragon? "Ning Yudie stared at the little guy. She couldn't tell that this little guy was actually a dragon!
Luo Zheng didn't deny it. He nodded. The Ancestral Dragon seemed to understand Ning Yudie's words. It made a "gugu" sound at Ning Yudie, revealing a strong hostility!
"Whether it's a dragon or not, it looks so cute," Xi Youqin said with a smile.
"Gugu!"
The Ancestral Dragon seemed to be able to understand everyone's words. It could even accurately distinguish their intentions. Perhaps it was because Xi Youqin praised it. After a strange sound, it actually left Luo Zheng's arms and pounced on Xi Youqin. It used its soft tongue to lick Xi Youqin very intimately.
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng's face was full of confusion. He didn't know why this little guy would treat it so differently …
Ning Yudie pursed her lips and glared fiercely at the Ancestral Dragon, but her eyes soon fell on Gu Pei Er. This woman had just casually made a move, but she was able to restrain the little dragon's aura. Such a move showed that her attainments were extraordinary! She couldn't see what method this woman used!
Under such observation, the surprise in Ning Yudie's eyes became more intense. She couldn't see through this woman's cultivation!
She had already entered the Life and Death Realm. She could even distinguish the cultivation of a Divine Sea Realm expert. However, she felt that the True Yuan emitted by this woman was purer and more condensed than that of a Divine Sea Realm expert!
"Luo Zheng, this is …" Ning Yudie asked immediately.
Luo Zheng said, "She's the one I just told you about. She's from the Demi-Immortal Tribe. The seven of them also came to the Central Domain."
"Why did you come to the Central Domain?" Ning Yudie asked again.
Luo Zheng shook his head and smiled, "This time, I'm going to return to the Central Domain. I might only stay for a short while. I'll leave in a few days."
This time, Luo Zheng returned to the Central Domain with the Demi-Immortal Tribe. It was Gu Pei 'er who helped Luo Zheng persuade the Demi-Immortal Tribe. Otherwise, the Demi-Immortal Tribe would have directly taken Luo Zheng to the Sea God Continent to search for the Tower of Sin.
"You're leaving again!"
Xi Youqin and Ning Yudie's voices rang out at the same time. They didn't expect Luo Zheng to leave again after staying in Heaven Awaken City for less than half a day.
Luo Zheng nodded, "Yes, but I'll be back as soon as possible …"
At that moment, an impatient voice rang out from outside the Heavenly Palace, "Luo Zheng, I've given you enough time!"
It was the voice of Yu Ze from the Demi-Immortal Tribe!
Luo Zheng frowned and lowered his voice. Then, he led Ning Yudie and the others out of the Heavenly Palace's main hall.
At the main entrance of the Heavenly Palace, Shi Kefan and the others were cautiously looking at the Eight Trigrams Wheel not far away. The Eight Trigrams Wheel rotated slowly, and there were six people standing on it. There was also a woman lying on one side of the Eight Trigrams Wheel. She was Feng Nianyun from the Dragon Bloodline Tribe.
Whether it was the six people standing or the woman lying on the ground, their cultivations were unfathomable. The aura emitted by any one of them far surpassed that of a Divine Sea Realm expert!
Yan Yueshan and the others smiled bitterly. The people Luo Zheng brought back were more and more terrifying. But the worst thing was that these people didn't seem to be friendly to Luo Zheng either! For people like them, any one of them could destroy the entire Central Domain with a wave of their hand. Angering them was definitely not a wise thing to do.
However, these people all had unfriendly expressions on their faces. Even if Shi Kefan was very good at talking, he couldn't find a chance to speak at all. There was no other way. The difference in strength was too great, and he often lost the right to talk.
Facing Yu Ze's cold face, Luo Zheng's expression was indifferent. He said, "It's only been two hours. Is your time so tight?"
"Hmph, my time isn't tight, but there's no need to waste it on you," Yu Ze replied with a cold snort.
Luo Zheng smiled faintly. His gaze went past Yu Ze, and he cupped his hands towards Boss Mo, who was not far away, and said, "Boss Mo, thank you for sending me back to the Central Domain. But before we depart, I have something to do! Please lend me your Eight Trigrams Wheel. "

Chapter 914
Yu Ze's face was full of dissatisfaction, but Boss Mo said, "There's no need to rush these few hours. But Luo Zheng, what's the point of borrowing this Eight Trigrams Wheel?"
Luo Zheng smiled and said, "I have an enemy in the world. He's hiding in the Central Domain. I want to kill him before we depart!"
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Shi Kefan, Yan Yueshan, and the others were all surprised. Luo Zheng wanted to kill Wu Zhanhe now?
However, although Wu Zhanhe was accumulating power, he wasn't as arrogant as Cui Xie. He was also cautious. Even if the World Business Alliance's intelligence network was the best in the Central Domain, they wouldn't be able to find him in a short time!
And these people who surpassed the Divine Sea Realm didn't seem to have good tempers. They were already impatient from waiting. What if he angered them? It seemed that their relationship with Luo Zheng wasn't good either …
Therefore, Shi Kefan couldn't help but remind Luo Zheng, "Brother Luo Zheng, that Wu Zhanhe is cunning and has three burrows. We won't be able to find him in a short time. If he knows that you've returned to the Central Domain, he won't dare to show himself. There's no need to rush …"
Luo Zheng waved his hand at Shi Kefan. How could he allow others to sleep beside him? If Wu Zhanhe stayed in seclusion and was a peaceful hermit, Luo Zheng might have let him go. After all, Luo Zheng wasn't good at killing. He believed that the main evils must be eliminated. If the others weren't unforgivable, Luo Zheng would have let them go.
But since this guy didn't know what was good for him and tried to restore the strength of the Tian Xie Sect while he was away from the Central Domain, then he couldn't blame himself.
"Oh, so it's such a small matter. It won't take much time." Boss Mo nodded and said, "Point out the direction. I'll let Ha Peng control the Eight Trigrams Wheel and go with you."
Yu Ze's face was full of indignation. In Yu Ze's opinion, if Luo Zheng was really outstanding, then it would be fine, but this guy was most likely a swindler. Not to mention, his cultivation base was only at the Life and Death realm. How could he have the face to order them around? In other words, Yuze felt that Luo Zheng was not qualified.
Luo Zheng didn't look at Yu Ze's face, and said to Boss Mo, "Thank you, Boss Mo!"
Then Luo Zheng turned to Ning Yudie and Xi Youqin and said, "I'll be back soon!"
"But Luo Zheng, that Wu Zhanhe …" Shi Kefan was still worried.
Luo Zheng smiled faintly and said, "Chief Shi, I know what I'm doing."
After Ancestral Gragon left Xi Youqin's arms and jumped into Luo Zheng's, Luo Zheng stepped onto the Eight Trigrams Wheel.
Although the Central Domain wasn't big, with Luo Zheng's speed, it would take him two to three days to kill Wu Zhanhe. If he relied on the Eight Trigrams Wheel's speed, it would take him less than an hour to cross the Central Domain.
After stepping onto the Eight Trigrams Wheel, Luo Zheng thought and the memories in his mind appeared.
The memories of the entire Vast Thousand World covered everything in this world, including all the continents, all the oceans, all the skies, every bird, every baby. Moreover, the memories were from the moment the ancient world was born until now!
With this investigation, Luo Zheng locked onto Ha Peng's position. "So you're here." Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly, and he told Ha Peng the location.
As the Eight Trigrams Wheel rotated gently, Luo Zheng and the others began to rise slowly, and then suddenly accelerated. Like a shooting star, they turned into a straight line and disappeared into the horizon in a breath's time.
"This speed …" Shi Kefan looked at the disappearing Eight Trigrams Wheel and sighed.
Yan Yueshan also shook his head. "Luo Zheng's level of contact is no longer at a level that we can understand."
Only three years ago, Luo Zheng was just a new star born in the Central Domain, but now, Luo Zheng had become an existence that they could only look up to, or even look up to.
However, Ning Yudie said with a smile, "But Luo Zheng is still Luo Zheng, isn't he?"
Hearing this, the three Alliance Masters nodded at the same time. When Luo Zheng was in the middle stage of False Tribulation Realm, he could already kill Cui Xie. Now that he had broken through the Life and Death Realm, his strength was even more unfathomable. However, he still didn't show the slightest bit of unfamiliarity, nor did he show the slightest bit of arrogance. It could only be said that it was a great blessing for them to be friends with Luo Zheng.
South of the Central Domain, Heaven Qin Mountain.
South Heaven Qin Mountain was a famous mountain in the Central Domain, but not long ago, all the mountains in the Central Domain were annihilated in an instant …
Naturally, the South Heaven Qin Mountain was not spared. After the mountain was annihilated, a tributary of the Rain River spread out and flooded the area, turning it into a swamp.
Looking at this swamp, Wu Zhanhe's eyes revealed a trace of resentment.
In the past, he had lived in seclusion in the Cloud Dream Swamp for hundreds of years. He had long since grown tired of this kind of life. Later, he followed Cui Xie and established the Tian Xie Sect with great difficulty, but it was destroyed by that brat Luo Zheng alone! And Cui Xie was actually killed by Luo Zheng.
At that time, Wu Zhanhe was indeed scared witless, which was why he fled. Now that he thought about it, his temperament was still too steady and cautious. It could even be described as cowardly. He had missed a great opportunity to kill Luo Zheng!
Ning Yudie had been hit by the Wind of Life and Death, and was already in a state of certain death. And that ancient tycoon of the World Business Alliance had also been killed by Cui Xie. As for the three Alliance Masters, there was nothing to be afraid of. As long as he killed Luo Zheng, who in the Central Domain would be able to resist him, Wu Zhanhe?
Wouldn't he be able to establish his own Divine Kingdom, unify the entire Central Domain, and become the first Divine Kingdom King in thousands of years?
After escaping from Tianqi City, this unwillingness was like a worm, constantly gnawing at Wu Zhanhe's heart of martial arts. However, there was no medicine for regret. After missing this opportunity, it would be very difficult to find another one.
But after learning that Luo Zheng had left the Central Domain, Wu Zhanhe was ready to make a move.
Although the three fat men were powerful together, in the end, they couldn't do anything to him. If he were to establish a new Tian Xie Sect, what could Tianqi City use to resist him?
Once this idea hatched, it would be difficult to suppress it. Luo Zheng had gone to the Divine Kingdom Continent, where experts were as common as clouds, and the possibility of him not returning was even greater. For Wu Zhanhe, this was probably his second chance.
He had already wasted one chance, and he really didn't want to waste a second one. In the end, he still took this step.
During this period of time, Wu Zhanhe had constantly sent people to spy on the situation in Tianqi City. The only Life and Death expert in Tianqi City was Ning Yudie, but that woman seemed to have fallen into a deep sleep. If it were Cui Xie, he would have directly killed his way into Tianqi City.
However, Wu Zhanhe's character was originally more conservative and careful. He was afraid that there was a trap, and planned to send people to assassinate Ning Yudie.
But the Tianchen Hall was heavily guarded, and so far, he hadn't made a move.
Today, Wu Zhanhe had gathered some of the old members of the Tian Xie Sect to discuss this problem.
At this moment, he suddenly saw a thin line appear in the sky. The moment that thin line appeared, it shot towards him at an extremely fast speed. Its speed was almost indescribable!
"What is that?" Wu Zhanhe's eyelids twitched. He didn't know why, but he had a bad premonition in his heart. But he couldn't tell where this premonition came from.
Several subordinates of the Tian Xie Sect heard Wu Zhanhe's words and raised their heads to look.
"It's a natural phenomenon!"
"Could it be that some secret treasure has appeared?"
"Should we fly up and take a look?"
Everyone couldn't tell the origin of the thin line, and each of them had a strange look on their faces.
However, in the process of flying, the thin line actually changed its direction slightly. This time, it actually went straight for Wu Zhanhe's group.
Wu Zhanhe's eyes widened, and a sense of crisis directly emerged from the depths of his soul. Although he still couldn't see clearly what it was, it was definitely not a good thing for him. This was what Wu Zhanhe thought at this moment.
From the appearance of the Eight Trigrams Wheel to the front of Wu Zhanhe's group, it only took two breaths of time. Then, it steadily stopped and began to slowly descend.

Chapter 915
"Luo, Luo, Luo … It's Luo Zheng!"
One of Wu Zhanhe's subordinates reminded him while trembling with fear.
But did Wu Zhanhe need him to remind him?
In fact, the only person these Lascivious Sect members were afraid of was Luo Zheng. The image of Luo Zheng killing Cui Xie that day was like a nightmare that lingered in their minds. The impression it left was too deep, and they would probably never be able to erase it in their lifetime.
Although Wu Zhanhe was extremely nervous, his mind was spinning rapidly. He first sized up Luo Zheng and found that Luo Zheng had already broken through to the Life and Death Realm!
First level of Life and Death Realm. Fortunately …
Even so, he still had a chance to escape. Since Luo Zheng had returned to the Central Domain, then he could choose to leave the Central Domain and flee overseas, never setting foot in the Central Domain again.
But when Wu Zhanhe glanced at the few people beside Luo Zheng, his face turned even uglier. His face revealed an incomparable look of despair.
He couldn't see through the strength of these people at all. This could only mean one thing: the difference between him and them wasn't as small as a whole realm. A bitter taste spread out from Wu Zhanhe's mouth. Even the most poisonous poison he refined wasn't this bitter.
"Is this the person? Do you need help? If you want to kill him, then kill him quickly. One slash is enough. "Yu Ze raised the black blade in his hand and glanced at Wu Zhanhe indifferently.
As for Qing Feng and the others, they closed their eyes and focused on cultivation. They couldn't be bothered to pay attention to a Life and Death Realm warrior from the lower realm. This kind of person was no different from an ant in their eyes. Who would be curious about an ant?
Luo Zheng shook his head. Of course, he knew that Yu Ze didn't want to help him. He just didn't want to waste time here. "I'll deal with this person myself."
At this moment, Wu Zhanhe suddenly took out two black beads from his hand. Although Wu Zhanhe was in despair, there was still a desire to live in his heart. Seeing that Luo Zheng was about to attack, Wu Zhanhe's Zhen Yuan suddenly gushed out.
At the same time, he crushed the two black beads in his hand. The black beads emitted a black poisonous mist, and the agitated Zhen Yuan instantly dispersed the poisonous mist!
"Monarch! Ah … "
"How could you …"
"I … I …"
The subordinates beside Wu Zhanhe didn't expect that Wu Zhanhe would directly activate the black beads at this moment.. The poisonous mist was extremely poisonous, and the faces of the surrounding Void Tribulation realm warriors turned black, and they instantly died.
Whoosh …
Using the cover of the poisonous fog, Wu Zhanhe turned into a streak of light and shot backwards.
"He didn't chase! There is still a glimmer of Hope! " Hope was still a Peipei was was, Peiqiu, Pee, Peipeon Pee's, Pee, Pee..
Gu Pei 'er was sitting on the Eight Trigrams Wheel. At this moment, she turned around and reminded him, "He's running away!"
Luo Zheng nodded, but responded, "I'm fine!"
After saying that, Luo Zheng took out a dark golden longbow from his hand, and at the same time, took out a black arrow from the quiver.
"Oh? A semi-divine bow, and a divine arrow, "Yu Ze said in a strange tone, not knowing whether it was envy or ridicule.
As for Qing Feng, he opened his eyes slightly and glanced at it, then closed his eyes again and didn't say anything. Divine weapons were rare in the Upper Realm, but Luo Zheng's bow was only a semi-divine weapon. The black arrow had the power of a divine weapon, but it was a consumable, so they didn't put it in their eyes.
After obtaining this bow, this was the first time Luo Zheng was trying it out. After all, as long as they were in the Greater Thousand World, there was no way Wu Zhanhe could escape his lock. Luo Zheng was indeed using him as a target.
The moment Luo Zheng put the arrow on the bow, he seemed to have a telepathic connection with the black arrow. It was as if the black arrow could understand Luo Zheng's intentions, and Luo Zheng could control the black arrow.
"This divine weapon can communicate with people's minds?" Luo Zheng thought curiously.
So far, the best weapon Luo Zheng had obtained was only this dark golden bow, but it was only a semi-divine weapon. As for a divine longsword, Luo Zheng wanted to have one, but it was a little difficult now. He estimated that he could only find one in the Upper Realm.
"Draw the bow!"
Weng …
Under Luo Zheng's pull, the bowstring emitted a faint humming sound. To draw the bow, Luo Zheng had used up nearly 200 dragon scales!
In front of him, Luo Zheng's line of sight was blocked by a cloud of black poisonous fog, and he could not see Wu Zhanhe's figure clearly with his naked eyes. He had just shot out like that, and he did not know how far he had escaped. But with a thought, Luo Zheng closed his eyes.
He directly searched for Wu Zhanhe's location through the will of the Upper Realm in his mind!
"Found it …" He said in a low voice, and then his fingers gently loosened.
Whoosh —
The black arrow shot out at this moment, directly piercing through the dense poisonous fog.
The black arrow's speed wasn't particularly fast at the beginning, about the same as the speed of an ordinary Divine Sea Realm expert at full speed.
However, when the black arrow shot out, Luo Zheng still felt that he was in a state of mind with the arrow. He felt that the black arrow seemed to contain three divine runes, and as long as he willed it, the divine runes would be activated!
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng's mind was stirred.
"Pa!"
A crisp explosion came from afar.
When this sound came, the black arrow's speed suddenly doubled!
"Explode again!"
"Pa!"
The black arrow's speed increased again. The faster the arrow was, the more powerful it was!
"Explode again!"
"Pa!"
At this time, the black arrow's speed had reached its limit. At this speed, even a Divine Sea Realm expert couldn't dodge it. Even when Luo Zheng controlled Feng Nianyun's body, he could only barely dodge it!
And Wu Zhanhe was just a Life and Death Realm warrior …
At this moment, Wu Zhanhe maintained his speed and madly fled. As he flew, he released his perception, but found that Luo Zheng didn't chase after him.
However, the moment he used his perception, he found an arrow behind him!
"You want to kill me with an arrow?"
At this moment, Wu Zhanhe thought it was impossible. With his speed, an ordinary arrow couldn't catch up with him.
Unfortunately, the black arrow's speed had exceeded the speed of Wu Zhanhe's mind. When he thought of this, the black arrow had already pierced through his mind, passing through the back of his head and drilling out between his eyebrows in an instant, leaving only a small bloody hole.
His body was frozen in mid-air. The remaining True Yuan was still working, allowing him to float in the air.
However, after a breath, the True Yuan dispersed. His body was like a heavy sandbag, falling straight down from the air and smashing into a hill with a loud bang.
Huff, huff, huff …
Luo Zheng gently turned the longbow and directly put it into the Sumeru Ring. With a smile on his face, he said to Boss Mo, "Thank you, Boss Mo. I've killed those who should be killed!"
Although there were former members of the Tian Xie Sect here, the remaining False Tribulation Realm warriors were nothing to be afraid of. They weren't worth his time.
From the beginning to the end, the time spent from Tianqi City to South Heaven Qin Mountain to kill Wu Zhanhe was probably less than the time it took for three incense sticks to burn.
Boss Mo nodded his head slightly. Now that they were done with what they needed to do, they could finally set off.
Then, Ha Peng controlled the Eight Trigrams Wheel and returned to Tianqi City. At this time, the crowd was still gathered in front of the Tianchen Palace and hadn't dispersed. In the end, seeing the Eight Trigrams Wheel return, the three leaders and Ning Yudie revealed a strange expression. It had only been a few incense sticks' worth of time. Why did it turn back?
Who knew that Luo Zheng would tell them that Wu Zhanhe had been killed by him in South Heaven Qin Mountain …
This efficiency was indeed extremely fast. It directly stunned everyone. Other people had to sharpen their knives to kill a chicken, but Luo Zheng had already taken the life of Wu Zhanhe, who was thousands of miles away.

Chapter 916
The group of Divine Realm experts from the Demi-Immortal Clan had given Luo Zheng a lot of time, and it was time for him to leave.
Ning Yudie and Xi Youqin both looked bitter. They had only met for less than two hours, and now they had to leave in a hurry. However, Ning Yudie could see that the group of super experts on the Wheel of Eight Trigrams was not particularly friendly with Luo Zheng. Now was not the time to throw a tantrum. She could only hope that Luo Zheng would quickly return to the Central Domain after he finished his business.
This time, it was inconvenient to bring Feng Nianyun with him to the Sea God Continent. Luo Zheng temporarily put her down and let the World Business Alliance take care of her.
However, because of this, Gu Peier was not happy. However, Qinglong told Luo Zheng that although the Soul Seal Technique was difficult to break, it had its own ways. There was no need to seek help from the Demi-Immortal Clan. Luo Zheng naturally did not care about Gu Peier's expression. After all, Feng Nianyun seemed to hate Gu Peier very much.
After making some preparations, Boss Mo waved his hand again and left a blood-red cross-shaped rune in Tianqi City. He also left a locator talisman in Tianqi City. Then, the Wheel of Eight Trigrams slowly rose again and rushed towards the southeast direction that Luo Zheng had pointed out.
Through the memory of the Vast Thousand Worlds, Luo Zheng had a concrete outline of the Vast Thousand Worlds. In this Vast Thousand Worlds, only twenty percent of the land was occupied by land, while the vast and boundless ocean took up eighty percent! A few main continents were concentrated in the west of the Vast Thousand Worlds, such as the Divine Kingdom Continent and the Central Domain. In addition, there was a huge wild land in the north of the Divine Kingdom Continent.
The Sea God Continent was an exception. This continent was isolated on the other side of the Vast Thousand Worlds, so there was a completely different culture on the Sea God Continent.
After leaving the southeast corner of the Central Domain, the Wheel of Eight Trigrams maintained a straight line at high speed, and began to fly steadily forward.
Going to the Sea God Continent without using the Teleportation Formation would take one across half of the Great Thousand World. Even for a Divine Realm warrior, it would be impossible to arrive there in an instant.
Although Luo Zheng could return to the Immortal Tomb of Heaven Mist and use the teleportation portal to reach the Sea God Continent, Luo Zheng still had some reservations about the seven Demi-Immortals. He didn't want to disturb Daoist Heaven Mist's slumber.
At this speed, other than the blue horizon and blue sky, it was as if there was nothing between heaven and earth.
Most of the time, the Wheel of Eight Trigrams was silent. Other than the occasional whimper of the Ancestral Dragon, everyone else was sitting cross-legged, meditating like monks. Ultimate God Realm warriors had already opened up their Inner Worlds. Luo Zheng had also seen Feng Nianyun's Inner World. Ultimate God Realm warriors had already opened up their Inner Worlds. Luo Zheng guessed that these people were probably managing their Inner Worlds.
"Caw caw …"
The Ancestral Dragon that had been curled up beside Luo Zheng suddenly cried out. Its large eyes stared at Luo Zheng without moving.
Seeing this little thing, Luo Zheng smiled faintly. Only then did he remember how he should deal with this little thing.
Qinglong had asked him to take this Ancestral Dragon egg. However, ever since the Ancestral Dragon hatched, Luo Zheng hadn't had the time to think about what to do with it.
When Luo Zheng asked Qinglong, Qinglong's voice drifted over. "This Ancestral Dragon has already been tainted with your imprint. If you forcefully remove it, it will affect it to a certain extent. That's probably why the Demi-Immortals didn't take it from you."
The key reason why the Demi-Immortals didn't take the Ancestral Dragon away was that they recognized Luo Zheng's identity. At the same time, they also knew that forcefully removing the imprint would negatively affect the Ancestral Dragon. After confirming the location of the Exquisite Pagoda, they would most likely bring Luo Zheng to the Demi-Immortals and raise the Ancestral Dragon exclusively …
Of course, this also depended on Luo Zheng's talent. If it was as Luo Zheng said, that he had obtained the title of Golden Demi-Immortal, then everything would be completely different.
Azure Dragon knew that Luo Zheng wasn't lying. "So, let's see what the Demi-Immortals plan to do," he said. "The way I see it, they'll let you raise the ancestral dragon. Heh heh." Obviously, Azure Dragon knew that Luo Zheng wasn't lying. Someone as talented as him would shine in any corner of the world. Once the Demi-Immortals knew how talented he was, they wouldn't offend him.
"Let me raise it!" Luo Zheng's face revealed a look of astonishment.
From beginning to end, Luo Zheng had never intended to do this. It was Qinglong's idea to enter the Heavenly Stellar Holy Land to obtain the Ancestral Dragon's egg. He didn't want to take care of the little guy.
Qinglong said indifferently, "What's wrong? You don't want to? This imprint is even stronger than the contract between Huo Yuner and the True Phoenix. Moreover, the Ancestral Dragon's talent is much stronger than the True Phoenix's. When this little guy grows up, it will be a great help to you in the future. "
"But I don't know how to raise this little guy!" Luo Zheng knew that from Qinglong's standpoint, he wouldn't harm him. Since the Ancestral Dragon was the object of worship of the entire True Dragon Clan, its talent must be extraordinary. The problem was that Luo Zheng really didn't intend to raise it.
Qinglong smiled faintly and said, "The Ancestral Dragon grows very quickly. It won't be long before it grows up. You won't need to worry about it, so you should try your best to fight for it with the Demi-Immortal Clan."
Luo Zheng wasn't convinced by Qinglong, but when he saw the little guy curled up next to him and fall asleep, Luo Zheng blinked and finally nodded.
In Luo Zheng's mind, Qinglong's huge dragon eyes revealed a trace of loneliness for the first time. The red dragon felt Qinglong's emotions and communicated with him, "Ninth brother, if they are heartless, we won't be righteous either. Even if we don't care about our clan, wouldn't it be better for us nine brothers to set up our own clan?"
The red dragon's temperament was straightforward and fiery, so he didn't think as much as Qinglong. Qinglong's dragon head shook slightly and he said faintly, "Alas, we nine brothers are from the True Dragon Clan after all. It's not wrong to rely on the Ancestral Dragon to set up our own clan, but alas!"
There were many disputes within the True Dragon Clan. Back then, the nine True Dragons had paid a huge price because of this dispute. Up until now, only two True Dragon souls had been awakened.
If it weren't for Luo Zheng, perhaps this dispute of the "Mystery of the Nine Dragons" would have slowly been drowned in the long river of history. However, as Luo Zheng grew stronger step by step, Qinglong realized that he had found an opportunity. If the Ancestral Dragon and Luo Zheng grew together, Luo Zheng would help them rebuild the Sky Spill Body in the future, and they would be able to set up a new True Dragon Clan. Qinglong was happy, but when he thought of the past, he sank into sorrow …
The Wheel of Eight Trigrams flew at an extremely fast speed. It only took a few hours to cross the Chaotic Astral Ocean from the Divine Kingdom Continent to the Central Domain. However, from the Central Domain to the Sea God Continent, it took a full two days and two nights!
On the way, Luo Zheng also encountered several huge Fierce Beasts. For example, there were Fierce Beasts in the sea that were two thousand feet long, and there was also a giant bird with a wingspan of more than ten thousand feet, like a palace in the sky. However, these Level 11 Fierce Beasts, which were comparable to Divine Sea Realm experts, were keenly aware of the group of Divine Realm experts on the Wheel of Eight Trigrams. Their faces were filled with fear, and they didn't dare to provoke them.
As for Gu Pei 'er and the others of the Demi-Immortal Clan, they weren't interested in these Fierce Beasts. There weren't many things in the lower world that could interest them. Their goal was only the Exquisite Pagoda.
After two days and two nights, a continent appeared in front of them again. It was the Sea God Continent that they hadn't seen for a long time.
"Where is the Exquisite Pagoda located on this continent?" Qingfeng asked indifferently.
Luo Zheng nodded, and then said, "It's right in the center of this continent!"
"Very good." Qingfeng smiled coldly, and said to Ha Peng, "Let's go."
At this time, Luo Zheng shook his head and said, "If you want to get close to the Exquisite Pagoda, I'm afraid it won't be that easy …"
"What do you mean?" Not only Qingfeng, but the others also frowned at the same time. As the saying goes, seeing is believing. Although Luo Zheng said it as if it was true, before they saw the Exquisite Pagoda, they kept a skeptical attitude towards anything Luo Zheng said.

Chapter 917
In the past, Daoist Tian Miao had also stepped into the Sea God Continent. However, he was intimidated by the cursed land. In the end, he could only build a huge transportation array to connect the two continents. Then, he waited for countless years in the Tian Miao Immortal Tomb before Luo Zheng arrived.
If Luo Zheng's guess was correct, Daoist Tian Miao was also a Peak Divine expert. With his strength, he couldn't enter the cursed land. Then, the seven people in front of him couldn't enter either.
Previously, Luo Zheng could also enter the cursed land unimpeded. However, at that time, he was only in the Divine Reflection Realm. Now that Luo Zheng had advanced to the Life and Death Realm, he would naturally be affected by the power of the curse.
Luo Zheng briefly explained the cursed land to everyone. Boss Mo's face slightly darkened. However, he still ordered Ha Peng to control the Eight Trigrams Wheel to continue forward.
As they sped through the skies of the Sea God's Continent, they soon saw a distinct line on the Sea God's Continent. Everything on the outside of the line seemed normal, but the inside of the line was pitch-black. There were no plants, no living creatures, just like a monochromatic black world ….
"This is the cursed land?" Boss Mo stared below and said.
After Luo Zheng nodded, Qing Feng said, "Let's go down and take a look."
As Peak Divine experts, they weren't reckless people. Since they already knew that the cursed land was so strange, they wouldn't rely on their cultivation base to force their way in.
After the Eight Trigrams Wheel slowly descended, the seven people stepped down. Luo Zheng also jumped down with the Ancestral Gragon in his arms. The little thing in his arms looked at the cursed land with fear in its eyes. It seemed that it was also afraid of the power in the cursed land. It was afraid that Luo Zheng would bring it in.
"Ga Gu!"
Seeing the little thing's scared expression, Luo Zheng rubbed its head and said with a smile, "Don't worry. I won't bring you in."
Luo Zheng was also curious. Logically speaking, the Ancestral Dragon had just been born. Although it was extremely talented, it should only be treated as a Level 1 Direbeast and should not be affected by the power of the curse. Perhaps its fear came entirely from its keen instincts.
Looking at the cursed land, Boss Mo frowned and asked, "Qingfeng, what do you think?"
Qing Feng took two steps forward and was already close to the edge of the cursed land. If he took another step forward, he would enter the cursed land. After pausing for two breaths, he actually stretched out a hand into the cursed land!
"Be careful, the power of the curse is very strong!" Luo Zheng warned.
Back then, Luo Zheng had personally witnessed two Battle-Saints being turned into a pool of blood. The power of the curse was not something that could be resisted by humans.
Qing Feng didn't listen to Luo Zheng's advice and insisted on reaching his hand into the cursed land!
It would take some time for the power of the curse to take effect. Qing Feng wanted to test how strong the power of the curse was.
After a while, Qing Feng's expression suddenly changed. He instantly withdrew his hand and retreated quickly. At the same time, he shouted, "Pei 'er!"
Gu Pei 'er took a step forward and the flower in her right eye began to rotate rapidly. At the same time, a light pink light was released from the stamen of the flower!
It was under the illumination of this light that some things were revealed!
Luo Zheng looked over and found that it was a group of ugly little ghosts!
These little ghosts were about the size of a finger. Although they were small, they looked extremely disgusting. There were hundreds of them densely stacked together. They swarmed toward Qing Feng's right hand and kept biting it!
"Buzz, buzz, buzz …"
At this time, Qing Feng had already activated his protective Zhen Yuan. At the same time, he released a small enchantment to protect his right hand. However, these little ghosts were completely speechless. Under the biting of his enchantment and protective Zhen Yuan, the flesh on Qing Feng's hand turned into pus and flowed slowly. It looked very miserable!
After a breath, those little ghosts had already bitten Qing Feng's right hand clean. They even wanted to spread along Qing Feng's arm.
However, he was a bold person. He calmly glanced at Yu Ze, who was beside him. Yu Ze had already slashed out with his Onyx Blade.
"Puchi!"
Qing Feng's right hand, which was only left with the bones, instantly broke and fell to the ground.
Those little ghosts couldn't spread along Qing Feng's flesh and blood. They turned around and continued to gnaw on Qing Feng's bones!
"Damn it, what the hell is this!" Yu Ze looked at those densely packed little ghosts and felt his scalp go numb. These little ghosts weren't strong, but they could ignore any Zhen Yuan defense. They could even penetrate Qing Feng's enchantment. This was too terrifying.
Qing Feng's eyes were indifferent. He gently waved his right arm, and a stream of golden blood flowed out from the broken arm. The blood flowed continuously and gathered into the shape of a hand. Finally, after the golden light dissipated, it began to form the bones and meridians. Soon, it formed the skin. In a short while, a right hand was formed!
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng was amazed. This Qing Feng was so arrogant. He indeed had a great ability. Even in the lower realm, a broken arm could be reconstructed. However, after the reconstruction, his strength would be greatly reduced. It could even become a flaw in his body.
However, looking at Qing Feng's nonchalant expression, he was afraid that this ability of his could completely restore his broken arm without leaving any flaws …
"Da da da …"
Under the light pink light emitted by the flower above Gu Pei 'er's right eye, those little ghosts couldn't hide. At the same time, everyone could hear the densely packed sounds of gnawing, which made them shudder.
Boss Mo slowly walked over. He lowered his head and looked at those little ghosts. He asked, "This thing, could it be …"
"It's the Ghost Curse Technique. However, it's really rare to release such a large-scale Ghost Curse Technique in the lower realm!" Gu Pei 'er said indifferently.
In other aspects, Boss Mo and Qing Feng knew a lot. However, in the aspect of Soul Curse Technique, Gu Pei 'er was an expert.
"Da da da …"
Under the continuous gnawing, the bones in Qing Feng's hand quickly disappeared. Then, those little ghosts scattered and flew toward the cursed land. Soon, they disappeared in front of everyone.
Boss Mo looked at the cursed land. He frowned and asked Luo Zheng, "You're saying that only warriors below the Life and Death Realm can enter this cursed land?"
Luo Zheng nodded and said, "In this continent, False Tribulation Realm warriors are called War Revered. As long as the warriors of the Sea God Continent cultivate to the peak of War Revered, which is the late stage of False Tribulation Realm, they must leave the cursed land. Otherwise, they will be cursed to death."
This cursed land was a forbidden place for War Sages and Divine Sea Realm warriors.
Gu Pei 'er said indifferently, "This Ghost Curse Technique has a target set. It's not difficult!"
"What should we do? How do we go to the Exquisite Pagoda?" The burly man asked.
Qing Feng stared at the cursed land and said, "I don't believe that this cursed land is filled with this Ghost Curse Technique!"
Gu Pei 'er smiled, "Why don't you take a look?" As soon as her voice fell, the flower in her right eye rotated gently and released a light pink light that was a hundred times larger than before.
Under this light, Luo Zheng, Boss Mo, Qing Feng, and the others were shocked.
In this cursed land, there were countless little ghosts densely packed together. Because there were too many of them, they were squeezed together and crazily surged!
Perhaps because the space was too crowded, these little ghosts seemed to be devouring each other. However, after devouring, new little ghosts were born …
"Cough cough cough …" Seeing this scene, Yu Ze's hair stood on end. He hurriedly said, "Senior sister, quickly extinguish your light!"
Not only Yu Ze, but other than Boss Mo and Gu Pei 'er, the others showed uncomfortable expressions.

Chapter 918
Only the light pink light released by Gu Peier could reveal the true form of the cursed land!
When Luo Zheng entered the cursed land, if he knew that it was filled with small ghosts, no matter how bold he was, it would be difficult for him to enter …
Probably because Yu Ze, Qing Feng, and the others were not used to it, Gu Peier smiled slightly and withdrew the light pink light.
The small ghosts that were originally densely packed suddenly disappeared, and the cursed land in front of them became empty again.
"Huff, huff …"
Yu Ze touched his chest and muttered, "It's so disgusting!"
Boss Mo shook his head and said, "So, it's impossible to force our way in."
Qing Feng nodded, "These small ghosts can't stop us at all. If we enter, we will be eaten up by them. It seems that we can only report this matter to the higher-ups."
Hearing Qing Feng's words, Boss Mo frowned and said slowly, "This time, when we descended to the lower realm, we didn't get rid of the evil dragon, and the ancestral dragon …" He looked at Luo Zheng. Qing Feng shrugged, "What else do you want?"
Boss Mo still stared at Luo Zheng and said, "I still want to enter the Linglong Pagoda to take a look."
Qing Feng rolled his eyes. In his heart, he felt that Boss Mo's words were nonsense. The problem was that everyone had clearly seen the scene just now. Was this cursed land a place where they could enter?
Yu Ze suddenly thought of something and stared at Luo Zheng with a sneer, "I know, Luo Zheng, you already knew that we were destined to not be able to enter the cursed land, so you boasted that you got some title. Anyway, we can't verify it."
Luo Zheng's face revealed a cold smile. He was too lazy to pay attention to this guy.
At this moment, Boss Mo's eyes fell on Ha Peng and asked, "Ha Peng, this time, did you bring a puppet?"
"Puppet!"
"Yes, Ha Peng's puppet!"
Everyone's eyes suddenly lit up. Ha Peng was not only proficient in all kinds of flying tools, but he was also a puppet master!
If everyone borrowed Ha Peng's puppet, they would also be able to enter the Cursed Land unimpeded. Because of the existence of the Cursed Land, they didn't expect to bring the Linglong Pagoda with them. However, they still wanted to confirm it. Especially Yu Ze, he really wanted to expose Luo Zheng's lie. If they entered the Linglong Pagoda and didn't see Luo Zheng's title on the Innate Talent Monument, then they would see how this brat would explain himself!
When Ha Peng saw that everyone was looking at him, he said helplessly, "Unfortunately, I didn't bring any puppets this time …"
Hearing Ha Peng's words, everyone immediately felt discouraged. They had come all the way here, and their last trace of hope had been obliterated.
However, Boss Mo said, "It shouldn't be difficult for you to make a few sets of puppets using local materials, right?"
Ha Peng nodded, "Not at all. However, the puppets made from materials from the Lower World will have their power greatly reduced. They won't be able to make puppets that are comparable to the Spirit Sea Realm."
"Spirit Sea Realm puppets …"
Hearing Ha Peng's tone, it seemed that he could make such powerful puppets. Luo Zheng sighed in his heart. No wonder these people from the Demi-Immortal Tribe were so arrogant. The difference between the Lower World and the Upper World was indeed shocking.
"Okay! Why do we need to be so powerful? We're not going in to fight. We'll just look for materials on this continent. "Yu Ze sneered as he stared at Luo Zheng. Normally, when a person's lie was about to be exposed, there would be some anxiety on their face. He wanted to find this kind of expression on Luo Zheng's face, but he was a little disappointed this time.
Luo Zheng didn't have the slightest anxiety on his face. Instead, he said, "I'm familiar with several races on this Sea God Continent. If we lack any resources, they should have a reserve. It will be much easier than us finding them ourselves."
"That's also very good," Boss Mo said with a smile.
Then everyone returned to the Wheel of Eight Trigrams. This time, they followed Luo Zheng's directions and headed straight for the Heavenly Feather Holy Land!
The Sea God Continent wasn't big, especially since the cursed land occupied the center. The surrounding area was probably much smaller than the Central Domain. In less than an incense stick's worth of time, the Wheel of Eight Trigrams had arrived above the Heavenly Feather Holy Land.
But just as the Wheel of Eight Trigrams arrived, Luo Zheng's eyes suddenly flashed. He saw a long column of smoke rising from the Holy Tree in the distance. At the same time, a powerful True Essence fluctuation came from the distance!
"War?" Luo Zheng's eyes twitched and he frowned.
The Heavenly Feather Holy Land had Liu Yu in charge. How could the other races dare to challenge the Demon Night Clan?
When Luo Zheng was in trouble, the Purple Heart Holy Land joined forces with the Heavenly Feather Holy Land to send many Battle-Saints to the Central Domain. Even Situ Haotian and several Divine Sea Realm experts of the Demon Night Clan personally came to the Central Domain!
The reason why the experts of the two Holy Lands dared to come out in full force was because Liu Yu was in charge of the Demon Night Clan. As the strongest existence on the Sea God Continent, she was also the one with the highest talent. She alone was enough to guard the Purple Heart Holy Land and the Heavenly Feather Holy Land!
It could be said that as long as Liu Yu was here, the Twin Devil Holy Land wouldn't dare to act rashly.
But now, a war had broken out in the Heavenly Feather Holy Land? Could something have happened to Liu Yu?
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng's face darkened. He jumped down and rushed towards the Purple Heart Holy Land.
Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh …
Hundreds of streaks of light intersected in the sky. Those were Battle-Saints fighting!
Luo Zheng sized them up and a cold light flashed in his eyes. "It really is the Demon Race! The Twin Devil Holy Land … They actually dared to attack the Heavenly Feather Holy Land! "
At this moment, two streaks of light intersected. One of the streaks of light dragged a long and narrow white light, and within that white light, there was a flourishing light.
Ling Yan? Following that streak of light, Luo Zheng saw a woman flying at high speed.
At this moment, Ling Yan was already in an extremely sorry state. Her slender body was covered with injuries. Her waist had a bloody wound that was so deep that the bone could be seen, and the clothes on her body were also extremely messy. However, she still clenched her teeth and continued to fight with her opponent!
Ling Yan's opponent was a Battle-Saint of the Demon Race!
At that time, Ling Yan was a peak War Reverend. Aside from Luo Zheng, she was the most powerful existence in the Tower of Sin. However, after leaving the Tower of Sin, her cultivation base increased and she became a first level Battle-Saint. Although she had used the Light of Creation accumulated in the Tower of Sin to break through several times and finally became a second level Battle-Saint, it was still difficult for her to deal with the fourth level Battle-Saint of the Devil Race.
"Hahahahaha! Ling Yan, I think you should just surrender. Woman of the Demon Night Clan, tsk tsk! I won't kill you. Although you and I are not of the same race, if you become my captive, I guarantee that I will make you wish you were dead for the rest of your life! " The Battle-Saint of the Demon Race laughed arrogantly. However, he did not relax the fiery-red broadsword in his hand. Streaks of light from the broadsword went straight for Ling Yan's vital points.
At this moment, Luo Zheng's figure flashed and approached Ling Yan.
Ling Yan's perception was very sharp. Although she was concentrating on dodging the broadsword of the Battle-Saint of the Demon Race, her perception was still paying attention to her surroundings. When Luo Zheng rushed toward her, she almost subconsciously judged Luo Zheng as a Demon Race. Therefore, her figure suddenly spun and flowers bloomed. Those flowers uniformly changed direction and pierced toward Luo Zheng!
"Ling Yan, it's me!" Luo Zheng's figure flashed and dodged those flowers.
Hearing this familiar voice, Ling Yan suddenly turned her head and looked over. Originally, there was a trace of confusion on Ling Yan's face, but the moment she saw Luo Zheng, her face immediately revealed an expression of great joy. Within this joy, there was also an expression of disbelief. She never thought that Luo Zheng would actually come back.
"Luo Zheng!" Ling Yan called out.
When the Battle-Saint of the Demon Race, who was hot on her heels, heard the name "Luo Zheng", he was slightly taken aback. Then, he used his gaze to size up Luo Zheng.
To the Twin Devil Holy Land, the name Luo Zheng was like a nightmare. It was this guy who single-handedly drove the Demon Race out of the Tower of Sin. However, although Luo Zheng's name was resounding, the majority of the Demon Race had never seen his true appearance.

Chapter 919
Just like the Devil Clan Battle-Saint in front of her, he had already left the Tower of Sin a few years ago, breaking through the shackles of a Peak Battle-Saint and stepping into the ranks of Battle-Saints.
Naturally, he could no longer enter the Cursed Land, nor could he enter the Tower of Sin.
Therefore, Luo Zheng's name was engraved in his heart like a brand, but he had never seen Luo Zheng.
Hearing Ling Yan shout out the two words "Luo Zheng," the Devil Clan Battle-Saint suddenly revealed an excited expression, "Luo Zheng! Luo Zheng … Hahahahaha, I heard that you had already left the Sea God Continent, and I felt very regretful! I didn't expect that you would actually dare to come back to court death! Interesting, interesting! "
After Ling Yan saw Luo Zheng, she was excited, but she suddenly thought of something! Now was not the right time for Luo Zheng to appear. Although she had already seen Luo Zheng's cultivation breakthrough, he was still only a first level Battle-Saint!
Luo Zheng's strength naturally could not be measured by cultivation. Back then, when he was only a Battle-Saint, he had already killed a Battle-Saint!
The problem was that the Twin Devil Holy Land and the Heavenly Feather Holy Land had launched a huge sect war, and there were several almighty Spirit Sea Realm experts among them. In a moment, the almighty Spirit Sea Realm experts would arrive, and Luo Zheng would only have a dead end.
"Luo Zheng, run! The Heavenly Feather Holy Land is about to fall, run! "Ling Yan warned with all her might.
Luo Zheng was indifferent to Ling Yan's warning. His eyes darkened, and he asked, "The Heavenly Feather Holy Land has Liu Yu overseeing it. Why does the Twin Devil Holy Land dare to attack?"
Before Ling Yan could explain, the Devil Clan Battle-Saint sneered, "Since you're here, you still want to escape! You're dreaming! " The Devil Clan Battle-Saint had long heard of Luo Zheng's name, and all the Devil Clan people in the Twin Devil Holy Land hated Luo Zheng to the bone.
After all, when Luo Zheng drove the Demon Race out of the Sin Tower, it was akin to cutting off the Twin Devil Holy Land's path of retreat. Without the help of the Sin Tower, the Twin Devil Holy Land's decline was only a matter of time.
At that time, when many peak Battle-Sages of the Demon Clan returned to the Twin Devil Holy Land, everyone in the Twin Devil Holy Land, from Battle-Sages to Battle-Sages, was cursing Luo Zheng like boiling water. As for this Battle-Sage of the Demon Clan, he was so angry that he directly tore down his house!
If it wasn't for the fact that anyone who entered the Cursed Lands would definitely die, he probably would have directly charged into the Cursed Lands and torn Luo Zheng to pieces.
Now that he saw Luo Zheng again, how could he let Luo Zheng off so easily?
"Sou!"
The body of the Devil Race was huge. This Devil Race Battle-Saint was like a small mountain. He directly rushed towards Luo Zheng. The large blade in his hand was mixed with a heavy pressure. He wanted to cut Luo Zheng in two.

Ling Yan had a certain amount of confidence in Luo Zheng. She believed that after Luo Zheng entered the Life and Death Realm, his strength had also made a qualitative leap. After merging with the will of the boundless world, Luo Zheng formed a connection with the boundless world. As long as the boundless world wasn't destroyed, Luo Zheng couldn't be killed. The Life and Death Realm was known as the minor heavenly tribulation, while the Spirit Sea Realm was known as the major heavenly tribulation. The major heavenly tribulation was much more powerful and terrifying than the minor heavenly tribulation.
Although Liu Yu's talent was great, according to common sense, the major heavenly tribulation that descended would be even more terrifying.
In the past, because the Tower of Sin maintained the delicate balance of the entire Sea God Continent, the various races were harmonious and didn't have a falling out. At that time, even if Liu Yu was undergoing his major heavenly tribulation, the Twin Devil Sacred Ground wouldn't take such a huge risk to attack the Purple Heart Sacred Ground.

Chapter 921

Limit!

Chapter 922

Just as Luo Zheng was about to be enveloped by Mo Xiao's Domain, a blue light shone down. Lan Bing's Water Domain cut down from the sky and blocked Luo Zheng's path.

Mo Xiao sneered and shook his head, "Lan Bing, you are no match for me. As for Luo Zheng … he is definitely not a junior. Even if he is only a beginner Battle-Saint, he is still qualified to be my opponent!"
"What an honor." Luo Zheng stood behind Lan Bing. The dark golden longbow appeared in his hand again and the black arrow was nocked on the bowstring. Then, he said, "Lan Bing, think of a way to hold this guy back."
Mo Xiao's eyelids twitched when he saw the black arrow in Luo Zheng's hand. On the other hand, Lan Bing had already witnessed the might of the black arrow. If she could keep Mo Xiao busy, then she might be able to kill him!
"Okay, I will do my best!"
As soon as Lan Bing finished speaking, she gently bit the tip of her tongue. Her body swayed and she burned her essence blood. Suddenly, numerous blue water tornadoes appeared in her Water Ripple Domain. They were like hundreds of octopus tentacles that swept toward Mo Xiao!
"Hold me back? In your dreams! " Mo Xiao gently clenched his remaining left hand, and flaming blades shot out from his Domain, instantly slicing the water tornadoes apart!
However, when the two Divine Sea Realm experts were fighting, the black arrow in Luo Zheng's hand shot out again!
"Whoosh … Pa pa pa!"
The three accelerated black arrows arrived in an instant. However, Mo Xiao was prepared this time. He swayed and dodged Luo Zheng's arrow by a hair's breadth.
After missing the first arrow, Luo Zheng nocked another arrow on the bowstring. The second arrow arrived in an instant …
Then the third arrow …
The fourth arrow …
With the cooperation of Luo Zheng and Lan Bing, Mo Xiao was indeed having a hard time. Although he was strong enough to suppress Lan Bing, it would take some time for him to take her down. Although he was stronger than Lan Bing, Lan Bing's Water Technique was very effective in restraining him.
Now, he had to divert his attention to dodge Luo Zheng's deadly black arrow. With one less arm, he could not concentrate on fighting against Lan Bing's water tornadoes.
For a moment, Mo Xiao was in danger. Luo Zheng's black arrow flew past Mo Xiao's ear several times and almost pierced his head!
"Go help Old Ancestor Mo Xiao!"
Some of the Battle-Saints below saw that Old Ancestor Mo Xiao was in danger and rushed up to help.
However, the Battle-Saints of the Fiendish Night Clan naturally wouldn't let them. Soon, the Battle-Saints of the Sky Feather Holy Land began to fight again …
Mo Xiao was also complaining incessantly. He didn't think that he would be in such trouble. If this continued, it would only be a matter of time before he was killed by Luo Zheng!
However, at this moment, three more streaks of light flew over from the horizon!
Another three Divine Sea Realm experts!
However, when Mo Xiao noticed the three Divine Sea Realm experts, he was overjoyed. On the other hand, Lan Bing and Luo Zheng's faces darkened.
"Mo Ji, Mo Wuliang! Hurry up and help me kill the kid who shot the arrow! "Mo Xiao shouted to the sky as he fought against Lan Bing.
Two of the three Divine Sea Realm Almighties in the sky were the other two Divine Sea Realm Almighties from the Flame Devil Holy Land. Extreme Mo and Endless Mo were chasing after one of the Divine Sea Realm Almighties from the Sky Feather Holy Land, Tian Xuan!

Chapter 923

No matter how much advantage they had, it was useless. Once the Divine Sea Realm experts of their clan were defeated, the curtains of the war would fall. Therefore, when the creatures of the Demon Night Clan's holy land saw the three Divine Sea Realm experts of the Demon Race appear, all of their faces were filled with despair.

She wasn't the only one. The other women from the Demon Night Clan were also praying. Perhaps the three kings of the Demon Night Clan would bring them a miracle at the final moment!
The Eight Trigrams Wheel floated far away in the sky. The Ancestral Dragon was hopping around on the Eight Trigrams Wheel, constantly making gurgling noises from its mouth. It seemed to be very anxious.
The Ancestral Dragon's soul had been imprinted with Luo Zheng's imprint, so it could sense the danger Luo Zheng was in. However, Qing Feng had used a barrier to envelop the Ancestral Dragon. It was still too small. No matter how hard it tried, it couldn't pass through the barrier!
"Ai, let's go down and help. We can't really let this kid die here," Qing Feng said coldly.

Gu Pei 'er nodded and jumped down from the Eight Trigrams Wheel. Yuze helplessly waved the Onyx Blade in his hand. Then, he turned into a streak of light and flew towards the Sky Feather Sacred Land.
When Lan Bing saw the other Demon Race Divine Sea Realm expert charging down, she clenched her teeth and bit the tip of her tongue again, burning her essence blood again.
Burning essence blood could, for a short period of time, increase her strength. However, she couldn't burn her essence blood indefinitely. After burning it, she needed a long time to recover her essence blood.
"Hualala …"
The water tornadoes danced even more wildly. However, this time, it was impossible for her to stop two Demon Race Divine Sea Realm experts by herself.

What was going on?
Had he given up?

At this moment, Lan Bing noticed that two more people were rapidly approaching from the distance!

Could they be Divine Realm experts? At this moment, such a guess suddenly appeared in Lan Bing's heart.
The Yao Night Clan wasn't cut off from the Upper Realm. It wasn't difficult to obtain information about the Upper Realm through the King Statue, so Lan Bing came to this conclusion easily.

The Yao Night Clan and the Demon Race fell into fear and confusion. At the same time, both sides were trying to guess the intentions of this man and woman. Which side were they on?
Without a doubt, with their strength, it would be too easy for them to destroy the Sky Feather Sacred Ground and the Twin Demon Sacred Ground …
But why would such powerful existences interfere in a battle of this level?
The answer was quickly revealed. Gu Peier slowly floated towards Luo Zheng and said, "That's enough. Let's end this quickly. We still need to find materials for Ha Peng to make a puppet!" Gu Peier and the others didn't care about the battle below at all. A battle of this level was as boring as a child's play in front of the Demi-Immortals.

Luo Zheng shrugged his shoulders. This time, Luo Zheng didn't show any signs of weakness when facing Yuze. He just smiled faintly and said, "When I step into the Extreme Divine Realm, I'll beat you ten times over!"
"Pfft." When Gu Peier heard this, she instantly laughed. Then, she said, "That's really possible!"

However, Yu Ze didn't like Luo Zheng from the very beginning. That was why he mocked him.
Luo Zheng, Gu Peier, and Yu Ze were chatting happily. On the other hand, the Demon Night Clan and the Demon Race were in a state of ice and fire!
Lan Bing, Tian Xuan, and the other Battle Saints of the Demon Night Clan were burning with passion! It was as if a taut string had been loosened. Some of the Demon Night Clan women even felt like collapsing! Because at this moment, they knew that the Sky Feather Sacred Ground had made it through!
As for the Demon Race … Mo Xiao, Mo Ji, and Mo Wuliang, the three Divine Sea Realm experts, as well as the many Battle Saints and War Reverends of the Demon Race, their hearts fell into a bottomless abyss at this moment.

Chapter 924
The two Divine Sea experts looked indifferent, but at this moment, the pressure they exerted on the Demon Race was as great as a mountain.

In the Upper World, the Demon Race was not weak, but they were extremely aggressive and domineering!
The Demi-Immortal Race was a neutral existence in the Upper World. Their goals were poles apart from the other races. The Human Race, the Demon Race, and the Demon Race all had the core goal of expanding their own race. However, the Demi-Immortal Race was formed by countless geniuses from other races. This race was originally very unique in the universe. Their goals were completely different from the other races in the sense of the word.
Because the Demi-Immortal Race was neutral and incomparably powerful, they had good relations with most races in the universe. For example, the Human Race, the Demon Race, and the other powerful races in the universe.
However, the Demi-Immortal Race and the Demon Race had always been in a state of hostility. On one hand, the Demon Race had a strong sense of identity with their own race. They did not allow any Demon Race genius to join the Demi-Immortal Race. If they were found to be traitors, they would be hunted down in the universe.
On the other hand, the Demon Race had a fierce temper. They had fought with the Demi-Immortal Race several times, and even Celestial Masters had made a move.
Because of this, the Demi-Immortal Race had a very bad impression of the Demon Race. Now that they were in the Lower World, how could they let these Demon Race people go?

Cultivators revered the strong as the heavens, and if they wanted to attack, they could find a hundred reasons to do so. . "If the two seniors don't interfere, the Twin Demon Holy Land will give you all the treasures of our clan and bow to you …" Mo Xiao continued.

No one was willing to sit and wait for death. Knowing that they were no match for them, the three chose to escape at the same time!
"Escape?" Yu Ze's face revealed a bit of coldness. His body suddenly turned into a black shadow. After circling once on the spot, his figure flashed and disappeared in front of everyone.

As for Gu Peier, she chased after Mo Xiao with a bell-like laughter. Compared to Yu Ze's sharp blade technique and terrifying speed, Gu Peier only followed behind Mo Xiao slowly. The flower in her eyes began to emit a faint green light.
Being enveloped by the light, Mo Xiao's speed gradually slowed down. Soon, a look of fear appeared on Mo Xiao's face as he continued to let out earth-shattering roars. The Ghost Head Staff in his hand constantly danced with flames as he frantically attacked the empty space in front of him.
"Roar, roar, roar …"
As the speed of the Demonic Howl became faster and faster, an arrow of blood suddenly burst out from his chest. His heart actually burst open on its own. The Demonic Race was huge, and their hearts were far stronger than the average living creatures. After the heart burst open, the arrow of blood that shot out actually shot dozens of feet away.

Gu Peier was still smiling. With her hands behind her back, the flower in her right eye stopped spinning and flew toward Luo Zheng.
