Now that the three Divine Sea Realm experts of the Demon Clan had been killed in an instant, the rest of the Demon Clan's morale plummeted. They all scattered and fled …
As for the members of the Demon Night Clan, their morale was greatly boosted. They began to chase after the Demon Night Clan.

Chapter 925

Hearing Yu Ze's long speech with Liu Yu, Luo Zheng couldn't help but roll his eyes. He was also recognized by the Demi-Immortal Clan, but this guy didn't even smile at him.
After Liu Yu understood the purpose of Luo Zheng's visit, she instructed Lan Bing and Tian Xuan to deal with the aftermath of the Heavenly Feather Sacred Ground while she personally helped them gather the materials. The seven people from the Demi-Immortal Clan could only rest in the Yao Ye Clan …
At this time, Situ Haotian of the Purple Heart Holy Land also rushed over. The Twin Devil Sacred Ground's attack on the Yao Ye Clan was very sudden. By the time the humans received the news and rushed over, the dust had already settled.
Liu Yu stood on a branch at the top of the Sacred Tree and looked down at the scorched Heavenly Feather Sacred Ground. Her face revealed a pained expression.

The Divine Sea Realm experts of the Twin Devil Sacred Ground had all perished. Before a new Divine Sea Realm expert was born, even the Purple Heart Holy Land was strong enough to destroy the Twin Devil Sacred Ground!
Liu Yu nodded slightly, then turned her head and asked, "Luo Zheng, I want to know how my king is doing."
A while ago, the light on Xun's king statue had almost completely dissipated. Liu Yu had almost thought that Xun had died, but the light had slowly stabilized as it was about to dissipate. Now, it was gradually recovering. She was worried about Xun's current situation!
If it weren't for the fact that she was about to face a major tribulation, Liu Yu would have gone to the Central Domain to look for Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng nodded, then took out a brown branch from his body.
"Soul Nourishing Wood!" Liu Yu's eyes trembled.
Under normal circumstances, the Soul Nourishing Wood was only a means to prolong one's feeble existence. If the situation wasn't critical, it would be impossible to use the Soul Nourishing Wood.
Luo Zheng's face also revealed a sorrowful expression. "Xun helped me block a soul attack. Her soul was on the verge of collapse. I had no choice but to put her soul into the Soul Nourishing Wood."
Liu Yu clenched her teeth, a hesitant expression on her face. "Let's use the sacred tree's life force one more time. At least it'll be able to awaken my king …"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng shook his head. "I think it's best if we don't."
After the chaotic battle with the Twin Devil Sacred Ground, the entire Heavenly Feather Sacred Ground was in a mess. If they wanted to restore the Sacred Ground, they had to borrow the sacred tree's power!
From Luo Zheng's point of view, the giant sacred tree was already riddled with holes. Moreover, the sacred tree had already used its power to nourish Xun. If they used too much of the sacred tree's power, the entire sacred tree would probably wither away …
"But my king is the most important. I can feel the sacred tree's will. It's willing to sacrifice itself to save my king!" Liu Yu insisted.
Luo Zheng sighed, then said, "Xun only fell into this state because of my will. Moreover, the sacred tree's nourishment isn't enough to completely heal the damage to her soul. I will find a way to revive her."
In truth, the Soul Nourishing Wood in Luo Zheng's hand wasn't inferior to the sacred tree's. It was just that the power of the Soul Nourishing Wood was gentle, and its effects were slow. However, it was enough to protect Xun's soul.
At that moment, a yellow-clothed woman from the Night Demon Clan walked over from the sacred tree's branch. She bowed to Liu Yu.

Chapter 926
Liu Lan had kept her head down the entire time.
When she heard Liu Yu's question, she suddenly raised her head. Her eyes shone with a blue light, like two fireflies in the darkness.
The moment she raised her head, she charged at Luo Zheng and reached for the Soul Cultivation Wood in Luo Zheng's hand.
Luo Zheng didn't expect that a member of the Night Demon Clan would attack Xun!
Xun's soul was extremely weak and couldn't take any more torture. Moreover, there was only one piece of the Soul Cultivation Wood. If this person broke it, Xun's soul would be beyond saving!
Luo Zheng reacted quickly. With a flip of his hand, he tossed the Soul Cultivation Wood into the air.
Liu Lan's figure flashed as she leaped up. Just as she was about to grab the Soul Cultivation Wood, Luo Zheng snorted coldly. He grabbed Liu Lan's leg and pulled her down.
"Get down!"
With a plop, Liu Lan was pulled down. The Soul Cultivation Wood wobbled and fell back into Luo Zheng's hand.
The two guards beside Liu Yu had already pinned Liu Lan to the ground, one on her left and one on her right.
Liu Yu's gaze was like lightning as he stared at Liu Lan. He asked in a deep voice, "Liu Lan, what are you doing?"
Liu Lan didn't care about Liu Yu's words at all. She sneered, "You're Luo Zheng, right?"
"This voice …" Luo Zheng frowned. He had heard this voice before. It came from the King Statue that Luo Zheng had destroyed in the Hall of the Holy Tree.
"My King!"
When they heard this voice, everyone from the Night Demon Clan knelt on one knee.
The Night Demon Clan had a total of three King Statues. Xun was the King who controlled slaughter, and this was the King who controlled punishment. He must have used a secret technique to take over Liu Lan's body. This time, he wanted to kill Xun.
Liu Yu could roughly guess the dispute between the two kings. However, a dispute of this level was far from something she could participate in. Therefore, Liu Yu had no choice but to maintain a neutral attitude and watch how the situation developed.
Liu Yu and the others were from the Demon Night Clan, but Luo Zheng wasn't. When he thought about how Xun's soul would have disappeared if he had been a bit slower, a hint of ruthlessness appeared on his face.
"Yes, my name is Luo Zheng." In spite of his anger, Luo Zheng laughed. "What's your name? The Demon Night Clan's King of Punishment! "
Liu Lan said indifferently, "You don't have the right to know my name, but I'll give you a piece of advice. Hand over the Soul Wood and let Xun's soul dissipate from this world. Then, I can consider letting you go."
Hearing this, Luo Zheng stood on the spot. After a moment of silence, he suddenly threw his head back and laughed wildly. "Hahahaha!"
"What are you laughing at?" Liu Lan asked.
Luo Zheng laughed coldly as he walked towards Liu Lan. When he saw the azure light in Liu Lan's eyes, he said, "No wonder, no wonder …"
"No wonder what?" Liu Lan asked again.
"No wonder Xun said you're a b * tch. Her soul is in danger right now, but you're determined to kill her. You are indeed a b * tch!" Luo Zheng scolded Liu Lan.
It was reasonable to say that insulting a king of the Demon Night Clan like this in the Demon Night Clan's holy land was extremely disrespectful. If it were anyone else, Liu Yu would definitely kill them. However, this matter involved the dispute between two kings. Liu Yu only knelt on one knee and didn't help either side.
Liu Lan smiled faintly when she heard Luo Zheng's insults. "You're quite brave, and your life is quite tough. So what if you're destined to participate in the Battle of the Worlds? This Battle of the Worlds wasn't created for you alone. Every time a Battle of the Worlds descends, many people will die. Believe me, you'll definitely be one of them … "
Luo Zheng's eyes were calm as he looked at Liu Lan indifferently. Then, he said coldly, "It doesn't matter whether I'm one of them or not. What matters is that you're a b * tch."
"If you're willing to hand over the Soul Cultivation Wood, I can use the Demon Night Clan's power to …" Liu Lan frowned and continued to tempt Luo Zheng.
"You're a b * tch!"
"My status is higher than Xun's. My abilities are far beyond your imagination …"
"You're a b * tch!"
"I …"
"You're a b * tch!"
No matter what the King of Punishment said, Luo Zheng repeated the same sentence. After seven or eight sentences, the King of Punishment finally exploded. She controlled Liu Lan's body and roared, "Liu Yu, kill him!"
Liu Yu didn't do anything. She was still kneeling on the ground respectfully. The other members of the Demon Night Clan were also kneeling on one knee. They didn't move.
Since she couldn't count on anyone else, the King of Punishment could only do it herself. However, Liu Lan was a third-rank Battle-Saint. She couldn't do anything to Luo Zheng. Furthermore, she was controlling Liu Lan's body, so her strength was greatly reduced. It was just like how Luo Zheng controlled Feng Nianyun's body with his soul core. He couldn't unleash Feng Nianyun's strength either.
"Bang bang …"
After a few moves, Liu Lan was subdued by Luo Zheng. However, this wasn't the end. Luo Zheng's body shook as he flew towards the Hall of the Holy Tree.
When they saw Luo Zheng enter the Hall of the Holy Tree alone, one of the members of the Demon Night Clan whispered to Liu Yu, "Lord Liu Yu, Luo Zheng has entered the Hall of the Holy Tree …"
Liu Yu shrugged and could only say, "Just pretend you didn't see anything."
After Luo Zheng entered the Hall of the Holy Tree, he walked towards the azure statue of the King of Punishment. He patted the statue twice and smiled as he said, "Do you have anything else to say?"
The King of Punishment's statue suddenly trembled. She opened her eyes again. Just as she was about to say something, Luo Zheng beat her to it and said, "You're a b * tch!"
The face on the exquisite statue started to contort in anger. "Luo Zheng, how dare you insult me like this? If you ascend to the Upper Realm one day, I'll …"
"Clang!"
Before the statue could finish speaking, Luo Zheng punched out again and smashed the statue to pieces. Then, he shook his fist and said with a smile, "B * tches are so pretentious!"
After smashing the woman's statue, Luo Zheng returned to Liu Yu's side with a relaxed expression. The members of the Demon Night Clan didn't say anything and pretended that nothing had happened. However, this scene left Situ Haotian and the others dumbfounded. They couldn't figure out what the Demon Night Clan was trying to do.
Although Luo Zheng's expression was very relaxed, he was secretly thinking that once he recovered Xun's soul and found a body, he would definitely help her regain her position and take back everything that belonged to her. As for that b * tch, he would make her feel like a b * tch!
The Heavenly Feather Holy Land's recovery ability was astonishing.
After all, the entire Holy Land was built around the Holy Tree. However, the Holy Tree seemed to be heavily damaged. Even the main trunk was burned, but the roots of the Holy Tree were unharmed.
The roots of this sacred tree extended hundreds of feet into the ground. Some of the horizontal roots even extended for hundreds of miles. It was constantly absorbing the nutrients of the earth. In just one day, the scars on the surface of the sacred tree had recovered by quite a bit.
Not only that, but while the Holy Tree was recovering, it was also nourishing the entire Heavenly Feather Holy Land. Therefore, many green sprouts appeared in the Heavenly Feather Holy Land. It seemed that it was only a matter of time before the Heavenly Feather Holy Land fully recovered.
As for Situ Haotian and the others from the Purple Heart Holy Land, they had already joined forces with Lan Bing and the other Divine Sea Realm experts to kill their way to the Twin Devil Holy Land. The other races on the Sea God Continent were also in shock.
When the Demon Race was expelled from the Tower of Sin, the other races knew that the Demon Race was destined to decline. However, they didn't think that it would decline so quickly. In less than a year, the once famous Twin Devil Holy Land was about to be destroyed!
However, the destruction of the Demon Race wasn't a bad thing for the other races. This powerful race was too tyrannical and ruthless. In comparison, the Human Race and the Demon Night Clan were much gentler. For example, the Human Race had already taken control of the Tower of Sin, but they weren't like the Demon Race, who casually broke the rules and killed other races.
Two days later …
The Demon Night Clan had gathered all the materials that Ha Peng had listed.

Chapter 927
What made people click their tongues in amazement was that these eight puppets were exquisitely crafted. Each puppet's appearance was almost the same as Boss Mo and the others.
Luo Zheng sized up these puppets, and finally, his gaze fell on his own puppet. He was suddenly speechless, "Why is my puppet's strength so much weaker than yours?"
The materials used to make these puppets were very complicated. The seven Demi-Immortal puppets were roughly equivalent to the Life and Death Realm, but Luo Zheng's puppet was equivalent to the False Tribulation Realm …
Yu Ze coldly said, "Isn't this normal? Anyway, your strength is much weaker than ours. "
Luo Zheng rolled his eyes, too lazy to refute this guy.
Boss Mo indifferently said, "Since it's like this, we can control these puppets and enter the cursed land."
Everyone nodded. After waiting here for a few days, they were eager to enter the Exquisite Pagoda. Thus, they poured a wisp of their soul into the puppets, and the puppets opened their eyes and moved freely.
However, at this time, Luo Zheng was somewhat at a loss … He didn't know how to split his soul.
Although Luo Zheng's soul was comparable to the existence of the Blue Spirit Realm, he still hadn't stepped into the Blue Spirit Realm.
The key was that he could control the shape of his soul, but he didn't practice the Soul Splitting Technique.
The so-called Soul Splitting Technique was to peel off a part of one's soul, forming a unified but completely independent soul.
Xun's soul was actually a Soul Splitting Technique, but her body and main soul had already fallen. That wisp of Soul Splitting Technique was the only soul left.
Seeing Luo Zheng's helpless appearance, Yu Ze cast a mocking smile. Gu Pei 'er slightly smiled, and then said, "Luo Zheng, I'll help you. First, I'll separate a part of your soul from your body."
Hearing Gu Pei 'er's words, Luo Zheng released a wisp of his soul from between his eyebrows. Then, the flower in Gu Pei' er's right eye slightly rotated, and an invisible and gentle force quietly floated over. Soon, Luo Zheng felt that his soul was pulled by a light force, and a wisp of his soul was naturally separated.
Then, Luo Zheng controlled that wisp of soul, drilling into his puppet.
The eight people sat quietly, and the eight puppets directly jumped onto the Eight Trigrams Wheel. Without further delay, they went straight to the cursed land.
The puppets' bodies weren't rejected by the cursed land, and the Eight Trigrams Wheel almost didn't stop, directly breaking through the cursed land. After a while, a huge pagoda appeared in front of everyone. the Evil Tower, the Exquisite Pagoda of the Demi-Immortal clan.
"As expected." Boss Mo's puppet smiled and said, "It is indeed the Exquisite Pagoda!"
Qing Feng said indifferently, "It seems that the Linglong Pagoda hasn't been opened yet."
....... "....... com
Luo Zheng asked curiously, "Unfold? What do you mean by unfold? "
Boss Mo smiled. "You'll know in a while."
"Whoosh …"
Ha Peng's puppet controlled the Eight Trigrams Wheel, and quickly landed at the door of the first floor of the Exquisite Pagoda.
Seeing the Eight Trigrams Wheel and the puppets on the Eight Trigrams Wheel, many races of the Sea God Continent showed a curious expression.
No one on the Sea God Continent had the skill of making puppets, and they couldn't feel any trace of life from these puppets. For a while, there was a lot of discussion.
"What are these things? They don't look like humans! "
"Luo Zheng! One of them looks like Luo Zheng! "
"Did he return to the Sea God Continent? But looking at it carefully, this guy seems to be made of mica gold … What is this thing? "
Boss Mo didn't care about these creatures at all, and walked towards the Exquisite Pagoda. As for the two giants at the door of the Exquisite Pagoda, they quickly opened the door when they saw the people of the Demi-Immortal Tribe. Although they were puppets, they were still comparable to Life and Death Realm puppets. Moreover, they were controlled by several Ultimate God Realm masters. Who in the Tower of Sin could be their opponent?
Along the way, they naturally attracted the attention of countless people.
First floor, second floor, third floor, fourth floor …
Climbing up floor by floor, Luo Zheng sighed when he saw the familiar world in the pagoda. He didn't expect that he would return to the Tower of Sin, and in such a way. He couldn't help but sigh in his heart.
Yu Ze looked at Luo Zheng's expression and sneered. "I'm about to expose your lie. Why do I feel sad?"
Luo Zheng smiled faintly and retorted. "Someone is about to swallow his weapon. I feel sad too."
Seeing Luo Zheng's confident look, Yu Ze was startled. Could it be that he really obtained the title of Gold Demi-Immortal in the Talent Stele? Impossible. Yu Ze shook his head, his face still full of disbelief.
As for the others, they were too lazy to think about this problem. Whatever it was, everything would be clear once they entered the tenth floor.
The battle on the tenth floor of the Tower of Sin was as fierce as ever!
As soon as they entered the tenth floor, they saw a circle of people surrounding the Talent Stele. It seemed that someone had entered the Talent Stele to take the test.
After Boss Mo took a few steps on the tenth floor, he suddenly stopped. His eyes looked over the heads of all the creatures and stared at the wall.
Luo Zheng's name was originally at the top of the first row, so he didn't need to squeeze into the crowd to see it clearly. Boss Mo saw Luo Zheng's name and suddenly stopped.
Not only Boss Mo, but Qing Feng also stopped. Ha Peng's puppet was a work of art. Even its expression and eyes were vivid. At this moment, Qing Feng's gaze was incomparably complicated, as if he had fallen into deep thought!
As for Gu Pei Er, her eyes were shining. She laughed softly and said, "It seems that my premonition was quite correct!"
The others were all silent. Yu Ze's expression was the most exaggerated. His mouth was wide open, and his face even revealed an almost lifeless expression.
When Luo Zheng obtained the title of Gold Demi-Immortal in the Talent Stele, many creatures on the Sea God Continent were incomparably shocked. However, they didn't know what Gold Demi-Immortal meant.
In the eyes of the natives of the Sea God Continent, Liu Yu was the strongest genius in the history of the Sea God Continent. Even Liu Yu could only be ranked above the second row, which was considered second-grade for the time being. However, Luo Zheng directly surpassed him by one grade and was listed at the top of the Talent Stele alone. This meant that Luo Zheng's talent was two grades higher than Liu Yu's!
However, for the people of the Demi-Immortal Clan, they were even more clear about the ranking of the Talent Stele.
Gold, silver, and bronze were divided into three major ranks here! Within each color, there were various titles. Although the difference between them wasn't big, there would still be a big gap if one progressed step by step.
Demi-Immortal was the top title!
"Gold Demi-Immortal, it's really Gold Demi-Immortal. Since Evil Moon became a Celestial Master 400,000 years ago, there hasn't been a full title in the Demi-Immortal Clan!" Boss Mo murmured.
400,000 years was a long time, but compared to a Gold Demi-Immortal, it was a very short time.
In terms of time, an Era of Evolution was 3.6 billion years, and in this era, there were only six people who obtained the title of Gold Demi-Immortal. If one were to count from "Evil Moon", the last person to obtain the title of Gold Demi-Immortal was probably tens of millions of years ago, or even hundreds of millions of years ago. This span of time was almost unimaginable.
Gu Pei Er's face revealed a trace of pride. Previously, she didn't make a move against Luo Zheng, and it was indeed an incomparably correct premonition. This time, she chuckled and said to Yu Ze, "Did someone make a bet with me?"
Yu Ze's expression became extremely unnatural. However, his temperament was extremely serious. This puppet's face didn't have any blood vessels, so it didn't blush. However, the real body controlling the puppet in the Heavenly Feather Holy Land was already blushing. It was obviously impossible for him to swallow the Dark Nether Blade, but he couldn't find a way to get out of this embarrassing situation.
Boss Mo naturally didn't care about the bet between Yu Ze and Gu Pei Er, but he suddenly remembered a very important matter.

Chapter 928
Life and Death Realm warriors had to go through the first minor tribulation.
Any warrior who could get a title on the Talent Stele would open their lotus during the heavenly tribulation!
Luo Zheng went through the minor tribulation on the Feather Emperor Island. At that time, these Divine Realm warriors were still in the Heavenly Stellar Holy Land, thinking about how to lure the evil dragon out. Naturally, they didn't know about Luo Zheng's tribulation.
Now, Boss Mo thought of this crucial question.
Most of the warriors who could get a title would open their lotus. If so, what kind of lotus did Luo Zheng have when he went through his first minor tribulation?
Three leaves? Five leaves? Seven leaves? Nine leaves?
Although this was his first time undergoing a Heavenly Tribulation, there was a high chance that he would sprout the Seven-leaf stage or even the Nine-leaf stage. Perhaps, he had even formed his phenomenal Lotus Flower.
"Luo Zheng, when you went through the minor tribulation, did you have a lotus?" Boss Mo asked in a low voice.
Boss Mo's voice had a hint of respect for Luo Zheng. It was a world of difference from his previous attitude.
Boss Mo knew very well what a golden Heavenly Realm warrior meant to the Heavenly Realm warriors. As a Divine Realm warrior, he was much stronger than Luo Zheng. However, Luo Zheng was now a seed with unlimited potential. He could only look up to him in the future.
Even the arrogant Qing Feng was swept away at this moment. A fact that he didn't believe even if he was beaten to death had been verified. He didn't have the right to be arrogant in front of Luo Zheng.
The eyes of the Heavenly Realm warriors were focused on Luo Zheng's face, waiting for Luo Zheng to answer Boss Mo's question.
Facing Boss Mo's question, Luo Zheng hesitated for a moment and then answered, "Yes."
"How many? How many lotus did you open? "Qing Feng seemed to be more anxious than Boss Mo.
The symbol of lotus was different from one's talent. The more lotus one opened, the higher the possibility of surpassing the Heavenly Path. The higher one's talent, the faster one's growth. However, in general, lotus and talent were related to each other to a certain extent. Those with low talent couldn't open their lotus, but those with high talent couldn't open their lotus.
Luo Zheng smiled and asked, "Boss Mo, how many lotus leaves did you open?"
"You're asking me?" Luo Zheng did not answer Mo Wuji's question. Instead, he asked Mo Wuji a question. Boss Mo smiled faintly. "After entering the Extreme Divine Stage, the Lotus Flower blooms and opens seven leaves."
Having seven lotus leaves was already an incredible feat. Even among the geniuses of the Demi-Immortal Clan, there weren't many who were able to do so.
Qing Feng said indifferently, "In the last heavenly Tribulation, I opened five leaves."
Gu Pei 'er smiled sweetly. "I've also attained the Five-leaf stage …"
She, Gu Peier, didn't get a title on the Talent Stele. Her talent was much lower than Boss Mo and Qing Feng's, but she also opened five leaves.
"Yes." Luo Zheng nodded and then said, "In the last heavenly tribulation, I opened 35 lotuses."
"35 …" Yu Ze almost choked on his breath.
Qing Feng and Gu Peier's eyes widened. 35 lotuses was a number they couldn't imagine.
However, Boss Mo was a little calm. The birth of a golden Heavenly Realm title meant that Luo Zheng's future was beyond everyone's imagination. He said indifferently, "Only one leaf can reach perfection. Hehe …"
"What if it's perfect?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
Boss Mo laughed and said, "When you open the last lotus leaf, you'll know."
Just as everyone was discussing, a sudden noise came from the Talent Stele.
Luo Zheng and the others stood on the tenth floor while controlling the eight puppets. Although they attracted a lot of attention, the creatures on the tenth floor seemed to be more curious about a certain warrior who was being tested on the Talent Stele!
Looking past everyone's line of sight, the door of the Talent Stele suddenly opened, and a human youth walked out from the door.
The human youth's appearance was ordinary, and he only had the cultivation base of a tenth-grade warrior. However, his expression was full of confidence!
Then, the Talent Stele began to change. A layer of faint silver light covered the wall, and the silver light eventually formed the word "Asura."
"Asura: Zhao Li."
The huge words hung below Luo Zheng's name!
"Ah! Zhao Li, this kid! He's really amazing! He actually surpassed the first row and is ranked behind Luo Zheng! "
"Doesn't that mean Zhao Li's talent is second only to Luo Zheng? Heavens, what's wrong with the human race? In less than a year, such a monstrous genius has appeared one after the other! "
"The rise of the human race was originally unstoppable. Situ Haotian has already led people to attack the Twin Devil Holy Land. I'm afraid the Twin Devil Holy Land will be destroyed by now …"
Qing Feng, Boss Mo, Yu Ze, Gu Peier, and the others all stared blankly at the name on the Talent Stele. Their faces were full of expressions as if they had seen a ghost …
After a while, Qing Feng said, "I've really seen a ghost. This Great Thousand World is too strange!"
Silver Asura title …
This kid called Zhao Li, on the Talent Stele's test, had already surpassed the seven people of the Demi-Immortal Clan!
Even Boss Mo only obtained a silver title, and it wasn't a particularly high title …
In the Demi-Immortal Clan, those who could obtain a gold title were few and far between, and those who could obtain a silver title were few and far between. This kind of talent was enough to make one a core member of the Demi-Immortal Clan, just like Boss Mo, who was placed in an important position by the Demi-Immortal Clan. Therefore, when carrying out missions, the other six people had to follow Boss Mo's lead.
In a mere lower realm, Luo Zheng had appeared, which was already shocking enough. Now, a young human with the title of Silver Asura had appeared in front of them. The seven people of the Demi-Immortal Clan felt incomparably strange and shocked!
When this young man called Zhao Li came out, several peak War Reverends of the human race appeared from the upper ranks.
Now, the status of the human race in the Tower of Sin was completely different from before. The human race and the Demon Night Clan had become the true rulers of the Tower of Sin, and the other races had no way to resist.
Zhao Li, surrounded by the crowd, seemed very happy. Although he was very confident in his talent, to be able to surpass the first row and obtain a title, placing him below Luo Zheng, he was very satisfied! To Zhao Li, surpassing Luo Zheng was something he didn't have any hopes of.
Although Luo Zheng had left the Sea God Continent, he had become a mythical existence in the Purple Heart Holy Land. Who could surpass a mythical figure?
At this time, Boss Mo controlled his puppet and walked towards the young man called Zhao Li.
Zhao Li's face revealed a trace of doubt. He had just walked out of the Innate Stele, so he didn't notice the eight strange things that had appeared on the tenth floor. Although these people looked like humans, he could only sense a trace of soul fluctuations from them. There was no aura of life.
The peak War Reverends of the human race also looked over vigilantly. These people's origins were unknown, and their sudden appearance in the Tower of Sin made them very vigilant. However, everyone was attracted by Zhao Li's results and didn't have time to pay attention to them.
However, there were still people with sharp eyes who noticed Luo Zheng not far away!
"Luo Zheng! It's Luo Zheng! "
"Luo Zheng returned to the Sea God Continent?"
"Something's not right. Although this guy looks like Luo Zheng, he doesn't seem to be human …"
"It's just a replica. How dare he pretend to be Luo Zheng? What is he up to?"
There were no puppets on the Sea God Continent. Even if there were, they were very simple and crude existences. It was impossible for someone like Ha Peng to create a lifelike puppet.
The puppet that Ha Peng made for Luo Zheng was almost no different from Luo Zheng's main body. However, a real person and a puppet were different in the end. In the eyes of a martial artist, they only needed to use their perception to lightly sweep over the puppet and they would be able to detect that something was wrong.

Chapter 929
Originally, Boss Mo had something to say to Zhao Li.
According to the rules of the Demi-Immortal Clan, all warriors recognized by the Talent Stele would be invited to join the Demi-Immortal Clan.
Of course, Boss Mo would not let Zhao Li get away with the Silver Asura title.
In fact, Liu Yu should have been invited as well. However, Liu Yu could only be ranked in the second row of the Talent Stele. Although this result was one of the best in the Sea God Continent, it was not outstanding in the Demi-Immortal Clan. After all, the second row was the lowest limit of the Demi-Immortal Clan.
However, before Boss Mo could speak, he suddenly heard a woman from the Human Clan say such words. She could even see through the origin of these puppets at a glance. Boss Mo, Qing Feng, and the others immediately became alert.
Luo Zheng glanced over and met Yun Luo's eyes. A faint smile appeared on his face.
Yun Luo nodded and smiled at Luo Zheng. Then, she continued, "Zhao Li comes from the Human Clan's Purple Heart Holy Land. After he ascends in the future, he will be important to the Human Clan. Your Demi-Immortal Clan should not interfere."
Hearing Yun Luo's words, Qing Feng and the others immediately frowned.
Now that they had confirmed Luo Zheng's talent and unexpectedly found another genius, the Demi-Immortal Clan could not let him go. Luo Zheng would be fought for by the Demi-Immortal Clan. Similarly, they would not let this little guy named Zhao Li go.

Yun Luo's gaze was indifferent. "You're just a bunch of kids at the Ultimate Divine Realm. How could you be so hard to stop?"
The faces of the seven Demi-Immortals turned unsightly. How could this mysterious human woman be able to determine the level of their cultivation bases with a mere fragment of her soul? Who was this person?
Seeing the ugly expressions on the faces of the Demi-Immortals, a rare smile appeared on Yun Luo's face. "Don't worry. Given Zhao Li's fate, he shouldn't have joined your Demi-Immortal Clan. As for Luo Zheng, that's his own fate. If Luo Zheng wishes to join your Demi-Immortal Clan, I won't stop him."
When Luo Zheng had obtained the Fragment of the Heavenly Dao, Yun Luo had said the same thing. She had told Luo Zheng that she would not interfere with Luo Zheng's choice. Everything would be up to Luo Zheng.
"What does that mean …" Boss Mo and the others were confused when they heard that.
They could give up on Zhao Li. Although the number of Silver Asura warriors was extremely small, there was still a considerable gap between them and Gold Demi-Immortals.
However, Luo Zheng was also a human. Why would this mysterious woman be willing to capture him? Logically, they couldn't figure it out.
Seeing the expressions of the Demi-Immortals, Yun Luo said indifferently, "Some words don't need to be said so clearly. I'm just telling you that Luo Zheng's fate seems to be the Battle of the Great Era. However, in reality, he's a fateless one."
"A fateless one?"
When they heard Yun Luo's words, it was as if they had fallen into a fog.
Even Gu Pei 'er, Yu Ze, and the others of the Demi-Immortal Clan were puzzled.
Only Qing Feng seemed to have thought of something. His face was filled with shock. "A fateless one? He's a fateless one? " After saying this, he shook his head like a rattle-drum, as if he was going to shake his head off.
Luo Zheng's expression was also strange. What the hell? A fateless one? What did that mean?
Yun Luo gently turned around. "It's not good to stand here and talk. Everyone, let's move to the living area!"
Everyone followed Yun Luo out of the Talent Monument. Many warriors were whispering in private. Yun Luo had just said that they were from the Demi-Immortal Clan!
Although the Exquisite Tower was called the Tower of Sin on the Sea God Continent, through the Book of Demi-Immortal at the top of the Tower of Sin, they understood that this tower seemed to belong to the Demi-Immortal Clan. All peak War Venerables could read the Book of Demi-Immortal, so it wasn't a secret.
"What does that mean? These people are at the Ultimate God Realm? Are they even stronger than Divine Sea Realm experts? "
"Demi-Immortal Clan. That means they're the original owners of the Tower of Sin! Did they come here to take the Tower of Sin? "
Many of the living beings on the tenth floor became nervous when they heard someone say this. The Tower of Sin was far too important to the Sea God's Continent.
The Sea God Continent had always been in chaos. It was originally a barren land. If it weren't for the Tower of Sin, not to mention the Divine Kingdom Continent, even the Central Domain would be far inferior to it.
Now that everyone heard that the Demi-Immortal Clan had come to the Tower of Sin, how could they not be nervous?
"Did Luo Zheng bring them here?"
"That's too much! Luo Zheng also obtained a lot of benefits in the Tower of Sin, although he … "
"Don't talk nonsense. Luo Zheng is different from you guys. He has his own plans for what he does!"
Over here, many creatures were arguing endlessly. In the living area, Yun Luo had already sat down peacefully. The small wooden clogs on her feet gently knocked on the floor, making crisp sounds.
"What does the Fateless mean?" Yu Ze finally couldn't help but ask Qing Feng.
Qing Feng frowned and thought for a while. Then, he said, "When I was in the Great Realm Pavilion of the clan, I read a book called 'Destiny'. It recorded a strange truth. According to 'Destiny', everything in the universe is predestined. Our fate has been firmly fixed. There is no deviation at all!"
"Every smile, every sentence, every step, and every consideration has already been arranged. The rest is just a meaningless rehearsal," Yun Luo continued to say.
Gu Pei 'er puffed up her cheeks and shook her head. "I still don't understand!"
"It's normal that you don't understand," Qing Feng said indifferently. Then, he stood up and looked around. He saw a wooden plate on a stone table beside him. He grabbed the wooden plate and said, "What will happen if I let go of it?"
"Nonsense, the tray will definitely fall to the ground," Gu Pei 'er said doubtfully.
Qing Feng nodded and let go of the tray. The tray in his hand fell to the ground, making a crisp sound. "You predicted that the tray would fall."
"Pfft …" Gu Pei 'er smiled. "You're talking nonsense!"
Qing Feng continued, "Because you knew that I would drop the tray. You knew the height I raised the tray, the speed I fell, and the place I fell. So you predicted the cause and effect."
"Hmm." Gu Pei 'er nodded. She felt that Qing Feng's words were getting more and more mysterious.
"If someone witnessed the birth of the Yan Era, he could predict everything that would happen in the 3.6 billion years of the Yan Era?" Qing Feng said.
"That's impossible!" Yu Ze couldn't help but stare at Qing Feng. "Can you predict what I will do next? In the billions of years of the Yan Era, the number of creatures in the universe is more than a trillion. There are powerful figures in our clan who can predict the future, but they can only see a trace of it in the chaos. Qing Feng, you should know better than me. "
Seeing Yu Ze's excited look, Yun Luo, who was slowly kicking her wooden clogs, said, "What if it's not a person, but the Heavenly Dao?"
Qing Feng nodded. "The Way of Heaven has deduced everything. The birth of every living creature and the end of every life have long been settled. Everything is predestined, including the fact that I'm standing here and saying this!"
"The Heavenly Dao …" Yu Ze's face was a little dazed.

Chapter 930
In theory, the entire Evolution Era was started by the Heavenly Dao. Therefore, the Heavenly Dao was able to predict every person's fate.
Just like a wooden plate, it would fall from a certain height to a certain place. How everyone lived, how they died, when they laughed, when they cried, everything was under the control of the Heavenly Dao.
However, in this world, there were always some unexpected things. In this universe, there existed a small number of people whose fates were not under the control of the Heavenly Dao.
In other words, the Heavenly Dao could not predict how these people were born, nor what their fates would be in the future …
Luo Zheng's face was extremely shocked. Even Qinglong in his mind sighed. "Hehe, I didn't expect that I was not as clear as this little girl of the human race. Luo Zheng, you are a Fateless …"
Yun Luo slowly stood up and said, "So when Luo Zheng appeared here for the first time and obtained the title of Golden Heavenly Immortal, I didn't affect his decision! As for the Demi-Immortal Tribe, if you can persuade Luo Zheng to join you, I won't interfere. However, you can't touch Zhao Li, who just obtained the title of Silver Asura. "
The seven members of the Demi-Immortal Tribe were silent. In their eyes, Luo Zheng was like an alien.
Boss Mo turned to Luo Zheng and said, "So, Luo Zheng, are you willing to join our Demi-Immortal Tribe?" Whether Luo Zheng was a Fateless or not, in Boss Mo's opinion, since Luo Zheng had the title of Golden Heavenly Immortal, they could not let him go. If they let Luo Zheng go and the higher-ups of the Demi-Immortal Tribe found out, it would be a great crime.
Luo Zheng pondered for a moment. His eyes flashed, and then he smiled. "I don't think I have a choice …"
Yu Ze laughed coldly, "At least you have some self-awareness!"
"Anyway, no one can see my fate. Joining the Demi-Immortal Tribe doesn't sound like a bad thing, but I have a request." Luo Zheng's expression suddenly changed.
"What request?" Boss Mo asked again.
Luo Zheng said, "The Exquisite Pagoda will remain on the Sea God Continent for 500 years."
Actually, Luo Zheng had made up his mind from the very beginning. The Sin Tower was too important to the various races of the Sea God Continent, especially the human race and the Demon Night Clan. Most importantly, the Purple Heart Holy Land had yet to fully develop.
Although the threat of the Twin Devil Holy Land would no longer exist in the future, if the Demi-Immortal Clan were to take away the Sin Tower now, it wouldn't affect the Demon Night Clan too much. However, it would be a huge problem for the Purple Heart Holy Land.
"500 years?" Boss Mo muttered.
No matter how one looked at it, this deal was extremely cost-effective. At least they could smoothly persuade Luo Zheng to join the Demi-Immortal Clan, and they didn't need to go through a lot of trouble.
But at this time, Qing Feng said, "300 years! Right now, the Linglong Pagoda is only displaying half of its power. We can help you fully unfold the Linglong Pagoda! The effect of the Light of Creation will be far greater than it is now, and the number of floors of the Exquisite Pagoda will reach 30. "
"Fully unfold?" Luo Zheng's eyes suddenly lit up.
Qing Feng nodded. The Demi-Immortal Clan's three Trial Pagodas all had 30 floors. However, the Exquisite Pagoda in front of them only had 15 floors …
Although this plan was good, Luo Zheng suddenly shook his head, "Forget it. In the Exquisite Pagoda, you can only cultivate to the peak war saint level. Even if you unfold the entire Exquisite Pagoda, it won't make much sense."
The Exquisite Pagoda was located in the Cursed Land, and the highest cultivation level could be controlled at the peak war saint level. If one exceeded this limit, they would be attacked by the power of the curse. Therefore, unfolding more floors was meaningless.
"This problem is too easy to solve. Just inscribe a teleportation array in the Exquisite Pagoda," Qing Feng said.
Luo Zheng still shook his head, "If we inscribe a teleportation array in the Exquisite Pagoda, won't it be eaten by the pagoda?"
Qing Feng smiled faintly, "The teleportation array you inscribe will naturally be swallowed, but the one we leave behind won't be!"
His words convinced Luo Zheng. If they could really leave a teleportation array in the Exquisite Pagoda, then regardless of whether it was a war saint or a Divine Sea Realm expert, they would be able to freely enter and exit the Exquisite Pagoda. However, they would still be affected by the power of the curse in the Exquisite Pagoda, but it would be fine as long as it didn't exceed a month.
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng looked at Yun Luo and asked, "Brother Fen Qin and Brother Zhuo He, are they still at the bottom of the Evil Pagoda?"
Yun Luo nodded, "Yes, after becoming war saints, they have been cultivating at the bottom of the pagoda."
"It seems that they will be able to see the light of day again," Luo Zheng said with a smile, "Let's call them up first …"
Entering the pagoda from the bottom of the pagoda, they could stay for about a month. If they could set up a teleportation array, they would be able to leave the Exquisite Pagoda freely.
Luo Zheng and the Demi-Immortal Tribe reached a simple agreement. Then, Yun Luo sent a message to the Human Clan and the Demon Night Clan, ordering them to evacuate all living beings from the Evil Pagoda.
"Evacuate the Evil Pagoda? What is the meaning of this? "
"It can't be true, right? The Demi-Immortal Tribe is really going to take the Evil Pagoda away …"
"Sigh, it's a pity that I've only cultivated to the eighth grade. I didn't expect the Evil Pagoda to be taken away at this time. What bad luck!"
Many beings from various races were immediately unhappy. One by one, they began to shout, their faces filled with unwillingness.
Yun Luo didn't explain much to them. She went to the top of the Evil Pagoda with the seven war saints of the Demi-Immortal Tribe. The two war saints of the Human Clan, Zhao Fenqin and Zhou Zhuo He, were also invited up from the bottom of the Evil Pagoda. When they saw Luo Zheng, their faces were naturally filled with joy.
However, when they heard that the people around Luo Zheng were going to help them build a transportation array, they were wild with joy!
In fact, having cultivated to this level, their state of mind was very stable. Although the Evil Pagoda was very boring, they could still return to the Evil Pagoda from time to time for a walk. However, when they thought about how they would never be able to leave the Cursed Lands and be trapped in the Mysterious Underworld Cave and this small pagoda, the psychological pressure was considerable.
If the transportation array was built, they would be able to return to the Purple Heart Holy Land with the cultivation of war saints. For them, this was indeed something that would make them go wild with joy.
Outside the Cursed Lands, beings from various races crowded around the Evil Pagoda. Looking at the huge pagoda in front of them, many of them revealed unwillingness on their faces.
"Sigh, those people are too overbearing. They just took it away like that. The Evil Pagoda belongs to our Sea God Continent!"
"Although I'm not willing to accept it, according to legend, this pagoda does belong to the Demi-Immortal Tribe. Taking away something that belongs to us isn't overbearing, is it?"
"So what if you're not willing to accept it? Have you heard? They are existences at the Extreme Divine Realm. Do you know what the Extreme Divine Realm is? They are even more powerful than Divine Sea Realm experts. They can destroy your Holy Land with a wave of their hand … "In fact, the old man who spoke didn't know what the Extreme Divine Realm was. However, he only knew that the Extreme Divine Realm was stronger than the Divine Sea Realm.
Under the watchful gazes of many living beings, the entire Tower of Sin suddenly erupted with a violent tremor!
"Rumble …"
The buildings in the villages around the Sin Tower began to shake as well. It was as if a small earthquake had occurred. Some of the less sturdy houses collapsed due to the earthquake.
"They can't be controlling the Evil Pagoda to fly away, right …" Someone muttered.
Under this constant shaking, the tail of the Evil Pagoda actually began to stretch.
This huge pagoda was originally like a living body. Under this kind of stretching, it constantly "grew" in front of everyone's eyes. The original first floor of the Evil Pagoda rapidly rose into the air. As for the two giant warriors guarding the entrance, they could only stand on the ground in a daze.
Flying was forbidden in the Cursed Lands. No matter what, they didn't dare to fly up.
This tremor lasted for about half an hour. The part of the Evil Pagoda that extended from the surface was sixteen floors high. The part buried deep underground was fourteen floors high.
Qing Feng fulfilled his promise and completely opened the Evil Pagoda.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 931
Even after the tremors stopped, many living beings still had looks of confusion on their faces.
"It stopped, just like that? What's going on? Weren't they going to fly away? "
"They don't have the strength to take the Tower of Sin away?"
"Perhaps …."
After a long time, they finally understood that the Tower of Sin hadn't been taken away, and the current Tower of Sin was the complete form of the tower!
However, they also learned an important piece of information. The Tower of Sin could only exist on the Sea God Continent for three hundred years. Only after three hundred years would the Demi-Immortal Tribe take it away!
This news was both good news and bad news for the Sea God Continent. However, the fact that the Demi-Immortal Tribe was willing to give them a buffer was already a great consideration.
It was said that Luo Zheng had agreed to certain conditions of the Demi-Immortal Tribe in exchange for this buffer period ….
In addition to this buffer period, the Tower of Sin had two more transportation arrays. One array led directly to the Purple Heart Holy Land, while the other led directly to the Heavenly Feather Holy Land. This way, the Demon Night Clan and the Human Race's control over the Tower of Sin was even stronger. After all, if the other races wanted to cultivate in the Tower of Sin, they would have to go through the Human Race and the Demon Night Clan's Holy Land.
After the Wheel of Eight Trigrams flew back to the Heavenly Feather Holy Land, Luo Zheng withdrew a fragment of his soul from his puppet. Looking at the puppet that was almost identical to him, Luo Zheng smiled and said to Ha Peng, "Can you give this puppet to me?"
"Of course," Ha Peng said with a smile.
Although Ha Peng wasn't the strongest of the seven Demi-Immortals, he knew a lot. However, he didn't speak much and had a straightforward personality. He left a good impression on Luo Zheng.
And so, Luo Zheng put the puppet, which was comparable to a False Tribulation Realm expert, into his spatial ring.
The Demi-Immortal Tribe had come to the Sea God Continent to take away the Twinklegem Pagoda. However, the Twinklegem Pagoda had verified Luo Zheng's title. Because of this, Luo Zheng had helped the Sea God Continent complete a deal with the Demi-Immortal Tribe ….
Luo Zheng wasn't against joining the Demi-Immortal Clan. Although Feng Nianyun called the Demi-Immortal Clan a race of lunatics, they were still considered a neutral force compared to the other forces in the universe.
Moreover, joining the Demi-Immortal Clan would have to wait until he ascended. He had just stepped into the Life and Death Realm, so it was still unknown when he would ascend.
They had stayed on the Seagod Continent for about a month. Compared to when they had first arrived, the Demi-Immortal Clan was much more eager to return. After being persuaded by Luo Zheng, the seven of them took a rare trip around the Seagod Continent.
The reason why Luo Zheng chose to stay for a month was because of Liu Yu.
At first, Liu Yu wanted to squeeze out the life force from the Sacred Tree and directly inject it into Smoky. At that time, the Sacred Tree was severely injured, so Luo Zheng naturally refused.
However, in the next few days, the Heavenly Feather Sacred Ground displayed a powerful life force. Almost every day, it changed. The originally messy Heavenly Feather Sacred Ground recovered, and all the plants began to grow crazily.
The surface of the Sacred Tree also recovered a lot. Under Liu Yu's repeated requests, Luo Zheng decided to stay for a month. When the Sacred Tree accumulated some life force, Luo Zheng gave the Soul Nourishing Tree to Liu Yu.
However, this time, Luo Zheng was much more careful. Although he had already destroyed the statue of the Punishment King, the Demon Night Clan had erected another one in the blink of an eye. Luo Zheng was afraid that damned woman would control the other members of the Demon Night Clan to attack Smoky's soul again.
At the top of the Sacred Tree, only Liu Yu and Luo Zheng were left. Then, the Sacred Tree began to gently sway, and a vigorous life force began to gather along the branches of the Sacred Tree.
This time, the amount of life force squeezed out from the Sacred Tree was far less than the last time. However, after instilling the life force into the Soul Nourishing Tree, Luo Zheng's perception entered the Soul Nourishing Tree to investigate. He sensed that Smoky's soul had recovered a lot.
Although she was still asleep, as time passed, it was only a matter of time before she woke up.
However, if she wanted to control the Sword Spirit in her body to fight like before, it would be a bit difficult.
After dealing with this matter, Luo Zheng and the Demi-Immortal Clan returned to the Central Domain. However, this time, Boss Mo and the others didn't use the Eight Trigrams Wheel. They stood on a branch of the Sacred Tree and gestured for Luo Zheng to come over.
"Don't we have to cross …" Luo Zheng asked.
Qing Feng smiled faintly. "No need. We left a transmission mark in the Central Domain. Naturally, we don't need to cross such a long sea!"
Hearing Qing Feng's words, Luo Zheng remembered that they left a blood-red cross mark in the Heavenly Palace. They also left a similar mark in front of the cemetery on the Divine Emperor Island. By leaving a mark, they could teleport at will. The divine ability of the Demi-Immortal Clan was extremely convenient. Luo Zheng sighed and stood together with Gu Pei 'er and the others.
Then, a blood-red flag appeared in Ha Peng's hand. The entire flag contained rich spatial energy. As Ha Peng gently waved the flag, a blood-red ribbon of light floated around the eight people. After a short while, a great gravitational force began to pull Luo Zheng.
"Don't resist this force," Gu Pei 'er warned.
Luo Zheng nodded. Then, the force pulled him into a transmission channel.
"Whoosh …"
He didn't know how the Demi-Immortal Clan did it. Even with a large-scale transmission array, Luo Zheng needed a lot of time to travel such a long distance. When Luo Zheng was transmitted from the Heavenly Mist Immortal Tomb to the Sea God Continent, he was seriously injured. When he returned from the Sea God Continent, he was also seriously injured.
However, with their transmission mark, it only took a few dozen breaths to return to the Central Domain. Moreover, the transmission channel they built was very stable.
In Heavenly Awakening City, the blood-red cross in front of the Heavenly Palace flickered with light. Luo Zheng and the seven people of the Demi-Immortal Clan appeared in the light.
The Demi-Immortal Clan didn't stay in the Central Domain for long. Boss Mo told Luo Zheng that they would report his matter to the higher-ups of the Demi-Immortal Clan. When the time came, someone else would take over. After that, they continued to deal with the evil dragon. Therefore, after a short pause, they activated the transmission mark again. This time, they should be heading to the Divine Kingdom Continent.
Watching the Demi-Immortal Clan slowly disappear, Luo Zheng thought to himself. The Demi-Immortal Clan, the White Brow Daoist, and Bai Yuerong of the Secret Sect had a dispute. However, this dispute didn't have much to do with Luo Zheng.
Now, Luo Zheng suddenly felt his whole body relax …
Since he stepped onto the path of a warrior, time had been like a huge rock that pressed down on Luo Zheng's body, pushing Luo Zheng forward. It was as if every breath was pressed. Luo Zheng still had some things to do, such as helping Xun regain her status and finding his sister.
However, relatively speaking, these things were relatively distant goals. For example, the strength of that young man was beyond Luo Zheng's imagination. Even now, Luo Zheng didn't doubt that that young man could kill him a thousand times over with a single glance. Even the Great Thousand World wouldn't be able to withstand that young man's attack.
Now, Luo Zheng had a rare moment of leisure.
He glanced at the Heavenly Palace. It was empty. After Ning Yudie recovered, she should have returned to the Cloud Hall. Then, would Xi Youqin follow Ning Yudie back to the Cloud Hall?
At this time, he saw a big fat man flying over. It was the Merchant Union's Union Master, Shi Kefan. After seeing Luo Zheng, Shi Kefan's face was covered in sweat. He shouted, "Luo Zheng, you're finally back! Something bad has happened! They're fighting! "
Luo Zhengcai asked curiously, "Fighting? Who's fighting? "

Chapter 932
Shi Kefan had been paying close attention to what was happening in the Heavenly Palace. When Luo Zheng returned, he immediately rushed over after receiving the news.
Looking at Shi Kefan's anxious expression, Luo Zheng had a strange expression on his face.
After Shi Kefan explained everything to him, Luo Zheng finally understood. The so-called fight was actually between Xi Youqin and Ning Yudie! Why would they fight?
There was a huge gap in cultivation between Xi Youqin and Ning Yudie. Ning Yudie was a Life and Death warrior, while Xi Youqin was only at the Divine Core realm. There was a False Tribulation realm between them, and the gap between their cultivations was very obvious.
However, Xi Youqin's strength couldn't be measured by ordinary cultivators. From the beginning to the end, she didn't rely on her own strength, but the help from the Extreme Purple Realm …
Currently, in the entire Central Domain, both Cui Xie and Wu Zhanhe had been killed by Luo Zheng. Aside from a few independent cultivators who had hidden themselves from the world, the strongest and most destructive were probably Ning Yudie and Xi Youqin. If these two girls were to fight, it would definitely cause a huge commotion.
What Luo Zheng didn't know was that after he destroyed the Tian Xie Sect, he had also killed Wu Zhanhe with a thunderous strike. The small group of people from the Tian Xie Sect who had just risen from the ashes had finally dispersed, and the entire Central Domain was slowly stabilizing.
The Void Spirit Sect and Void Sky City were slowly and orderly rebuilding, and the other sects were steadily developing as well. The Cloud Hall and the World Business Alliance's positions were further stabilized, and the topics of discussion between cultivators were gradually decreasing …
However, the fight between Xi Youqin and Ning Yudie was like a burning straw that had been thrown into a pool of oil. In an instant, it had become a new hot topic in the Central Domain, and it had spread across the entire Central Domain!
Originally, Luo Zheng and Ning Yudie had a definite relationship in the eyes of those who were good at making groundless accusations. As for this young lady from the Xi family, although most people didn't know that she was the one who had summoned the huge swords that destroyed the Tian Xie Sect, they knew about the relationship between her and Luo Zheng.
Now, these two women were in a heated argument, and it had even reached the point of a fight. The speed at which this topic spread was really fast!
However, at the same time, it also attracted a burst of envy. Not to mention that Xi Youqin was the great-granddaughter of Taoist Qing Xu, Ning Yudie alone was the dream lover of all cultivators in the Central Domain. However, Ning Yudie always had a high and mighty appearance in front of others. Whether it was her cultivation or status, she was far from what ordinary people could reach. Most people could only fantasize about her in their hearts to comfort themselves.
However, Luo Zheng, that brat, could actually cause these two women to fight over him and enjoy the happiness of having two lovers. Other than envy, there was only jealousy left. If it was some cultivators who were extremely unbalanced, they would even feel indignant.
In fact, this was also Luo Zheng's own negligence.
He left the Luo Family at the age of sixteen, and all the way, he had been practicing Martial Arts. Although he was already a young man in his twenties after several years, and compared to the cultivators in the Mortal World, he might have achieved achievements in Martial Arts that others could not reach. However, he was still ignorant about the Dao of Love.
Although Xi Youqin's temperament had changed greatly, and she was very well-behaved in front of Luo Zheng, her nature was still a person who refused to admit defeat. Although she even took the initiative to take care of Ning Yudie when she was asleep, this did not mean that Xi Youqin would choose to give in. On the contrary, her heart was tied to Luo Zheng. She could give in on other things, but she would not back down in this matter.
As for Ning Yudie, she was a proud daughter of heaven since she was a child. At a young age, she was in charge of the Cloud Hall. Luo Zheng was only a disciple of the Cloud Hall. In addition, she was older than Luo Zheng. Although a cultivator's lifespan was long, and a few years older did not mean anything, her performance in terms of emotions was, after all, much more reserved.
However, Ning Yudie was a person who pursued perfection. Even if she was immersed in Martial Arts and was not in a hurry to choose her Dual Cultivation partner, a woman's fantasy of love was basically magnificent and perfect. As long as there was a trace of flaw, it was like a black spot in a precious jade, which she could not bear!
The most troublesome thing was that Luo Zheng came back in a hurry, woke up Ning Yudie, and then left in a hurry. He did not have a proper arrangement for this matter.
After Luo Zheng left, the atmosphere between the two women underwent a drastic change. Although they did not quarrel, they did not get along well with each other. Shi Kefan and the others could see this in their eyes.
The most troublesome thing was that Ning Yudie did not immediately return to the Cloud Hall. Instead, she waited for someone in the World Business Alliance.
At this time, Granny Yu, who had gone overseas, returned!
Life and Death Realm cultivators would make thorough preparations before crossing the Life and Death Calamity.
However, the Life and Death Calamity was not so easy to cross. Life and Death Realm cultivators would seize the time to search for opportunities, and use all means to cross the calamity.
At that time, Granny Yu was old and her strength was declining. Knowing that she could not cross the second Life and Death Calamity, she had only one path to take, and that was to go overseas to search for new opportunities.
In fact, it was not only the cultivators of the Central Domain who said this. The War Emperors and War Emperors of the four Divine Kingdoms would also walk this path when they reached the end of their lifespan.
However, how could the opportunities of the overseas be so easy to find? In the vast majority of cases, these cultivators who were nearing the end of their lifespan would never return. They would be buried in some inaccessible sea region of the Great Thousand World.
Therefore, Ning Yudie also knew what Granny Yu and Ning Yudie's parting meant.
However, she never expected that when she woke up and checked her Sound Transmitting Talisman, she would receive a message from Granny Yu. Granny Yu had already returned to the Central Domain and was rushing back to Awakening Heaven City.
Ning Yudie was naturally wild with joy. Not only did her master not die overseas, she should have already crossed the second Life and Death Calamity. Not to mention her master's great increase in strength, as long as Granny Yu was still alive, it was the best thing for Ning Yudie!
After all, when Ning Yudie's entire family was annihilated, her master had become her only family.
The three leaders of the Merchant Union were also very happy when they learned of this matter. They even organized a banquet to welcome Granny Yu.
Not many cultivators were invited to this banquet, but the leaders of the fourth-grade sects of the Central Domain were invited. Among them were Taoist Priest Qing Xu and the others who were busy with the construction of the Void Spirit Sect.
When Granny Yu learned that so many things had happened after she left the Central Domain, she sighed in her heart. Fortunately, the Tian Xie Sect had been annihilated, and the Central Domain had returned to its peaceful state. There were no major hidden worries.
However …
Halfway through the banquet, Taoist Priest Qing Xu and Granny Yu were chatting. They brought up his great-granddaughter, Xi Youqin. Taoist Priest Qing Xu had originally said something polite. He had said that in the future, his great-granddaughter would marry Luo Zheng of the Cloud Hall. They could be considered relatives by marriage.
Granny Yu had been in a good mood when she heard that Luo Zheng had killed Cui Xie and even found some super experts to kill the almighty of the Divine Nations. Suddenly, she heard about Xi Youqin. Her expression immediately changed. When she turned around and saw the unnatural expression on Ning Yudie's face, the hot-tempered Granny Yu immediately turned hostile.
She knew that Ning Yudie liked Luo Zheng. If it was before, she would have felt that Luo Zheng was not worthy of her most outstanding disciple. But now, looking at them again, the two were really a perfect match. One was the leader of the Cloud Hall, the leader of the strongest sect in the Central Domain, and the other was the number one person in the Central Domain! They were a match made in heaven. Why did Xi Youqin suddenly appear?
So, Granny Yu immediately said, "A sparrow is a sparrow. How can you become a phoenix?"
Everyone present was a reputable figure. Although the Void Spirit Sect was still being rebuilt, Taoist Priest Qing Xu was not easy to deal with. With the Void Spirit Sect's foundation, it was only a matter of time before it regained its former glory. Taoist Priest Qing Xu's expression immediately turned ugly. Xi Youqin was his most beloved grandchild. How could he tolerate such words?
Therefore, Taoist Priest Qing Xu and Granny Yu quarreled on the spot. The atmosphere suddenly became very tense. As for Shi Kefan, Yan Yueshan, and the others, their faces were full of bitterness. It was too late for regrets. They should not have hosted this banquet. Who would have thought that such a small matter would become like this?
If it was just two elders quarreling, this matter would have passed. Everything would have to be discussed after Luo Zheng returned. However, Xi Xiaojie was unhappy to see his sister being talked about like this. He jumped out to speak for Xi Youqin.

Chapter 933
In the beginning, Ning Yudie sat quietly in her seat and didn't say anything due to her modesty.
However, as the quarrel became more and more intense, Granny Yu attacked Xi Youqin several times.
Xi Youqin was obedient in front of Luo Zheng, but in front of outsiders, she wouldn't show any face. So what if Granny Yu was a Life and Death Realm expert? Her great-grandfather was also there …
So, after mulling over it for several times, Xi Youqin said indifferently, "Granny Yu, you said I'm a sparrow and that I'm not a good match for Luo Zheng, but so what? Although Luo Zheng and I are husband and wife in name, we're already husband and wife in reality. "
As soon as these words were spoken, the whole place fell silent.
A cup of hundred-year-old top-grade Imperial Fen Wine was placed on the corner of Ning Yudie's tea table. She hadn't drunk a single sip during the banquet, but at this moment, she stretched out her jade fingers and drank the wine in the cup in one gulp. With her cultivation, she wouldn't get drunk, but the piercing taste of the strong wine still made her face blush. She said coldly, "Shameless!"
Between martial artists, the boundaries between men and women weren't that strict. Xi Youqin's admission of her relationship with Luo Zheng didn't mean anything. At most, it could be said that she was a little bold.
When Daoist Qing Xu heard Ning Yudie's words, he immediately became furious. "How can you talk like that …"
How could Granny Yu let an outsider teach Ning Yudie a lesson? So, the quarrel escalated and they almost came to blows.
No matter how good Shi Kefan's mouth was, it was difficult for him to persuade them. The quarrel eventually turned into a battle. Xi Youqin actually challenged Ning Yudie.
"Their battle was in Cloud Sea City?" Luo Zheng listened to Shi Kefan's recount of the events of that day as he flew towards Tianqi City's teleportation array.
"Yes, it was in Cloud Sea City!" Shi Kefan said with absolute certainty.
From the perspective of the Merchant Union, peace was the most important thing in the world. Moreover, this was not a big deal. Why did it escalate to the point where both sides had to fight?
Fortunately, at least Luo Zheng had come back. Perhaps only Luo Zheng himself could solve this problem.
After entering the Teleportation Formation in Heavenly Awakening City, Luo Zheng swiftly flew through the teleportation passageway and swiftly rushed towards Ocean of Clouds City. Fortunately, this sort of short distance teleportation passageway was extremely stable, so Luo Zheng executed his movement technique and sped through the passageway. In just a few dozen breaths of time, he'd arrived at the end of the passageway.
"Whoosh …"
As a blazing white glow arose from the teleportation formation, Luo Zheng's figure appeared within the teleportation formation in the city.
Several guards of Cloud Sea City were guarding this teleportation formation. Although the remnants of the Tian Xie Sect had been wiped out, all the main cities were still in a state of alert. Cultivators who entered and exited the teleportation formation had to be checked for their nameplates. Merchants who came and went had to be checked for the exclusive documents of the World Business Alliance.
Those Divine Illumination Realm guards sized up Luo Zheng a few times. After trying to determine Luo Zheng's cultivation, they revealed a trace of astonishment on their faces. They were completely unable to see through Luo Zheng's cultivation!
Although they knew that Luo Zheng was an existence they couldn't afford to offend, one of the guards still braced himself and dutifully asked, "Friend, may I ask who you are …."
Luo Zheng was in a hurry to stop the battle between Xi Youqin and Ning Yudie, so how could he possibly pay attention to these people? His figure suddenly floated, and those guards felt a flash in front of their eyes. Luo Zheng had already disappeared before their eyes.
That guard obeyed the order and was about to sound the alarm to stop Luo Zheng, but another guard stopped him. "There's no need to sound the alarm!"
"Why? Why would such an expert of unknown origin barge into our Cloud Sea City …. "That guard was puzzled.
"That's not an unknown origin. That's Luo Zheng from the Cloud Hall!" Another guard said.
"He's Luo Zheng!" That guard opened his eyes wide and looked at the sky in shock. However, Luo Zheng's figure was nowhere to be seen.
Above Cloud Sea City, several False Tribulation Realm cultivators were floating. Apart from them, there was no lack of Life and Death Realm cultivators, such as Granny Yu, Ning Yudie, and Daoist Qing Xu ….
