However, Zong Rui's talent in the Dao of Runes wasn't bad. By studying Luo Zheng's divine runes in reverse, his attainments in Runes had increased by a lot. In the Central Domain, except for Luo Zheng, there was probably no one better than him. This time, Zong Rui had a place to use his talents.
He ingeniously used runes to decorate the entire Cloud Hall. The power of these runes wasn't strong, but as long as they were replenished with True Origin Stones, they would emit a regular color. Some of the light even projected onto the "Light of Abundant Water" barrier, condensing into flying dragons and phoenixes, vivid and lifelike as they danced on the barrier.
On the training field of the Cloud Hall, warriors from all over the Central Domain stood densely on both sides. Those who were able to stand here were basically the heads of various aristocratic families and sects. For example, the head of the Jade Dragon Sect and so on had personally come today.
Of course, the heads of the Mysterious Yin Mansion, Blood Wood Cliff, Black Mountain Sect, and other fourth-grade sects naturally wouldn't be missing.
When these people first saw Luo Zheng, he was just a junior in the Divine Reflection Realm. However, right under their eyes, Luo Zheng soared into the sky, and no one in the Central Domain could resist him. This time, when these people came to congratulate him, they couldn't help but sigh in their hearts. The little guy they used to look down on had now become the person they looked up to.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 937
In one of the side halls of the Cloud Hall, Xi Youqin was holding a kitchen knife in her hand. She was dancing rapidly on the chopping board.
Today was Ning Yudie and Luo Zheng's big day. However, Xi Youqin's face revealed a trace of stupefaction. The kitchen knife, which was made of Gengjin, was extremely sharp. She was cutting deep marks on the ironwood chopping board below …
"Crash …"
Hearing the noise coming from outside, Xi Youqin curled her lips. Some of the vegetables were chopped into pieces by her.
"Ga Gu …"
Ancestral Gragon's small body curled up and whimpered.
Ever since Ancestral Gragon ate Xi Youqin's dishes, it had almost forgotten about Luo Zheng. This foodie had been circling Xi Youqin almost every day.
Perhaps because Xi Youqin's attention was focused on the noise outside, the chopping in her hands slowed down. Ancestral Gragon urged Xi Youqin.
"Wait a little longer." Xi Youqin had a smile on her face. She was completely unaware that the vegetables, which had been chopped into pieces, had now become a vegetable paste …
"Rumble, rumble …"
Above the Cloud Hall, specks of sparkling and translucent light rose into the sky, transforming into multicolored auspicious clouds that drifted away. Through the window, Xi Youqin could clearly see that the kitchen knife in her hand had not stopped. As for the iron wood chopping board below, it had already been chopped into several pieces.
"Ga Gu …" Ancestral Gragon whimpered again.
"Wait a little longer …" Xi Youqin still had a faint smile on her face. However, this smile did not change at all. It was as if a mask was stuck on her face, incomparably stiff.
"Ga Gu …"
"Bang!"
The kitchen knife in Xi Youqin's hand was embedded in the stove. Xi Youqin's soft body trembled slightly. An imposing aura erupted from her body. "Didn't I say to wait a little longer?"
"Gah."
Ancestral Gragon's heart trembled when he saw Xi Youqin on the verge of going berserk. He curled up his small body and buried his head in it …
On the Martial Stage, the dual cultivation ceremony proceeded in an orderly manner. Although a martial artist's dual cultivation ceremony was extremely complex, and any part of it could cost hundreds of years of a mortal family's expenses, there was still a great deal of red tape and red tape.
Luo Zheng stood in the middle of the Martial Arts Practice Field, waiting for the arrival of his bride.
While everyone was waiting, Ning Yudie, dressed in a red dress and surrounded by many well-dressed maids, slowly arrived on a cloud.
Even without makeup, Ning Yudie's appearance was incomparably beautiful, bright, and moving. Not to mention that today was the dual cultivation ceremony. Under such meticulous dressing, every detail was handled perfectly. Just a glance at her would cause one's heart to beat wildly.
The moment Ning Yudie landed, the sound of gongs and drums filled the air, and nine-coloured auspicious clouds fluttered in the air, while the dragon and phoenix projected onto the Light of Abundant Water Barrier danced in the air.
Numerous mortals throughout the city gazed up at the multicolored Cloud Hall in the sky, and all of them revealed envious expressions.
"I heard it's a dual cultivation ceremony held by Immortals! What a grand spectacle! "
"Hmph, what Immortals? It's just a group of martial artists with extremely high cultivations …"
"Aren't you a martial artist as well? When will such a dual cultivation ceremony be held? "
This grand dual cultivation ceremony was spread by word of mouth among the mortals. There were countless versions of it, such as eating a peach of immortality that could extend one's lifespan by ten thousand years, bejeweled nectar that could make one immune to all kinds of diseases, and so on.
Just as Luo Zheng was about to welcome Ning Yudie, many people in the Central Domain heard a laugh. That laughter seemed to come from the horizon and suddenly spread down, causing everyone to be slightly shocked.
Luo Zheng's brows furrowed. He didn't know who had come. Today was his big day. It wouldn't be nice to let someone disturb it. That wouldn't be nice.
Whoosh!
Then, a figure flew down from the sky. It was an old man in white clothes!
The strange thing was that this old man actually directly pierced through the Abundant Light Barrier. The originally tough barrier didn't pose any hindrance to this old man!
Even for Luo Zheng, if he wanted to sneak into the Abundant Water Light, he would have to sneak in through the cracks at the bottom. However, the old man was able to casually pass through the Abundant Water Light. Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly at the sight of the old man's ability.
Everyone looked up. As they sized up this old man, their eyes more or less revealed a look of horror.
They couldn't see through this old man's strength at all!
Most of the martial artists present were in the False Tribulation Realm. Among them, there were two or three independent martial artists who were in the Life and Death Realm. Even they couldn't see through this old man's strength. This old man must be above the Divine Sea Realm!
However, Luo Zheng knew best that this old man's strength wasn't as simple as the Ultimate God Realm!
Previously, when Luo Zheng stayed with the seven experts of the Demi-Immortal Clan, he had a rough understanding of the Ultimate God Realm experts' Qi Power. If the cultivation base was one level higher, he could tell. However, if the cultivation base was two levels higher, he couldn't tell. For Luo Zheng, Ultimate God Realm experts and warriors stronger than the Ultimate God Realm, Luo Zheng couldn't see through their cultivation base.
Based on his intuition, Luo Zheng believed that this old man was an existence that surpassed the Ultimate God Realm …
Among the crowd, most people were more nervous, such as the people from the Merchant Union and the Void Spirit Sect. Although the Dual Cultivation Ceremony wasn't held for Xi Youqin, Luo Zheng was the man that Xi Youqin relied on for her whole life. However, at this moment, Xi Xiaojie curled his lips. He knew that his sister was very unhappy right now. She couldn't wait for this old man to come out of nowhere and mess up the Dual Cultivation Ceremony so that Ning Yudie wouldn't have an easy time.
As for some people, such as the Black Mountain Sect and other forces, families that once had conflicts with Luo Zheng had to submit to the Cloud Hall. This time, they were just here to watch the fun. On the surface, they were respectful to Luo Zheng, but in their hearts, they couldn't wait for Luo Zheng to be defeated.
Seeing Luo Zheng's unnatural expression, the old man laughed out loud. "Don't be nervous. Today is your Dual Cultivation Ceremony. This old man is here to join in the fun. What's wrong? Am I not welcome? "
Hearing this, Luo Zheng immediately breathed a sigh of relief. The newcomer didn't seem to have any evil intentions. No matter who he was, Luo Zheng could only greet him with a smile. He said loudly, "Since you came from afar, you are a guest. Since Your Excellency is willing to participate in my Dual Cultivation Ceremony, Luo Zheng naturally welcomes you!"
"Boy, you're forthright. I like it very much." The old man didn't beat around the bush. He chuckled and said, "However, I came in a hurry this time and didn't bring any gifts. What should I do?" As he spoke, he frowned.
Luo Zheng smiled faintly. "A congratulatory gift is just a token of appreciation. We cultivators don't care about material things. Everyone is already giving me, Luo Zheng, face by coming to support me! Your Excellency, please take a seat on the side! "
Many guests nodded. The two maids next to them added a table at the head of the table. These maids didn't know how strong this old man was, but seeing the reactions of the top experts of the Central Domain, how could they not understand? Although this old man's image wasn't good, he was probably an existence far beyond their imagination. Naturally, he was qualified to sit at the head of the table.
Unexpectedly, the old man suddenly shook his head. "No!"
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly. "Why not?"
"This Dual Cultivation Ceremony is a major event in one's life. How can I not prepare a congratulatory gift?" The old man shook his head like a rattle-drum.
Luo Zheng's face revealed a trace of helplessness. He rolled his eyes inwardly. Why did these old men become so clingy when they got older? Would they become like this when he grew old in the future?
However, before Luo Zheng could speak, some of the bolder guests around them began to shout.
"Since you want to prepare a congratulatory gift, quickly take it out!"
"Are you trying to keep us in suspense? Actually, you've already prepared it?"
"Then let everyone see!"
The cultivation of these guests wasn't high. They were only at the early stage of the False Tribulation Realm. However, since today was a big day, no matter how strong this old man was, they shouldn't turn hostile.
The old man chuckled and nodded. "Hehe, how did you know I prepared a congratulatory gift?" After saying this, the old man's figure flashed and appeared in front of Luo Zheng!

Chapter 938
The old man's sudden action caused everyone's expression to change greatly!
However, a small group of warriors revealed gloating smiles on their faces. This Luo Zheng had risen like a comet, and for a time, he was in the limelight. In the end, someone was going to deal with him?
Ning Yudie's face revealed a trace of fierceness. She stomped her feet, and the pearl crown on her head shook. She was about to rush toward Luo Zheng.
Granny Yu, Daoist Qing Xu, and the three fat alliance leaders also had ugly expressions on their faces …
However, before anyone could react, time suddenly stopped.
In the Heavenly Stellar Holy Land, when hatching the Ancestral Dragon's egg, the Heavenly Immortal Clan accelerated the flow of time by a thousand times! In that barrier, time was like sand between one's fingers, passing at a visible speed. One year inside was equivalent to a thousand years in the outside world.
Now, in the Cloud Hall, time was still a thousand times faster, but it was slowed down by a thousand times!
Everything was moving at one-thousandth of the normal speed. Everyone's movements were countless times slower than a snail's. It was difficult to see them moving with the naked eye.
What was even more frightening was that the speed of time slowed down was also very large. With the Cloud Hall as the center, it spread out for thousands of miles. Time was completely frozen …
The birds stopped flying in the air, and the smoke from the kitchen chimneys solidified into a column of smoke that would not disperse. The ripples on the surface of the lake were also completely maintained in their original state.
Xi Youqin's kitchen knife was still embedded in the stove. Only the Ancestral Dragon was not affected in the slightest.
"Caw, caw!" The Ancestral Dragon whimpered. Its intelligence was only equivalent to that of a human child. Although it was not affected by the flow of time, it found it strange that Xi Youqin was not moving.
Suddenly, out of instinct, it seemed to have felt something. It flew out of the window and headed toward the training field.
The old man smiled, looked at the sky, and sighed. "Time waits for no one. Although your cultivation speed is already very fast, in this lower realm, resources are scarce. In the end, the speed is still a bit slow. This old man will be the main force to help!"
As soon as he finished speaking, the Ancestral Dragon flew over.
"Ga Gu, Ga Gu!"
Seeing Luo Zheng lying on the ground with his eyes closed, Ancestral Gragon also cried out anxiously. At the same time, he showed a strong hostility towards this old man.
The old man chuckled and said to Ancestral Gragon, "You little guy …"
He ignored Ancestral Gragon and walked towards Luo Zheng. At the same time, he stretched out a withered hand, and there was a dark green pill in his hand!
As soon as the pill was taken out, it shot towards the sky!
"You want to run away after gaining a little bit of intelligence? Dream on! " The old man gently twisted his hand, and an invisible force pulled the pill down from the sky!
The strange thing was that within the pill, green halos suddenly appeared. Those halos instantly condensed into a green bolt of lightning that could even counterattack the old man.
The old man was really unhappy. He coldly snorted, and then stretched out his hand. The round pill was torn apart, and then it turned into a mass of green clouds that suddenly spread out.
This small pill contained a huge green cloud, and this green cloud floated motionlessly in the sky above the Cloud Cloud Hall.
"Go!" The old man gently exhaled. The huge green cloud began to slowly spiral, and finally turned into a trickle, continuously gathering in Luo Zheng's mouth.
After a while, Luo Zheng completely absorbed the green cloud. Then, the old man walked to his seat, and without asking, poured out a cup of wine from the wine pot. He blinked his eyes, and then the flow of time returned to normal.
Ning Yudie's figure swooped down a step, and then she stabilized her body, but her expression was still somewhat unnatural.
Although the old man had slowed down the flow of time, he had only slowed down the flow of time by a thousand times. He hadn't put it in an absolute state. In fact, it was almost impossible to completely stop time. In the universe, there was a conclusion about the law of time. Any creature who cultivated the law of time could only slow down time indefinitely, but they couldn't achieve a state of complete prohibition!
Therefore, everyone could still see the scene in front of them, and their brains could still think. Everyone also understood that this old man really didn't have any hostility.
But the time law that the old man used in actual combat just now stunned everyone.
In the Central Domain, there were very few warriors who were proficient in the Law of Space, and the difficulty of cultivating the Law of Time was above that of the Law of Space. So far, there had not been a single warrior who was proficient in the Law of Time. Of course, Luo Zheng was an exception. What's more, he had never shown his supernatural power of the Law of Time in front of others!
But this old man, without seeing how he made a move, changed the flow of the Law of Time in the entire Cloud Hall. Everyone felt as if they had been frozen in place. This was an unfathomable method. Although everyone thought that this old man was very strong, they now found that their understanding was still wrong. How was this strong? This was simply too horrifying!
As for the medicinal pill that the old man had just taken out, it was even more incredible. Not to mention the effects of this medicinal pill, this medicinal pill could actually escape and attack the old man at a critical moment. Not to mention the old man's unfathomable strength, just the green lightning released from the medicinal pill was probably enough to kill 99% of the warriors in front of them.
Could this kind of medicinal pill be bad?
What made people feel even more unbalanced was that, looking at Luo Zheng's appearance, he didn't seem to know this old man. They also didn't know where this old man came from. He actually came from thousands of miles away to deliver a congratulatory gift. Some of the warriors who were already feeling unbalanced, as well as those who were gloating, were now so jealous that they wanted to vomit blood.
"Your Excellency, what did you do to Luo Zheng?" Ning Yudie couldn't help but take a step forward and stare at the old man as she asked.
The old man shook his head in a relaxed manner and said casually, "Sending a congratulatory gift!" As soon as he finished speaking, he suddenly rolled his eyes and patted his head, "Right, since the groom has a congratulatory gift, the bride must also have a congratulatory gift. But this congratulatory gift is a bit shabby, don't mind it!"
After saying this, he patted his stomach lightly, but his hands were empty. The old man then lowered his head and said to himself, "Don't be greedy. I put these divine weapons here, not for you to use. I won't say anything if you use them. Do you really think they are yours?"
Seeing the old man's crazy appearance, everyone's face revealed an extremely strange expression. What was this old man saying to his stomach? Was he a lunatic?
At this time, Luo Zheng stood up and said with a smile on his face, "Your Excellency's Inner World has already given birth to a powerful warrior who can use divine weapons?"
The old man rolled his eyes, "There is a guy in my Inner World who has already reached the Divine Sea Realm. In my Inner World, he directly occupied a mountain and called himself the Saint Emperor. He even hid the thousands of divine weapons I put in my Inner World. He really thinks they are his own! Damn it, take them out for me! "
After saying this, the old man slightly pointed his finger. Suddenly, a bright golden bracelet appeared out of thin air and floated in the air. The old man laughed, "I came in a hurry, so I didn't prepare anything good. After thinking about it, I only have this bracelet. Although it's a bit rotten and a bit unpresentable, it is still a divine weapon. I'll give it to you!" After saying this, the bracelet floated towards Ning Yudie.
"Divine weapon …"
Everyone's face revealed a look of horror. When they heard the old man's words, some people almost fainted. What did he mean by rotten? Divine weapon or rotten?
Damn it … Some people began to curse in their hearts.
The best treasures in the Central Domain were only middle-grade divine weapons. There were pitifully few divine weapons. As for divine weapons, they might have heard about them in ancient books, but where had they seen them?

Chapter 939
Luo Zheng was also shocked. However, Luo Zheng wasn't shocked by the Divine Artifact.
Although divine artifacts were rare and precious, with Luo Zheng's current horizons, they would not shock him too much.
What shocked him was the old man's words!
Only warriors in the Divine Sea Realm could open up a Divine Sea, while warriors in the Extreme God Realm could solidify their True Yuan and create a world. For example, Feng Nianyun had cultivated for a hundred thousand years, but she had only created an island inside her body. As for the living creatures inside, they were still in a very primitive stage.
At this time, the things in the Sumeru Ring were no longer useful. The world in one's body had completely replaced the Sumeru Ring.
The old man had put some treasures in his Dantian. However, according to the old man, there was a Divine Sea Realm warrior in his Inner World. This was incredible!
The Inner World was formed by the solidification of True Essence. Thunder and lightning were drawn into the body to create creatures, and then they slowly multiplied and evolved …
After the creatures in the Inner World gained intelligence, they could use the True Essence in the Inner World to cultivate.
However, the space in the Inner World was limited, and so were the resources. It was probably countless times more limited than the mortal world!
In this environment, the old man could still give birth to a Divine Sea Realm warrior. How big was his Inner World? Just how strong was this old man?
Seeing Luo Zheng looking at him in shock, the old man waved his hand and said to Luo Zheng, "Well, I've given you my gift. Your Dual Cultivation Ceremony is more important. Don't worry about this old man! We can talk later! "
However, Ning Yudie still bowed respectfully. The congratulatory gift was just a token of her sincerity. Although this divine tool was just a "junk" in the mouth of this old man, in Ning Yudie's eyes, it was incomparably precious. It had far exceeded the value of the congratulatory gift. However, this old man had taken it out for Luo Zheng's sake. She had no reason to return it, so she could only accept it.
At this moment, Xi Youqin, dressed in casual clothes, slowly flew over from not far away.
"Youqin."
"Sis!"
Daoist Priest Qing Xu and Xi Xiaojie shouted at the same time when they saw Xi Youqin appear.
Today was the Dual Cultivation Ceremony of Luo Zheng and Ning Yudie. The people of Xi Clan weren't very happy, but they couldn't be absent, so they could only brace themselves to come.
This time, they didn't see Xi Youqin appear. Taoist Priest Qingxu and Xi Xiaojie were very worried about Xi Youqin. Presumably, Xi Youqin wouldn't be happy today.
Xi Youqin nodded at Xi Xiaojie and Grandpa Zeng. Her gaze landed on Ning Yudie's face, and she felt a little sour. The reason why she stayed in the kitchen and only cooked was because she didn't want to see this scene. She had already convinced herself not to be jealous of Ning Yudie, but she wasn't an idiot. This kind of emotion could only be controlled and couldn't completely disappear.
How could she not feel bitter when she saw Luo Zheng and Ning Yudie being a perfect match?
It was just that the little guy, Ancestral Gragon, suddenly disappeared just now. She was afraid that she would lose Ancestral Gragon, so she looked all the way here.
"Quack!"
Ancestral Gragon originally rushed over because he felt that Luo Zheng was in danger. Now that he saw that Luo Zheng was unscathed, he suddenly remembered the delicious food he hadn't eaten yet. He called out twice, then turned back and threw himself into Xi Youqin's arms.
This small episode naturally wouldn't disturb the process of the Dual Cultivation Ceremony. Luo Zheng and Ning Yudie continued the ceremony.
However, at this moment, the old man not far away stared at Xi Youqin. He suddenly opened his mouth and said, "Little girl!"
"Hmm?" Xi Youqin looked around curiously, but she didn't know who was calling her.
"Little girl, come here, over here!" The old man was talking to Xi Youqin using his Divine Sense.
Xi Youqin turned around and found out that the man who was talking to her was a sloppy old man. She furrowed her brows, and a strange expression emerged on her face. Originally, Xi Youqin didn't want to talk to this man. With Xi Youqin's Divine Core cultivation base, there was no way she could tell he was a mighty warrior.
Xi Youqin didn't cultivate much, and she didn't have much interest in the martial dao. Even if you put a Life and Death warrior and a Divine Peak warrior in front of her, they would still be the same.
Therefore, with Ancestral Gragon in her arms, Xi Youqin prepared to leave. She didn't want to watch Luo Zheng and Ning Yudie being lovey-dovey. She was a generous person, but she wasn't that generous!
Seeing that Xi Youqin was about to leave, the old man couldn't hold himself back any longer. It seemed like this old man was really good at reading people's expressions. He let out a snort and said, "Little girl, do you like that Luo Zheng, and you're jealous that he married someone else?"
Hearing this, Xi Youqin's expression changed. She fiercely glared at the old man, then she said in an annoyed manner, "What does that have to do with you?"
The old man let out a mischievous laugh, "I just want to enlighten you. Although you're the bride today, you're the one who will spend countless years with Luo Zheng in the future!"
A strange expression immediately appeared on Xi Youqin's face. What did he mean by that?
Although Xi Youqin didn't understand, she still walked towards the old man. A maid brought a chair over, and Xi Youqin sat beside the old man.
Everyone's face revealed a curious expression. Even Luo Zheng's attention was somewhat distracted. Ning Yudie, who was concentrating on holding Luo Zheng's hand, sent a stream of Zhenyuan from her fingertips. That stream of Zhenyuan was transmitted from her fingertips to Luo Zheng's hand. Seeing Ning Yudie's angry gaze, Luo Zheng's heart suddenly trembled. He could only reveal a trace of apology.
After all, it was Ning Yudie's big day. If he focused his attention on Xi Youqin, it would be strange if Ning Yudie didn't get angry.
"Old grandpa, what do you mean by that?" Xi Youqin asked curiously. Because the old man mentioned Luo Zheng, Xi Youqin's tone eased a lot. A gentle smile also appeared on her elegant and clean face.
The old man let out another mischievous laugh, "Nothing. I just want to ask you, you have the Extreme Purple Yin Body, right?"
Xi Youqin nodded.
"The Black Purple Body is the First Grade Extreme Purple Yin Body. Hehe, don't you know what it means?" the old man asked again.
Xi Youqin shook her head. Her face was a little stupefied. She wasn't very interested in martial arts. Diligent cultivation couldn't even compare to learning a new cooking skill …
Her ambition was very small. Now, she just wanted to stay by Luo Zheng's side. Although she knew that she had the Black Purple Body, she didn't know how strong it was or what it could do.
In fact, it wasn't difficult for Xi Youqin to find out. The Extreme Purple Realm was a very special realm. There were also countless powerful people in it. As long as she was willing to walk around in the Extreme Purple Realm, she could easily find many super powerful people to help her.
It should be known that the Black Purple Body was an existence that could control the Extreme Purple Realm. It was also extremely rare in the universe.
The problem was that after Xi Youqin opened up the Extreme Purple Yin Body, other than borrowing many giant swords from it several times, and temporarily dragging Luo Zheng and Ning Yudie into the Extreme Purple Realm to hide outside Tianqi City, she had never used the ability of the Extreme Purple Yin Body at any other time. She had never entered the Extreme Purple Realm at all.
"No wonder, no wonder the old woman didn't find you." The old man shook his head and then asked, "Do you want to become very strong? How about I find you a master? "
Xi Youqin looked at the old man strangely. This guy was getting further and further away from the topic. She was willing to sit here obediently only because the old man said that she could accompany Luo Zheng for countless years. Now, he was suddenly talking about becoming strong and a master? What was going on?
Therefore, Xi Youqin's attitude became perfunctory. "Not interested."
"Not interested? Do you know what this means? It will make you a top existence in the universe who can look down on everything! " The old man didn't know that he had grasped the main point with his previous sentence, but now he had grasped the wrong point again …
"So what if I can look down on everything? Is it fun? " Xi Youqin felt more and more that the old man was teasing her. Therefore, she got up and was about to leave with Ancestral Gragon in her arms.
Seeing Xi Youqin's actions, the old man's brain spun and he found the crux of the matter. This little girl was completely uninterested in martial arts. It didn't matter. He naturally had a reason to persuade her!

＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 940
"Don't go, little girl. Listen to this old man for a moment!" The old man hurriedly said. This was his miscalculation. He never thought that there would be such a strange junior in this world who treated martial arts like dirt.
But then again, everyone had their own ambitions. He had seen all kinds of strange fellows in the Demi-Immortal Clan. Since this girl was interested in Luo Zheng, he could only use Luo Zheng as bait.
Seeing the old man's expression, Xi Youqin suppressed her temper and returned to her seat. She asked with a cold expression, "What's wrong now?"
The old man chuckled. "Although you won't be the bride in today's dual cultivation ceremony, I can guarantee that you will stay by Luo Zheng's side for the longest time in the future."
"What do you mean?" Xi Youqin blinked and asked.
The old man shook his head, then glanced at Ning Yudie and said, "The bride's talent is not bad. She might be a top-notch existence in the Lower Realm, but in the Upper Realm, she's not worth mentioning."
Ning Yudie's talent might be similar to Huo Yuner's or Liu Yu's, but at most, her name could only be placed in the second row of the Talent Monument. This level of talent was only just enough to be recognized by the Demi-Immortal Clan. In the old man's eyes, it was far from amazing.
"What does this have to do with me?" Xi Youqin said with a blank expression. This old man was beating around the bush, and Xi Youqin didn't know what he was trying to say.
The old man chuckled again. "It has a lot to do with you. Think about it. What kind of person is this Luo Zheng? How can such a person be limited to a small Central Domain? "
Xi Youqin glared at the old man. "What do you mean limited to a small Central Domain? The Central Domain is vast and has abundant resources. Does Luo Zheng need to go there? "
In Xi Youqin's eyes, the Central Domain was the entire world. Perhaps she vaguely knew that there was a Divine Kingdom Continent not far from the Central Domain. Perhaps she also knew that the world was divided into many levels, but what did this have to do with her?
In her opinion, there was nothing bad about the Central Domain. With Luo Zheng's current strength, there was no longer any threat in the Central Domain. Being able to live peacefully in this paradise was the greatest happiness. This was not to say that Xi Youqin was short-sighted, but she was not interested in anything else.
For example, after awakening the Purple Extreme Yin Body, Xi Youqin had vaguely known that her body was very special..... but she had never thought she would become stronger..............
Hearing Xi Youqin's answer, the old man could not help but roll his eyes. How could the Central Domain be called vast and abundant in resources? To this old man, the entire Central Domain was a distance that could be crossed in a few steps. The area was not even as big as one of his courtyards, nor was it as big as his inner world …
As for resources, they were even more lacking. Look at this group of poor bastards. When they saw his Divine Weapon, their eyes all lit up like hungry ghosts that had been starved for a hundred years. They were too useless. Divine weapons were also good things for False Tribulation Realm martial artists in the Upper Realm. However, in the eyes of the False Tribulation Realm martial artists in the Upper Realm, it was not rare to this extent.
Therefore, the old man could not bear to use the word "remote" to describe the Central Domain. This was because this place was not qualified to be called a remote place!
Of course, Xi Youqin had a strange temperament, so the old man would not say such words. He just laughed and continued, "The Central Domain is vast and abundant in resources in your eyes. However, with Luo Zheng's talent and the Revelation Pill that I gave him, Luo Zheng's cultivation speed will increase by leaps and bounds. When Luo Zheng breaks through the Spirit Sea Realm, he will be rejected by the Greater Thousand World and will have to ascend to the Upper Realm …" In fact, there was also the problem of repairing the Ascension Passage in order to ascend to the Upper Realm. However, the old man knew that Xi Youqin was not interested in this. She might not even know about the Ascension Passage at all. Therefore, he simply did not mention it.
However, he did not expect that Xi Youqin's face would reveal an angry expression. Instead, she blamed him and said, "Old man, you're not doing a good thing!"
The old man was speechless. He had given Luo Zheng a Revelation Pill, and it was called not doing a good thing. Instead, he had become a bad person? Therefore, he stared at Xi Youqin with a very funny expression for a long time. Finally, he said, "Even if I don't give him the pill, he will still ascend."
"And then?" Xi Youqin continued to ask.
The old man continued, "This is the problem. With your first-grade Extreme Purple Yin Body, your cultivation speed will not be slower than his. In the future, when he gallops in the Upper Realm, the bride will definitely not be able to ascend in a short time. Unless Luo Zheng makes a name for himself and comes back to pick her up. Otherwise, with her own strength, it will take at least one or two hundred years for her to ascend, right?"
"And then?" Xi Youqin asked again.
The old man rolled his eyes again. "Isn't this very obvious? You can also ascend in advance. With your physique, you can completely catch up with Luo Zheng's footsteps and provide him with great help. Won't you be able to stay by his side? Alas, as for his bride, I'm afraid she will have to stay alone in an empty room for a hundred years! "
Hearing the old man's sigh and what he meant by staying alone in an empty room for a hundred years, Xi Youqin's eyes suddenly lit up. "I see!"
"That's right." The old man chuckled. "So, I'll find you a master. You can enter the Extreme Purple Realm to cultivate. When your cultivation reaches a certain level in the future, you can be together with Luo Zheng in the Upper Realm!" After saying that, he intentionally threw a disdainful glance at Ning Yudie, then he continued, "Don't think that she's better off than you right now. The one who laughs last is the one who laughs the brightest, isn't that right?"
Xi Youqin's eyes were completely lit up. She had never pursued the path of cultivation, and she had relied on all sorts of pills to reach the Divine Core realm. Without the help of the Extreme Purple Yin Body, her overall strength was weaker than many Divine Reflection Realm warriors.
But, a person's heart could change, especially when the old man had given her a beautiful vision!
For Luo Zheng, she could endure Ning Yudie, and she could maintain a smile on her face during the dual cultivation ceremony between Luo Zheng and another girl. However, it was extremely difficult for her to maintain her smile, and now that she had another path to take, she would never give it up.
"En, I understand now." Xi Youqin tilted her head and asked, "You said you'll find me a master, don't tell me it's you?"
The old man shook his head like a rattle-drum. "Of course it's not me, it's that old woman in my family!"
99% of the warriors who possessed the Extreme Purple Yin Body were females, and only a few were exceptions. Therefore, this old man naturally wouldn't teach Xi Youqin anything.
"But … where is she?" Xi Youqin asked curiously.
The old man stretched out his withered hand and said to Xi Youqin, "Give me your hand!"
Xi Youqin had a strange expression on her face, but she still stretched out her right hand.
"Open your hand." The old man ordered again.
Xi Youqin obediently opened up her fair palm in front of the old man.
After that, a blood red cross appeared above the old man's finger. The Divine Rune contained within this cross was similar to the Positioning Talisman used by the Heavenly Clan. However, this blood red cross was many times smaller.
"Whoosh!"
As soon as this blood red cross appeared, it disappeared into Xi Youqin's palm. This dark red cross was very eye-catching in her fair and delicate palm.
Xi Youqin raised her hand and looked at the rune. She still looked worried. "What is this?"
The old man said, "It's just a Positioning Talisman. The next time you enter the Extreme Purple Realm, my old woman will be able to find you!"
"The next time I enter the Extreme Purple Realm … But I don't plan to go in," Xi Youqin said truthfully. Unless someone wanted to kill Xi Youqin, she wouldn't hide in there out of boredom.
The old man's eyes widened. "How much do you hate cultivation? No, you have to go in there when you have the time. As long as you go in, my old woman will be able to find you! Little girl, if you want a lifetime of happiness in the future, you have to work hard to fight for it! " After saying that, he pursed his lips toward the martial arts arena. At this time, many guests were already in a mess. Luo Zheng and Ning Yudie's faces were filled with happy smiles.

Chapter 941
The old man couldn't help but touch his forehead and sigh in his heart. What was wrong with this world?
His old woman was a super powerful figure in the Purple Extreme Realm, one of the rulers. Countless warriors in the world cried and knelt down, begging to be her disciple. However, he had to waste so much effort to persuade this little girl to be his disciple. In the end, it was done!
As for Luo Zheng, there were some things that he couldn't say today, so he could only wait.
The Dual Cultivation Ceremony lasted for several hours. When the sun set and the Cloud Hall welcomed the guests, the hustle and bustle gradually subsided.
The new bedsheets were made of soft black gold silk and silk from brown silkworms. They were as soft as the clouds in the sky, as if there was nothing there. Ning Yudie, who was sitting upright on the bed, was as stiff as a popsicle.
A girl from an ordinary family would be married off at the age of fourteen or fifteen. Although Ning Yudie's age was still considered a rising sun among cultivators, compared to the children of ordinary mortals, she was already considered a big girl.
Ning Yudie had kept her chastity until now. Naturally, she couldn't help but feel nervous and uncomfortable, and even a little scared. This was human nature.
In addition to her nervousness, Ning Yudie couldn't help but find some topics to talk about. She lowered her voice and asked, "That old man today, who is he?"
Luo Zheng smiled faintly and shook his head. "I'm not sure!"
The old man didn't reveal his identity and was still in the Cloud Hall. Luo Zheng guessed that the old man was so powerful that he was likely to be a powerful figure invited by the Demi-Immortal Clan. Of course, it was also possible that he was from the Human Clan. That White Brow Taoist wasn't easy to deal with, so he would definitely report this matter to the Upper Realm …
"If you're not sure, why did you eat other people's pills?" Ning Yudie's face showed a trace of blame, but there was also a hint of playfulness in her expression, which made her look very cute.
Seeing Ning Yudie's expression, Luo Zheng took the opportunity to sit down and helplessly said, "It's not like you didn't see it. That old man forced it on me …"
But then again, although he didn't know what the pill was for, Luo Zheng hadn't had any reaction after absorbing it. It seemed that the pill hadn't taken effect yet. The effects of many medicinal pills were immediate, but he did not know what effect that medicinal pill had.
"Do you feel any discomfort?" Ning Yudie turned her small face over and asked.
Luo Zheng snickered and hugged Ning Yudie in his arms. "No matter what discomfort you feel, it'll be no problem to deal with tonight …"
After he finished speaking, he pressed his lips against Ning Yudie's.
"You!" Ning Yudie originally wanted to struggle, but a burst of masculine breath assaulted her, causing her to panic. Moreover, she really didn't have any reason to struggle tonight, so she simply made up her mind and curled up like an obedient lamb.
Her curling up was due to a trace of fear. How could Luo Zheng let her have her way? The key point was that he was no longer a "virgin." Although he wasn't very experienced, compared to Ning Yudie, it was naturally like heaven and earth.
Under Luo Zheng's gentle caresses, Ning Yudie's fear gradually faded. However, at this moment, she was both resisting and embarrassed by her resistance. Under this kind of contradictory state of mind, her pair of beautiful eyes were like spring water, about to drip out …
In the end, he reached out and grabbed Ning Yudie's slender and white calf. After gently pushing it aside, he pressed his whole body down.
Wax illuminated half a cage of golden jadeite, musk slightly embroidered hibiscus …
This night's scenery was undoubtedly infinitely good for Luo Zheng and Ning Yudie. However, for Xi Youqin, this kind of feeling was hard to describe.
Under the immersion of the night, through the barrier formed by the Abundant Light, looking at the layers of clouds, one could only vaguely make out a trace of light. That was the last quarter moon.
"Ga Gu." This Ancestral Dragon's energy was very good, and it rarely slept. It was unaware that Xi Youqin was unhappy, so it didn't dare to be too active. It just quietly guarded by Xi Youqin's side.
They sat like this for a long time. Under the cold wind, she rubbed her shoulders and suddenly said to the Ancestral Dragon, "It's not fun here. I'll take you to a fun place, okay?"
"Ga Gu!" The Ancestral Dragon didn't have the slightest resistance. The Ancestral Dragon's intimacy with Luo Zheng came entirely from the first impression, which came from biological instincts. However, under the careful care of Xi Youqin these days, the Ancestral Dragon became closer to Luo Zheng, who was somewhat distant.
"Then let's go!" Xi Youqin stood up and clapped her hands at the Ancestral Dragon. The Ancestral Dragon's tail suddenly flicked and shot into Xi Youqin's arms.
Immediately, a purple aura coiled out of Xi Youqin's body, and a black-purple light coiled in her eyes. Then, her figure quietly disappeared and entered a space node. Through this space node, she could easily step into the Extreme Purple Realm.
The old man had said that if she entered the Extreme Purple Realm, she would be found by his wife. Now, she wanted to go in and have a look.
In the Extreme Purple Realm, there was dazzling purple light everywhere. This realm was not complete. In fact, it was a broken realm …
Of course, the width and breadth of a realm were far beyond that of the Lower Realms. Even if the Extreme Purple Realm was only a quarter of its original size, it was still countless times larger than the Vast Thousand World.
"Ga Gu! Ga Gu! "
However, after the Ancestral Dragon entered the Extreme Purple Realm, he seemed very cheerful. He circled around in this vast and boundless space, constantly making lively cries.
Although the surroundings were empty, Xi Youqin was not the slightest bit afraid. On the contrary, the Extreme Purple Realm gave her a sense of security, as if this place belonged to her and belonged to her home. She had a very mysterious connection with the Extreme Purple Realm.
"Didn't you say that someone would take me as a disciple? The old man lied … "Xi Youqin curled her lips, looking a little unhappy.
Although she did not want to practice martial arts, Luo Zheng was an exception. In Xi Youqin's mind, an image had already appeared. It was when Luo Zheng was commanding the world, and she was standing proudly by Luo Zheng's side. How could she not be happy to help her husband?
Thinking of this, she even felt a sense of urgency and wanted to practice quickly.
"Humph, when the time comes, I will be countless times stronger than you." She was thinking about this problem fiercely. An imaginary enemy appeared in front of her. Of course, it was Ning Yudie, wrapped in red.
"Practice, I want to practice too!"
"Even if my" master "does not appear, I can still practice. A resolute look appeared in Xi Youqin's eyes. This look was extremely incompatible with Xi Youqin's. Only warriors who were extremely obsessed with martial arts would show this look. However, Xi Youqin had just changed her target. She was not obsessed with martial arts, but with Luo Zheng.
"Whoosh …"
She gently waved her hand, and a giant sword descended from the deep sky.
"One …"
"Whoosh …"
"Two …"
"Whoosh, whoosh, whoosh, whoosh …"
Countless giant swords descended from the deep sky of the Extreme Purple Realm.
The 999 giant swords of the Extreme Purple Realm were arranged in a certain pattern, densely packed in the deep sky. Usually, when Xi Youqin casually summoned them, the giant swords would slowly disappear and return to their original positions after slashing.
But now, it was different. She was in the Extreme Purple Realm, and when she summoned the giant swords, they all left their original positions …
These giant swords were controlled by people from different heavens in the Extreme Purple Realm.
Above the South Horizon Region, a large, jeweled ship was sailing in the sky. At the front of the ship stood a woman in a purple robe. The woman looked to be only 30 years old, with a proud figure and a charming face.
As she cruised around, she saw the giant swords in the South Horizon Region sinking rapidly!
"What's going on?" Seeing this scene, the woman immediately frowned.

Chapter 942
The problem was that these giant swords were not being requisitioned, but were being pulled down from the South Horizon Region one by one. This naturally attracted the purple-robed woman's attention.
"Could it be that someone is practicing the Myriad Red Sword Array?" The purple-robed woman wondered curiously.
The Myriad Red Sword Array was a sword array that was passed down in the Extreme Purple Realm. When practicing it, it required 11,000 giant swords!
But soon, the purple-robed woman immediately denied her speculation because the number of giant swords being pulled down from the deep sky was increasing!
At first, it was only one or two, then dozens, then hundreds, thousands, tens of thousands …
Then, the number of giant swords in the sky above the South Horizon Region began to decrease rapidly in units of 100,000!
"There are so many giant swords. Are they being pulled down to be eaten?" The purple-robed woman's expression changed greatly.
Although there were many giant swords in the Extreme Purple Realm, there were also many warriors with the Extreme Purple Yin Body in the entire universe. Countless giant swords were loaned out every day.
However, after the giant swords were swung, they would be returned immediately. It was normal for these giant swords to be loaned out and returned continuously.
There were very few people who would lend out 99.9 billion giant swords in one go. To borrow such a large number of giant swords, one had to have a first-grade Extreme Purple Yin Body, which meant that only warriors of the Black Purple Grade were qualified to do so. However, such people were either extremely powerful and had no use for these giant swords under normal circumstances. Although these giant swords were enough to destroy a Great Thousand World, they were not much of a threat to the experts in it. Therefore, the giant swords were only a form of deterrence to the experts!
For example, when two experts confronted each other, one might not be able to defeat the other, but one could summon a large number of giant swords and wipe out the other's disciples and grand-disciples. Would the other be afraid?
Of course, this situation was very rare. A year ago, there was a large number of giant swords being loaned out in the South Horizon Region. Although it was not clear who loaned them out, the large number of giant swords loaned out at that time had already attracted the attention of the super experts in the Extreme Purple Realm.
Under such circumstances, generally, some high-grade Extreme Purple Yin Body had been born. It was at least a third-grade Deep Purple or second-grade Pure Purple Body!
Even so, they had sent many people to search the Extreme Purple Yin Body in the Extreme Purple Realm to find this person …
However, after searching the entire Extreme Purple Realm, they still could not find any trace of this person. For a time, the super experts in the Extreme Purple Realm were very puzzled.
After the awakening of the Extreme Purple Yin Body, it was almost an instinctive sense of belonging to the Extreme Purple Yin Body. Obviously, the woman who awakened did not stay in the Extreme Purple Realm for long. This Extreme Purple Yin Body was a very unusual thing, but there was no result. In the end, it was left unsettled by the space node. If the woman did not appear, she would be found. It was almost impossible to find her through the space node.
"It can't be that guy from a year ago, right?" The purple-robed woman guessed.
Generally speaking, the warriors in the Extreme Purple Realm knew the rules. Unless they wanted to borrow the giant swords to practice the sword array, they would not be so bored as to pull the giant swords down. However, the woman had pulled down hundreds of thousands of giant swords. A warrior who could effortlessly control these giant swords must have a high-grade Extreme Purple Yin Body. Unfortunately, she did not know the rules. The purple-robed woman easily thought of the incident where a large number of giant swords were borrowed a year ago.
Thinking of this, the purple-robed woman gently stomped her feet. The big ship turned around and swooped down.
Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh …
She controlled the ship to descend, and the giant swords in the sky above the Heaven Domain were still being pulled down. Some of the giant swords' trajectories inevitably overlapped with her ship, so the purple-robed woman had to control the ship to avoid the giant swords that were descending.
"This guy!" The purple-robed woman dodged the giant swords, and there was a trace of anger in her heart. She said she did not know the rules. She really did not know the rules. What was she trying to do by pulling all the giant swords down? She was not really going to eat them, right? It would be a huge project to return hundreds of thousands of giant swords to their original positions!
Following the trajectory of the giant swords, the big ship began to dive down rapidly!
Then, the purple-robed woman standing at the bow of the ship revealed a look of shock …
There were hundreds of thousands of giant swords gathered below! Obviously, this person had not only pulled down the giant swords from the South Horizon Domain, but also from the North Horizon Domain, West Horizon Domain, and East Horizon Domain!
Millions of giant swords slowly circled in the sky, forming a vast sword river. It was a magnificent sight!
"This …"
It was not difficult for the Extreme Purple Yin Body to borrow a giant sword from other realms. The whole process was very simple. Once the sword was lent, it would slash down with inertia, and then disappear. There were only three steps in total. The person with the Extreme Purple Yin Body only needed to complete the first step and did not need to worry about the rest.
However, the giant swords in the sword river were all under the control of one person. They slowly flowed. Every giant sword that flowed to the end of the "sword river" had to change direction, and like nimble fish, they merged back into the sword river.
What was even more terrifying was that these giant swords did not make the slightest sound as they flowed. What did this mean? This meant that this person was controlling hundreds of thousands of giant swords, but he did not make any two giant swords collide.
The scene before them was not only terrifying, but also strange. How strong did one's control have to be to be able to achieve this?
Xi Youqin rolled up the sleeves of her casual clothes, revealing two white wrists. She constantly waved her hands towards the sky, and her slender fingers trembled slightly.
She did not know how she should cultivate. On one hand, she controlled these giant swords out of instinct, and on the other hand, it was purely out of boredom …
She waved her hands towards the sky, and with a faint smile on her face, she said to the Ancestral Gragon, "Is it nice? Little guy! "
"Caw, caw!" The Ancestral Gragon looked at the sword river in the sky, and his little tail suddenly wagged.
Hearing the Ancestral Gragon's cry, Xi Youqin smiled. "Then let's do it again!" After saying that, Xi Youqin suddenly stopped waving her hands, and instead, she straightened all ten swords!
"Whoosh, whoosh, whoosh …"
At the same time, all the giant swords were straightened neatly.
"Turn!"
"Whoosh!"
Hundreds of thousands of giant swords, this time, formed a circular vortex, and began to rotate slowly!
Seeing the transformation of these giant swords, the purple-robed woman standing on the big ship was stunned …
The Myriad of Colors Sword Formation was an extremely powerful sword formation in the universe.
A beginner could control 11,000 giant swords!
However, after successfully cultivating it, one could only control 110,000 giant swords with ease. Of course, the giant swords in the Extreme Purple Realm were not of high grades. For warriors in the Extreme Divine Realm, if they were rich enough, they could forge 110,000 low-grade divine weapons …
However, there were many mighty warriors in the Extreme Purple Realm who could control 1.1 million giant swords at the same time, and all of them were made from high-grade saint weapons!
As for some super mighty warriors, they could control 11 million giant swords … Of course, in the eyes of these super mighty warriors, although divine weapons weren't that valuable, they wouldn't go looking for 11 million giant swords. Therefore, they could only integrate a small amount of divine weapons into this sword formation.
Right now, this young man was able to control millions of giant swords with ease!
The most frightening thing was that the sword formation operated according to a certain pattern, and through constant practice, it was a process that went from being unfamiliar to being familiar with. But now, these giant swords weren't arranged in the same way, they were arranged in different shapes.
"Swoosh …"
The purple-robed woman adjusted the angle of the big ship, allowing it to go past the countless giant swords. Then, she saw a girl wearing white casual clothes, who had a big smile on her face as she wantonly controlled the giant swords.
"Just as I thought, she's just playing around …" The purple-robed woman suddenly felt a sense of injustice. "She's just a little Divine Core brat, why?!"
The jealousy between women was very strong. In the Upper Realm, a Divine Core warrior was just like a newborn baby, and they couldn't be considered to have stepped onto the path of cultivation. But, this girl in front of her was just a Divine Core warrior!

Chapter 943
Although she was jealous, the purple-robed woman still had a clear mind. Putting aside this woman's insane control, as she was able to control millions of huge swords at will, this woman's Extreme Purple Yin Body must be of a high grade!
With this thought in mind, the purple-robed woman controlled the ship to slowly descend.

Looking at the quatrain in the sky, Xi Youqin's eyes revealed a lonely expression. Although she was having fun, she still couldn't get rid of the loneliness in her heart.

The purple-robed woman smiled, "With your condition, of course you're qualified to be my disciple. Wait a moment, my master … will be here soon!"
Recommending this woman to her master would definitely be a great achievement. Of course, she wouldn't let Xi Youqin get away!
But she wasn't the only one in the Extreme Purple Realm. This little girl had drawn out millions of huge swords, and she wasn't the only one who was alarmed. She had to make the first move.

Since the woman said that her master would be here soon, Xi Youqin didn't take it to heart. Except for her emotional intelligence, Xi Youqin was very weak in other aspects. After thinking for a while, she said, "Cultivating martial arts requires you to strengthen your heart. Only then can you break through your mental barriers and reach the peak. Naturally, you have to focus on yourself. How can you cultivate for others?"
"But I'm cultivating for others. What's wrong with that?" Xi Youqin tilted her head and said suspiciously.
The purple-robed woman was speechless. Forget it, she would leave this little girl to her master. There was no need for her to get involved. Communicating with her was too tiring.
After a while, rays of purple light continued to condense, and a person slowly appeared on the big ship.
The purple-robed woman turned her head and a respectful expression appeared on her face. Then, she knelt on the ground and said, "Disciple greets master!"
The person on the ship was wearing a light purple robe. Judging from her appearance, she was a beautiful woman in her thirties or forties. After she appeared, her eyes were fixed on Xi Youqin. At the same time, she glanced at the giant swords floating in the air. Her face revealed a look of astonishment.

The number of Extreme Violet Yin Physiques in the universe was not small, but most of them were either light purple or green purple! They were either sixth-grade or fifth-grade. As for fourth-grade Deep Purple Bodies, they were extremely rare. Any woman with a fourth-grade Deep Purple Body was qualified to join the Myriad Purple Jade Tower unconditionally!
So when she heard that her disciple had a Deep Purple Body, how could she not be anxious? That was a third-grade Deep Purple Body.
However, when she saw her, she was shocked. The little girl in front of her was not a third-grade Deep Purple Body, nor was she a second-grade Deep Purple Body. She was a first-grade Deep Purple Body. How could a first-grade Deep Purple Body appear? This … The beautiful woman could not figure it out.
"Mas … master?" The purple-robed woman knelt on the ground and called out softly.
After realizing that she had lost her composure, the beautiful woman said to the purple-robed woman, "Hong Yue, please rise."
