After realizing that this was her master, Xi Youqin bowed slightly. "My surname is Xi, and my name is Youqin."
"Xi Youqin?" The beautiful woman asked.
Xi Youqin quickly nodded. "Yes!"

Chapter 944
The Ten Thousand Purple Jade Tower was famous in the universe, but Xi Youqin couldn't understand why he would say such a thing to her.
Since she had to join the Myriad Purple Jade Tower as a disciple, she did not care. She smiled and said to the beautiful woman, "Yes! No problem! "
At this moment, the beautiful woman raised her head and glanced at the large words that were pieced together by the huge swords, forming a seven-word poem of a resentful woman.
Thus, the beautiful woman frowned and said, "The cultivation method of my Ten Thousand Purple Jade Tower emphasizes on a pure heart and few desires. If you have any mortal bonds that you have not yet severed, you must sever them one by one. Don't have any other thoughts."
"Ah …" Xi Youqin blinked. Why was it different from what the old man said? However, she still shook her head firmly. "Impossible. I'd rather not join the Myriad Purple Jade Tower than be your disciple!"
The beautiful woman did not expect the little girl to change her mind so quickly.
However, Xi Youqin had a First Grade Extreme Purple Yin Body. How could she let her go?
The beautiful woman sneered. "You're still young and ignorant. How can I allow you to do as you wish?"
No matter who the beautiful woman was, Xi Youqin did not give her any face. "I'm not joining! What can you do to me? "
The woman called Hong Yue saw that her master's expression was not good and quickly persuaded her. "Miss Youqin, master is doing this for your own good. I think you should …"
Xi Youqin's expression darkened. She did not know that there was such a rule when it came to becoming a disciple. At this moment, she did not say anything and turned to leave.
However, just as she was about to turn around, she felt as if her body was stuck in mud. She could not move a single step.
"Ga Gu!" The Ancestral Dragon was not affected at all. It leaped onto Xi Youqin's shoulder and roared at the beautiful woman.
"Eh, this little dragon … is actually not affected?" The beautiful woman's face also revealed a trace of surprise.
In fact, the Ancestral Dragon could break free from most of the Origin Source Laws. Even the high-level Space and Time Laws had no effect on it.
"Let me go!" Xi Youqin wanted to leave the node and return to the Great Thousand World. Since she could not move, she waved her hand and all the giant swords in the sky came crashing down.
The beautiful lady let out a faint smile. She gently waved her hand as well, causing all the huge swords to leave Xi Youqin's control and return to their original positions. "You're only a Divine Core warrior, but you're able to control millions of huge swords. Your talent is indeed amazing, but if you think those huge swords can hurt me, then you're dreaming! You're still young, but you're still a little stubborn. I won't care about this. You'd better obediently follow me to the Myriad Purple Jade Tower! Master will treat you well. When you have a good education, Master will definitely help you choose the most outstanding genius in the universe … "
"You're not my master, who wants you to help me choose?" Xi Youqin scolded with a cold voice.
The beautiful lady didn't get angry, she still had a smile on her face. Hong Yue was stunned by what she saw. She knew her master had a bad temper, but she didn't get angry even after being scolded by this little girl. She could imagine how much her master valued this Xi Youqin …
Too bad, she didn't know what was good for her, and she was asking for trouble. Hong Yue sighed in her mind.
When the beautiful lady walked in front of Xi Youqin, she glanced at Xi Youqin with an indifferent expression, then said with the same kind voice, "If you don't want to go to the Myriad Purple Jade Tower, then I'll just kill that man and stop you from thinking about him. What do you think?"
"Ah …"
Hearing this, Xi Youqin became anxious.
She didn't know how strong this woman was, but she was definitely an existence that made people despair. Just like that old man, Luo Zheng didn't have the slightest ability to fight back in front of him. If this woman really wanted to kill Luo Zheng, what should she do?
Seeing Xi Youqin's panicked expression, the beautiful lady smiled faintly, "Don't worry, after entering the Myriad Purple Jade Tower, you'll be able to clear your mind and cultivate. It won't take long for you to forget about him. Feelings are ultimately useless for cultivators …"
Who knew that Xi Youqin's panicked expression only lasted for a moment. She turned around and said coldly to the beautiful lady, "Don't even think about it. If you dare to kill Luo Zheng, I'll kill myself immediately. I'd rather die than acknowledge you as my master!"
"Luo Zheng?" The beautiful woman mulled over this name. She even compared it to the hundreds of young elites in the universe that left the deepest impression on her. But, this name wasn't among them. She understood that since this person was nameless, then there was no need for her to fear this name. Before this, the beautiful woman had some reservations about this name. If this person she admired was an Empyrean descendant, then things would be a bit troublesome.
Xi Youqin realized that she had blurted out Luo Zheng's name and hurriedly covered her mouth. Her heart was filled with regret.
However, the beautiful lady didn't expect that although the name Luo Zheng wasn't famous now, it was destined to shake the universe in the future. It was a name that she would remember for the rest of her life!
"Hehehehe …"
At this moment, a wave of laughter suddenly came from the sky.
The voice was soft and sweet, but it was endlessly charming. Just this laughter alone made people's faces and ears turn red, and their hearts beat faster.
The beautiful lady's expression didn't change in the slightest, but Hong Yue's face had already revealed an awkward expression. As for Xi Youqin, her cultivation was the lowest. When the seductive laughter entered her ears, her face flushed red, and her breathing became more rapid. She had given herself to Luo Zheng, so she was no longer a virgin. Her body had an extremely unnatural reaction.
"The Ten Thousand Purple Jade House is a nuns' house. Those who enter it are like living widows. If you take this woman as your master, then you'll have no interest in life. You might as well die." The voice gently floated over, as if someone was blowing air into Xi Youqin's ear. It made all the hair on her body stand on end, and she couldn't help but tremble.
Not long after, a large bed inlaid with gold appeared in the sky. This bed was about ten feet wide and ten feet long. However, the person sleeping on this bed wasn't a giant, but a delicate woman.
This woman looked as pure as a white lotus. Her appearance was extremely pure, but she was wearing very little clothes, and she exuded a foxy charm. Not to mention ordinary men, even Xi Youqin's mouth became dry when she looked at her.
What made Xi Youqin most dumbfounded was that at the edge of this bed, there were ten topless men neatly sitting upright. Those men were all strong and powerful. At this moment, they were sitting cross-legged with their eyes closed.
"Zhao Xiaoxiao, you slut. You don't know how to restrain yourself. What right do you have to criticize me?" When the beautiful woman saw the woman appear, her face immediately darkened.
This woman called Zhao Xiaoxiao lifted her seemingly boneless body. Her legs were like two thin snakes, and then she placed her arms on the shoulders of a strong man. Looking at the Seven Words Quatrains in the sky, she smiled charmingly and said, "Little girl, I see that you're already a virgin. You must be thinking of men to compose this poem. Why don't you come to my Ecstasy House? You can taste all the men in the world, and you can also cultivate …"
Before Zhao Xiaoxiao could finish her words, the beautiful woman directly scolded her, "Slut!"
Zhao Xiaoxiao wasn't angry at being scolded like this. She just smiled faintly and said, "There's more than one path on the martial path. Hua Bingyan, I've debated with you many times, but you haven't been able to convince me even once."
Xi Youqin stood where she was, hugging the Ancestral Gragon. Her mind was in a mess, and she felt that she was about to collapse. What were these people talking about?
She didn't know that the Three Storey Towers in the Extreme Purple Realm were also very famous in the universe. Hua Bingyan was the owner of the Myriad Purple Jade Tower, and Zhao Xiaoxiao was the owner of the Ecstasy House.
The Two Storey Towers were quite extreme. The Myriad Purple Jade Tower was a typical nunnery. Once one entered, one's heart would be pure, and all thoughts would be cut off.

Chapter 945
The reason was simple. Even the most lustful cultivators were afraid of death.
People could still enjoy themselves while they were alive, but after they died, they would have nothing left …
The women in the Soul Stirring Tower did not force people to do what they wanted. However, the Art of Seduction that they cultivated made men unable to extricate themselves. In the end, their bodies would be squeezed dry. If it was light, their vitality would be greatly damaged, and their hundreds or thousands of years of cultivation would be destroyed. If it was serious, their vitality would be damaged, and they would become useless. There were even many who lost their lives.
Obviously, Xi Youqin wanted a man, but she only wanted a Luo Zheng. How could she enter such a place?
As for the Myriad Purple Jade Tower, it directly cut off the root of love. She could not do it either.
She was deceived by that damned old man …
Xi Youqin thought bitterly. The problem was that she was already here. Facing the two people in front of her, how could she escape? Even if she escaped back to the Central Domain through the node, would she bring disaster to Luo Zheng? These were the things that Xi Youqin was very worried about.
At this time, Xi Youqin was thinking gloomily in her heart. The two Tower Masters beside her were arguing about Xi Youqin!
The two of them would not let go of Xi Youqin's First Grade Extreme Purple Yin Body. The argument was at a stalemate. Seeing that the argument between the two women was about to escalate into a fight, an old and dignified voice rang out again.
"Get out of my way!"
After this voice rang out, Zhao Xiaoxiao and Hua Bingyan's faces darkened.
Then, an amiable but dignified old woman appeared in front of everyone, holding a walking stick.
Although Zhao Xiaoxiao and Hua Bingyan's faces were gloomy, they still respectfully greeted the old woman, "Tower Master."
In the Extreme Purple Realm, there were three floors and one tower. Other than the Myriad Purple Jade Tower and the Soul Stirring Tower, there was also the Moon Embracing Tower! And the tower that held the absolute advantage was this tower, called the Indefinite Tower!
This old granny was the Indefinite Tower's Tower Master. The outside world called her "Indefinite Old Woman".
Supporting himself with his walking stick, Wuding Old Man slowly walked towards Xi Youqin with a faint smile on his face. "Not bad, not bad. That old fellow has found me a good seedling!"
"Pagoda Master, Xi Youqin was my first …" Hua Bingyan couldn't accept Indefinite Old Man's words and tried to argue.
As for Zhao Xiaoxiao, she smiled lightly and said, "So what if you go first? I don't want to be a nun with you! "
"You first?" Indefinite Old Man glanced coldly at Hua Bingyan, then smiled at Xi Youqin. "Little girl, show me your right hand."
At this moment, Xi Youqin was completely bewildered. She no longer dared to call her Master. Instead, she timidly extended her right hand towards the old lady in front of her.
Everyone could clearly see that in Xi Youqin's palm was a location talisman unique to the Demi-Immortal Clan.
Seeing this location talisman, Hua Bingyan and Zhao Xiaoxiao naturally understood. The Pagoda Master was not a member of the Demi-Immortal Clan, but her old man was an important figure in the Demi-Immortal Clan. Presumably, this girl was sent by that old man?
Indefinite Old Man looked indifferently at Hua Bingyan and said, "See? Do you need me to explain? "
Hua Bingyan's expression was slightly awkward as she shook her head. "No need, Pagoda Master. I didn't know before."
"It's good that you didn't know." Indefinite Old Man looked kindly at Xi Youqin and smiled. "Little girl, the Master that my old man wants you to acknowledge is this old man."
Xi Youqin curled her lips. After all this commotion, she had already given up on the idea of becoming Luo Zheng's disciple. However, on second thought, it seemed that she could only continue to cultivate. No matter what, it was a good thing for Luo Zheng that she had become stronger.
But she still asked warily, "You … want me to think of a man?"
These words came out of nowhere, but Indefinite Old Man was baffled, and all sorts of strange expressions appeared on his face.
Zhao Xiaoxiao laughed from the side. "Hahaha, this little girl looks decent and virtuous, but she has inherited the true teachings of my Soul-Dousing House! Looks like she's the most suitable to join my House! "
"No, no, no … that's not what I meant!" Xi Youqin's face turned red again. Her head was spinning from the two House Masters' actions, and her words were all jumbled up, but it was really embarrassing to say those words out loud.
However, Indefinite Old Man understood Xi Youqin's words and said indifferently, "The old man has already told me about you. I also know that you have no interest in martial arts and cultivate for that person. If you acknowledge me as your master, I will not restrict you too much. But the path of martial arts ultimately depends on one's heart. If you can persevere, you will definitely gain something. It all depends on whether you can persevere for that person …"
Hearing this, Xi Youqin was relieved. She nodded silently and said, "I can!"
"Very good." Indefinite Old Man smiled faintly. "In that case, you will enter the Tower with me."
"Can we not do it now?" Xi Youqin asked again. Her motivation to acknowledge a master was very strong, but she was not willing to enter the Tower now. Luo Zheng had just returned to the Central Domain, and she wanted to spend more time with him during this period.
Indefinite Old Man smiled faintly. "As you wish! However, you will enter the Tower with me. In the future, when you return to the Extreme Purple Realm, you will naturally connect the space node to the Tower's entrance. "
When warriors with the Extreme Purple Yin Body first entered the Extreme Purple Realm, their location was randomly assigned. However, if the location was changed, the next time they entered, they would enter directly from the node they left from last time. Indefinite Old Man wanted her to change the node to somewhere near Indefinite Tower.
"Mm!" Xi Youqin finally smiled. This old woman was very easy to talk to. Xi Youqin could not stand too many restrictions, so she naturally felt that Indefinite Old Man was amiable.
…
…
A night of spring.
Ning Yudie lazily stretched her well-proportioned body. When she saw Luo Zheng beside her, a blush appeared on her face.
For a cultivator, this little bit of energy was like a drop in the ocean. It was an absurd night, and neither of them slept at all!
"Are you asleep?" Ning Yudie lay on Luo Zheng's chest, her fine black hair scattered over Luo Zheng's chest.
She was petite, so sitting on Luo Zheng's body was like sitting on a boat. Realizing that Luo Zheng was only pretending to sleep, she mischievously reached out to pinch Luo Zheng's nose.
However, before her little hand could touch Luo Zheng's nose, Luo Zheng grabbed it like lightning.
"You're so annoying. Let go!" Ning Yudie said coquettishly.
How could Luo Zheng let go? After grabbing her hand, he pulled her into his embrace and held her tightly in his arms.
"Hmph, last night you …" Ning Yudie said again.
Luo Zheng interrupted Ning Yudie's words. He chuckled and said, "You're still talking? You're freezing me to death!"
"Hmph, who told you to be naughty? If you dare to be naughty in the future, I'll freeze you to death! "Ning Yudie's face revealed a complacent expression.
"I'm not afraid of the cold! If you don't believe me, why don't we try again? "Luo Zheng laughed.
"I don't want to. Do you think I'm a fool …"
After flirting for a while, the two of them freshened up. Ning Yudie tied her fine black hair into a bun, leaving her delicate earlobes exposed. This was the custom of the Central Domain. Women who got married had to tie their hair up for three days. Under this style, Ning Yudie lost some of her playfulness and liveliness, but gained a sense of nobility.
Yesterday, the old man didn't leave the Cloud Hall. Luo Zheng didn't even know the purpose of the old man's visit. Therefore, after waking up early this morning, he went straight to the old man.
The Cloud Palace had arranged for a tea house, and this old man was drinking tea in the tea house without being salty or bland!
As soon as Luo Zheng entered the tea house, he bowed to the old man. The old man chuckled and looked outside the tea house. At this time, a figure flew over from outside the tea house. It was Xi Youqin, who left last night.
Looking at Xi Youqin's travel-worn appearance, where did she leave the Cloud Hall yesterday? Luo Zheng's face revealed a trace of curiosity. He wondered where Xi Youqin went.
The key point was that he saw the old man talking to Xi Youqin yesterday. And this morning, Xi Youqin went straight here. What happened?
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 946
Xi Youqin flew into the tea house with a faint smile on her face. She called out sweetly, "Grandpa, let me pour some tea for you!"
The old man chuckled and nodded his head in agreement.
In fact, since Xi Youqin had become Indefinite Old Man's disciple last night, she should have called him Grandmaster. However, Xi Youqin wanted to hide it from Luo Zheng, so she could only call him Grandpa.
After receiving the old man's approval, Xi Youqin smiled. She reached out and washed the tea set. Then, she sifted the green tea with a funnel. She poured a full cup of green tea and handed it to the old man.
The old man's face lit up. He laughed and said, "My disciples and grand-disciples aren't as quick as you. Good! Good … "
After Xi Youqin had become his disciple, that old woman of his family should thank him. Now that he saw how intelligent Xi Youqin was, the more he looked at her, the more he liked her. He was able to see through people's hearts. Yesterday, during the dual cultivation ceremony, how could he not see the bitterness in Xi Youqin's heart? In front of Luo Zheng, he naturally praised Xi Youqin.
Xi Youqin smiled. She poured another cup and handed it to Luo Zheng. Then, she retreated to the side.
Luo Zheng took a sip of tea and asked, "Yesterday, I was fortunate enough to receive your medicine. I …"
Who knew that just as Luo Zheng opened his mouth, the old man waved his hand. He was a person who didn't like to be long-winded. He just said, "Little Mo and the others have already returned to the Demi-Immortal Clan! That medicine isn't anything good, but it can make your cultivation speed double with half the effort. As for Lei Fa and the others, I have already sent them away! I don't have a name, so you don't need to ask me … "
Luo Zheng's mouth was wide open. He hadn't even asked anything yet, but this old man had already spouted out a bunch of words. The old man already knew what he wanted to ask, so why did he speak like this? Luo Zheng felt a little speechless.
The old man chuckled and drank the tea in his hand. He then turned his head to Xi Youqin and said, "Little girl, pour this old man another cup of tea!"
Xi Youqin walked over and filled the celadon teacup, then retreated to the side.
Before Luo Zheng could respond, the old man went on. "You are a Fateless One, and the Demi-Immortals can't control your fate. Therefore, I won't be taking you to the Demi-Immortals."
"Ah?" Luo Zheng was slightly stunned.
The old man chuckled. "Ah, it's useless. If I don't bring them, then I won't bring them. Although those old guys almost fought with me over this matter, my point of view is right! You are free to ascend! My Demi-Immortals will not interfere with your path. "
This time, Luo Zheng was even more confused. He thought for a moment and was about to speak again. This time, he wanted to ask about the Linglong Pagoda. He had promised Boss Mo and the others, who were also Little Mo, that the old man spoke of. The agreement was that the Linglong Pagoda would be fully unfurled and remain in the Sea God Continent for three hundred years!
But before he could speak, the old man continued, "Let's leave the Linglong Pagoda here first. It's not very useful anyway."
Luo Zheng was stunned. He had seen people rush to speak, but he had never seen someone rush to speak like this. He hadn't even said anything, and this guy had already answered. Luo Zheng could only choke speechlessly.
Xi Youqin, who was next to him, laughed. Luo Zheng's appearance was unprecedentedly funny …
Therefore, Luo Zheng wanted to ask for a reason, but just as he opened his mouth, he closed it. He was sure that the old man would not let him speak.
Sure enough, just as he opened his mouth, the old man continued, "There's no reason! I've thought about it a lot. Fateless people have their own paths. You are a participant in the battle of the world, but you are also a Fateless person. Therefore, you have to walk your own path. It's best that others don't interfere … "
Luo Zheng could only silently take a sip of tea. Yun Luo had said the same thing before.
She didn't want the Demi-Immortals to take away Zhao Li, who had obtained the title of Silver Asura. But she didn't interfere with him. Even after he obtained the Heavenly Dao fragment, she didn't react. Instead, she allowed him to make his own judgment …
This way, Luo Zheng was indeed quite free. But he still needed someone to show him the way. Luo Zheng felt somewhat conflicted.
This time, Luo Zheng didn't want to speak at all. The old man patted Luo Zheng's shoulder. "The path of a Legend has always been unprecedented. No one is qualified to show you the way, not even Celestial Venerables! But you have to remember, I won't take you to the Demi-Immortals. You won't change your blood. But in the end, you are still a member of the Demi-Immortals. Just treat it as an agreement with the Exquisite Pagoda! "
After saying this, the old man stood up and waved the celadon teacup in his hand. "Can you give this old man another cup of tea?"
Xi Youqin smiled slightly and walked over to fill his cup. After the old man drank it in one gulp, he looked at the Ancestral Dragon beside Xi Youqin and said, "I forgot, this Ancestral Dragon will be taken care of by you. This old man should also go! Goodbye … "
This time, Luo Zheng didn't say anything. If he opened his mouth, the old man would most likely seal his mouth again …
But this time, the one who spoke was not Luo Zheng, but Xi Youqin.
Xi Youqin smiled faintly and asked, "Grandpa, what is your name … Can you tell me?"
The old man turned his head and smiled at Xi Youqin. His mouth moved slightly, and a wave of True Essence was transmitted to Xi Youqin.
After saying this, his figure flashed and disappeared directly in front of Luo Zheng.
"Nameless … So Grandmaster's name is Nameless. I see." Xi Youqin learned the old man's name through True Essence Sound Transmission. As for Xi Youqin's Master, it was called Indefinite.
"Wu Ming and Wu Ding." This couple was also a famous martial artist in the universe.
Luo Zheng looked at Xi Youqin strangely and asked, "Youqin, what is this old man's name?"
The corners of Xi Youqin's mouth twitched, and she snorted. "I'm not telling you!"
"No, you have to tell me!" Luo Zheng was not happy. This girl was still keeping him in suspense. How could he allow it?
"I won't!" Xi Youqin smiled faintly. Her eyes were full of lust, but her body slowly retreated.
How could Luo Zheng let Xi Youqin go? He took a step forward, intercepted her, and pulled her into his arms. Since Xi Youqin refused to let go, Luo Zheng could only not let go. After a while, a wave of True Essence spread out and enveloped the tea house. Xi Youqin gently placed the teapot in her hand on the table, and her whole body went limp in Luo Zheng's arms …
For a moment, the small tea house was filled with spring.
In the end, Luo Zheng still did not get the old man's name.
As for what the old man said to Xi Youqin before, and where Xi Youqin went last night, Luo Zheng could not ask.
He was curious, but since he could not get the answer, he could only give up in the end. Therefore, in Luo Zheng's eyes, Xi Youqin seemed a little mysterious. What was this obedient girl hiding from him? And what was her relationship with the old man of the Demi-Immortal Clan?
Xi Youqin did not want to tell him the truth. Frankly speaking, if she could stay by Luo Zheng's side, she would not have the slightest desire to pursue martial arts.
But now she understood that the Lower Realm would not be able to keep Luo Zheng. Sooner or later, he would ascend …
She wanted to give Luo Zheng a surprise. After Luo Zheng ascended, she could enter the Extreme Purple Realm to cultivate with peace of mind. She hoped to be able to reunite with Luo Zheng in the Upper Realm. As for this Ning Yudie, if she had to cultivate for a hundred years before ascending to the Upper Realm, then she could slowly wait in the Lower Realm. Xi Youqin was immensely proud of this plan.
For half a month, other than accompanying Ning Yudie and Xi Youqin, Luo Zheng spent the rest of his time cultivating. But after half a month, Luo Zheng stood in the Cloud Hall, looking toward the east.
Eastern Region …
Since he stepped on the Blue Clouds Road, four years had passed in a flash.
For a martial artist, four years was just the blink of an eye. However, Luo Zheng was only twenty years old. Four years was equivalent to one-fifth of his life. These four years were also the most important four years for him!
It was time to go back.

Chapter 947
As he looked towards the south, a figure suddenly appeared in Luo Zheng's mind …
It was Su Lingyun, the Eldest Princess of the Sky-Scorching Dynasty, who was dressed in green and cared for him a lot in the Blue Cloud Sect.
After he left the Eastern Domain, he didn't have any time to rest because of Luo Yan. Su Lingyun was still waiting for him there, but he couldn't return until now.
He thought about how he had dragged Su Lingyun all the way through the Imperial Palace, smashing one wall after another. His face was covered in dirt, but Su Lingyun had a nonchalant smile on her face. Her requirements had always been very low. Perhaps it could be said that she had always been like a mentor to him. She had always done her best and never had any extravagant demands.
Thus, a trace of guilt rose in Luo Zheng's heart.
However, Luo Zheng's fame in the Cloud Hall had spread throughout the Central Domain. With such a great reputation, it was enough to ensure Su Lingyun's safety.
The Sect Master of the Blue Cloud Sect, Shi Jingtian, wasn't a fool. How could he dare to let Su Lingyun suffer the slightest harm?
Luo Zheng wasn't worried about Su Lingyun's safety. However, his promise to return to the Eastern Domain had been delayed for a long time. For others, four years was an unimaginable speed. However, Luo Zheng had several opportunities to return to the Eastern Domain, but because of Luo Yan's problem, he ultimately chose to use the time to cultivate.
In other words, Luo Zheng had put Su Lingyun behind him …
There were two ways to return to the Eastern Domain. One was to use the Transmission Array and enter the ruins of the Sixth Grade Sect. The other was to directly travel through the Central Domain.
If it was in the past, Luo Zheng would probably choose the shortest way, which was to use the Transmission Array.
But now, Luo Zheng was suddenly relaxed. Time seemed to have suddenly increased by countless times. He took the two women all the way to the east. He chose to go through the entire Central Domain to reach the Eastern Domain.
"I haven't been to the Eastern Region yet." Ning Yudie smiled faintly.
On the way to the east, the two women seemed to be in a good mood. Luo Zheng didn't know if there was still a knot in their hearts, but at least they didn't show it on the surface.
Xi Youqin blinked and said with a smile, "I haven't been there either, but I want to see the place where Luo Zheng grew up and his hometown!"
Strictly speaking, the Eastern Domain was just an extension of the Central Domain. However, compared to the Western Domain and other places, it was still much larger. That was why an existence like the Burning Sky Empire was born in a corner of the Eastern Domain.
With Luo Zheng and the two women's speed, it would only take them a day to travel across the Eastern Domain. But, the three of them were walking leisurely, and they treated this as a vacation. Therefore, they slowed down, and it took them six to seven days to arrive at the Burning Sky Empire.
Luo Zheng brought the two goddess-like women and stood in front of the Burning Sky Empire's city gate. In an instant, they attracted countless gazes.
Especially Xi Youqin and Ning Yudie. With their temperament, they looked like goddesses from a painting …
"Look, that guy is so arrogant, he actually brought two beautiful women into the Imperial Capital!"
"If those bastards from the Imperial Capital set their eyes on them, they'll be in trouble …"
"Are you kidding me? Look at that guy's cultivation, and those two women's cultivation, I can't see through them at all. I'm afraid they are at least Divine Reflection realm, especially that man, I'm afraid he's a Divine Core warrior! Who dares to cause trouble for them? The eight great families? They're just courting death! "
Some commoners and cultivators were whispering behind them.
Naturally, the three of them didn't care about these voices at all …
In the Eastern Domain, the highest cultivation realm for cultivators was the Divine Core realm, and the strongest cultivator in the entire Eastern Domain was a sect under the Cloud Hall, the Sect Chief of the Blue Cloud Sect, Shi Jingtian.
Ning Yudie and Xi Youqin didn't have much interest in the Burning Sky Empire's city. Ning Yudie was the one who controlled the Cloud Hall, and that was equivalent to controlling the entire Cloud Sea City. Compared to the Cloud Sea City that was home to many powerful families, the Burning Sky Empire's city couldn't be compared at all.
As for Xi Youqin, she had grown up in the Void Sky City. The Void Sky City was a big city that was famous for its resources. Although it was destroyed by the Tian Xie Sect, before that, the Void Sky City didn't put any other big cities in the Central Domain in their eyes.
Of course, Luo Zheng's feelings were completely different. After revisiting this place, he couldn't help but sigh in his heart.
After traveling for a while, Luo Zheng and the two girls finally arrived at the entrance of the Blue Cloud Sect.
It seemed like some kind of event was being held at the entrance of the Blue Cloud Sect. A few deacons of the Blue Cloud Sect were standing on top of the Flying Chariot, guarding the entrance of the Blue Cloud Sect.
When one of the deacons saw Luo Zheng, Ning Yudie, and Xi Youqin walking towards them, he glanced at them, then a shocked expression emerged on his face.
The deacons couldn't see through Luo Zheng and Ning Yudie's cultivation bases, but as for Xi Youqin, he could tell that she was a Divine Core warrior!
Although without the Extreme Violet Yin Body, Xi Youqin's combat strength was weaker than some Divine Core warriors, she was still able to intimidate some warriors with her Divine Core cultivation base. This was especially true in the Eastern Domain, where Divine Core warriors were the strongest warriors.
The deacons drove the Flying Chariot and flew towards them. He leapt down from the Flying Chariot and cupped his fist toward Luo Zheng and the girls. "Excuse me, why have you come to the Blue Cloud Sect?"
Luo Zheng smiled. He looked at the deacons in front of him and said, "Deacons Cao, don't you remember me?"
This deacons Cao was the deacons who were responsible for guarding the Illusory Fish Pond. Perhaps he had been transferred to be a patrol deacons, and it looked like he had been demoted …
Deacons Cao's heart skipped a beat. He stared at the man in front of him with a surprised expression.
Actually, Luo Zheng gave him a familiar feeling, as if he had seen this man somewhere before.
But, Luo Zheng had been away for more than four years, and during these four years, Luo Zheng had grown from a teenager to a young man. During this time, his overall strength had increased by leaps and bounds, and after tempering his body several times, his body had undergone a complete transformation. Even his temperament had changed completely.
How could Deacons Cao recognize him?
After carefully inspecting Luo Zheng, his eyes suddenly widened, and a possibility suddenly appeared in his mind!
Luo Zheng!
Although the Central Domain and Eastern Domain didn't have much information, a lot of information was still transmitted to them through the Blue Cloud Sect.
Luo Zheng was a disciple from the Eastern Domain, a disciple from the Blue Cloud Sect! How could the Blue Cloud Sect not pay attention to him? In fact, every single action of Luo Zheng's would be transmitted to them from the Central Domain whenever they had the chance.
Seeing Deacons Cao's astonished gaze, Luo Zheng nodded slightly and made a gesture to keep quiet.
Deacons Cao nodded slightly and controlled himself, not shouting out loud.
When Luo Zheng was training in the Illusory Fish Pond, Deacons Cao still remembered it clearly. He had specially reported to the higher-ups for Luo Zheng, but he was stopped by the Enlightened Masters.
Those Enlightened Masters were punished by Shi Jingtian, and one of them was even killed by Shi Jingtian on the spot during the All-Peak Competition. However, after the matter slowly died down, those Enlightened Masters decided to settle the score. However, Deacons Cao's matter wasn't the main reason for Shi Jingtian's rage. Therefore, those Enlightened Masters found an excuse to demote him to Patrol Deacons.
"Why were you demoted to Patrol Deacons?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
Deacons Cao was delighted. He knew what kind of status Luo Zheng had now. However, since Luo Zheng asked about it, there might be a chance for him to turn the situation around. Some things might be extremely difficult for someone like him, but for the current Luo Zheng, it was just a matter of a few words.
Therefore, he gave a brief explanation …
Luo Zheng nodded slightly, but before he could speak, Ning Yudie frowned slightly and said indifferently, "I have never been to the Blue Cloud Sect before, so I thought that there wouldn't be such boring matters in a second-grade sect. I wonder how Shi Jingtian manages the sect. If I see him, I will punish him."
Deacons Cao's face revealed a strange expression when he heard Ning Yudie call out the Sect Leader's name. She wants to punish the Sect Leader? Who is this woman?
Deacons Cao didn't dare to ask recklessly, but he was guessing in his heart.
Looking at Deacons Cao's expression, Luo Zheng smiled and said, "Alright, since the main character has spoken, there shouldn't be any problems."
"Main character …" Since Luo Zheng had spoken, Deacons Cao dared to ask, "This fairy-like lady is …"
Luo Zheng shrugged and said, "She is the Hall Master of your Cloud Hall."

Chapter 948
As a second-grade sect, the Blue Cloud Sect was a subordinate sect of the Cloud Hall.
However, to the majority of the disciples and deacons of the Blue Cloud Sect, the Cloud Hall was only a legend.
The number of disciples that the Cloud Hall accepted from the Blue Cloud Sect was simply too few.
Every three years, there would be a Sect Wide Competition. Only the top three would have the qualifications to step onto the Azure Cloud Road. There were hundreds of thousands of disciples in the Blue Cloud Sect. It could be said that only one in a hundred thousand was chosen.
Just like that, the elites of the Blue Cloud Sect still had to compete with the other four second-grade sects on the Blue Cloud Sect.
As for their Blue Cloud Sect's Sect Master, he could only be considered a deacon in the Cloud Hall. He didn't even have the qualifications to be an Elder. From this, one could imagine how high and mighty the Cloud Hall was.
This time, Luo Zheng brought Ning Yudie back. He didn't have any intention of hiding anything, so he directly said it.
However, he didn't consider Deacon Cao's tolerance. His mouth was agape as he looked at Ning Yudie. His face was filled with fear, and he was momentarily speechless.
In the Cloud Hall, there were a few Elders who patrolled the Blue Cloud Sect. However, the Sect Master had never personally visited. Deacon Cao couldn't tell for a moment. However, Deacon Cao's reaction was swift. He bowed toward Ning Yudie and respectfully said, "The Sect Master has personally visited our Blue Cloud Sect. This lowly one will immediately inform the Sect Master!"
Regardless of whether the person in front of him was the Sect Master or not, it was still up to Shi Jingtian to determine. He had to do his job properly.
After saying that, Deacon Cao hurriedly jumped onto the Flying Chariot and sped toward the Blue Cloud Sect.
"Old Cao, why are you in such a hurry?" A patrolling deacon at the side asked.
How could Deacon Cao care about others? Without the slightest hesitation, he directly rushed toward the Azure Cloud Hall.
"You're crazy!" The patrolling deacon scolded when he saw that Deacon Cao was ignoring him. However, the other deacons were also curious as to what had just happened. They turned their heads and saw three people standing at the entrance of the Blue Cloud Sect. They couldn't see through the cultivation levels of these three people, so they were all shocked and nervous. When they thought about how Deacon Cao left in a hurry, their faces immediately turned solemn.
No matter who these three people were, they were not people they could afford to offend.
Shortly after Deacon Cao left, a burly figure rushed over. It was the sect leader of the Blue Cloud Sect, Shi Jingtian.
"Sect Master!"
"Sect Master!"
These deacons were good at reading people's expressions. When they saw Shi Jingtian rushing over, they knew that the three men standing in front of the Sect's entrance must be someone important. However, they couldn't figure out who these three men were.
Shi Jingtian floated over. When he saw Luo Zheng, a pleasantly surprised expression emerged on his face. When he turned to Ning Yudie, his expression became respectful. "Greetings, Sect Master! I didn't know Sect Master was here, so I didn't welcome you properly. Please forgive me!"
Within the Cloud Hall, Ning Yudie was a mysterious figure. Shi Jingtian was just a deacon. He had endured for so many years, but because his cultivation level was stuck at the Divine Core realm, he wasn't qualified to be promoted to a Sect Master.
When the deacons saw the Sect Master greeting Shi Jingtian, their hearts started pounding when they heard the words Sect Master.
There was no Divine Kingdom in the Central Domain, but the Sect Master of the Cloud Hall had the same status as the Divine Emperor of a Divine Kingdom. As for the Eastern Domain and the Blue Cloud Sect, they were just territories under the Cloud Hall!
They never expected that the Sect Master of the Cloud Hall would come to a place like the Eastern Domain for something like this.
However, these deacons were quick to react. They quickly followed behind Shi Jingtian and bowed deeply toward Ning Yudie. "Greetings, Sect Master!" In fact, with their strength, they weren't even qualified to be Ning Yudie's subordinates. But, no matter what level they were in, they all had the desire to work hard. Although the chances were slim, they still had to show off their abilities.
Right at this moment, Deacon Cao had arrived with the Flying Chariot. When he saw this scene, he immediately understood that the goddess-like figure was the Sect Master of the Cloud Hall!
He jumped down from the Flying Chariot and stood with the other deacons. Then, he bowed deeply toward Ning Yudie.
Ning Yudie glanced at Deacon Cao faintly and said, "But I just heard that, that …"
When she said that, Shi Jingtian felt his scalp go numb. How could he not be nervous when the Sect Master questioned him? He suddenly remembered that Deacon Cao's incident was related to Luo Zheng.
At that time, he had indeed severely punished the few Spiritual Masters. However, these messy Spiritual Masters were inextricably linked to the seven great noble families. It was not appropriate for him to suppress all of these Spiritual Masters. Moreover, this Deacon Cao himself did not have any background. It was likely that these Spiritual Masters had taken revenge on him …
However, he was just a Sect Master. How could he care so much? This time, Luo Zheng happened to bump into him. When Ning Yudie asked him about it, he hated those damned Perfected Masters to the core. After this incident, it was time for him to be ruthless!
"This subordinate deserves to die. I didn't manage the Blue Cloud Sect strictly. In the future, I will strictly reorganize the Blue Cloud Sect! As for Deacon Cao, I will rearrange him … "Shi Jingtian lowered his head even more.
The other deacons looked at Deacon Cao and their hearts trembled. The Cloud Hall Sect Master had asked about it and the Sect Master had made a promise. Deacon Cao's fate had been completely rewritten! When Deacon Cao was demoted, the other deacons had bullied him. Now, Deacon Cao's luck had finally turned for the better …
Deacon Cao was also extremely excited. He did not expect that his fate could be reversed with just a few words!
Seeing Shi Jingtian's serious expression, Ning Yudie smiled faintly and said, "Alright, I came to the Eastern Region this time to accompany Luo Zheng. There's nothing else. You don't have to be so nervous."
Her words made Shi Jingtian relax a little. It was the first time in history that the Sect Master had personally appeared in the Blue Cloud Sect. Moreover, the first thing he did was to ask for an explanation. This really scared him.
"Sect Master Shi, you don't have to be so nervous." Luo Zheng smiled faintly.
Shi Jingtian smiled faintly and said, "Luo Zheng, you don't have to call me Sect Master. It's better for us to address each other as equals …"
Cultivators used strength to determine seniority. Even if Luo Zheng was from the Blue Cloud Sect, Luo Zheng's strength had already reached a level where Shi Jingtian could only look up to him. He couldn't accept Luo Zheng calling him Sect Master.
However, Luo Zheng said seriously, "I'm from the Blue Cloud Sect. It's normal for me to address you as Sect Master. There's nothing wrong with it."
Since Luo Zheng had said so, Shi Jingtian couldn't refuse. However, Shi Jingtian sighed. Luo Zheng was a sentimental person. In fact, before Luo Zheng participated in the All-Peak Competition, Shi Jingtian didn't give him any help at all. Everything depended on Luo Zheng. Who would have thought that in just a few years, he would become the strongest person in the Central Domain?
Previously, Shi Jingtian also knew about the Dual Cultivation Ceremony between Ning Yudie and Luo Zheng. However, the Blue Cloud Sect only prepared a generous gift for them. That day, they invited the leaders of the various third-grade sects. Although the Blue Cloud Sect belonged to the Cloud Hall, as a second-grade sect, they weren't qualified to attend.
This time, Luo Zheng suddenly returned to the Eastern Domain … Although Luo Zheng's family was in the Eastern Domain, the biggest burden for Luo Zheng was the Princess of the Vast Sky, Su Lingyun.
Under Shi Jingtian's guidance, Ning Yudie and Xi Youqin followed behind and toured the entire Blue Cloud Sect.
At this time, Luo Zheng transmitted his voice to Shi Jingtian, "Sect Master, where is Instructor Su?"
Shi Jingtian had already expected Luo Zheng to ask this question. He answered truthfully, "After you left the Central Domain, Su Lingyun was still under house arrest in the Imperial Palace. However, half a year later, I brought her back to the Blue Cloud Sect. She returned to Xiao Yufeng as an instructor."
Shi Jingtian had once promised Luo Zheng that as long as he was alive, Su Lingyun wouldn't be harmed by anyone. Although he couldn't help Su Lingyun regain the throne, he could ensure her safety.
A year later, the news of Luo Zheng's rise in fame during the Martial Arts Conference reached Shi Jingtian's ears. Naturally, this promise was changed.
Shi Jingtian knew about the relationship between Su Lingyun and Luo Zheng. In the future, Luo Zheng would definitely become the core figure of the Cloud Hall!
On the second day after receiving this news, Shi Jingtian personally went to the Burning Sky Palace and kicked the Crown Prince, Su Rui, off the throne in front of the civil and military officials. Then, he released Su Lingyun from house arrest.

Chapter 949
After Su Qian went missing, Shi Jingtian became the only representative of the Cloud Hall in the Eastern Region. Who in the entire Burning Sky Palace could be his enemy?
The entire Burning Sky Dynasty was just a dynasty in a mortal kingdom, and it was as weak as a baby in front of a warrior. It only took a few words from a mighty warrior to eliminate someone and replace someone else!
All the officials in the Burning Sky Palace were dumbfounded, but none of them dared to say anything. As for the few Divine Core warriors who lived in seclusion in the Burning Sky Palace, none of them wanted to interfere …
He wanted Su Lingyun to make her own choice. Would she eliminate Su Rui and become the Empress herself, or would she go to the Central Domain and seek refuge from Luo Zheng?
However, Su Lingyun's choice surprised Shi Jingtian. She didn't choose to kill Su Rui, she didn't become the first Empress of the Burning Sky Dynasty, and she didn't leave the Eastern Region either. Instead, she returned to the Blue Cloud Sect, and returned to the Xiao Yufeng to continue her career as a teacher.
Shi Jingtian told Luo Zheng about Su Lingyun's situation after Luo Zheng left.
Luo Zheng lightly nodded his head, and a smile emerged on his face. He had to admit that Shi Jingtian had done a good job. However, the fact that Su Lingyun was willing to stay on the Xiao Yufeng surprised Luo Zheng.
Both men were communicating with each other using their True Energies. Ning Yudie asked curiously, "What are you talking about?"
"This …" An awkward expression emerged on Shi Jingtian's face. Since Luo Zheng was communicating with his True Energies, he obviously didn't want these two girls to hear their conversation. So, how could he tell them the truth? But, since the Palace Master asked, he didn't know how to answer.
Luo Zheng smiled and said, "Nothing much, we're just talking about something that happened in the Blue Cloud Sect. Xiao Die, Youqin, since you're here, you can ask Sect Master Shi to show you around. I have something I need to deal with."
Xi Youqin didn't say anything, but she was staring at Luo Zheng. When she saw the flickering look in his eyes, she immediately understood what was going on.
On the other hand, Ning Yudie was still confused and asked curiously, "What is it?"
Shi Jingtian, who was at the front, quickly said, "Hall Master, please take a seat in the Azure Cloud Hall. I will bring you around the various peaks of the Blue Cloud Sect …"
Luo Zheng found an excuse and left. Xi Youqin and Ning Yudie followed behind Shi Jingtian and went straight to the Azure Cloud Hall.
When Shi Jingtian entered the Azure Cloud Cloud Hall, a maid was arranged to welcome him. However, before he entered the hall, Xi Youqin stood at the entrance of the main hall.
Ning Yudie glanced at Xi Youqin and asked, "What are you doing?"
Xi Youqin smiled. "It's nothing. I just feel that something is wrong."
"What's wrong this time?" Ning Yudie asked again.
Xi Youqin didn't say anything. She raised her head and entered the Azure Cloud Hall.
Ning Yudie frowned. She didn't know what this girl was thinking. She moved her body and blocked Xi Youqin's path. She faced Xi Youqin and asked, "What's the matter? Is it about Luo Zheng? "
Xi Youqin knew Ning Yudie's character very well. She could see things that Ning Yudie couldn't. However, Xi Youqin didn't want to interfere with Luo Zheng's matters. She didn't dare to interfere, but Ning Yudie dared to. She kept Ning Yudie in suspense so that Ning Yudie would ask more questions.
As expected, Ning Yudie wouldn't let go of her small action.
Xi Youqin smiled. "You can say that. However, I think we should forget about it."
"What's the matter?" Ning Yudie's voice deepened.
In the hall, Shi Jingtian had asked the maids to prepare exquisite tea and pastries. He even changed the set of tables and chairs as he waited for Ning Yudie to enter the hall. At this time, Shi Jingtian was still thinking about organizing the elite disciples of the Blue Cloud Sect's 33 peaks to practice for the Hall Master to take a look.
However, the two girls were standing at the entrance of the hall. He didn't know what they were talking about.
Xi Youqin said indifferently, "Luo Zheng was born in the Eastern Region, which means he was born in a second-grade sect. Although he isn't a promiscuous person, I'm afraid there are people who are merciful. I'm wondering who that person is."
Hearing this, Ning Yudie's heart was filled with jealousy. She was different from Xi Youqin. She was a woman with a strong nature. Allowing Xi Youqin to be by Luo Zheng's side had already crossed her bottom line. Now, it seemed like Xi Youqin wasn't the first and probably wouldn't be the last.
Although she tried her best to control her emotions, her expression couldn't help but change.
Seeing Ning Yudie's uncomfortable expression, Xi Youqin smiled and comforted her. "Isn't it normal for a man to have multiple wives? It would be abnormal if he didn't have any. "
"Preposterous!" Ning Yudie rushed into the Azure Cloud Hall angrily. Shi Jingtian was about to welcome Ning Yudie to her seat when Ning Yudie angrily leaned against the huge dragon throne in the middle of the hall.
Seeing the Hall Master's angry expression, Shi Jingtian became nervous and didn't dare to speak.
As for Xi Youqin, she walked towards Ning Yudie with a relaxed expression. She sat beside Ning Yudie and suddenly said seriously, "No matter what, this is Luo Zheng's choice. He is already your husband. You can't be so angry at him."
Although Ning Yudie and Xi Youqin didn't get along well, they had been together for a period of time. Although their relationship wasn't as close as sisters, it was very harmonious on the surface. At this moment, Ning Yudie felt like they were in the same boat.
"Don't you want to go and take a look?" Xi Youqin suddenly whispered.
Ning Yudie didn't even think and immediately replied, "No!"
"You really don't want to?" Xi Youqin sat closer and closer to Ning Yudie.
"I don't want to …" Ning Yudie's attitude wasn't as firm as before. When she saw Xi Youqin's mocking expression, she said, "Let's secretly go and take a look …"
Shi Jingtian hesitated for a while. Just as he was about to speak, Ning Yudie said coldly, "Deacon Shi, where did Luo Zheng go?"
This question was difficult to answer. Shi Jingtian's face revealed a difficult expression again.
"Do you want me to go and find him personally?" Ning Yudie stared at Shi Jingtian and said.
"I don't dare. Luo Zheng went to Xiao Yufeng. I will bring you there now," Shi Jingtian quickly said.
After all, Ning Yudie was the master. Shi Jingtian would rather offend Luo Zheng than offend Ning Yudie. Furthermore, Luo Zheng was a man and would definitely be more magnanimous than a woman. Even though the Sect Master was usually swift and decisive, when it came to relationships, Shi Jingtian didn't believe that Ning Yudie would be magnanimous.
Hence, under Shi Jingtian's lead, the two ladies went straight to Xiao Yufeng.
Xiao Yufeng …
A few months ago, in order to fight against the black energy, he destroyed the mountains in the Great Thousand World. However, he managed to preserve the mountains in the Eastern Region.
If he didn't do that, the 33 mountains in the Blue Cloud Sect would have been destroyed.
After staying at the foot of the mountain for a while, he flew up!
The waist of Xiao Yufeng was where the outer disciples were located. Further up was where the mentors were located. When Luo Zheng flew past the waist of the mountain, he saw a large number of warriors gathered there.
"An arena?" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. He retracted his aura and slowly approached the arena.
On the arena, there were two black-robed disciples fighting.
"Why are the inner disciples fighting here?" Luo Zheng was puzzled. When he focused his gaze, a trace of surprise appeared on his face. "It's actually Mo Can!"
Mo Can was the first friend that Luo Zheng made after entering the Blue Cloud Sect. Compared to when Luo Zheng left, Mo Can's temperament had undergone a great change. Most importantly, he had been promoted to an inner disciple!
Mo Can's talent was average. Even his perseverance was lacking. Most of the time, he had to be encouraged by Luo Zheng along the way. Previously, Luo Zheng even fought with the head of the Mo Family because of Mo Can …
It seemed that after he left, Mo Can had put in a lot of effort. At this moment, in the arena, Mo Can was releasing waves of true energy. However, his opponent's strength wasn't weak. In fact, he was even stronger than Mo Can. However, Mo Can's perseverance was extremely strong. No matter how strong his opponent's counterattack was, he gritted his teeth and refused to take a step back!
Mo Can had indeed changed …
Luo Zheng sighed in his heart. However, after observing for a while, he frowned.

Chapter 950
That inner sect disciple's face was filled with arrogance as his attacks became increasingly fierce.
However, on the surface of Mo Can's body, a trace of green-grey aura appeared. Even if his opponent's attacks fell like raindrops, they were unable to knock him down!
That green-grey aura was extremely unique. It was likely that Mo Can had his own fortuitous encounter.
"Mo Can, do your best!"
"You absolutely cannot lose to this brat from the Lone Sky Peak!"
"Defend the dignity of our number one peak!"
Hearing the crowd's cheers, Luo Zheng faintly smiled. It was still the Lone Sky Peak!
Back then, after Luo Zheng defeated Hua Tianming and Pei Tianyao, Xiao Yufeng was ranked first. As for Hua Tianming, who came from the Lone Sky Peak, he was ranked second. Who would have thought that this situation would remain until now …
"Bang!"
After blocking countless attacks from his opponent, one of the attacks finally hit Mo Can. It directly sent Mo Can flying, rolling to the edge of the stage.
That black-robed disciple sneered as he walked towards Mo Can. However, he raised the weapon in his hand, "Are you going to get off by yourself, or do you want me to cripple you?"
Mo Can clenched his teeth as a stubborn expression surfaced on his face. The true energy within his body began to gather. Even under such circumstances, Mo Can still wanted to counterattack!
"If you don't want to give up, then I'll cripple you!" The black-robed disciple from the Lone Sky Peak sneered as he raised the weapon in his hand and was about to smash it down.
Just at this moment, a voice suddenly sounded in everyone's ears. "Enough."
A lady walked out from the crowd. That lady wore a light yellow vest with a pale yellow dress that outlined her graceful figure. Her appearance was still as beautiful as a fairy's. However, after four years, her face had lost some of its youthfulness and gained some maturity …
"Instructor … Su." Luo Zheng's gaze flickered.
Su Lingyun naturally did not discover Luo Zheng. She walked to the side of the stage and said to Mo Can, "You've already done enough. Admit defeat, Mo Can."
"But instructor Su …" Mo Can's face revealed an unconvinced expression.
Su Lingyun faintly smiled, "Perseverance is not a path to the end. If that's the case, it's not perseverance, but recklessness."
Looking at Mo Can, Su Lingyun's thoughts still drifted. After all, not everyone had the talent like Luo Zheng. perseverance was admittedly an important quality, but only when it was combined with talent could it bring out the greatest effect.
In the end, however, Mo Can gritted his teeth. He got up and charged toward his opponent.
"Luo Zheng. Since you don't admit defeat, I'll cripple you! " The inner court disciple sneered. His speed suddenly increased by several times. With a kick as fast as lightning, he swept Mo Can to the ground. Then, he raised his fist and smashed it toward Mo Can's Dantian.
Su Lingyun frowned. She wanted to stop the inner court disciple, but her speed was still a bit too slow.
At this moment, a figure suddenly flashed down from the sky and stood on the arena. With a gentle push of his hand, he gently pushed the inner court disciple back.
With just a gentle push, the inner court disciple felt an irresistible force. He retreated a dozen steps toward the other side of the stage before stopping. Then, he stared at Luo Zheng with a gloomy gaze.
As for Mo Can, he originally thought that he was going to be miserable. However, a white figure suddenly appeared. He raised his head and looked over. His face suddenly revealed an expression of disbelief.
"Luo … Luo …" For a moment, Mo Can didn't know how to address Luo Zheng.
At this moment, several inner court disciples appeared in the crowd. It was Zhang Wuxian and the others!
They naturally paid close attention to Mo Can's battle. However, they never expected that someone would suddenly appear at this time. Zhang Wuxian and Luo Zheng were naturally more familiar with each other. Even though Luo Zheng's temperament had changed a lot, they could still recognize his overall appearance.
"Luo Zheng!"
"It's really Luo Zheng!"
"Luo Zheng is back!"
At first, many of Xiao Yufeng's disciples didn't recognize Luo Zheng. However, when the first person shouted out his name, the others were immediately awakened. In an instant, the mountainside boiled with excitement!
The name Luo Zheng was the glory of Xiao Yufeng!
Any news regarding Luo Zheng in the Central Domain would be spread to Xiao Yufeng at first notice. Then, it would spread to the entire Blue Cloud Sect and the entire Eastern Domain …
"Luo Zheng!" The inner court disciple from Lone Sky Peak was overwhelmed with shock. The person in front of him was the strongest person in the Central Domain … Was he the disciple who walked out of Xiao Yufeng?
When Su Lingyun saw Luo Zheng, her body suddenly trembled. She stared at him for a moment before resolutely turning her head and walking away. She hid in the crowd.
When Luo Zheng felt Su Lingyun's movement, he pulled Mo Can up from the ground. His figure flashed and disappeared in front of everyone. He chased after Su Lingyun.
Su Lingyun wore embroidered shoes with silk flowers and quickly walked forward. As for where was in front of her, she didn't know.
"Instructor Su!" Luo Zheng caught up to Su Lingyun and shouted from behind.
Su Lingyun looked forward. Her eyes were a little hazy. She pulled her hair behind her ear and shook her head. "I'm no longer your instructor."
She had already been promoted to the Cloud Hall. Naturally, she couldn't be considered Luo Zheng's instructor.
Luo Zheng smiled. "Alright, then I'll call you Ling Yun."
"I used to be your instructor. I'm also older than you. How can you call me by my name?" Su Lingyun, who had her back facing Luo Zheng, said indifferently.
"Then … Sister Ling Yun?" Luo Zheng's face was suffused with a smile. His expression also relaxed.
Su Lingyun didn't reply.
When Shi Jingtian entered the palace and kicked Su Rui off the throne, Su Lingyun had two guesses in her heart.
It was either Luo Zheng or her father, Su Qian, had returned.
Very quickly, Su Lingyun got her answer. She had heard of Luo Zheng's deeds in the Central Domain. That brat wasn't inferior to the geniuses of the fourth and fifth grade sects. With his Divine Reflection Realm strength, he suppressed all the geniuses in the Central Domain.
At that time, Su Lingyun had many choices in front of her. Shi Jingtian's attitude was very positive. No matter what she wanted, Shi Jingtian would do his best to cooperate. He was even willing to help her ascend the throne and rule the world.
However, she gave up.
Su Lingyun returned to Xiao Yufeng and silently took up the role of a mentor.
In fact, Su Lingyun knew deep in her heart that she was waiting. As for what she was waiting for, she didn't know. Or rather, she didn't dare to think about it.
The martial path was long. At this time, Luo Zheng had stepped onto a brand new level. A level that she couldn't reach or even hope to reach.
However, she was still waiting. She placed all her energy on Little Rain Peak to find even more talented disciples and give them the greatest help. She wanted to strive for a better result. Only in this way would she have more patience.
Four years wasn't a long time. She didn't expect Luo Zheng to return. In these four years, she heard that Luo Zheng had offended the experts of the Central Domain. She also heard that Luo Zheng had gone missing. She also heard that Luo Zheng had returned. She also heard that Luo Zheng was looking for a famous doctor. She also heard that Luo Zheng had left the Central Domain for a place that was far beyond her imagination to become the Hall Master of the Cloud Hall …
Excitement, worry, hope, relief, and relief. The Eastern Domain and the Central Domain were too far apart. These news couldn't be transmitted smoothly. Therefore, each emotion would accompany Su Lingyun for a long time until the next news came. Then, it would be replaced by another emotion.
Today, Luo Zheng had returned. Was she waiting for him? Su Lingyun's heart was in a mess. She couldn't think and didn't know the answer.
At this time, Su Lingyun's body suddenly trembled.
A pair of hands wrapped around her from behind. Luo Zheng walked up and hugged her.
"Let, let go of me …" Su Lingyun looked nervous.
She was a teacher after all. Even though she wasn't much older than Xiao Yufeng's disciples, many of the disciples on the mountainside were looking in their direction. As a teacher, how could she be seen by Xiao Yufeng's disciples?
When Luo Zheng heard Su Lingyun's words, he hugged her from behind. After a long time, he said in a relaxed manner, "Sorry, I came back late …"
He promised to come back. He would definitely come back.

Chapter 951
Ning Yudie pouted and revealed an unconvinced expression. "He's fickle in love. He's not a good person!"
Seeing Ning Yudie's expression, Xi Youqin smiled. Seeing a certain someone's jealousy was a happy thing. It even offset the bitterness in Xi Youqin's heart. She continued to tease, "In the end, Luo Zheng knew this girl first!"
"Yes, he's fickle in love. He's not a good person." Ning Yudie became more and more aggrieved.
Seeing her like this, Xi Youqin continued, "If Luo Zheng wasn't a good person, he could've just left and didn't have to come back. It's because he's loyal and righteous that he kept his promise …"
"He's not a good person. He's not a good person!" Ning Yudie became anxious and tears welled up in her eyes.
Seeing Ning Yudie's expression, Xi Youqin quietly reached out her hand towards Ning Yudie.
"What are you doing?!" Ning Yudie took a step back and avoided Xi Youqin's hand.
However, Xi Youqin still took a step forward. She grabbed her sleeve and wiped away the tears on Ning Yudie's face. While wiping, she said, "He's not a good person. He's still our husband."
Ning Yudie was stunned. In the end, she could only sigh. She looked at Luo Zheng with reddened eyes. As the saying goes, husbands are enemies. Luo Zheng would probably be her enemy for life.
As for Shi Jingtian, he was floating in the distance. He looked at the Temple Master, then looked at Luo Zheng and Su Lingyun. He sighed in his heart. This Luo Zheng was too f * cking powerful. No wonder he was from the Blue Cloud Sect!
Although it was normal for cultivators to have three wives and four concubines, Shi Jingtian had dozens of concubines. Luo Zheng paled in comparison to him.
But that also depended on who he married!
Shi Jingtian wasn't sure of the identity of the elegant girl beside the Palace Master. However, he knew that her background wasn't low. She was most likely from a prestigious family. Furthermore, Ning Yudie was the leader of the largest sect in the Central Domain! What kind of status does she have? How could she be compared to an ordinary woman?
However, it was Luo Zheng after all. He had just gotten married and he had returned to find Su Lingyun. Shi Jingtian could only admire the Palace Master from the bottom of his heart when he saw how angry the Palace Master was. He couldn't do anything to Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng comforted Su Lingyun for a long time. After the two of them talked, Su Lingyun finally shifted the topic to her Imperial Father.
As the Deacon of the Cloud Hall, Su Qian had disappeared while carrying out his mission. Until now, there had been no news about him. The Cloud Hall didn't seem to have any news about him either.
At this time, Luo Zheng's mind moved. He could search for Su Qian's orientation in the memories of the World Will. The memories included all the information about the Great Thousand World. As long as it was a creature from the Great Thousand World, there wasn't anything left out!
With this thought, Luo Zheng instantly obtained what he wanted. His face revealed a trace of silence. Then, he said to Su Lingyun, "Your father … should have passed away in a relic in the Central Domain."
"Is that so?" Su Lingyun's eyes flickered. In fact, after disappearing for so long, she already had this premonition. However, because there had been no news about him, she only had a trace of hope in her heart. Although she didn't know how Luo Zheng knew about it, she believed in Luo Zheng's words.
"I will ask the Cloud Hall to send someone to bring your father's bones back," Luo Zheng continued.
Su Lingyun nodded. Compared to Ning Yudie, she was more reserved. This time, Luo Zheng let go of her hand. She deliberately distanced herself from Luo Zheng.
On the other side, Xi Youqin saw that Ning Yudie's emotions had stabilized. She took her hand, but Ning Yudie's hand suddenly shrank back. "What are you doing!"
"Let's go over and say hello," Xi Youqin said with a smile.
"No!" Ning Yudie said stubbornly.
"In the end, you will have to get to know him. How can you avoid it?" Xi Youqin replied.
"…"
After thinking about it, Ning Yudie could only helplessly follow behind Xi Youqin and fly to the mountainside.
Xi Youqin's psychological burden was indeed much lighter than Ning Yudie's. She had a belief in her heart that no matter how many women Luo Zheng had, the one who would accompany Luo Zheng in the end would be her. Therefore, she was much more relaxed now. "John Doe" said it well. Let's see who will have the last laugh!
Xi Youqin and Ning Yudie slowly flew over. Luo Zheng's face revealed a somewhat unnatural expression. Luo Zheng had no idea how to deal with the relationship between the three women. He could only brace himself and introduce them to each other.
Fortunately, Xi Youqin gracefully greeted Su Lingyun, while Ning Yudie stood at the side. The three women sized each other up, and their eyes revealed an indescribable feeling …
As for these feelings, of course, they were all different. However, this scene could be considered harmonious, and it made Luo Zheng feel that he had passed by luck.
Luo Zheng stayed in the Blue Cloud Sect for two days. It was said that Mo Can had used his own strength to regain his position in the Mo Clan, and Zhang Wuxian had now officially taken over the Zhang Clan's business. As such, everyone's situation was different, but overall, it was smooth sailing.
After that, Luo Zheng took the road to the south and returned to the Luo Clan in Chongyang County.
The current Luo Clan was already a force that could not be ignored. Although it was not located in the Burning Sky Imperial City, its current growth was faintly showing signs of surpassing the seven major clans. There was only one reason for this, and that was Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng rose and fell in the Central Domain, and even disappeared for two years. However, no one could guarantee that he would not return. If someone really provoked the Luo Clan, there was no guarantee that Luo Zheng would suddenly return one day. That would be a disaster that would wipe out the entire clan!
Without other clans holding them back, the Luo Clan's power rapidly expanded. In Chongyang County, there was no other clan that could match up to them.
Luo Zheng's return was naturally a joyous occasion for the Luo Clan. With this return, Luo Zheng also left behind some treasures and cultivation techniques.
Of course, Luo Zheng did not dare to keep anything that was too valuable. An ordinary man's wealth was his own ruin. The Luo Clan could not keep anything that was too valuable. Instead, it would lead to a disaster. However, the weapons and cultivation techniques that were very common to Luo Zheng were extremely precious to the Luo Clan …
Luo Zheng also knew that on the path of martial arts, the further one walked, the higher one climbed. Although family ties were important, in a long life, the inheritance would gradually be diluted by time. Next, he needed to concentrate on his cultivation and prepare for his ascension. He did not know how many years it would be before he returned to the Eastern Domain again. He needed to do his best to arrange some things properly.
However, the Eastern Domain was originally the world of the Blue Cloud Sect, and the Blue Cloud Sect was controlled by the Cloud Hall. Even if Luo Zheng did not say a word, the Blue Cloud Sect would do their best to take care of the Luo Clan so that the Luo Clan would not suffer losses. However, if the Luo Clan wanted to give birth to a character, it would still depend on the efforts of the younger generation in the clan.
Several days later, Luo Zheng left the Luo Clan. However, he took a path to the west …
The three women were curious. This time, after returning to his hometown, Luo Zheng should be rushing back to the Central Domain. Why did he take a path to the west?
Luo Zheng kept them in suspense and pretended to be mysterious. He told them that he would take them to a secluded place …
In the end, they arrived at the Nine Yin Mountain, Jade Dragon Valley. That was the Immortal Mansion that Luo Zheng had entered before.
The origin of this Immortal Mansion was very mysterious. Even now, Luo Zheng found it hard to guess. Daoist Tian Miao had also entered the Immortal Mansion with Luo Zheng.
Even though Daoist Tian Miao's strength was at the Extreme Divine Realm, he still couldn't control the Immortal Mansion. He only had one more token than Luo Zheng. That was the "Zhen" token he gave Luo Zheng. Together with the "Xun" token in Luo Zheng's hand, he had two Immortal Mansion tokens.
This Immortal Mansion was no small matter. According to Daoist Tian Miao, this Immortal Mansion didn't have a name. Moreover, in the entire universe of one hundred thousand Upper Realms, no one was qualified to give this Immortal Mansion a name!
Perhaps Daoist Tian Miao's words were very exaggerated, but Luo Zheng understood that regardless of whether it was exaggerated or not, the secret of this Immortal Mansion must be explored before he ascended. Now that Luo Zheng had broken through the Life and Death Realm, he didn't know whether he could obtain more secrets or even control this Immortal Mansion.
"Is this the place?" Ning Yudie looked at the empty valley and asked curiously. She really couldn't think of anything in the Eastern Domain that was worth Luo Zheng's attention.
"Follow me!" Luo Zheng walked towards a corner of the valley. Then, with a flash of green light, Luo Zheng disappeared between two bamboos.
