Chapter 952
Seeing Luo Zheng's figure suddenly disappear, a curious look flashed across Su Lingyun's face. "Is this … a teleportation array?"
There weren't many teleportation channels built in the Eastern Region. The only one was built in the Blue Cloud Sect. It was the teleportation array that led to the Blue Cloud Road. Moreover, it had been in disrepair for a long time.
To teleport a person, it would consume a top-grade True Origin Stone. Even Shi Jingtian was reluctant to use it.
Therefore, Su Lingyun was a little surprised.
As for Ning Yudie and Xi Youqin, their expressions were indifferent. In the Central Domain, teleportation arrays were common. Even some rich mortal merchants would sometimes choose to use teleportation arrays to transport goods. Although the cost was high, it was safe and convenient.
Luo Zheng pretended to be mysterious. However, in Ning Yudie's eyes, even if there were any secrets in this small Eastern Region, they were not worth mentioning.
However, since her husband was leading the way, she was willing to accompany him wherever he went.
However, as soon as Ning Yudie stepped into the two bamboos, a white light flashed and she suddenly found herself in an unfamiliar place, causing a strange look to appear on her face.
"This teleportation array doesn't seem to have a spatial channel, and I don't feel the power of the Space Law. This is a little strange," Ning Yudie muttered.
Then, Xi Youqin and Su Lingyun also passed through the two bamboos and entered this world.
Looking over, the three women were stunned by the scenery ….
"These trees are so huge. Is this the world of giants?" Su Lingyun looked at the trees that were as tall as mountains. Her face was filled with shock.
At first glance, the surrounding scenery looked very normal, but upon closer inspection, everything around seemed to have been magnified countless times. Not to mention the huge trees, even the weeds under the trees were comparable to the size of a house. It was as if this place was a huge transformed world.
Seeing the shocked expressions of Ning Yudie and the other two girls, a faint smile appeared on Luo Zheng's face. When he first entered this place, his strength was only at the Xiantian realm. At that time, he was scared out of his wits. Moreover, at that time, there was an unbelievably huge White Tiger on the other side. Fortunately, there was a barrier blocking it, allowing Luo Zheng to escape.
"Where is this place?" Xi Youqin also asked curiously. In fact, from the bottom of her heart, it didn't matter where this place was. However, seeing that Luo Zheng seemed to want to give them a surprise, Xi Youqin of course had to 'suit' the occasion and ask.
As for Ning Yudie and Su Lingyun, they were really curious. They looked at Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng smiled faintly and said, "Follow me!" After saying that, he was about to lead the way.
Just as he took two steps, he suddenly heard Xi Youqin and Su Lingyun scream. Ning Yudie's body erupted with true energy. She looked behind her with extreme vigilance. Behind the barrier, dozens of majestic leopards appeared!
Like the white tiger that Luo Zheng encountered, these leopards were extremely huge. Just the fangs they revealed when they opened their mouths were already as tall as a person!
"Are there wild beasts again?" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. He said, "Don't worry, these wild beasts can't come in. Once they touch the barrier, they will die!"
As he spoke, he sized up these leopards. At that time, Luo Zheng was only a Xiantian warrior. Naturally, he couldn't distinguish the white tiger's cultivation level. However, now that Luo Zheng had broken through the Life and Death Realm, they couldn't be mentioned in the same breath.
However, after sizing up these leopards, he was shocked. He still couldn't detect the cultivation level of these leopards!
According to the division of cultivation level, a tenth level vicious beast was equivalent to the Life and Death Realm, an eleventh level vicious beast was equivalent to the Spirit Sea Realm, and a twelfth level vicious beast was equivalent to the Extreme Divine Realm experts …
With Luo Zheng's current eyesight, he could distinguish the cultivation level of a Spirit Sea Realm expert, or even the Extreme Divine Realm expert. However, Luo Zheng still couldn't see the cultivation level of these leopards. This could only mean one thing. The cultivation level of these leopards was still beyond Luo Zheng's imagination.
It seemed that the location of this immortal mansion, as well as this world, was still at a level that Luo Zheng couldn't fathom.
No wonder even with Daoist Tian Miao's strength, he couldn't truly control this immortal mansion. He only had an extra token with the word "Zhen" on it.
"Roar … Roar …"
Those leopards let out deep roars and slowly approached the barrier.
When Su Lingyun and Xi Youqin heard Luo Zheng's words, they didn't look so nervous. The two girls firmly believed Luo Zheng's words. If he said it was safe behind the barrier, then it must be safe.
As for Ning Yudie, her face was still a bit pale. Of course, she also believed Luo Zheng's words. However, the higher her cultivation level, the more she could sense the strength of these vicious beasts. These huge leopards actually had cultivation levels that she couldn't see through. How could Ning Yudie not be surprised?
On the other hand, Luo Zheng's expression was indifferent. If these leopards dared to pounce, their final fate would be the same as the white tiger three years ago. They would become nothing more than a corpse.
However, these leopards circled back and forth outside the barrier. They seemed to be very afraid of this barrier. In the end, they stayed outside the barrier and roared helplessly. They didn't choose to throw away their lives.
The spiritual intelligence of vicious beasts and their strength didn't have a linear relationship. Some vicious beasts had very low cultivation levels, but their spiritual intelligence was abnormally high. They were even more intelligent than humans. There were also some vicious beasts that were incomparably strong, but their spiritual intelligence was still in an undeveloped stage.
These leopards were a bit smarter than the white tiger that threw away its life. However, it seemed that they weren't that smart. Otherwise, they wouldn't have stayed in front of the barrier and wasted time.
"Let's go!" Luo Zheng went up and grabbed Ning Yudie's hand.
Seeing this scene, Xi Youqin ran over with a smile. "I want it too!" She took the initiative to grab Luo Zheng's hand.
As for Su Lingyun, she just smiled faintly and silently followed behind.
As they walked forward, an immortal mansion appeared in front of them. The gate of the immortal mansion was thousands of feet high. Even a giant as big as a mountain could pass through it without a hitch.
"What a huge immortal mansion!" Ning Yudie said in amazement.
"The person who lives in this immortal mansion must be really tall …" Xi Youqin sighed.
Luo Zheng smiled faintly and took the lead to step through the gate. Not long after they walked along the spacious square, the ground suddenly began to tremble.
"Dong dong dong dong …"
Following this, two enormous puppets dashed out from the inner courtyard!
Seeing such huge puppets, the three women gasped softly.
"Husband …"
"Luo Zheng …"
However, Luo Zheng instantly took out a token from his pocket. It was the "Xun" token he received from Afo when he left the immortal mansion. Afo said that this "Xun" token would allow him to control the outer court and let him enter without a hitch.
It was just a simple token. Nominally, he was the owner of this immortal mansion, but Luo Zheng didn't have any authority …
The moment Luo Zheng took out the token, the two huge puppets suddenly slowed down. Although they were still walking, it was obvious that they had no intention of attacking.
"Dong dong dong …"
The two huge puppets actually walked up to Luo Zheng and knelt down on one knee.
Luo Zheng nodded slightly, then called out to Ning Yudie and the others and continued to walk into the inner court.
In fact, with Luo Zheng's current strength, these puppets couldn't hurt Luo Zheng at all. It was just that he had the immortal mansion's token, so he naturally wouldn't waste any effort in front of these puppets.
After entering the inner court, they saw a huge pond in front of them. Countless koi fish were swimming in the pond.
With the token in hand, Luo Zheng led the three women straight in. They walked directly into the huge pavilion, then moved the statues in the pavilion at will and arranged them according to a certain pattern. It was easy to pass through the pavilion.
Then, Luo Zheng's voice sounded. "Luo Zheng, long time no see. I've been waiting for you for four years."
Not far away stood a middle-aged man in a brocade robe. It was Afo, whom he hadn't seen for several years.
This Afo was a puppet, but hidden in the puppet was a genuine soul!
Luo Zheng smiled and nodded to Afo. "Long time no see."

Chapter 953
As soon as Afo said this, Ning Yudie's eyes widened in shock.
In four years, he reached the Life and Death Realm from the Innate Mystic Realm, and he only said "not bad"?
This kind of speed could be said to be rare in the world, alright?
At least as far as Ning Yudie knew, no genius in the Central Domain had ever achieved this kind of speed!
How high was this Afo's standards?
However, what he said next surprised Ning Yudie even more. Afo sighed and said, "I can see that although you've only reached the Life and Death Realm, your foundation is far more stable than ordinary warriors. Your strength is comparable to the Divine Sea Realm. But it's still not enough. You can't break the barrier of True Extinction, and you can't walk the Path of True Extinction. Although I'm a little disappointed, you should cultivate for a few more years before coming back!"
"Not enough …" Ning Yudie was speechless. Was this still the Eastern Domain? Even Life and Death Realm cultivators weren't qualified. What kind of inheritance was this?
Hearing Afo's words, Luo Zheng wasn't upset at all. He smiled and said, "What if I had this token?"
Then, Luo Zheng took out another token from his Sumeru Ring. The word "Zhen" was engraved on the token in Luo Zheng's hand.
Luo Zheng got this token from Daoist Tian Miao. Daoist Tian Miao had already passed the Five Decay of Heaven and Man, and only left a wisp of his soul. It was useless for him to have this token, so he gave it to Luo Zheng. He told Luo Zheng that with this token, he could open the Path of True Extinction.
This Immortal's Mansion was of great importance. Before going to the Upper World, Luo Zheng had tried his best to control this Immortal's Mansion. Luo Zheng had no choice but to take a trip to the Real Impasse.
Afo glanced at the token in Luo Zheng's hand and said with a wooden expression, "That's Tian Miao's token. He … should have already died. I didn't expect him to give you the 'Zhen' token. With the 'Zhen' token, you don't need to open the True Extinction Barrier and can directly enter the True Extinction Path. But with your strength, even if you enter the True Extinction Path, I'm afraid you won't be able to pass the first stage."
"What are the benefits of passing the first stage?" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed as he asked.
Afo answered, "There are eight tokens in total: Qian, Kun, Kan, Li, Zhen, Xun, Gen, and Dui. Apart from the Qian and Kun tokens, the other six tokens can all be obtained."
"Six tokens? But I already have the 'Zhen' and 'Xun' tokens. What if they're the same … "Luo Zheng asked curiously.
Afo smiled faintly, "That depends on your luck. If they're the same, then they're the same."
Luo Zheng's face showed a trace of helplessness, and then he asked, "But what's the use of the other tokens?"
Afo waved his hand, "The 'Xun' character goes to the front courtyard, allowing you to enter the Immortal's Mansion at will. The 'Zhen' character opens the True Extinction Path, allowing you to enter the True Extinction Path. The 'Dui' character smells the medicine chest, allowing you to open the alchemy room. The 'Li' character cleaves the weapon, allowing you to control the weapon-refining workshop. The 'Kan' character is all-knowing, allowing you to enter the library. The 'Gen' character …"
With this answer, Luo Zheng suddenly understood. These six characters could gradually control everything in the Immortal's Mansion, but each character could only correspond to one thing.
He didn't know why the first owner of the Immortal's Mansion set it up like this. It was really a bit difficult!
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng opened his mouth again, "Then what's the use of the two characters' Qiankun '?"
Afo continued to smile faintly, "Ask me again when you gather the first six characters. I won't tell you now."
Luo Zheng shrugged. He also knew Afo's duty. If he didn't want to answer, he wouldn't answer no matter how many questions he asked. No matter what, he had to take the True Extinction Path. When he discovered the Immortal's Mansion, he didn't get anything except the Sun, Moon, and Stars Map.
As for the weapon-refining workshop, alchemy room, library … Don't even think about it. Even the current Luo Zheng couldn't force his way in.
Now that Luo Zheng had to prepare for ascension, he certainly couldn't back down.
"Is the True Extinction Path dangerous?" Xi Youqin asked worriedly. Although she knew that even if there was danger, Luo Zheng wouldn't back down at all, she still asked.
Afo replied, "Whether the True Extinction Path is dangerous or not depends on Luo Zheng himself …"
After all, the greater the reward, the greater the risk. This was the case every time Luo Zheng went on an adventure. If there were no risks, wouldn't everyone be able to get it? If everyone could get it, how good would it be?
Luo Zheng was about to let Afo guide him into the True Extinction Path, but at this time, an idea flashed in his mind. He said, "Last time I passed the trial, can others also choose to take the trial?"
Ning Yudie had her own cultivation method. She visualized an ice phoenix, but this didn't mean that the visualization diagrams in the Immortal Mansion were useless to Ning Yudie and Xi Youqin. If the three women could also enter the trial and get their own visualization diagrams, that would be great.
Afo nodded and said, "Yes, but they won't be able to get the token."
Luo Zheng was now the nominal owner of this Immortal Mansion. Only he was qualified to get the token. The previous owner of this Immortal Mansion was Taoist Priest Tian Mo. When Luo Zheng entered the mansion, Taoist Priest Tian Mo had already passed the Five Decay of Heaven. In fact, he was already dead. Only a wisp of his soul remained. Therefore, Luo Zheng was qualified to get the token. Now, unless Luo Zheng died, no one else was qualified to be recognized by Immortal Mansion again.
"Okay, I understand." Luo Zheng nodded seriously. He looked back at the three women. He asked Afo for an answer, but he didn't know if the three women would agree.
Sure enough, the three women had different reactions.
After Ning Yudie learned about the opportunity in the trial, she was very enthusiastic. As the beloved daughter of the Cloud Hall, someone as outstanding as her wasn't afraid of any trial or challenge!
As for Su Lingyun, a hesitant expression emerged on her face. Among the three women, she had the lowest cultivation base, and was only at the fourth level of the Divine Reflection realm. Although she was willing to participate in the trial, she didn't have the confidence to pass. Afo told Su Lingyun that the difficulty of the trial would be adjusted according to one's cultivation base, so she didn't need to worry too much.
The last one was Xi Youqin. Right now, Xi Youqin was as strong as two people. Although she was indeed a Divine Core warrior, if she didn't use the abilities of the Extreme Purple Realm, perhaps even Su Lingyun would be no match for her … Her cultivation base was all accumulated with resources, and her foundation wasn't stable at all!
"Can I use external forces?" Xi Youqin asked.
Afo threw a glance at Xi Youqin. "You seem to have the Heavenly Purple Body, and there are nodes hidden in your body that allow you to connect to the outside world …" After saying that, Afo shook his head. "You can't use external forces."
When Afo mentioned the Extreme Purple Yin Body, it seemed like he had changed the way he described it. Xi Youqin didn't care about the Heavenly Purple Body. Since she couldn't use external forces, she might as well give up on it. With her current strength, she wouldn't be able to pass the trial.
Xi Youqin had chosen to give up on the trial, and Luo Zheng couldn't persuade her either. Under Afo's lead, Ning Yudie and Su Lingyun were the first to go through the trial.
The trial didn't last long. After half an hour, the two girls actually passed!
However, Ning Yudie's expression was calm, and she looked very relaxed. Su Lingyun, on the other hand, was very reluctant. Even after leaving the illusion used for the trial, her face was still pale. It was only after Luo Zheng gave her a calming pill that she calmed down.
Ning Yudie obtained the Flying Birds in Clouds Diagram, which was second only to the Sun, Moon, and Stars Diagram.
However, her talent and strength were already very strong, so it wasn't surprising that she obtained this result. As for Su Lingyun, it was surprising that she obtained the third ranked Nine Heavens Reincarnation Diagram!
Seeing that Ning Yudie and Su Lingyun both obtained their own visualization diagrams, Xi Youqin felt a little unbalanced. However, she knew that if it wasn't for the Extreme Purple Yin Body, her strength wouldn't be enough. When she entered the Indefinite Tower in the future, she would cultivate well with Indefinite Old Man. She didn't want Ning Yudie and Su Lingyun to look down on her.

Chapter 954
"This wall is the True Precipice Barrier," Afo said while looking at the pale yellow wall.
Luo Zheng stepped forward and touched the wall. The path ahead was blocked by this barrier. According to Afo, if they wanted to go to the True Precipice Barrier, they had to open it.
However, Luo Zheng had the "Tremor" token, so he was qualified to pass through the True Precipice Barrier.
Looking at the pale yellow wall, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed, "Can I really not break through this barrier?"
"You can try," Afo smiled.
However, Ning Yudie, who was at the back, suddenly said, "Can I try?"
"No problem," Afo said.
Seeing Ning Yudie's interest, Luo Zheng smiled and stepped aside.
Ning Yudie was also competitive. She and Luo Zheng were both 1st layer Life and Death realm. In Ning Yudie's eyes, there might be a gap between her and Luo Zheng, but the gap shouldn't be too big. She wanted to test it out.
After everyone stepped aside, Ning Yudie lightly stepped forward and stood in front of the True Precipice Barrier. She gazed forward, and the Zhen Yuan in her body began to surge.
"Yo!"
Ice crystals began to condense, and an ice phoenix appeared beside Ning Yudie.
Fu said indifferently, "The Nine Heavens Fairy Heart Sutra is not a bad technique. However, this technique cannot be fully mastered. It can be considered incomplete."
Hearing Afo's words, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed, and Ning Yudie's heart also jumped.
In the Central Domain, Ning Yudie's cultivation method was definitely top tier. However, Afo's standards were unimaginably high. To be able to get "not bad" from Afo's mouth, this Nine Heavens Fairy Heart Sutra was probably an extremely powerful cultivation method. However, he said that it was incomplete?
This Nine Heavens Fairy Heart Sutra was a cultivation method that Ning Yudie's master, Granny Yu, passed on to her. As for the origin of this cultivation method, even Granny Yu didn't know, let alone Ning Yudie.
"Incomplete?" Ning Yudie asked curiously.
Afo nodded again, "Yes, this Nine Heavens Fairy Heart Sutra isn't complete. I've read thousands of cultivation methods in the library, so there's no mistake."
Even if it was incomplete, there was no way. Ning Yudie could only grit her teeth and persevere.
Thinking of this, Ning Yudie's finger lightly twirled, and the ice phoenix coiled around her arm. Then, under her gaze, a cold aura emitted from her body!
"As expected of the Hall Master of the Cloud Hall, such a powerful aura!" Su Lingyun exclaimed. Ning Yudie's strength was indeed much higher than hers. It was a realm that Su Lingyun couldn't even imagine.
As for Xi Youqin, she snorted faintly. She only said in her heart, "What's so great about that? Wait until I enter the Unpredictable Tower, then …"
The ice phoenix in Ning Yudie's hand spiraled faster and faster, and the cold aura it emitted also became more and more piercing. The temperature within several zhang of her also plummeted.
Xi Youqin and Su Lingyun couldn't bear the cold aura emitted, but Luo Zheng casually waved his hand, and a Protective True Yuan spread out, shielding the two behind him.
Then, Ning Yudie's figure suddenly flashed, and the fist in her hand was aimed at the True Precipice Barrier …
"Rumble …"
The fist struck, and a snow-white imprint spread out on the True Precipice Barrier. However, after the imprint appeared, it didn't spread. Instead, it began to condense and shrink, and finally disappeared.
Looking at Ning Yudie's fist, Luo Zheng nodded. Because the fist struck the True Precipice Barrier, it didn't seem to have a strong effect. In fact, the might of Ning Yudie's fist was something that no one in the Central Domain could withstand. Even Granny Yu and a few Life and Death rogue cultivators couldn't deal with it.
She only broke through the Life and Death realm after dispersing her cultivation nine times. This strength was indeed astonishing!

Chapter 958

So many Life and Death experts besieging Luo Zheng, and these experts were proficient in all kinds of Law Powers. This was indeed a huge test for Luo Zheng.
Su Lingyun muttered softly. She knew that she couldn't change anything, so she could only pray silently in her heart.
Xi Youqin took a step forward and raised her hand.
"What can you do?" Afo asked indifferently.
"Break this True Path of Despair!" Xi Youqin didn't know how to reason with others. She wasn't as rational as Ning Yudie.
However, before Xi Youqin could make a move, Afo said, "Although the Heavenly Purple Body is powerful, you can't use your spatial node here!"
How could Xi Youqin listen to this guy? She moved her fingers slightly and tried to communicate with the giant swords in the Extreme Purple Realm. However, after circling her hands a few times, there was no reaction at all. Just as Afo said, she couldn't communicate with the Extreme Purple Realm here. The spatial node had failed!
Now, she was helpless. Watching Luo Zheng struggle in all kinds of Law Powers, her expression became more and more unsightly!
Thinking of this, she was about to jump into the True Path of Despair …
"What are you doing?" Ning Yudie shouted coldly and grabbed Xi Youqin.
If Xi Youqin jumped into the True Path of Despair before, she wouldn't be in too much danger. However, the experts in the True Path of Despair were all Life and Death experts. How could Xi Youqin fight against them?
"But Luo Zheng …" Xi Youqin said worriedly.
Xi Youqin was indeed smarter than Ning Yudie in some aspects, such as guessing Luo Zheng's thoughts. Her EQ was much higher than Ning Yudie's, but in terms of martial arts, she was far inferior to Ning Yudie. Now that Xi Youqin had entered the True Path of Despair, she was simply courting death.
Fortunately, Xi Youqin's strength was far inferior to Ning Yudie's. No matter how she struggled, she couldn't escape from Ning Yudie's control.
However, Su Lingyun said indifferently, "Don't worry."
Xi Youqin looked at Su Lingyun and snorted, "Of course you're not worried about Luo Zheng's life …"
"No," Su Lingyun shook her head, "I believe in Luo Zheng. He has the strength to fight back!"
Ning Yudie also nodded, "Luo Zheng's limit is far beyond this. Don't listen to this guy," Ning Yudie pointed at Afo.
The three women didn't have a good impression of Afo. However, Afo didn't care. He was still indifferently watching Luo Zheng's every move on the True Path of Despair.
"Boom …"
When the various Law Energies hit Luo Zheng's body, a violent light burst out.
Seeing this, the three women clenched their fists tightly, and their faces were filled with nervousness.
After the light dissipated, Luo Zheng's body was still standing, but the clothes on his body were annihilated by the energy flows …
"This … this kid … seems to have transferred his power?" Afo was surprised again.
As for Xi Youqin and Su Lingyun, they breathed a sigh of relief and smiled. They didn't care about the process, nor did they want to know how Luo Zheng resisted these attacks. They only knew that their husband was incomparably brave and could remain unscathed under such attacks.
Ning Yudie's eyes were filled with shock. "Energy transfer? How did he do it?"
"I can't tell for now," Afo said faintly. "He seems to have fused with some kind of will. Maybe it's a spiritual object, and he transferred his power to that spiritual object..." Even Afo couldn't tell that Luo Zheng had fused with the will of the entire Vast Thousand Domains. He had just transferred his power to the Divine Essencefused Mountain. Anyway, an attack of this level wouldn't be able to shake the Divine Essencefused Mountain at all.
Relying on the transfer of power, Luo Zheng rushed forward again. No matter how the Sacred Race people used all kinds of methods, Luo Zheng didn't stop for a moment!
Not long after, Luo Zheng felt that the path ahead gradually became clear, and Afo and the three girls were actually right in front of him.
When Luo Zheng stepped on the True Path of Extinction, the three girls were behind him, but he didn't expect to return to the starting point at the end of the path. This surprised Luo Zheng a little.
"Whoosh!"
Luo Zheng jumped out of the True Path of Extinction and said to Afo with a smile, "Did I pass?"
"The first stage passed," Afo said faintly.
"Where's the token?" Luo Zheng asked.
Afo pointed behind Luo Zheng. Luo Zheng turned his head and looked. The yin and yang energies above the ridge slowly floated and condensed. At the same time, the ridge disappeared and a token slowly descended from the air.
The three girls also looked up. Ning Yudie said faintly, "It's the word 'Kan'."
A token with the word 'Kan' engraved on it slowly floated down and landed in Luo Zheng's hand. Luo Zheng looked at it and heard Afo say, "With this token, you can enter the library and read the books!"
"Library!" Luo Zheng's eyebrows suddenly raised.
Everything in this immortal mansion was no small matter. For Luo Zheng at this stage, other things might not be that important, but the library was definitely indispensable!
Afo smiled faintly, "Your strength is stronger than I thought. Even a Divine Sea Realm expert wouldn't be able to survive the siege of so many Life and Death Realm experts of the Sacred Race. But you seem to have transferred your strength to a spirit item. If you're confident in this spirit item, you can challenge the second stage of the True Path of Extinction."
Afo thought that Luo Zheng had a spirit item, but the spirit item itself didn't exist. Of course, the entire great thousand domains could be regarded as Luo Zheng's "spirit item" …
The carrying capacity of a great thousand domains was still quite terrifying. Even if some experts in the universe could destroy the creatures in the Central Domain, it was basically impossible for them to destroy the entire great thousand domains.
Luo Zheng also couldn't let the great thousand domains bear too much of a terrifying attack. Last time, in order to fight against the black eye, Luo Zheng destroyed all the mountains in the great thousand domains. This time, Luo Zheng transferred his strength to the Divine Essencefused Mountain.
"Of course!" Luo Zheng smiled faintly.
Afo nodded, and the fog below dispersed again. In front of Luo Zheng, the True Path of Extinction opened again!
"The second stage is more difficult than the first. Be careful," Afo said, "But since you can transfer your strength, as long as your spirit item can bear it, there shouldn't be any danger …"
So Luo Zheng once again rushed into the True Path of Extinction without hesitation.
At this time, Xi Youqin turned her head and smiled at Su Lingyun, "Okay, you can turn around now …"
Su Lingyun turned her head, her face still full of shyness. Luo Zheng's clothes were all gone, so she just turned her head shyly. As for Ning Yudie and Xi Youqin, they naturally weren't as shy as Su Lingyun …
The second round of slaughter took an hour. The strength of the saint martial artists had also risen to a new level. Although they didn't use any cultivation methods, the martial artists manifested from the yin and yang energies all had a cultivation at the Divine Sea realm. Even the power of their Laws had risen to the third or even fourth level!
Fortunately, these guys didn't know how to use Domains. In the Chaotic Astral Ocean in the south of the Eastern Domain, the Divine Essencefused Mountain completely absorbed these forces.
Luo Zheng focused on sprinting on the True Path of Extinction, so he didn't have the mind to investigate the Divine Essencefused Mountain …
Even if the Divine Essencefused Mountain was invulnerable, there was a limit to the power it could withstand. Under the attacks of dozens of Divine Sea Realm experts of the Sacred Clan, just how terrifying was the power contained within?
The Divine Essencefused Mountain, which was mostly at the bottom of the sea, already had a shallow crack at the bottom, and that crack was constantly expanding.
"Done!" Luo Zheng passed the second stage of the True Path of Extinction. On the other side, he saw Su Lingyun suddenly turn around. Seeing Su Lingyun blushing, Luo Zheng smiled, then took out a set of clothes from his spatial ring and put them on.
As the vast fog descended, another token appeared in the sky.
This time, it was a "Gen" token.
"What's the use of this token?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.

Chapter 959
Afo's puppet body was created by the first master of Immortal's Mansion. He didn't have the Five Decay of Heaven and Man, so the puppet couldn't collapse. In other words, he couldn't commit suicide even if he wanted to. His soul should be firmly sealed inside, and he couldn't disperse the power of his soul …
For Afo, guarding Immortal's Mansion was a boring job. Death was an extravagant hope for him.
If someone could stay in Immortal's Mansion, it would be a good thing for him.
The previous masters had all tried to take control of Immortal's Mansion, but they all failed. For example, Taoist Tian Miao. Afo had also hoped for Tian Miao's return, but he still died from the Five Decay of Heaven and Man …
Now, Luo Zheng was slowly taking control of Immortal's Mansion. Not only did he get the "Kan" token, but he also got the "Gen" token!
"Carry Immortal's Mansion with me? Could it be that the immortal abode can fly away? "Luo Zheng asked curiously.
Afo nodded, then shook his head. "The" Gen "token is a special one among the six. It's a projection of Immortal's Mansion itself. It's equivalent to the gate of Immortal's Mansion. Of course, you can also treat it as Immortal's Mansion. If you carry it with you, you can return to Immortal's Mansion at any time." Afo was in a good mood. After thinking for a while, he added, "You can treat it as that woman's Heavenly Purple Body. It's a space node." Then Afo pointed at Xi Youqin.
"I see." Luo Zheng was enlightened. In this way, if he wanted to return to Immortal's Mansion in the future, he wouldn't have to travel thousands of miles to the Eastern Region. He could enter directly with the "Gen" token in his hand.
"Do you want to continue?" Afo asked again.
In a short time, Luo Zheng had already obtained two tokens. But this time, Luo Zheng chose to give up.
In the next round of the True Path of Extinction, a large group of Extreme Divine Realm experts would most likely appear. Although Luo Zheng could completely transfer his power, the problem was that there was a limit to the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain's capacity. If a large group of Extreme Divine Realm experts used the fifth layer of Source Laws, the power would be unimaginable!
If the Divine Essencefused Mountain were to be destroyed, the overflowing energy would damage the foundation of the entire Great Thousand World. Luo Zheng was unwilling to take this risk. Moreover, now that he had the "Gen" token, he could wait for his strength to increase in the future before returning to the Immortal Mansion to obtain the other tokens.
Now that he had a "Kan" token in his hand, he smiled and said, "Can we head to the library now?"
"No problem," Afo nodded.
At this time, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly, and he asked, "Can other people enter?" Luo Zheng was naturally referring to Ning Yudie and the others.
"Possessing the medallion is equivalent to being the master of the Immortal Abode. It's up to you to decide whether or not they can," Afo said.
"Understood!"
Previously, although Luo Zheng was the "master" of the Immortal Mansion, he couldn't control anything at all. The "Xun" token was at most a ticket to enter, while the "Zhen" token gave him the qualifications to challenge the True Path of Extinction.
Now that he had the "Kan" and "Gen" tokens, his control over the immortal estate was increasing step by step. There would be a day when he would be able to obtain the other four tokens, or even the two Qiankun tokens!
Thus, everyone turned around and began to walk back. However, halfway through, a violent and vicious aura suddenly erupted from the depths of the Immortal Mansion!
"Roar …"
At the same time, a strange cry was transmitted.
Even though they were separated by such a great distance, that vicious aura still made people feel abnormally terrified!
Luo Zheng's face darkened, while Ning Yudie's face turned deathly pale. As for Xi Youqin and Su Lingyun, they directly fainted …
"What is this!" Luo Zheng coldly asked Afo. After that, he helped Su Lingyun up, while Ning Yudie went to help Xi Youqin.
That vicious aura wasn't released from the front, and it was quite far from Luo Zheng, but it still made Luo Zheng extremely fearful. Then what kind of terrifying existence was this?
Afo's expression changed, then said indifferently, "There's a guy who's already impatient …"
"Where did that sound come from?" Luo Zheng asked.
"The doghouse," Afo answered, then continued, "Luo Zheng, you've obtained the" Kan "token, so you can go to the library yourself. I'm going to take a look …"
After that, Afo didn't wait for Luo Zheng's response, his figure flashed, and his whole body flew towards the depths of the Immortal Mansion!
In the depths of the Immortal Mansion, there was an exquisite backyard. In a corner of the backyard, there was a chain that bound a ferocious beast. The ferocious beast was lying on the ground, motionless.
Afo walked towards the fierce beast expressionlessly, then said, "What's the hurry?"
"Roar …" The fierce beast's voice was much softer, but its head was still roaring towards a deep pit next to it.
"Are those guys in the prison not being obedient?" Afo said, then walked towards the deep pit. At the bottom of the pit, there was a row of fences, and under the fences was absolute darkness.
Afo said indifferently, "You've already endured so many years, what's the harm in waiting a little longer?"
No one in the pit answered.
Seeing this, Afo continued, "That young man is very talented, and his luck isn't bad either. He actually obtained four tokens, and none of them were repeated. Most importantly, he has the" Gen "and" Kan "tokens, so in the future … he might be able to obtain the two Qiankun tokens."
Still, no one in the pit spoke.
"I know you don't think highly of him, but this time it might really be different. Trust me, it's only a matter of a thousand years at most. We've endured countless years, and this little bit of time is just an instant to both of you. Be patient," Afo said again.
Seeing that there was no response, Afo turned to leave, but just as Afo was about to turn around, a voice came from the pit. "Then call him in, I'll train him. I guarantee that it won't take a thousand years, just a hundred years. He'll definitely become the strongest in this universe, and crush those bullshit Celestial Sovereigns."
Afo didn't turn around, he just shook his head and said, "Master's rules can't be broken. If he can't obtain the two Qiankun tokens, he can't enter here …"
As soon as he finished speaking, he paid no further attention to her and directly left the backyard ….
Next to the fence in the pit sat a long-haired middle-aged man in ragged clothes. He looked at the top of the pit and muttered, "Let's not talk about one or two thousand years, even if it's one or two hundred million years, I'm willing to wait. The problem is that those monsters in the lower levels of the prison can't wait that long!"
Luo Zheng didn't know what was going on here.
He and Ning Yudie woke up Xi Youqin and Su Lingyun, then gave them some calming pills before heading to the library.
The door to the library was naturally very tall, and it looked like a huge heavenly palace in front of Luo Zheng!
"Is the 'Kan' token enough to open this door?" At this point, Luo Zheng took out the "Kan" token in his hand. The moment Luo Zheng took out the token, a golden light flashed from the middle of the door. With a "Kacha" sound, a crack appeared on the door.
Luo Zheng jumped up and tried to push the door open. Originally, Luo Zheng looked at the mountain-like door and estimated that it would take some effort to push it open. Unexpectedly, just as his hand touched the door, the door opened with a loud bang. Immediately after, a scholarly smell assailed his nostrils!
Naturally, the books did not have any fragrance. The books in the Book Collection Pavilion must have been made from special wood. After countless years, the natural fragrance would precipitate out. When one sniffed it, it would even cause one to feel intoxicated.
"Let's go in!" Luo Zheng smiled at Ning Yudie and was the first to enter the library. Ning Yudie, Xi Youqin, and Su Lingyun followed closely behind.
Even though countless years had passed, the library was still spotless. In front of Luo Zheng, there were many bookshelves that were as big as a small mountain. As for the books in the bookshelves, each book was three feet tall and two feet wide...
"How do I read this?" Looking at the huge books, Luo Zheng's face revealed a depressed expression.

Chapter 960
Although Luo Zheng was depressed, he had entered the library with great difficulty. How could he be willing to return empty-handed?
With a leap, he jumped onto the first row of bookshelves!
The covers of these books were made of some kind of metal. They were very cold to the touch and were incomparably sturdy. Although Luo Zheng couldn't identify the material of these pages, he was certain that they were more than enough to refine tools.
He grabbed one side of this huge book and used all of his strength to pull. Unexpectedly, this big book didn't move an inch!
"This book is actually so heavy?" Luo Zheng's complexion suddenly changed.
His strength was enough to tear apart mountains and split the earth. He didn't expect this book to be so heavy!
He was born with divine strength, yet he couldn't even pull out a book from the library. Luo Zheng's face revealed an unconvinced expression. This time, the power of the dragon scales in his body suddenly erupted. He grabbed the corner of the book with both hands and used all his strength to finally pull the book out of the bookshelf.
However, while the three women were still looking up, they suddenly saw a book the size of a house crashing down. Their faces revealed astonishment.
Luo Zheng's complexion changed greatly. He shouted loudly, "Quickly dodge!"
He didn't know how heavy this thing was, but Luo Zheng estimated that it definitely wasn't lighter than a mountain range. It was almost equal to the weight of twenty-three mountains added together. If this time, even Ning Yudie wouldn't be able to escape, let alone Xi Youqin and Su Lingyun.
Fortunately, Ning Yudie's reaction was extremely fast. She gently stretched out her hands and grabbed Xi Youqin and Su Lingyun, one on the left and one on the right. Then, she quickly retreated!
Almost at the same time, the house-sized book directly fell to the ground!
It was unknown what material the ground was made of. When such a heavy thing fell, it didn't even leave a hole. It was as if it was an ordinary book that was thrown on the ground …
However, this immortal mansion had existed for an unknown number of years. If it was made of ordinary stone, how could it withstand the erosion of time? Most likely, it would have already turned into ashes. However, anything that could withstand the erosion of time was definitely not an ordinary object.
Luo Zheng jumped down and stood on the ground. His face revealed a trace of apology.
As for Ning Yudie, she fiercely glared at Luo Zheng. She somewhat blamed Luo Zheng for his recklessness. However, Luo Zheng just now understood that Ning Yudie could hold the two women. Otherwise, he could use the law of time to save the three.
After removing the book from the bookshelf, Luo Zheng walked to the edge of the book and forcefully flipped open the first page of the book.
Just the cover, the weight of the book's weight.
The moment Luo Zheng opened the big book, he saw rows of light golden words appear on the first page.
After Luo Zheng saw these words, his eyes suddenly flashed. Each word gave Luo Zheng a kind of invisible pressure. These words were twisted and distorted, like strange runes. However, Luo Zheng was also knowledgeable. He understood that these runes weren't divine runes, but something else …
After staring at the big book for a while, Luo Zheng's face revealed a trace of a helpless smile. "I don't understand …"
As for Ning Yudie, Xi Youqin, and the others, they also gathered around. Even with Luo Zheng's experience, he couldn't understand the words on the book. In their eyes, it was even more so. How could they understand the slightest bit of it?
It wasn't easy to get a "Kan" Command Token, but it was a Chinese character. Luo Zheng was a bit confused, but after thinking about it carefully, it could be considered normal.
After all, under the development of civilization, there were many different kinds of cultures. Almost every civilization had its own special language. Even those super powerful people couldn't understand all the words.
However, at this moment, on another bookshelf, a big book that was placed horizontally unexpectedly opened by itself. Clouds of smoke rose from the book, and slowly, an amiable-looking old man appeared from within the smoke.
After the old man appeared, he walked towards Luo Zheng. As he walked, the smoke drifted away, as if he was riding on the clouds, giving people a feeling of immortality.
"It's a good book, so why would I open it and read it? I'm afraid you can't even recognize a single word … "The old man said indifferently.
Luo Zheng's face was filled with curiosity as he asked, "Did I open it the wrong way?"
The old man nodded, then said, "Close the book, I'll teach you how to open it …"
In that case, Luo Zheng did as he was told. But when he closed the book, Luo Zheng asked again, "Who are you?"
At this moment, Afo had already rushed over. When he heard Luo Zheng's question, he directly replied, "He's the Book Immortal, a familiar who manages the library." After that, Afo smiled at the Book Immortal and said, "Old man, long time no see!"
The Book Immortal looked at Afo indifferently, but he snorted, as if he was very dissatisfied with Afo, "After so many years, you only opened the library now. Master entrusted you to guard it, let's see how you do it …"
Fu, on the other hand, had a helpless expression on his face. It was not that he was unwilling. It was just that the previous candidates he had looked for were too weak. The third "Mansion Master" had disappeared without even opening the True Path of Extinction. As for the fourth "Mansion Master", Tian Miao, although he had opened the True Path of Extinction, he had only obtained the Zhen Token. He had not passed through the first True Path of Extinction.
The Book Immortal didn't seem to want to argue with Afo. As a familiar of the library, his job was to serve the people who entered the library. He walked up to Luo Zheng and said kindly, "There's a house of gold in a book. Every book has its own universe, look …"
After that, the Book Immortal pointed at the book and a golden spiral pattern appeared on the cover. The Book Immortal slowly said, "Pour your perception into the book and you'll be able to read it. This book is called the Primal Chaos Herding Record. It records the master's travels in the endless chaos …"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng did as the Book Immortal said and poured his perception into the golden pattern. The moment Luo Zheng did so, a huge ball of light suddenly appeared in his mind. As the light slowly dissipated, everything around him began to float rapidly. In Luo Zheng's mind, a strange world appeared!
Standing in the void, Luo Zheng looked around, his eyes full of surprise. "This … is the world recorded in the Primal Chaos Herding Record?"
The world in this book was completely chaotic. Even Luo Zheng himself found it hard to describe this kind of space. It wasn't absolute darkness. There was a faint light in the surroundings, but it was shrouded in layers of gray fog. The surroundings were filled with black and white fog. Luo Zheng had already seen this fog before. It was the Yin and Yang Qi that he had seen on the True Path of Extinction …
"Boom …" A lightning bolt so large that Luo Zheng couldn't understand it streaked across the chaos. "This lightning … contains the power of law. I can't understand it at all!"
The Law of Thunder contained in the lightning bolt was in a perfect state. It was a perfection that Luo Zheng couldn't understand. Compared to this lightning bolt, Luo Zheng couldn't bear to look at the incomplete lightning bolts he had seen before. This wasn't just a comparison of power, but a comparison of the perfection of the Law. Although Luo Zheng couldn't see how many layers of Law the lightning bolt contained, he estimated that it was very likely to be at least ten layers …
Luo Zheng's vision was moving at an extremely high speed. He couldn't control his own vision. It was probably moving along with the vision of the author of the Primal Chaos Herding Record. Luo Zheng could only passively accept the amazing scenes recorded in the book.
After a long time, Luo Zheng withdrew from the book. His eyes were full of shock.
If Luo Zheng's guess was correct, this chaos … was the world outside the universe. What existed outside the universe? What did those grotesque scenes represent?
Luo Zheng didn't look for answers in this book. The author of this book had traveled an unknown distance in the chaos. But outside the chaos, there was still chaos, as if it was an endless world …

Chapter 961
Luo Zheng withdrew his perception from the book and found that Ning Yudie, Xi Youqin, and Su Lingyun had also released their perception. They were standing beside the book in a daze. They were probably also wandering in the book …
After a long time, the three girls also withdrew from the book. Their eyes were filled with endless shock.
Like Luo Zheng, they couldn't understand the book. However, the world shown in the book still shocked them. It was as if they had touched upon the truth and origin of the entire world. It was breathtaking.
The Book Immortal seemed very satisfied when he saw their expressions. As the familiar of the library, he had read almost all the books in the library. As the saying goes, sharing with others brings endless joy. Over the years, he had wandered in the ocean of books and read the most exquisite parts. His heart was filled with joy. However, he had no one to share the essence of the book with. It was impossible for him to find someone to recommend a good book. One must know that sharing a good book with others was also endless joy.
Of course, he knew that the people in front of him were shocked by the chaotic world. The Book Immortal was very happy. "This' Chaotic Cloud Herding Record 'is just a drop in the ocean in the library. The things left behind by the previous owner are the essence. If you want to read them one by one, it will take you many years to finish. Come, come, come … I will recommend a few more books to you."
Luo Zheng, Ning Yudie, and Su Lingyun had yearning expressions on their faces. However, Xi Youqin curled her lips and said, "It's just a hazy world. What's so good about it? I don't think the other things are good either! "
"Oh?" The Book Immortal wasn't happy when he heard Xi Youqin's words. It was as if the things he held as a standard were trash in the eyes of others. The Book Immortal couldn't tolerate this and asked, "Little girl, what are you talking about? The collection of books in this Book Collection Pavilion is as vast as the clouds and mist. There will definitely be books that are suitable for you. I will be able to recommend any books that you like! "
Xi Youqin had just flipped through the Primal Chaos Herding Record. Although she was shocked, she didn't like reading books. When she heard that the book immortal was going to recommend books for her, she turned her head and said with a smile, "I'm afraid there aren't any books here that I like to read."
"Hmph!" The Immortal of Books snorted. His amiable face was now filled with defiance. "How can a human kid like you understand the Mansion Master's work? Tell me what you like. If I can't pick one, I'm not worthy of the title 'Immortal of Books'!"
Seeing the Immortal in the Book's displeasure, Luo Zheng said to Xi Youqin, "Youqin, watch your mouth."
Unexpectedly, the Immortal of Books explained to Luo Zheng, "It's okay, it's okay. I'm not angry. I'm just not convinced. Little girl, tell me!"
Xi Youqin blinked her eyes and said with a smile, "I like reading stories about romantic nights. I don't think there are any here …"
Everyone was speechless. Luo Zheng could only shake his head with a bitter smile. Ning Yudie's face was wooden. She had already guessed that Xi Youqin would make some strange request. As for Su Lingyun, she covered her mouth and giggled.
In Xu Ling City, where Xi Youqin lived, there were many scholars. Some of them would write some "leisure stories" when they were free. Although these leisure stories were not presentable, they were circulated among many women. Some of them were extremely emotional and made some women cry.
When she was in the Xi family, when she had nothing to do, she would send some people to buy some articles to pass the time. From this, it could be seen that Xi Youqin did not set aside any time to cultivate martial arts...
As for Ning Yudie and Su Lingyun, one was a Temple Master and the other was a teacher of the Blue Cloud Sect. If they were not busy with cultivation, they were dealing with business. How could they have the time to read these things?
Hearing Xi Youqin's request, the Immortal of Books was stunned. Then, he raised his head and laughed. He said, "Little girl, come with me!"
"There really is one?" Xi Youqin asked.
The Immortal of Books did not answer her question. Instead, he led her to a bookshelf at the side. With a light tap of his finger, a huge book slowly fell from the shelf.
Seeing the Immortal of Books' ability, Luo Zheng's eyebrows suddenly jumped. The Immortal of Books could take the book effortlessly. The weight of this book was equivalent to a mountain range. Yet, he could take it down so easily! This immortal mansion revealed a terrifying strength everywhere. Even a demonic familiar in charge of the library had a strength that Luo Zheng could not imagine.
Under the guidance of the Immortal of Books, Xi Youqin stood in front of the book. Soon, her perception was immersed in the book …
Luo Zheng, Ning Yudie, and Su Lingyun were curious. They followed the Immortal of Books and entered the world inside the book.
After a long time, everyone exited the book.
At this time, Xi Youqin's eyes were filled with tears. Her little nose was still sobbing. Her eyes were immersed in the story inside the book.
Su Lingyun's expression also revealed a mournful expression. She was also moved by the story recorded in the book.
Even Ning Yudie revealed a thoughtful look. In fact, she had immersed herself in the Martial Dao and had never come across such a sentimental story before, so the impact of this story on Ning Yudie should be the greatest. However, her Martial Dao Heart was extremely firm, so although she was moved, it was not to the extent where it was difficult for her to control herself like Xi Youqin.
As for Luo Zheng, his face was full of helplessness. He was confused by the story and did not feel the slightest bit of emotion toward the various bizarre plots recorded in the book.
Xi Youqin sobbed as she threw herself into Luo Zheng's arms. "The woman called Zan Feng in the book is too pitiful. I won't read this kind of story in the future. Huhuhu …"
"Hehe, little girl, do you want to read more?" The Book Immortal's face revealed a complacent expression.
Xi Youqin wiped away her tears. Then, she said in surprise, "Do you have more?"
Seeing Xi Youqin's reaction, Luo Zheng was speechless again.

Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. He didn't expect that it would be so difficult to walk along the footprints. He couldn't even move a single step.
The power of 5000 dragon scales was already close to Luo Zheng's limit. Just now, when he poured this power into his legs and tried to pull them up, he felt a burning sensation in his legs. If he used more strength, his legs would probably break.
"If I can't pull them out, won't I be nailed to these footprints for the rest of my life?" Thinking of this, Luo Zheng tried to retreat.
Unexpectedly, as he retreated, his feet were lifted up easily. Because he used too much strength, Luo Zheng staggered back a few steps and almost fell to the ground.
After steadying himself, Luo Zheng looked at the footprints. His eyes gradually calmed down.
At this time, Book Immortal said, "Hehe, it's in line with Master's usual style. Although I don't know why Master set it up like this, we'll understand what he means by 'difficult to advance and easy to retreat'!"
Difficult to advance and easy to retreat …
Although it was extremely difficult to advance, it was extremely easy to retreat. This principle was simple, but how many people in history could actually do it? Most people understood this principle, but when it came to a critical moment, they would still retreat in the face of difficulties.
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng's eyes flickered. His eyes were filled with determination. He stepped on the footprints again!
The three women didn't say anything.
They knew that they shouldn't say anything now, but they were still slightly worried.
On the other hand, Xi Youqin looked at Luo Zheng in a daze. She was extremely happy with Luo Zheng's serious expression. At this time, men were the most charming.
Luo Zheng's feet were once again firmly glued to the footprints. Since the Palace Master had set up such a path, it was impossible for it to be completely unbreakable. This time, Luo Zheng was fully prepared. First, he relaxed his body. Then, he took a deep breath!
Since five thousand dragon scales couldn't move his feet, then he would use six thousand dragon scales!
After breaking through the Life and Death realm, Luo Zheng's cultivation base had increased, and the amount of dragon scales he could bear had also increased. However, when he reached the limit of 6000 dragon scales, his body had reached its limit!
Under this sudden increase in size, bloody wounds appeared on his body again.
"Puff, puff, puff …"
Three bloody wounds appeared on Luo Zheng's chest and back. Blood shot out like arrows.
"Rise!"
Luo Zheng tried to lift one of his feet.
"It still doesn't work …"
"Six thousand one hundred dragon scales!"
"Puff!"
The fourth bloody wound appeared.
"Rise again!"
His feet still didn't move.
"Six thousand two hundred dragon scales!"
"Puff!"
Still didn't move …
"Six thousand three hundred dragon scales …"
Six thousand four hundred dragon scales …
Six thousand five hundred dragon scales …
Six thousand six hundred dragon scales …
As the power continued to increase, more and more bloody wounds appeared on Luo Zheng's body.
The Book Immortal's face revealed a strange expression. "Where did this kid get such a powerful power? This power has already surpassed the limit of his body … "
Afo shook his head, his expression indifferent.
As for the three girls, their hearts were filled with more and more worry. They resisted the urge to say anything! Su Lingyun's heart was also filled with regret. If she had known earlier, she wouldn't have reminded him about the footprints on the ground. But then again, even if she hadn't said anything, Luo Zheng would have found out sooner or later.
"Six thousand seven hundred dragon scales! Rise! "
This time, Luo Zheng finally kicked his right foot! The moment his right foot stepped out, it took a step forward and firmly sucked on the footprints in front of him.
Luo Zheng forcibly held back this power and lifted his left foot!
"Step forward!"
Because the power was suppressing all the blood vessels in Luo Zheng's body, the small blood vessels in his eyes burst one after another. Therefore, his eyes looked completely red, as if they were bloodshot!
One step …
Two steps …
Three steps …
In fact, Luo Zheng could display his strongest combat strength by keeping it at five thousand dragon scales. If he forcibly raised it to six thousand dragon scales, his body would be close to its limit. Just suppressing the violent power in his body would give Luo Zheng a headache!
Therefore, Luo Zheng's speed was extremely fast. He was half as fast as an ordinary person's walking speed. It was as if he was walking while carrying a mountain!
Four steps, five steps, six steps …
With each step, Luo Zheng's body would burst open with a bloody wound. At the same time, a blood arrow would shoot out from his clothes.
One of the blood arrows shot toward Su Lingyun. Fortunately, Ning Yudie reacted quickly. She waved her hand and an ice crystal shield blocked the blood arrow. The power of the blood arrow alone made a dent in the ice crystal shield condensed by Ning Yudie. Looking at the dent on the shield, Ning Yudie's eyes were filled with horror.
What kind of terrifying power was Luo Zheng's body …
Su Lingyun stretched out her hand and touched the dent on the ice crystal shield. When the blood arrow hit the ice crystal shield, it was frozen by the cold air. Touching the blood mark, Su Lingyun was stunned and speechless.
Seven steps, eight steps, nine steps …
The limit …
Luo Zheng suddenly exhaled. That breath was also stained with Luo Zheng's blood. It spurted out a red mist — his internal organs couldn't withstand this powerful force and were injured.
"Huchi, huchi …"

Chapter 966
At this moment, Xi Youqin took the initiative to step forward. She took out a white silk handkerchief and wiped the sweat off Luo Zheng's face.
After wiping twice, the handkerchief was already soaked with sweat and blood!
Xi Youqin winked at Luo Zheng and gently wiped his face. She smiled and said, "Good luck!"
Compared to Ning Yudie and Su Lingyun, Xi Youqin wasn't too worried. Or rather, she didn't think too much about it. On the contrary, she admired Luo Zheng's struggling appearance the most. This was the time when a man was most charming.
Luo Zheng nodded and prepared to take out the elixir. However, Ning Yudie had already prepared a milky white elixir and handed it to him.
After receiving it from Ning Yudie, he swallowed it. The elixir from the Cloud Hall was naturally not bad. Moreover, although Luo Zheng's injuries looked terrible, they weren't that serious. Moreover, Luo Zheng's recovery speed was far beyond that of ordinary people. Under the stimulation of the elixir, Luo Zheng's wounds quickly healed. After the time it took for an incense stick to burn, other than the tears on his clothes caused by the blood arrows, he was completely healed!
At this moment, Luo Zheng finally figured out what was going on with these footprints. Not to mention Luo Zheng, even if those almighty experts of the Divine Ocean Realm or the Ultimate Divine Realm personally stepped on this path, their situation would probably be the same as his!
This path should be a test of Luo Zheng's extreme strength. Only by squeezing himself to the limit could he move forward on this path!
Retreat? That was easy. He could easily leave by taking a step back. However, all his previous efforts would be wasted!
"The thing I'm least afraid of is a test!" Although looking at the distance of several thousand steps ahead of him, Luo Zheng still felt a little dizzy. However, he didn't have the slightest intention of retreating.
"Go!"
The majestic power of the dragon scale once again poured into Luo Zheng's body. As arrows of blood shot out, he once again took a step forward.

Ning Yudie was worried that these pills wouldn't be enough to support Luo Zheng to cross this path.

Su Lingyun said, "I still have some elixirs here …"
Ning Yudie's eyes flashed, "Those pills of yours are useless."
Su Lingyun's face reddened. Naturally, the pills she had couldn't compare with the pills of the Cloud Hall's Hall Master. The best pill Su Lingyun had was only grade five. As for the pills in Ning Yudie's hands, they were grade seven and grade eight pills. In the Central Domain, they were the best pills. Just one pill alone was probably equivalent to the annual expenses of the Blue Cloud Sect.
"It's all the same! I can get through it. Besides, I still have some spare pills! "Luo Zheng smiled faintly.
The Book Immortal didn't say anything, but Afo shook his head and said, "It's a pity that the alchemy room hasn't been opened yet. I can't get the pills for you this time! But then again, the alchemy furnace in the alchemy room has been brewing for countless years. I remember that when the master left, there was a furnace of pills that hadn't been refined. I don't know how the alchemy room is doing now … "
Hearing this, Luo Zheng smiled. Generally speaking, the longer the alchemy, the better. In fact, if the alchemy room was brewed for too long, the furnace might explode.
However, the power of the master of the Immortal Mansion was naturally incomparable. If he could open the alchemy room in the future, he would benefit immensely.
Of course, this wasn't something he wanted to do at this stage. There was still a difficult road ahead of him!
Luo Zheng didn't stop for too long. When the wounds on his body had just healed, he took a deep breath and activated the power of more than six thousand dragon scales. He continued to move forward!
As he moved forward, the path behind him was filled with dense bloodstains. As for the white robe on his body, it was already riddled with holes. After being completely dyed red, it was a pure blood-stained robe!
Luo Zheng had no choice but to change his robe. However, after less than a hundred steps, it was also covered in blood.
After a full two hours, Luo Zheng finally reached the middle of the bookshelf. The Book Immortal couldn't help but shake his head. "This test set by the master is too cruel."
Afo also agreed. "Even if the master wanted to test Luo Zheng's limit, he didn't need to set so many footprints … One or two hundred steps are enough. Why bother with three thousand steps? Ordinary warriors can't bear it at all!"
As long as one was willing to grit their teeth, everyone could fight to the limit. Especially when between life and death, warriors could often display extraordinary potential. The problem was that in these thousands of steps, almost every step had to uncover one's limit. This was too much of a test of one's willpower.
Although Luo Zheng had already walked half the distance, they doubted whether Luo Zheng could bear it.
Luo Zheng's face was getting paler and paler …
Even if Luo Zheng had the body of a saint tool, and even if Luo Zheng had many high-grade pills to heal himself, the blood in his body was not infinite. Along the way, he had lost count of how much blood he had spurted out. He felt more and more tired, and his legs felt like they were filled with lead. Even with the support of the dragon scale's power, he found it difficult to take another step.
One thousand seven hundred steps …
But he had already walked more than half the distance. It would be a pity to give up now. Luo Zheng was so tired that he didn't even bother to think. In front of him were only the shallow footprints.
The remaining footprints extended to the front of the huge book. Each of the footprints looked like a demon, mocking Luo Zheng on the ground.
Even if he gritted his teeth, Luo Zheng was still weak. This was the manifestation of his strength being exhausted …
"Huchi, huchi …" Standing in place, Luo Zheng's eyes were a little sluggish.
Ning Yudie and Su Lingyun looked at Luo Zheng's appearance and felt very distressed. But other than encouraging Luo Zheng, they didn't know how to help him.
At this moment, Xi Youqin walked forward with light steps and hugged Luo Zheng. The blood on his body hadn't dried yet, and it soaked her chest red. Xi Youqin sniffed, and a sweet fishy smell rushed into her nose. She said, "What a strong smell of blood!"
"What are you doing?" Luo Zheng was almost leaning on Xi Youqin's body.
"Nothing, I'm just encouraging you!" Xi Youqin said with a smile.
Luo Zheng smiled, "Are you afraid that I'll give up?"
Xi Youqin shook her head, but her face was full of confidence, "Of course not. How could my husband give up so easily?"
In the depths of Xi Youqin's heart, there was still a big grudge against the Dual Cultivation Ceremony in the Cloud Hall. It was just that she didn't show it. She was thinking about holding an even grander Dual Cultivation Ceremony with Luo Zheng when she went to the Upper Realm in the future. She wanted to invite all the almighty figures in the universe. They had to be people with a reputation, like "Nameless" and "Indefinite". Not only that, she wanted to invite all the Celestial Venerables!
That Dual Cultivation Ceremony would definitely be a thousand times, ten thousand times grander than the one he and Ning Yudie had …
She only knew about the title of Celestial Venerables after she became Indefinite Old Man's disciple. In fact, she didn't realize what kind of person a Celestial Venerate was. If the creatures in the Upper Realm heard about it, they would probably be scared out of their wits.
"Of course I won't give up!"
After resting for a while, Luo Zheng started walking again.
Although there was no danger on this road, this was probably the most strenuous test Luo Zheng had ever taken. After all, with every step, Luo Zheng had to squeeze his limits. There were more than 3,000 footprints here, and Luo Zheng had to squeeze himself 3,000 times.
Even if it was a holy weapon, under such repeated squeezing, it would probably become slag …

Chapter 967
Luo Zheng always believed that the more effort he put in, the more he would get in return!
However, if he reached the end and found out that it was just a prank left by the Mansion Master, Luo Zheng would accept it!
He had always been lucky, but it was impossible for a person to have a smooth journey all the way. Luo Zheng knew that, so he could only hold on to the belief in his heart and keep moving forward.
However, this path could only be described with the word "tragic".
In the second half of the journey, Luo Zheng rested longer and longer, and it became more and more difficult for him to move forward. He spent most of his time resting!
The good thing was that there was no time limit on this path. After two thousand steps, Luo Zheng's rest time was calculated in hours.
After two thousand five hundred steps, Luo Zheng rested for one day and one night for every one hundred steps he took!
The patience of the three women was naturally very good. In any case, being able to follow their husband was a happy thing for them. It was the same wherever they were.
As for Afo and the Book Immortal, they had been in this world for too long … For them, this little bit of time was like the blink of an eye!
One hundred steps a day.
It took Luo Zheng seven days to complete the remaining seven hundred steps!
After seven days, Luo Zheng's right foot finally stepped on the last footprint …
At the same time, a ray of light suddenly burst out from behind Luo Zheng!
At the starting point of the footprints, a faint figure appeared. That figure quickly walked along Luo Zheng's footsteps. The distance of three thousand steps was covered in an instant. Then, the figure brushed past Luo Zheng and walked toward the huge book.
When the figure walked toward the huge book, he gently turned the book over. The cover of the huge book that couldn't be opened was suddenly opened!
"It's open!" The Book Immortal saw the figure. "Is this … Is this the Master?"
Fu also stared at the shadow for a long time, but he couldn't figure it out. "Maybe it is, maybe it isn't …" The shadow was too blurry, and there was no way to identify it. Maybe it was just a shadow left behind by the master.
The moment the book was opened, everyone saw a door on the first page of the book!
It was a door drawn on the book. The drawing of the door was very lifelike, but it was just a drawing after all.
However, at this moment, the figure walked towards the door and reached out his hand to touch the drawing of the door. He gently pushed the door, and the drawing moved on its own. The originally tightly shut door opened just like that.
"This is so fun! The drawing can move!" Xi Youqin said with a smile.
Ning Yudie glanced at Xi Youqin indifferently. Although she also thought that the drawing was amazing, she was very displeased when this girl added the word "fun" to everything. After all, Luo Zheng had put in a lot of effort to open the door.
After the door on the painting was opened, a pitch-black space appeared behind the door. The figure took a step into the space and pointed at Luo Zheng as soon as he took a step in.
"Let me in?" Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows.
That figure nodded his head.

"I'm going in too!" Xi Youqin said with a smile.
"Absolutely not!" The Book Immortal beside her quickly stopped her.
"Do you want to die?" Ning Yudie said angrily. Although the immortal mansion was not dangerous, it was strange everywhere. Luo Zheng was qualified to enter because he had to go through this path. One could imagine what would happen to Xi Youqin if she barged in like this!
Xi Youqin clicked her tongue and said with a smile, "I'm just kidding!"
Ning Yudie rolled her eyes and ignored Xi Youqin. As for Su Lingyun, she pursed her lips and snickered at the side.
Luo Zheng followed the figure into the painted door. This step was indeed a different world. However, just as Luo Zheng entered, the drawing began to move again, and the originally open door slowly closed.
As the door closed, the light from behind disappeared in an instant. Luo Zheng was immersed in darkness and couldn't see anything.
Just as Luo Zheng was hesitating, a voice rang out in this dark space. "You can pass my Extreme Path. Your perseverance can be considered a talent. However, you don't know what is in this book. What is it that supported you to pass this path?"
Suddenly hearing this question, Luo Zheng was slightly stunned. This question was somewhat difficult to answer. After thinking for a while, Luo Zheng said with a smile, "I just thought that since this path is so difficult, the reward shouldn't be bad …"
"Frank enough! This old man likes it. Hahaha! "
Amidst the maniacal laughter, the darkness disappeared in an instant, and a seven-colored light appeared in the surroundings. The light flowed slowly, outlining countless irregular patterns. The figure who had opened the book previously stood in front of Luo Zheng.
"This cultivation method this old man created must be cultivated before breaking through to the Divine Sea Realm. Once the Divine Sea is opened in the dantian, the embryonic form of the Inner World will be formed. At that time, it will be too late to exchange the Primal Chaos for True Origin …" the figure said indifferently.
Indeed …
The book immortal was indeed very knowledgeable. He knew the Palace Master very well!
The book immortal knew that the Palace Master had spent a lot of time to create a cultivation method. However, the book immortal didn't know why the Palace Master couldn't cultivate this cultivation method.
It turned out that the prerequisite for cultivating this cultivation method was to not open the Inner World!
Life and Death Realm experts had to liquefy their dantian. When Luo Zheng was in the late stage of the False Tribulation Realm, his True Origin had begun to liquefy. After breaking through the Life and Death Realm, it even began to solidify! However, he hadn't broken through to the Divine Sea Realm, so the Inner World hadn't been formed yet.
"However, this cultivation method I created is just an inference. After all, this cultivation method is just an embryonic form. I'm afraid that you will have to comprehend and explore on your own in the future. It's very possible that … the end of this cultivation method is a dead end, a dead end! However, it's also possible to open a new path. Do you dare to cultivate it? "the figure asked Luo Zheng again.
According to the Palace Master's words, he hadn't finished compiling this cultivation method. He had only deduced it based on his profound knowledge. Up until now, even he himself hadn't succeeded in cultivating this cultivation method …
There were many such cultivation methods in the entire universe. Even in the Central Domain and the Eastern Domain, there were many of them.
To put it bluntly, this was just a concept!
For instance, someone in the Central Domain had proposed the concept of 'Stellar Body Forging'. Perhaps this concept was correct. For instance, Luo Zheng's Stellar Battle Body was able to draw upon the power of the stars. It wasn't impossible to use this to temper one's body. But, that didn't mean that a martial artist from the Central Domain could compile a transcendent divine might like the Stellar Battle Body!
The gap between concepts and reality was often enormous. This enormous difference was enough to reach the point of being completely unrelated …
Of course, the disparity between the people of the Central Domain and this Palace Master was simply unimaginable. This Palace Master's supernatural powers had already surpassed the universe, and might even surpass the Heavenly Dao. This wasn't something that the average person could hope to achieve. According to his deductions, the concepts he deduced were naturally much more reliable!
From his words, Luo Zheng could hear a strong self-confidence!
Thus, Luo Zheng didn't hesitate. He immediately nodded and said, "I dare!"
The figure faintly smiled. "Very good. Then kneel!"
"Kneel?" Luo Zheng's eyebrows shot up.
Seeing Luo Zheng's expression, the figure chuckled. "You don't want to? You want to cultivate my inheritance, but you aren't even willing to take me as your master? "
Take a master …
Ever since Luo Zheng stepped onto the road of martial arts, he had never had a true master.
Su Lingyun was Luo Zheng's mentor, not a master in the traditional sense. As for Ning Yudie, she was the Hall Master of the Cloud Hall. At best, Luo Zheng could only be her subordinate …
Luo Zheng didn't think that after stepping into this book, he would have to take someone as his master. For a time, he hesitated.

Chapter 968
Seeing this, Luo Zheng no longer hesitated and knelt down respectfully towards the figure.
Luo Zheng's hesitation was due to the fact that he was about to become a disciple. However, a thought flashed through his mind in an instant. Since he had accepted the Mansion Master's mantle, it was inevitable for him to become a disciple. A man should not kneel down. How could Luo Zheng easily kneel to others?
However, since he had chosen to become a disciple, he would not be pretentious. Naturally, he bowed according to the rules.
However, the Mansion Master was an extremely detached person. He was indifferent to the ceremony of becoming a disciple. Just as Luo Zheng was about to kowtow, an invisible force stopped Luo Zheng and helped him up.
"Alright …" The Mansion Master nodded calmly. "Cultivators like us don't care about worldly etiquette. Since you're willing to acknowledge me as your master, I'll naturally impart the cultivation method in this book to you. Do you know why I set up such a path in front of this book?"
"Disciple does not know," Luo Zheng said, shaking his head.
The Mansion Master continued, "I have spent thousands of years and countless deductions. In the end, I have created a brand new path. I do not know what the end of this path is, but the starting point of this Cultivation Method is to replace the Qi with the Primal Chaos. That is to forcibly shatter the Dantian and then lead the Primal Chaos into the body. This pain is something that only those with great perseverance can endure! Although you have walked the path outside with your own perseverance, I still want to remind you that the pain of replacing the Primal Chaos with the Primal Chaos is far from what you can imagine. Do you understand? "
Luo Zheng nodded and said with a serious expression, "Disciple understands."
The Mansion Master smiled faintly and said, "It is useless to say 'understand' now. When the time comes, you will understand the pain." After that, the Mansion Master looked at Luo Zheng and said, "Strange, you seem to have the Primal Chaos Dipper in your body? You seem to have used the Primal Chaos Dipper to temper your body? "
Luo Zheng nodded, as if the secrets of his body could not be hidden from the Mansion Master's eyes.
"Your body is a little strange." The Palace Master nodded slightly, but he did not reveal the secret of Luo Zheng's body. "Body tempering is also a very painful thing, but the pain caused by using the Primal Chaos to replace vital essence is probably a thousand times more intense than body tempering …"
Pain was originally something that could not be quantified. Some pain might seem unbearable to some, but others could feel it.
However, this Manor Head knew that there was a difference of a thousand times. Presumably, he understood this pain very well. It was obvious that what he said was true.
Luo Zheng nodded, "Since you've already acknowledged me as your disciple, I've already made preparations!"
"Yes." Hearing Luo Zheng's words, the Palace Master said, "Since you were able to endure that path, your temperament is worthy of recognition. Then, I will plant that trace of chaos in you!"
After he finished speaking, the Mansion Master pointed into the distance, and a gray mist swept out from the depths of the space.
"This is the Primal Chaos?" Luo Zheng stared at the gray mist and raised his eyebrows slightly.
After the Primal Chaos circled in the air, it shot toward Luo Zheng's abdomen and instantly entered Luo Zheng's Dantian.
From beginning to end, Luo Zheng did not feel the slightest difference. However, when the mist entered his Dantian, he felt a slight pain in his abdomen, as if it was being pricked by needles.
"I have already planted this Primal Chaos in you," the Palace Master said faintly.
