Luo Zheng tried to circulate his vital essence. He did not feel anything unusual, nor did he feel any unbearable pain. A trace of confusion appeared on his face. He thought that he would be in excruciating pain right now, but now he felt nothing at all.
Seeing Luo Zheng's expression, the Palace Master smiled faintly, "The Primal Chaos has only been planted in your Dantian. Master will teach you a set of Cultivation Methods to cultivate the Primal Chaos. At that time, you will understand …"
After he finished speaking, the world around him spun, and a series of mysterious Cultivation Methods were directly injected into Luo Zheng's mind. However, when the Palace Master was injecting the Cultivation Methods into Luo Zheng's mind, he softly exclaimed in surprise. The Nine True Dragons … were actually suppressed by the Divine Flame Cauldron …
"Hehe, this disciple's opportunity is not small." With the Palace Master's magical power, how could he accept an ordinary person as a disciple?
In fact, in these endless years, he had only accepted a total of two disciples. It was just that he did not mention it to Luo Zheng. His disciples had their own opportunities, and as a master, he would not interfere too much. However, when he thought of the origin of the Divine Flame Cauldron, he could not help but remind him, "If you ascend to the Upper Realm in the future, you cannot reveal the Divine Flame Cauldron in your mind. Before you grow up, you must keep it as your biggest secret!"
"The Divine Flame Cauldron!" Luo Zheng also knew that it was impossible for him to hide his secret from his master. However, when his master mentioned the name of the mysterious furnace, Luo Zheng was naturally surprised.
Of course, Luo Zheng knew that the mysterious furnace's origin was not small. However, Qing Long was unwilling to tell him, and Luo Zheng had no way to look it up. How could he know the name of the mysterious furnace?
Now, he knew the name of the mysterious furnace from his master.
However, his master seemed to have no intention of talking about the Divine Flame Cauldron. He only said, "Although I only left a projection in this book, since I accepted you as my disciple today, I don't have any gifts for you. I'm afraid these things will be of use to you in the future …"
After he finished speaking, the figure gently waved his hand, and several shiny things floated towards Luo Zheng.
After Luo Zheng expressed his thanks, he collected the golden lights in his hand. He discovered that they were actually several shiny fragments. However, within these transparent fragments, there were several treasures sealed within them.
"Alright, I've been away for too long. This spiritual projection can only exist for a short period of time. Since I've already planted the seed of chaos in you and taught you a cultivation method … you must cultivate diligently. Perhaps one day, I'll be able to return from the endless Outer Realm. Then, I'll inspect the strength of my cheap disciple …" The figure's voice grew fainter and fainter.
"How do I use these things?" Luo Zheng held the fragments in his hand and asked. These treasures were not even as big as one of Luo Zheng's fingers. They looked too small.
"Just crush them …" The figure grew fainter and fainter, and his voice became inaudible.
"Crush them?" Luo Zheng's eyes flickered. Looking at the figure that had completely disappeared, he asked curiously, "Cheap disciple … How is that cheap?"
At this moment, Qinglong's voice floated out. "Hehe, he picked up a disciple like you for free. Of course, he's the one who took advantage of you!" Qinglong didn't dare to speak earlier, but he also knew that since Luo Zheng's master had discovered the Godly Flame Cauldron, he must have also discovered the nine true dragons. However, he wasn't interested in pointing it out. If he didn't point it out, Qinglong naturally wouldn't take the initiative to reveal himself.
"You call that taking advantage of me?" Luo Zheng didn't know whether to laugh or cry.
He had entered the Immortal Mansion and obtained a cultivation method. Now, he had also obtained these "free gifts". No matter how he looked at it, Luo Zheng had taken advantage of him.
Qing Long, on the other hand, chuckled and said, "To be able to take in a being who has cultivated a Great Success Lotus Flower as my disciple, this is naturally a bargain …" Qing Long's point of view was naturally different from Luo Zheng's.
Luo Zheng didn't dwell on this issue for too long. At this moment, another door appeared in the space behind him. It must be the door on the painting outside that had opened again. Luo Zheng carefully examined the glittering fragments in his hand as he flew outside.
During this process, Luo Zheng crushed one of the fragments according to his master's instructions. The fragment was hard but very brittle. With a light pinch, it shattered. In the first fragment, a palm-sized carving appeared. It was a carving of a bear with its mouth wide open.
"What's this?" Luo Zheng curiously sized up the carving, but his face was full of confusion.
Even the experienced and knowledgeable Qinglong stared at the palm-sized carving with a pensive look on his face.
At this moment, Luo Zheng had already stepped into the huge book and walked out of the painting. Seeing the carving in Luo Zheng's hand, the Immortal in the Book laughed and said, "Master really left his inheritance in this book. He even left the Roaring Token to you!"

Chapter 969
What the hell is the Roaring Command?
Luo Zheng stared at the Immortal of Books with a puzzled expression.
The Immortal of Books shook his head and was about to show off his knowledge. However, Afo said directly, "The Roaring Command is the Roaring Command. It's an old item that our master once used. It can be used to shout in the universe. When you use the Roaring Command to shout, your voice will spread throughout the universe. The Roaring Command is very common in the universe, but our master's Roaring Command is a little special. It can be used infinitely, hehe."
"Infinitely!" Qinglong roared in Luo Zheng's mind again.
"How can there be such a damn thing?" The Red Dragon, who didn't like to talk much, roared at the same time.
The two dragons' inexplicable excitement made Luo Zheng's mind shake. Afo's words made Luo Zheng feel a little dizzy. According to Afo's words, the Roaring Command was just a tool to shout, and it didn't seem to be anything important. Why were the two dragons so excited?
"What do you mean?" Luo Zheng helplessly asked Qinglong in his mind.
Qinglong let out a long sigh and said in a tone of disbelief, "Although I know that the master of this immortal mansion is probably a True God who has surpassed the Heavenly Dao, I didn't expect him to have such a treasure …"
"What do you mean? Can you explain it more clearly?" Luo Zheng was a little helpless. Since it was just a tool to shout, how could it be worth Qinglong's attention? When Luo Zheng got the Black Star Pickaxe, Qinglong didn't feel much. Could it be that the Roaring Command was even more valuable than a Supreme Artifact?
Qinglong said, "The Hundred Thousand Worlds are too vast. It's very difficult to communicate in the universe. However, the Roaring Command can let your voice spread throughout the universe!"
"Oh …" Afo had already explained it, so Qinglong naturally repeated it. Was it that important to spread the message throughout the universe?
Seeing Luo Zheng's disapproving face, Qinglong could only smile helplessly. "You'll know after you ascend. Spreading it around the world means you have a certain right to speak! Therefore, every race and every force has an almost unlimited demand for Roaring Tokens! "
Qinglong didn't know how to explain it to Luo Zheng.
Although Luo Zheng knew that the Upper Realm was made up of 100,000 Great Realms, he did not have a clear understanding of the scope of 100,000 Great Realms.
It was already extremely difficult to coordinate and mobilize the forces of a Great World, let alone some races. For example, the three great forces of the human race occupied almost ten thousand Great Worlds.
Communication, in such a wide range, became a huge problem! Fortunately, he had something like the Roaring Command.
Why was the Roaring Command named "Roaring"? It had to be explained from a type of Divine Beast. It was the unique Divine Beast in the Great World, the "Roaring Celestial Tapir".
The Roaring Celestial Tapir was not particularly powerful among the Divine Beasts, but after they reached adulthood, they had a kind of innate Divine Ability. This innate Divine Ability contained a kind of Karma Law. It was through this Karma Law that the Roaring Celestial Tapir could spread throughout the universe!
Some almighty Celestial Masters discovered this innate Divine Ability of the Roaring Celestial Tapir. With a flash of inspiration, they killed the Roaring Celestial Tapir and took its throat, allowing the craftsmen in the clan to create the "Roaring Command".
This Roaring Command was enough to spread the command of a Celestial Master throughout the universe!
From then on, the fate of Divine Beasts like the Roaring Celestial Tapir became extremely tragic!
At that time, the Roaring Command was once rampant. Even some Realm Lords in the universe had a large number of Roaring Commands. In the universe, one would often suddenly hear the voice of an unknown person!
The most ridiculous time was when two clans that regarded each other as mortal enemies used the Roaring Command to curse each other across hundreds of Great Worlds. That war of words lasted for a few months, causing countless living beings in the universe to suffer unspeakably.
Hence, some Celestial Masters had no choice but to use the Roaring Command to warn them. If the two clans continued to curse each other, they had to be careful of their own territories …
However, the warning was not very useful. If a reputable figure used the Roaring Command indiscriminately, the Celestial Masters would find trouble with them. However, they could use some nameless figures. The sound of the Roaring Command would spread throughout the universe. No one would be able to trace the source. If they did not reveal their identity, no one would be able to find out who used the Roaring Command.
Fortunately, the number of Roaring Celestial Tapirs was not endless.
There was definitely a large number of Divine Beasts in the Hundred Thousand Great Worlds. However, with so many clans, they all needed the Roaring Command. It could be said that whoever had more Roaring Commands would have a certain degree of authority.
A Roaring Celestial Tapir could only make one Roaring Command. Even the best craftsmen could only make about two. Hence, the number of Roaring Celestial Tapirs began to decrease rapidly.
In the end, they became extremely rare items!
As their numbers decreased, the value of the Roaring Celestial Tapirs naturally increased. In the end, ordinary Sector Lords could not afford to use the Roaring Command at all. Hence, after a few hundred years of clamor, everyone's ears finally quieted down …
Currently, there were still a considerable number of Roaring Commands in the universe. This was because some large factions had begun to raise Divine Beasts like the Roaring Celestial Tapirs. However, the growth of Divine Beasts was very slow. The value of the Roaring Command was still high. In the Hundred Thousand Great Worlds, some Sector Lords had one or two Roaring Commands. However, under normal circumstances, they could not bear to use them indiscriminately.
As for the Celestial Venerables, although they had more Roaring Commands, they would not use them indiscriminately.
After hearing Qinglong's words, Luo Zheng understood a little …
He did not expect that a tool for shouting would be so important. However, after thinking about it, he immediately understood the use of the Roaring Command in his hands …
To put it bluntly, this thing could be used to shout to the entire universe an unlimited number of times.
To give a simple example, if the Human Race and the Demon Race were to fight in thousands of Great Worlds, it would be extremely inconvenient for them to communicate with each other. Perhaps a fierce battle would break out in a certain Great World. By the time the reinforcements from both sides heard the news and rushed over, the fierce battle would have ended a few years ago. This was the use of the Roaring Command!
To put it even more simply, if two sides were to fight, if one side had more Roaring Commands, insulted and belittled the other side all day long, and even made up some false information to tell the other side, it would be a great blow to the other side's morale!
If one side had too few Roaring Commands, or none at all, then one could only watch helplessly …
Holding the Roaring Command in his hands, Luo Zheng laughed mischievously. This was indeed a good thing!
Luo Zheng did not forget that he still had an important purpose, and that was to find his sister. Although he judged that Luo Yan's life was not in danger, the problem was that how could he tolerate Luo Yan being taken away by some unknown person?
If he ascended to the Upper World in the future, Luo Zheng might use this Roaring Command to shout and see if Luo Yan would respond.
"I just don't understand how the Mansion Master could make this Roaring Command that can be used infinitely …" Qinglong still could not believe it.
"Who cares …" Luo Zheng was too lazy to think about this matter. As long as this thing was useful, it was fine.
After stuffing the Roaring Command into the Sumeru Ring, Luo Zheng immediately crushed the second fragment. As he crushed it, a shiny shackle appeared in the fragment. Luo Zheng pulled the shackle and said with a speechless face, "This … What the hell is this?"
Originally, he thought that his master's gift would most likely be a Supreme Artifact … At the very least, it would be a Divine Artifact! Why are there so many weird things here?
Afo looked at the shackle and said with a smile, "This is the Life Ending Shackle!"
"Life Ending Shackle?" Luo Zheng asked again, "What's the use of this?"
Afo said, "When you first stepped into the Immortal Mansion, did you trigger a 'Life Ending Battle' in the pavilion?"
Luo Zheng nodded. Luo Zheng still remembered the Life Ending Battle vividly. Everyone was connected by a special energy and fought to the death because only one could survive in the end!
"That's it. The Life Ending Shackle can bind you and your opponent. The shackle will only open after the battle of life and death. Even if you chain a Celestial Master with you, if that Celestial Master can't kill you, he can't leave either!"
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 970
Luo Zheng would never lock himself up with a Celestial Master unless he was out of his mind.
However, after listening to Afo's explanation, Luo Zheng immediately understood that the Life Extinguishing Shackles was indeed very useful. If he encountered an opponent whose strength was similar to his, as long as he was locked by the Life Extinguishing Shackles, he would not be able to escape.
Compared to the Roaring Command, the Life Extinguishing Shackles were much more practical for Luo Zheng. After all, it was up to Luo Zheng whether to lock himself up or not …
After stuffing the Life Extinguishing Shackles into the Sumeru Ring, Luo Zheng was not in a hurry to crush the remaining fragments. He was more anxious to understand the Cultivation Method that his Master had just taught him!
His master had directly imprinted the cultivation method into Luo Zheng's mind. This cultivation method was created by his master, so naturally, it was named by him as well. It was called the "Primal Chaos Secret Technique". It was just that the word "secret technique" was added after the word "primal chaos". This way of naming was suspected of being lazy.
However, just like how the strength of a warrior did not depend on the warrior's name, the strength of a Cultivation Method was naturally not determined by the name of the Cultivation Method.
Luo Zheng was actually somewhat worried about the Cultivation Method. After all, the Mansion Master had only deduced it, and he himself had not practiced it. However, this was the way of the world. If there were many people walking on a road, it would naturally be easy to walk on it. However, the benefits at the end of the road would have long been divided up.
The road that Luo Zheng was walking on might be a road that no one had ever walked before. Once he walked on it, who knew what he would find at the end?
Luo Zheng followed the method recorded in the Cultivation Method and slowly condensed the Qi in his Dantian. The wisp of Primal Chaos Qi that had originally disappeared suddenly jumped up and down in his Dantian. At this moment, Luo Zheng finally understood what pain was!
The moment the pain was transmitted, the blood on Luo Zheng's face suddenly disappeared. His face was as pale as a sheet of white paper. He could not move at all. Even his instinct to howl had disappeared …
"What happened?" Seeing that their husband had entered the book and seemed to have obtained some inheritance, the three women's faces were filled with joy. However, Luo Zheng's complexion suddenly changed, causing Ning Yudie to ask nervously.
This pain came from Luo Zheng's dantian. It was like a rough file was constantly being filed against his dantian. The dantian was the most important place for a martial artist. How sensitive was this place? The pain was transmitted, stimulating every nerve in Luo Zheng's body.
Xi Youqin also stepped forward. She looked at Luo Zheng and asked nervously, "Luo Zheng, are you feeling very uncomfortable?"
Luo Zheng didn't answer. He just rolled his eyes and fainted.
He really wished that he had fainted. As long as he was awake for a single breath, it would be a hellish torture for him.
Seeing Luo Zheng like this, the three women's faces changed greatly, and they surrounded Luo Zheng one after another.
"Husband! What's wrong with you? "
"How did he faint …"
"Wuwuwuwu …"
As for the immortal in the book, she glanced at Luo Zheng indifferently and said, "He just fainted. It's probably because … it's too painful."
After a long time, Luo Zheng slowly woke up. However, the pain was like a shadow following Luo Zheng. At this time, after letting out a howl, Luo Zheng's eyes rolled back and he fainted again.
It wasn't that Luo Zheng's willpower wasn't strong enough. This pain had already exceeded the limit of human endurance by countless times. His dantian originally contained pure True Yuan. How could it be converted into something else?
Under the excessive pain, Luo Zheng's body activated its self-protection mechanism, forcing Luo Zheng to faint.
Seeing Luo Zheng like this, the three women were speechless and worried …
They had no idea what had happened to Luo Zheng. Why was he in so much pain?
After a long time, Luo Zheng woke up again. However, this time, Luo Zheng still couldn't say a word. This time, however, he woke up much longer than the second time. The second time, he only lasted for a single breath. The third time, he lasted for five or six breaths before fainting again.
The fourth time, Luo Zheng lasted for twenty breaths of time. During this time, there were countless howls of pain.
The fifth time, Luo Zheng lasted for fifty breaths of time …
The sixth time, about a hundred breaths of time …
The seventh time, one incense stick of time …
The eighth time …
Repeatedly fainting and waking up, Luo Zheng hadn't adapted to this pain. This kind of pain wasn't something that humans could adapt to. However, his willpower to endure the pain was rapidly increasing.
After the tenth time, Luo Zheng's lips trembled. Finally, he said, "We'll take our leave first … We'll talk after we return to the Central Domain." Then, he fainted again …
Luo Zheng had the "Gen" token. After obtaining this token, it was equivalent to setting up a spatial node. It would be very convenient for him to return to the Immortal Mansion. However, Luo Zheng didn't have much time to think. Under such extreme pain, it was very difficult for him to concentrate. Everything would have to wait until they returned to the Central Domain.
Since Luo Zheng said so, the three women naturally had to bring Luo Zheng back to the Central Domain.
This time, Ning Yudie gently patted and took out the flood dragon puppet that she and Luo Zheng had worked together to obtain.
Luo Zheng woke up once again. After reluctantly saying goodbye to Afo and the Book Immortal, he and the three women sat on the flood dragon puppet and left the Immortal Mansion. They returned to the Eastern Domain and came out of Ao Xiang's Nine Yin Mountain …
The valley in the Nine Yin Mountain was called the Jade Dragon Valley. Originally, there were people who said that there was a dragon spirit in the Jade Dragon Valley.
When the flood dragon puppet left the Nine Yin Mountain, many people in Guanghan County saw this scene. This confirmed the legend that there was a dragon in the Nine Yin Mountain!
Since it was a dragon's lair, it naturally had to be worshipped. If a real dragon was provoked, it would be punished. Therefore, some of the big clans in Guanghan County issued a ban. Unrelated people were not allowed to approach the Nine Yin Mountain!
In the body of the flood dragon puppet, Luo Zheng was still constantly fainting and waking up. Generally speaking, the time he fainted was much longer than the time he woke up. Usually, he would wake up for one or two incense sticks of time, but he would faint for several hours.
A few days later, Ning Yudie controlled the flood dragon puppet and returned to the Cloud Hall.
She gave the order to prepare a unique residence for Su Lingyun. Then, she stayed in the Ice Palace and guarded Luo Zheng.
Time passed quickly. In the blink of an eye, three months had passed.
During these three months, Luo Zheng was still constantly fainting and waking up. For anyone, it was a great torture, and Luo Zheng was no exception.
The pain lasted for so long that it even changed Luo Zheng's mentality. He was extremely regretful. If he had known it would be so painful, he wouldn't have cultivated the Primal Chaos Secret Technique even if he was beaten to death.
This could not be blamed on Luo Zheng's lack of perseverance. Luo Zheng was not an omnipotent god. Even though his heart of martial arts was far stronger than that of ordinary people, even though his perseverance was far greater than that of ordinary people, this kind of pain was not something that ordinary people could endure. In other words, it had exceeded the limits of a living creature's endurance. Unless Luo Zheng was a rock, he would not be able to endure it.
Seeing Luo Zheng in such pain, how could the three women be in a good mood?
Ning Yudie was guarding the Ice Palace at all times. As for the other matters in the Cloud Hall, she basically let Daoist Dameng handle them on her behalf.
As the pain lasted longer and longer, Luo Zheng's waking time also became longer and longer. This kind of pain did not have the so-called "numbness" feeling. Every wave of pain was incomparably clear, making Luo Zheng's head feel like it was about to explode. He would rather wake up for a shorter time …
On this day, Luo Zheng slowly woke up. The moment he woke up, he knew that he would have to spend a few more hours in torment.
However, as soon as he woke up, he heard a pleasant zither sound. It was Xi Youqin gently stroking the zither strings on one side of the Ice Palace. The zither sound was soothing and gentle, like a spring breeze blowing, giving people a sense of tranquility.
Although the zither sound could not suppress the pain, it still made Luo Zheng feel a little better. He struggled to get up. Ning Yudie, who had been guarding him for countless days and nights, immediately came up to support Luo Zheng. "Be careful …"

Chapter 971
Pain could make a person go crazy. For Luo Zheng to not go crazy after enduring such intense torture for such a long time, his perseverance was amazing.
When the pain suddenly disappeared, Luo Zheng really did feel like going crazy. He was so happy that he went crazy …
After enduring for half a year, he finally got through this pain.
Xi Youqin and Ning Yudie didn't know that the pain had already disappeared. When they saw Luo Zheng get up, Xi Youqin's fingers trembled. Ning Yudie asked from the side, "Husband, are you feeling better?"
Luo Zheng shook his head slightly. His complexion suddenly changed, and he said with an extremely uncomfortable expression, "I, I'm afraid I won't be able to endure it …"
Ning Yudie and Xi Youqin's complexion suddenly changed. Not far away, Su Lingyun, who had been holding the Protective Talisman and praying for Luo Zheng, hurriedly threw away the Protective Talisman in her hand and ran over.
The three women nervously surrounded Luo Zheng. At this time, Luo Zheng opened his arms and directly hugged the three women. Xi Youqin and Su Lingyun screamed. Luo Zheng laughed loudly and directly jumped up from the Ice Palace's patio, floating in the air!
Seeing Luo Zheng like this, how could the three women not know that Luo Zheng should have recovered?
Su Lingyun's face revealed a happy expression. Being hugged by Luo Zheng like this, she still blushed a little. As for Xi Youqin, she used her soft fists to beat Luo Zheng, scolding Luo Zheng for being too naughty. Ning Yudie fiercely glared at Luo Zheng with a face full of anger. Feeling indignant, cold Qi burst out from her body.
"Hey! I'm freezing to death! "Xi Youqin said discontentedly.
The three women were huddled together in Luo Zheng's arms. Ning Yudie's release of cold Qi naturally affected Su Lingyun and Xi Youqin.
Ning Yudie snorted. Her body was like a nimble fish as she slipped out of Luo Zheng's arms and returned to her own Ice Palace.
In these few months, the three women could be said to have lived in harmony. However, after Luo Zheng recovered, he immediately revealed his true colors. The jealousy between women could at most be suppressed, but in the end, it couldn't be eliminated.
Luo Zheng could only bitterly smile. He let go of Xi Youqin and Su Lingyun and entered the Ice Palace alone to comfort Ning Yudie …
At night, after taking a bath and changing his clothes, Luo Zheng sat alone on a platform in the Cloud Hall. Luo Zheng didn't know what had happened to his Dantian. He comforted Ning Yudie for a long time before her tears turned into smiles. Luo Zheng also felt helpless.
Now that Luo Zheng had some time, he naturally had to investigate the changes in his body.
After sitting cross-legged, Luo Zheng sank his perception into his Dantian. The moment he entered the state of inner vision, his face revealed a shocked expression.
Previously, Luo Zheng's Dantian was filled with liquefied True Yuan. Moreover, Luo Zheng's True Yuan was special. After liquefied, the True Yuan was very viscous. However, after investigating, he found that the True Yuan in his Dantian had completely disappeared!
There wasn't even a trace of True Yuan left in his body. Instead, it was replaced by a gray chaotic energy.
"Did I succeed? Did I really use chaotic energy to replace True Yuan? "Luo Zheng murmured.
At this time, Qinglong leisurely said, "Chaotic energy … Hehe, this person who has surpassed the Heavenly Dao actually did it. Using the Dantian to seize the fortune of heaven and earth for his own cultivation is the iron law of the universe. Even a Heavenly Sovereign can't break this shackle. I didn't expect that Luo Zheng had cultivated such a strange cultivation method. It's really hard to predict." Qinglong couldn't help but shake his head.
At this time, Luo Zheng revealed a puzzled expression. "But according to the cultivation levels of martial artists, the Life and Death Realm liquefies True Yuan, the Divine Sea Realm opens up a deep sea, and the Extreme God Realm solidifies True Yuan and molds living things in the body … If this chaotic energy replaces True Yuan, how can I …"
Qinglong shook his head and said, "This kind of thing has never happened before. It's already beyond my ability to understand. How can I answer you?"
Not to mention that Qinglong couldn't return to Luo Zheng, even the Mansion Master who had deduced the "Secret Primal Chaos Technique" couldn't answer Luo Zheng's question. This was because he hadn't cultivated the "Secret Primal Chaos Technique". This cultivation method was completely deduced. As for how far Luo Zheng could go in the future, perhaps even God didn't know.
However, the Azure Dragon quickly added, "Regardless, this Primal Chaos Qi is something that exists outside of the universe. It is an extremely dangerous existence to all living things in the universe. Legend has it that all living things are created from Primal Chaos. Since you are able to absorb it into your Dantian, it is definitely not ordinary. However, can this Primal Chaos Qi really replace True Yuan?"
Martial artists cultivated True Yuan in their Dantian to drive their cultivation method …
However, the True Yuan in Luo Zheng's body had completely disappeared and was replaced by chaotic energy. In other words, the cultivation method that Luo Zheng used to cultivate was no longer suitable for him?
Hearing Qinglong's question, Luo Zheng's heart skipped a beat. Qinglong's words made sense!
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng suddenly operated his Dantian. According to Luo Zheng's previous operating method, he used his meridians to draw out the True Yuan from his body. However, under this method of extraction, what was drawn out was naturally not True Yuan, but chaotic energy …
When the chaotic energy passed through his meridians, he suddenly felt a sharp pain.
"Again?"
Luo Zheng clenched his teeth, and his face revealed a hint of grief and indignation. He had endured for half a year, and now the pain had shifted to another location and was beginning to test his meridians?
Fortunately, the pain only lasted for a few seconds before it quickly subsided. Luo Zheng circulated his true energy and gently waved it in front of him. A stream of vigorous Primal Chaos Qi gushed out from his palm.
"The Laws of Fire …."
Luo Zheng had never cultivated a fire type cultivation method, but merely activating the Laws of Fire was extremely easy for him.
Rumble ….
It was originally night, and all was silent. The martial artists in the Cloud Hall were cultivating day and night, but most of the people in Ocean of Clouds City had fallen into a deep sleep.
Right at this moment, a violent explosion suddenly resounded, and it woke everyone up from their slumber. At the same time, they looked out the window with their mouths agape …. It was clearly the middle of the night, and the night watchman had just finished his night watch. How could it have suddenly become bright?
So long as they raised their heads, they would see an enormous sun appear in the sky, and it illuminated the surroundings like it was daytime!
Luo Zheng had only casually shot out the fireball based on the first level of the Law of Fire, but he had never imagined that the fireball would be so powerful. The fireball spread out and was finally enveloped by the circular "Light of Abundant Water". Therefore, from the outside, it naturally looked like a circular sun.
"So, so powerful …." Luo Zheng's expression changed unpredictably.
Fortunately, he had shot the fireball towards the sky. The lingering flames lit up many places in the Cloud Hall, but it didn't injure anyone. After the flames exploded, it only lasted for one or two breaths of time before vanishing. However, many people in the Cloud Hall had to come out to put out the fire ….
Chaos transformed into Yin and Yang, and Yin and Yang transformed into all things. Luo Zheng fell into deep thought.
Other martial artists who cultivated True Energy seized the fortune of Heaven and Earth. However, what Luo Zheng was seizing now wasn't the fortune of Heaven and Earth, but the fortune of Chaos ….
Earlier, he had only used Chaotic Energy to activate the Laws of Fire. If he executed the method his master had deduced from the Secret Technique of Primal Chaos, wouldn't its might be even more terrifying? Moreover, as Luo Zheng became more and more familiar with Chaotic Energy, he might not be limited to his master's deduction.
After all, he hadn't cultivated the Secret Technique of Primal Chaos that his master had deduced. Now that Luo Zheng had completely replaced the True Energy in his body with Chaotic Energy, he would be able to escape his master's limitations and create his own cultivation method in the future.

Chapter 972
Everyone had different goals when they cultivated the martial dao.
Some people used the martial dao as a stepping stone, and their goal was to dominate the world. Some wanted to be outstanding, and some wanted to be beautiful …
Luo Zheng was obsessed with the martial dao, and his biggest motivation was the interest that came from the bottom of his heart.
When Luo Zheng was cultivating his sword skill, he had created the 'Formless' technique. The Secret of Primal Chaos Technique taught by his master was like a starting point for him to succeed. Relying on himself, Luo Zheng firmly believed that this path would be difficult, but at the same time, he also believed that this path would bring him great benefits.
The fireball had alerted everyone in the Cloud Hall, but after learning that Luo Zheng was cultivating, no one said anything.
In fact, Luo Zheng was still a disciple of the Cloud Hall, and he was still a disciple of the Cloud Hall's Elite Hall. After leaving the Cloud Hall, Luo Zheng had been running around, and he hadn't dealt with this matter. However, he was now the strongest man in the Central Domain, and he was also the husband of the Palace Master of the Cloud Hall. His status had reached a transcendent level …
After returning to the Eastern Domain, Luo Zheng's wish had been redeemed. During his stay in the Cloud Hall, everything seemed peaceful.
Aside from cultivating, he was accompanied by three beautiful women. It was a happy and carefree life. Of course, there were still some obstacles between the three women …
Xi Youqin continued her carefree life. Most of the time, aside from teasing the Ancestral Dragon, she would stick to Luo Zheng. As for cultivation … it seemed like she didn't know what cultivation was. After spending so much time together with the resources provided by the Cloud Hall, with Xi Youqin's talent, she should have broken through a long time ago.
However, Xi Youqin's cultivation was still stuck at the Divine Core realm, and she was still at the Early Divine Core realm.
In fact, Luo Zheng still had some doubts about Xi Youqin. During the dual cultivation ceremony, what did the old man from the Heavenly Clan say to Xi Youqin? In order to find out the truth, Luo Zheng had used the memory of the will of the world, but strangely, Luo Zheng couldn't find anything.
That old man was an outsider, not a native of the Vast Thousand Domains, so it was normal that he couldn't find anything. On the other hand, Xi Youqin was a native of the Central Domain, so it was strange that he couldn't find anything. Luo Zheng could only speculate that the old man must have used some great magical power to shield himself, causing the memory of the Vast Thousand Domains to be lost.
Luo Zheng continued searching the memory of the will of the world, and he found out that Xi Youqin had disappeared from the Cloud Hall that night. She must have entered the Extreme Purple Realm, but why did she enter the Extreme Purple Realm, and what happened in the Extreme Purple Realm? Luo Zheng had no idea. After all, the memory of the will of the world was limited to this dimension …
It wasn't Luo Zheng's intention to pry into Xi Youqin's privacy, but he knew that Xi Youqin was stupid in some aspects, and if she was deceived by someone, it would be very disadvantageous for her. Since it involved her privacy, Luo Zheng couldn't ask her directly. Besides, Xi Youqin had prepared herself for this, and she didn't want to tell him anything. Therefore, Luo Zheng didn't have any good ideas.
As for Su Lingyun and Ning Yudie, they were both unusually hardworking.
In addition to dealing with the government affairs of the Cloud Hall, Ning Yudie also spent the rest of her time cultivating. However, she had some complaints about Xi Youqin sticking to Luo Zheng all day long. Unfortunately, Xi Youqin wasn't a disciple of the Cloud Hall, and Ning Yudie couldn't control whether she was concentrating on cultivating or not …
What surprised Luo Zheng the most was Su Lingyun.
After entering the Cloud Hall, Su Lingyun was the most hardworking! After choosing a cultivation method in the immortal mansion, she wholeheartedly immersed herself in the cultivation room of the Cloud Hall.
During the half year when Luo Zheng was ravaged by the aura of primal chaos, Su Lingyun's cultivation jumped several levels, directly jumping from the fourth level of the Divine Illumination Realm to the eighth level of the Divine Illumination Realm. Not long ago, Su Lingyun's cultivation had reached the pinnacle of the Divine Illumination Realm, and it seemed that she was about to catch up to Xi Youqin.
Luo Zheng's cultivation was also increasing day by day. Not long after, Luo Zheng experienced the second Life and Death Tribulation. This time, the Life and Death Tribulation came with great ferocity. However, since he was able to transfer his power to the Divine Essencefused Mountain, the minor Heavenly Tribulation was no longer a test for Luo Zheng.
However, he still had to guard against that black eye …
If that black eye discovered that he wasn't dead and wanted to destroy him, Luo Zheng really wouldn't have a good way to deal with it!
Last time, he had to cross five million mountains to avoid a casual attack from that black eye. Luo Zheng didn't doubt that if that black eye was serious, it would be very easy to kill him. As long as the Vast Thousand Domains existed, Luo Zheng would also exist. The problem was that it wouldn't be difficult for that guy to destroy the entire Vast Thousand Domains.
Fortunately, the black eye didn't appear during the second Life and Death Tribulation. Luo Zheng didn't spend too much energy to overcome this mighty minor Heavenly Tribulation.
Ever since he swallowed that old man of the Demi-Immortal Clan's medicinal pills, Luo Zheng's cultivation speed reached a new level.
Half a year after the second Life and Death Tribulation, the third Life and Death Tribulation came once again …
During this time, some interesting things happened in the Central Domain. More and more geniuses emerged. Geniuses of the same generation as Luo Zheng also found their own positions.
For example, Hua Tianming, who was only in his early twenties, was already the youngest Elder of the Cloud Hall. The most terrifying thing was that the Heavenly Sword he comprehended was also becoming more and more terrifying. With his talent, if he continued to cultivate, he might be qualified to compete for the next Hall Master. Ning Yudie had already mentioned this matter to Luo Zheng. She knew that Luo Zheng was preparing to ascend, so she naturally had plans to follow her husband. Although Ning Yudie's cultivation speed couldn't catch up with Luo Zheng's, she wouldn't give up.
If one day she also ascended, she naturally hoped that there would be a successor for the Cloud Hall. Hua Tianming could be said to be the best candidate in terms of character, temperament, and talent. Of course, Hua Tianming wasn't the only genius in the Cloud Hall. If he wanted to be promoted to the position of Hall Master, he had to face the challenges of other geniuses.
Ever since Luo Zheng and this group of geniuses appeared, martial arts in the Central Domain became more and more prosperous. Although it still couldn't be compared with the four Divine Nations, it couldn't be mentioned in the same breath as before.
The Blood Wood Cliff, the Mysterious Yin Mansion, the Black Mountain Sect … and the rebuilt Void Spirit Sect were all doing their best to cultivate these young geniuses. In the future, with so many god-tier geniuses appearing in the Central Domain, as time passed, perhaps several almighty Divine Sea Realm experts would be born.
On this day, Luo Zheng obtained the "Gen" Command Token. After obtaining the "Gen" Command Token, Luo Zheng had yet to use it. Hence, he wasn't clear on the principles behind the Gen Command Token.
However, the moment Luo Zheng activated the "Gen" token, he understood that an Immortal Mansion had appeared in front of him.
Seeing the Immortal Mansion in front of him, Luo Zheng's face revealed a strange expression. This Immortal Mansion was almost exactly the same as the one in the Nine Yin Mountain and his Master's Immortal Mansion. However, it was extremely miniature. When it was placed in front of Luo Zheng, it was like a projection. At first glance, it looked incomparably real!
When Luo Zheng stepped into the miniature Immortal Mansion, he instantly appeared in the real Immortal Mansion. At this moment, he suddenly felt that he had shrunk countless times, and everything around him seemed incomparably huge. In fact, he hadn't shrunk at all. It was just that after entering the Immortal Mansion through the miniature Immortal Mansion, the whole Immortal Mansion had become bigger, giving him an illusion.
"So this is how the" Gen "token is used." Standing at the entrance of the Immortal Mansion, Luo Zheng's face revealed a smile. If he encountered any trouble in the future, he could hide in this Immortal Mansion …
Since he had entered the Immortal Mansion through the "Gen" token, Luo Zheng once again entered the Library Pavilion.
The value of the Immortal Mansion was really too great. Unfortunately, Luo Zheng only had the authority to enter the Library Pavilion. However, just the Library Pavilion alone was enough for Luo Zheng to benefit immensely.
This time, he didn't look for cultivation methods. Instead, he collected some information, such as some books on refining weapons and drawing divine patterns.
Although the book immortal had been nagging him and recommending him some information, Luo Zheng still flipped through many books on his own. However, in one of the books, Luo Zheng unexpectedly obtained a piece of information. It was information about the Divine Essensefused Mountain.
The Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain could actually be collected!
The Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain contained the power of the five elements. It was neither metal nor stone. Moreover, it was extremely sturdy and couldn't be damaged by weapons.
It was also because of this that if a large Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain was thrown there, no one in the four Divine Kingdoms would have any ideas about it.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 973
It had to be said that the breadth of his master's knowledge in all aspects was far beyond Luo Zheng's imagination.
This book was called "Ten Thousand Wonders", which recorded some extremely powerful and peculiar objects. The Divine Metagnet Mountain was one of them.
"Ten Thousand Wonders" not only recorded the method to refine the Divine Metagnet Mountain, there were also many other things that were far beyond Luo Zheng's imagination. However, the materials for those treasures were extremely rare, so Luo Zheng naturally couldn't find them at his current stage.
The Divine Metagnet Mountain was something that existed in this vast world. Although Luo Zheng didn't know its origin, it could be considered an ownerless object.
If it weren't for the fact that the Divine Sea Realm experts of the four Divine Kingdoms couldn't move it away, the Divine Metagnet Mountain would have already settled in the Divine Kingdoms' continents.
However, although Luo Zheng had obtained the method to subdue the Divine Metagnet Mountain, there were still a few difficulties.
The strange storm in the Chaotic Astral Ocean continued to wreak havoc. Those Wind Blades were a great threat even to Extreme God Realm experts. Although Luo Zheng had advanced two levels in a row and was already at the third level of Life and Death Realm, it was still unrealistic for him to resist those frightening Wind Blades with his current strength.
The most important point of subduing the Divine Metagnet Mountain was to peel off the bottom of the mountain. The Divine Metagnet Mountain was so sturdy that even dozens of Divine Sea Realm experts wouldn't be able to destroy it. Obviously, Luo Zheng alone couldn't do it.
After pondering for a while, Luo Zheng came up with a plan.
During this period of time, the World Business Alliance suddenly released a message asking for all kinds of rare materials. Those materials were very unpopular and had more or less appeared in the Central Domain. However, they were very hard to find.
However, when there was a great reward, there would be brave men. How many people could resist the temptation of the World Business Alliance's high price?
Some materials were originally stored in families and passed down from generation to generation. Now that they heard such a high price, they were naturally willing to sell them to the World Business Alliance.
The materials collected by the World Business Alliance were gathered together and sent to the Cloud Hall in batches. Because of the great momentum of this purchase, it attracted the attention of many sects. After some inquiries, they found out that the World Business Alliance was only helping the Cloud Hall, or rather, helping Luo Zheng.
However, what did Luo Zheng want these materials for?
There were rumors that Luo Zheng wanted to help the Cloud Hall build a Great Sect Protecting Array. There were also rumors that Luo Zheng was preparing to break through to the Divine Sea Realm.
However, these rumors couldn't stand up to scrutiny. The Cloud Hall already had a Great Sect Protecting Array, and Luo Zheng and Ning Yudie were top figures in the Central Domain. The Cloud Hall didn't need a Great Sect Protecting Array to protect itself. As for breaking through to the Divine Sea Realm, that was even more nonsense. Luo Zheng was only at the third stage of Life and Death Realm, which was still quite far from the Divine Sea Realm.
Luo Zheng was indeed making divine runes. According to the 《Wonders of Nature》, there were three steps to subdue the Divine Magnetic Mountain. The first step was to compress it into an extremely small space. The second step was to use the power of the five elements to suppress the entire Divine Magnetic Mountain. The third step was to subdue it!
Right now, Luo Zheng was doing the preparatory work …
Because of this, Luo Zheng's time started to become tight again. During the day, he would prepare materials and make divine runes. At night, he would concentrate on cultivating. As the days and nights alternated, another three months passed …
Luo Zheng's figure appeared above the southernmost sea of the Central Domain.
This time, Ning Yudie and Xi Youqin insisted on going to the Chaotic Astral Ocean to subdue the Divine Magnetic Mountain. However, they were flatly refused by Luo Zheng.
Although Ning Yudie's strength had improved rapidly, she still wasn't strong enough to deal with the wind blades in the Chaotic Astral Ocean. Those wind blades contained up to 50% of the Law of Wind. If Luo Zheng hadn't relied on his power to shift the blades, his saint tool body would've been instantly torn to shreds by those black wind blades.
After obtaining the will of the Vast Thousand Domains, Luo Zheng also tried to digest the memories in his mind. From the memories, Luo Zheng learned many secrets of the Vast Thousand Domains …
For example, in the northern part of the Divine Kingdom Continent, there was an extremely large wild continent. In fact, there were some extremely powerful ferocious beasts and even a few extremely powerful races. However, the culture of that wild continent was very backward. It was countless times more chaotic than the Sea God Continent.
Another example was that in a corner of the Vast Thousand Domains, there was once a glorious civilization. That civilization was destroyed five hundred thousand years ago because it involved the Tower of Sin. Under the dispute between the experts of the upper realm, just the power that leaked out destroyed the entire civilization. That continent also sank to the bottom of the sea …
However, no matter how Luo Zheng searched, he couldn't find any memories of the Chaotic Astral Ocean. How did this storm come about? What was in the center of the storm? The Vast Thousand Domains didn't know anything about it. This was a very strange thing.
Moreover, behind this area, there was a group of shadow creatures. The strength of these shadow creatures was even feared by the almighty experts of the Heavenly Realm. Why did they wander around the back of the Vast Thousand Domains? Would it affect the Vast Thousand Domains in some way? This was also an unsolved mystery.
Luo Zheng didn't have the ability to uncover this mystery. All he wanted now was to take the Divine Magnetic Mountain into his pocket.
"Huff, huff, huff …"
As soon as he entered the sky above the Chaotic Astral Ocean and entered the range of the storm, the raging wind swept over!
Waves of seawater were sucked up into the sky by the storm. They were like water dragons, connecting the sea and the sky. It was a magnificent sight.
Luo Zheng's small figure shuttled back and forth between these water dragons. Just outside the storm, there were already many wind blades, one after another, sweeping toward Luo Zheng.
These wind blades were light cyan in color, and their surface was white. They only contained one layer of the Law of Wind. A few cyan wind blades contained two layers of the Law of Wind, but they didn't pose much of a threat to Luo Zheng. He didn't dodge and let these wind blades hit his body.
"Puff puff puff …"
Even if he didn't use his power to transfer, these wind blades couldn't cause any harm to Luo Zheng.
Withstanding these wind blades, Luo Zheng's speed became faster and faster. He rushed forward, but his destination wasn't the Divine Magnetic Mountain.
Through the memories of the Vast Thousand Domains, Luo Zheng found out that the Divine Magnetic Mountain was located in the western part of the Chaotic Astral Ocean. Luo Zheng was now heading south, straight toward the center of the storm. He was going in the opposite direction from the Divine Magnetic Mountain.
He flew towards the center of the storm, not because he wanted to explore the mystery of the center of the storm. No matter what the mystery was, it was not something that Luo Zheng could unravel. With his strength, if there was a powerful existence in the center of the storm, he was afraid that he would die if he was not careful.
But if he wanted to collect the Divine Essencefused Mountain, the first step was to peel the Divine Essencefused Mountain from the bottom. With Luo Zheng's strength, he couldn't do this, but with the power of the storm, it was possible!
The power of these wind blades was ridiculously great, and Luo Zheng was able to transfer the power of these wind blades onto the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain through energy transfer!
One wind blade wasn't enough to peel off the Divine Magnetic Mountain, but what about a hundred thousand or a million wind blades? If he completely transferred the power of these wind blades, he didn't believe that he couldn't peel off the base of the Divine Magnetic Mountain.
After pondering for a long time, Luo Zheng finally came up with this method …
As Luo Zheng advanced toward the center of the storm, the wind blades he faced became fiercer and fiercer. Gradually, some wind blades containing the third and fourth level of the Law of Wind appeared around Luo Zheng. The color of the wind blades gradually changed from light cyan to cyan, and then to dark cyan …
"It's not enough. These wind blades are enough to pose a fatal threat to me, but they can't shake the Divine Magnetic Mountain," Luo Zheng thought indifferently as he looked at the wind blades sweeping toward him. He had already tried to transfer the power to the base of the Divine Magnetic Mountain, but the wind blades had no effect on the mountain. They couldn't even leave a mark on it.

Chapter 974
Seeing the Wind Blade, Luo Zheng's face revealed a trace of joy. He had finally found the black Wind Blade!
Seeing that the black Wind Blade was shooting in the wrong direction, Luo Zheng adjusted his angle and went straight for the black Wind Blade.
Puff!
The Wind Blade, which contained the fifth layer of the Law of Wind, directly hit Luo Zheng's chest, causing a muffled sound to ring out.
"Energy Transfer!"
Just as the black Wind Blade hit his chest, Luo Zheng did not forget to use the Will of the Great Thousand Domains to investigate the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain.
As he investigated, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly. A shallow white mark appeared at the bottom of the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain.
Even the Wind Blade, which contained the fifth layer of the Law of Wind, could only leave a faint mark. The Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain was so huge. How long would it take to cut it?
Luo Zheng's face revealed a trace of depression.
However, Luo Zheng would not give up so easily. During the days he stayed in the Central Domain, Luo Zheng not only needed to cultivate, but also needed to prepare for his ascension to the Great Thousand Domains.
He understood the principle of preparedness, success, and failure to prepare would result in failure. Since he could obtain it at a small price, why not?
As he continued forward, the number of black Wind Blades increased while the number of cyan Wind Blades decreased.
Puff puff puff puff puff …
The black Wind Blades moved in different trajectories. Luo Zheng could only try his best to use his body to collide with the black Wind Blades. Under this constant collision, the power of the black Wind Blades was transferred to the bottom of the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain.
"Ten …"
"A hundred …"
"A thousand …"
In the eyes of others, the black Wind Blades represented the existence of the Grim Reaper. However, Luo Zheng could not wait to charge at them.
Under the rapid collision, Luo Zheng smashed a total of three thousand black Wind Blades. He continued to investigate the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain. The power of the three thousand Wind Blades was transferred to the bottom of the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain by Luo Zheng. Now, he finally managed to cut a hole less than the thickness of a thumb.
"…"
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng was speechless.
Despite being sliced by several thousand black wind blades, such a thin mark had only appeared on the Divine Essencefused Mountain.
And a mountain was always thin at the top and thick at the bottom. How many black wind blades would it take to peel the Divine Essencefused Mountain from its base? Wouldn't it be tens of millions or even hundreds of millions of Daos?
This was definitely not going to work. Although there were a lot of black wind blades in the storm, they were also distributed in the sky above the Chaotic Astral Ocean. It was impossible for them to rush towards Luo Zheng together. Luo Zheng admitted that he did not have this kind of charm. If he had to rely on himself to collide with those black wind blades, it would probably take him several decades to peel off the Yuan Magnetic Divine Mountain.
It wasn't that Luo Zheng didn't have the spirit to move a mountain, but he really didn't have the time.
Therefore, he could only continue to approach the center of the Chaotic Astral Ocean …
Speaking of which, Luo Zheng had once entered the center of the Chaotic Astral Ocean when the storm had stopped. Apart from some powerful Ominous Beasts, he hadn't found anything special.
Now that the storm was raging, those Level 11 Ominous Beasts probably didn't dare to float to the surface of the ocean. There were only black wind blades left in the surroundings.
Just as Luo Zheng continued to move forward for more than a thousand li, his eyes suddenly flashed. He saw a strange black wind blade not far away, cutting towards him at high speed!
This black wind blade had the same appearance as the previous black wind blade, but what was strange was the way it moved. In the process of flying, it constantly disappeared and reappeared. Therefore, at first glance, it seemed to be transparent for a moment, then black for a moment. The two colors constantly flickered, and the Wind Element Law it emitted seemed to be even more powerful!
Luo Zheng couldn't determine which level of Law this wind blade contained. So far, Luo Zheng had only cultivated the first level of the Wind Element Law. As for the black wind blade, he had learned from the seven people of the Demi-Immortal Clan that it contained the fifth level of the Wind Element Law.
However, this strange wind blade, which was constantly changing colors, contained a higher level of Law than the black wind blade. He could only estimate that this wind blade contained the sixth level of the Wind Element Law.
Luo Zheng was a little worried. With the power of the black wind blade, he was afraid that it would take forever to cut it. Therefore, when he saw this dual-colored wind blade, he was naturally overjoyed!
"Go up!"
Just as Luo Zheng rushed up, the constantly changing color wind blade had already cut into Luo Zheng's body. Extremely fast, extremely powerful. The airflow from this collision with Luo Zheng's body caused a bottomless whirlpool to suddenly spread out in the ocean below him!
Just the power it emitted was already so terrifying. The majority of this dual-colored wind blade's power had already been transferred by Luo Zheng to the Divine Essencefused Mountain.
"Boom!"
The Divine Essencefused Mountain, which was tens of thousands of miles away from Luo Zheng, suddenly shook …
This Divine Essencefused Mountain, which contained the Power of the Five Elements, had stood here for countless years. Time had not left any traces on this mountain. Whether it was the erosion of the sea day after day, or the cutting of the wind blade, this mountain had not been shaken. After all, its position was far away from the center of the storm, so there were not too many powerful wind blades.
At this moment, the Divine Essencefused Mountain suddenly shook. The huge power directly cut into the bottom of the mountain, causing a large amount of seawater to roll over. The seawater around the Divine Essencefused Mountain was like boiling water, with a huge amount of bubbles emerging. There were so many bubbles that the originally blue ocean was refracted into a milky white color, like a bowl of rice soup …
"It's effective!" Luo Zheng's face was filled with joy.
It was merely a dual-colored wind blade, but it had left a nearly three feet long cut in the bottom of the Divine Essencefused Mountain. The cut was so sharp that it was as if a god had used a huge axe to chop at the bottom of the Divine Essencefused Mountain.
The power of this dual-colored wind blade was beyond Luo Zheng's imagination. If he had not transferred the power, Luo Zheng himself would have been cut into pieces by this dual-colored wind blade.
"Again!" However, under Luo Zheng's gaze, he did not find this dual-colored wind blade again.
But this time, Luo Zheng's heart was filled with hope. Since he could find the dual-colored wind blade here, Luo Zheng could continue to move towards the center of the storm.
He continued to fly forward. Along the way, there were only a few dual-colored wind blades, but Luo Zheng did not let any of them go. After encountering dozens of dual-colored wind blades, the cut at the bottom of the Divine Essencefused Mountain grew bigger and bigger …
However, as he continued to move forward, Luo Zheng became somewhat hesitant.
Who knew what was in the center of the storm? He was not afraid of these dual-colored wind blades, but he was afraid that there were some powerful existences in the center of the storm that he could not afford to provoke. If that happened, it would be difficult for him to escape.
After hesitating for a moment, Luo Zheng clenched his teeth and continued to rush forward.
The further he went, the more dual-colored wind blades he encountered. Each of these wind blades was able to leave a three feet long cut in the bottom of the Divine Essencefused Mountain. Although the Divine Essencefused Mountain was huge, it would eventually be peeled off under such cutting. Luo Zheng's state of mind also became more stable.
However, after Luo Zheng advanced for another two hundred li, he discovered a new type of wind blade. It was actually a golden wind blade!
What was even stranger was that these golden wind blades were not like ordinary wind blades that shot out aimlessly in all directions. Instead, they were in clusters, like migratory birds drifting in the air, gathering together.
"These wind blades have intelligence?" Luo Zheng looked at the golden wind blades with a flabbergasted expression on his face.
These wind blades were just things that were produced in the storm. The stronger the wind blade, the more advanced the Law of Wind it contained. However, no matter how advanced the wind blade was, it was impossible for it to have intelligence, right? To put it bluntly, wasn't it just a bunch of wind?
However, there were too many unanswered questions in the universe. For example, Luo Zheng had merged with the will of the Vast Thousand Domains. He was originally an omnipotent existence in the Vast Thousand Domains, but now, he was still very puzzled …
Just as Luo Zheng was sizing up the golden wind blades that had condensed into clusters from afar, some of the golden wind blades actually discovered Luo Zheng. One of the golden wind blades circled in the distance, and then the other golden wind blades moved as well. They actually started to chase after Luo Zheng!
"Run!"

Chapter 975
Three thousand black wind blades were only able to leave a thumb-deep mark on the Divine Essencefused Mountain.
On the other hand, a single two-colored wind blade was able to cut a few feet deep.
The difference in power between these two wind blades could be described as tens of thousands of times greater …
As for the power of these golden wind blades, if they were equivalent to tens of thousands of two-colored wind blades, just one of them would be enough to cut the entire Divine Essencefused Mountain!
If Luo Zheng could attract just one golden wind blade, he would be very happy. After all, it would save him a lot of effort and time. The problem was that there were hundreds of golden wind blades chasing after him.
They were more than enough to cut the Divine Essencefused Mountain, but where would Luo Zheng transfer the overflowing power to?
To the Divine Kingdom Continent or the Central Domain? That terrifying power could probably cut the Divine Kingdom Continent into countless pieces …
Therefore, Luo Zheng chose to run away at the first moment.
Fortunately, Luo Zheng had been very careful along the way. He kept quite a distance from the golden wind blades and quickly left after a brief encounter. Even so, after discovering the speed of the golden wind blades, Luo Zheng was still scared out of his wits.
Although it was such a long distance, it only took ten breaths of time for the golden wind blades to appear five or six miles behind Luo Zheng. In a few more breaths of time, they would probably catch up to Luo Zheng.
At this time, Luo Zheng was rapidly making a decision. If he used his power to transfer, he didn't want to harm the Divine Kingdom Continent or the Central Domain. Of course, he couldn't transfer his power to the Sea God Continent either. If the golden wind blades really caught up to him and peeled off the Divine Essencefused Mountain, he would probably have to sacrifice the Wild Continent …
Of course, Luo Zheng still had another method. He still had the Gen token with him, so he could enter the Immortal Mansion himself!
As Luo Zheng frantically ran away, the golden wind blades were getting closer and closer. Just as Luo Zheng was about to make a decision, the golden wind blades suddenly circled in the air and made a 180-degree turn. They actually went back.
"They really have intelligence." Seeing the golden wind blades go far away, Luo Zheng heaved a sigh of relief. It was really too dangerous just now.
The golden wind blades didn't dare to chase too far. It seemed like they were protecting something. Flying over the Chaotic Astral Ocean, Luo Zheng's curiosity about the center of the storm grew. However, he didn't want to die. No matter what was inside, it couldn't possibly be born in this world. This world belonged to the Lower Realm, so it couldn't possibly give birth to such a powerful thing. Even if he sacrificed this world, Luo Zheng wouldn't be able to get close to the center of the storm.
....................................................................................
..............................
Half a month had passed. Luo Zheng's body had been baptized by nearly thirty thousand wind blades. As for the bottom of the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain, it had been completely peeled off. There were only a few places on the edge that were connected to the bottom.
However, with Luo Zheng's current strength, even if the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain was only a foot away from the bottom, he wouldn't be able to do anything.
.. Puff. Puff. Puff. Puff. Puff.
...................... Finally...
. Luo Zheng let out a deep breath. He glanced indifferently at the center of the storm and shook his head. Curiosity killed the cat. This wasn't a place he could approach at the moment. He turned around and flew directly toward the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain.
.......... Luo Zheng only needed a few hours to cover the distance of ten thousand miles. Soon, Luo Zheng finally approached the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain.
Speaking of which, although he had observed the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain many times through the will of the Great Thousand World, this was the first time he had personally come to the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain.
Only one-sixth of the entire Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain was floating above the sea. The huge mountain was hidden under the sea. Only the tip of the mountain was above the sea.
"Whoosh …"
Luo Zheng flew over and stood at the top of the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain.
"Eh?" Seeing the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain below him, Luo Zheng's eyes suddenly flashed. He recalled the Divine Origin Magnetic Stone that Divine Kingdom Crown Prince Huo Chen took out in the Divine Emperor Secret Realm. That stone was as tall as a person and seemed to fit perfectly on the top of the mountain. However, he didn't know how the stone on the top of the mountain broke.
Now that the bottom of the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain had been peeled off, the next step was to arrange divine runes and collect the mountain.
A mountain wasn't a small thing. According to the method in the Ten Thousand Wonders, he had to arrange thirty-three six-star divine runes in various parts of the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain and set up a space to bind it. Then, he just had to refine it.
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng took out the things he had prepared in his spatial ring one by one. Then, he firmly imprinted a divine rune on the top of the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain.
After imprinting the first divine rune, Luo Zheng leaped and rushed down the crazily rolling waves. The divine runes in his hands circled around the mountain and imprinted on it one by one.
In the beginning, everything went smoothly. However, just as Luo Zheng was concentrating on imprinting the divine runes, dozens of creatures swept toward Luo Zheng from the deep sea.
Sound couldn't travel well in the sea. Luo Zheng could only feel the abnormality in the water behind him. He turned his head and immediately frowned. "Grade 10 Vicious Beasts?"
Grade 10 Vicious Beasts were equivalent to Life and Death Realm martial artists.
The Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain had been here for who knew how many years. Naturally, there were many creatures that relied on the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain to survive. When these Vicious Beasts discovered that Luo Zheng was near the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain, they immediately attacked Luo Zheng.
Among these Grade 10 Vicious Beasts, there were ugly and strange fish. There was also a huge and soft octopus with black teeth covering every inch of its body. There were also some strange creatures that Luo Zheng couldn't identify.
"You're courting death!"
Luo Zheng didn't put these Vicious Beasts in his eyes. In his eyes, even Vicious Beasts comparable to Life and Death Realm were no different from ordinary fish!
However, this time, Luo Zheng really wanted to use these Vicious Beasts to practice. The Primal Chaos Qi in his dantian swept out, and a longsword appeared in Luo Zheng's hand!
"Whoosh!"
Spurred by the Primal Chaos Qi, the Sword Intent formed a dusky cyclone, which created a string of tiny bubbles in the water.
"Puff Puff Puff …"
With a series of muffled sounds, the first batch of Vicious Beasts were cut into countless pieces by Luo Zheng. The degree of sharpness was even beyond Luo Zheng's expectations. After replacing the True Yuan in his body, Luo Zheng's strength had undergone a qualitative change. However, he was only facing Grade 10 Demonic Beasts. Although these Demonic Beasts were comparable to Life and Death Realm martial artists, their intelligence was far inferior to human martial artists.
However, the Primal Chaos Qi also had a weakness.
True Yuan was obtained by seizing the good fortune of heaven and earth. After a martial artist's True Yuan was used up, he could continuously absorb Yuan Qi of heaven and earth to replenish it. If he wanted to quickly replenish it, he could use True Yuan Stones.
The Primal Chaos Qi was somewhat troublesome. It seemed that it could only be refined quietly within his body. However, according to his master's deduction, everything in the world evolved from Primal Chaos. Therefore, everything could be restored to Primal Chaos. Luo Zheng hadn't cultivated to that level yet, so naturally, he couldn't do such a miraculous thing. Luo Zheng could only endure this shortcoming for the time being.
This sword strike had killed dozens of Vicious Beasts in front. How could the Grade 10 Vicious Beasts and even some Grade 9 Vicious Beasts not understand Luo Zheng's strength? At this time, they all fled.
As for Luo Zheng, he was too lazy to chase after them. He didn't know why these Vicious Beasts attacked him. Perhaps they knew that he was going to move the Divine Yuan Magnetic Mountain? No matter what, he had destroyed their home for thousands of years. If these guys hadn't attacked him without reason, Luo Zheng wouldn't have been willing to kill.
After driving away the Vicious Beasts, Luo Zheng quickly arranged all the Divine Runes. After these preparations were completed, Luo Zheng began to activate the Divine Runes one by one according to the method recorded in the 《Ten Thousand Wonders》. Then, he returned to the top of the Divine Yuan Magnetic Mountain. After activating the last Divine Rune, Luo Zheng suddenly patted the Divine Yuan Magnetic Mountain below and chanted, "Rise!"
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 976
At the same time, these special divine patterns erupted with a faint halo of light, encircling the entire Divine Essencefused Mountain.
Then, the entire Divine Essencefused Mountain trembled and slowly rose from the sea.
The Divine Essencefused Mountain's outer appearance was not much different from an ordinary mountain. It was over three thousand meters tall, and its overall weight was definitely much heavier than an ordinary mountain. Ordinary mountains were made of stone, but the Divine Essencefused Mountain was made of Divine Essencefused Stone.
However, although Divine Essencefused Stone was sturdy, it was actually not particularly heavy. The main reason was that it contained the power of the five elements, which could not be penetrated by any Law. This was the reason why it was indestructible. For example, there were other mountains that contained rich iron ores, and their weight was probably much heavier than the Divine Essencefused Mountain. However, those mountains were not of much use, so Luo Zheng naturally could not refine them.
As Luo Zheng continued to rise, the mountain submerged in the sea gradually rose as well, and the entire mountain finally left the sea.
Some of the creatures gathered on the Divine Essencefused Mountain left their nests and jumped into the sea …
At this moment, Luo Zheng lightly flicked his finger, and several dozen drops of blood flew out from his body.
"Blood Refining Technique!"
In order to collect the Divine Essencefused Mountain, not only did he need to set up these Six Star Divine Patterns, but the most crucial part was the Blood Refining Technique recorded in the 《Ten Thousand Wonders》.
When the drops of blood leaped out of Luo Zheng's body, they slowly rotated in the air. Under the operation of the Blood Refining Technique, the drops of blood fused with each other and quickly formed a palm-sized Blood Refining Rune.
The Blood Refining Rune continued to rotate, and at the same time, it covered the huge mountain.
Generally speaking, objects of the five elements like the Divine Essencefused Mountain were very difficult to collect, because no matter which Law was used, it would not be able to completely react with the Divine Essencefused Mountain. However, using the Blood Refining Technique to refine the Divine Essencefused Mountain had a miraculous effect.
It was also because of this that Luo Zheng admired his Master more and more. However, he did not know whether the various secret techniques of the 《Ten Thousand Wonders》 were created by his Master, or whether he had collected them while roaming.
As Luo Zheng's Blood Refining Rune hit the Divine Essencefused Mountain with incomparable clarity, the speed of the Divine Essencefused Mountain rapidly increased, matching the speed of the drops of blood and Blood Refining Rune from before.
"Huhu Huhu …"
However, under the rotation of such a huge mountain, its might was naturally incomparable. As the mountain rotated, it also brought about the sound of wind. From time to time, there would be some faint green wind blades striking the mountain, causing muffled sounds to erupt.
After the Blood Refining Rune was planted, it established a certain connection with Luo Zheng. However, this connection came from the connection between Luo Zheng and the Blood Refining Rune. After all, the Blood Refining Rune was formed from Luo Zheng's blood. Because of this, the connection was very weak, and Luo Zheng was unable to control the Divine Essencefused Mountain.
At this moment, the 33 Divine Essencefused Runes around the Rune Mountain erupted with resplendent light at the same time!
The 33 Divine Essencefused Runes each contained a different power. Luo Zheng had to expend a lot of effort to create these six-star Divine Essencefused Runes. Sometimes, he even had to discuss with Qinglong and ask for Qinglong's help. Qinglong had analyzed that the purpose of these 33 Divine Essencefused Runes was to temporarily suppress the Five Elements Power within the Divine Essencefused Mountain. Only in this way could the Blood Refining Rune be completely immersed within the Divine Essencefused Mountain.
And now, it was just as Qinglong had said. The Divine Essencefused Mountain originally had several colors slowly flowing within it, but as the 33 Divine Essencefused Runes took effect, the Five Elements Power within the Divine Essencefused Mountain suddenly stopped flowing!
The originally multi-colored Divine Essencefused Mountain instantly turned into a pale yellow mountain!
"The Five Elements Power has disappeared?" Luo Zheng's expression changed. He could sense that the other four Five Elements Power had completely disappeared. The only thing left in the mountain was the Law of Earth. In other words, in an instant, the Divine Essencefused Mountain had turned into an ordinary mountain …
"Pfff pfff …"
Several pale blue Wind Blades struck the Divine Essencefused Mountain, immediately creating deep dents in the 'mountain'. Before this, these Wind Blades that only contained the second level of the Law of Wind couldn't cause any substantial damage to the Divine Essencefused Mountain. Even the power of the Black Wind Blades could only leave a faint mark on the Divine Essencefused Mountain.
However, the Divine Essencefused Mountain's transformation was only temporary. If the Five Elements Power wasn't suppressed like this, wanting to subdue the Divine Essencefused Mountain was simply wishful thinking.
"The person who can think of this method to subdue the Divine Essencefused Mountain must be a peerless genius," Qinglong murmured as he watched this scene in Luo Zheng's mind.
"Refine the Divine Essencefused Mountain while its Five Elements Power is suppressed!" In an instant, Luo Zheng's hands formed several mysterious seals, and the Blood Refining Runes that were originally imprinted on the Divine Essencefused Mountain sank into the mountain's interior.
"Sumeru Realm, draw a hexagon world, collect!"
At this moment, six of the 33 Divine Essencefused Six Stars Runes erupted with the violent Law of Space.
The Law of Space instantly spread out, forming a hexagon world that enveloped the Divine Essencefused Mountain. This was actually a Sumeru Realm, and the Divine Essencefused Mountain was sucked into it.
As the Sumeru Realm gradually stabilized, the hexagon world began to rapidly shrink, and the Divine Essencefused Mountain also shrank in proportion. At the same time, the speed of rotation also became faster and faster. The thousand-meter-tall mountain in front of Luo Zheng turned into a palm-sized miniature mountain. It was still rotating, and its surface was still yellow.
After suppressing the Five Elements Power, it still needed some time to recover. Luo Zheng didn't need to worry about it. He only needed to wait until the energy of the Divine Essencefused Mountain was exhausted, and the Divine Essencefused Mountain would naturally return to its original appearance.
Holding the spinning miniature mountain in his hand, a satisfied smile appeared on Luo Zheng's face. Under the situation where the Five Elements Power was suppressed, the Divine Essencefused Mountain was indeed useless. A single punch from a Life and Death expert could probably shatter the entire mountain.
However, the method to suppress the Five Elements Power wasn't something that ordinary people could come up with. It was only recorded in the Wonders of Nature. The reason why Qinglong sighed was that the thirty-three Divine Essencefused Six Stars Runes actually disintegrated the Five Elements Power and converted it into pure Law of Earth. It was too miraculous …
According to Qinglong's knowledge, there were many holy grounds in the upper realm that used the Five Elements Power to construct defensive arrays. This was because the Five Elements Power had no absolute weakness! Therefore, the thirty-three Divine Essencefused Six Stars Runes could not only disintegrate the Five Elements Power of the Divine Essencefused Mountain, but if those powerful defensive arrays encountered this method, they would also become as fragile as eggshells.
After the Five Elements Power was restored, how sturdy would the Divine Essencefused Mountain be? Although Divine Sea experts couldn't do anything to this mountain, even Extreme God experts could only sigh when encountering this mountain.
