If one wanted to use brute force to shatter the Divine Essencefused Mountain, the level of power involved would most likely be at the fifth, sixth, or seventh layer of Law of Earth. However, there weren't many almighty experts in the upper realm who possessed this level of Law of Earth. Therefore, the Divine Essencefused Mountain would inevitably become a great help to Luo Zheng.
After obtaining the Divine Essencefused Mountain, Luo Zheng's goal had been achieved. However, just as he was about to collect the Divine Essencefused Mountain, his heart suddenly skipped a beat, and an extremely uncomfortable feeling arose in his heart.
"Is someone spying on me?" Luo Zheng's brows wrinkled. He swept his gaze around, and at the same time, spread out his perception.
Wind blades swept and waves rolled, but there was nothing between the heaven and earth. As for the creatures in the Chaotic Astral Ocean, they had already tasted Luo Zheng's sufferings, and this time, they had already gone into hiding.
He didn't discover any creatures spying on him, but the vigilance in his heart was even more intense!

Chapter 977
A shadow creature!
Luo Zheng's eyebrows suddenly twitched.
That black hook seemed to be made of animal bones, and its speed was incomparably fast. It headed straight for Luo Zheng, wanting to hook Luo Zheng into the crack.
Luo Zheng's reaction was also extremely fast. Just at that moment, a gust of wind swept over Luo Zheng. He immediately drifted along with the gust of wind, and his body abruptly moved a few feet to the side, barely dodging the hook!
The black hook didn't hit him. It floated in the air for a while, then unexpectedly changed direction and chased after Luo Zheng again.
"Chasing Cloud Movement Technique!"
How could Luo Zheng dare to stay any longer?
Even the seven Divine Realm experts of the Demi-Immortal Clan had to flee in front of the shadow creature. If he was really pulled into the crack, his fate could be imagined.
"Whoosh!"
The windstorm originally contained abundant Wind Element Law. When the Chasing Cloud Movement Technique was released, the Vital Essence in his body also erupted. Luo Zheng's speed instantly reached an extreme.
However, the black hook was even faster. It chased after Luo Zheng all the way, as if it wouldn't give up until Luo Zheng was hooked!
"I can't escape?" Relying on his perception, Luo Zheng knew that the black hook behind him was getting faster and faster, and it was getting closer and closer to him. His face also became gloomy.
Seeing that the black hook was only one or two feet away from Luo Zheng, and it would probably catch up to him in one or two breaths of time, Luo Zheng suddenly turned around while flying, and the power in his body madly surged out. If he couldn't escape, then he could only fight. This was probably what he thought in his heart.
However, right at this moment, a dramatic scene occurred. At the instant Luo Zheng concentrated, the hook became ramrod straight, and its tip emanated strands of cold light. However, it was unable to move in the slightest ….
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed, and a trace of a smile appeared on his face. The hook wasn't long enough.
This hook was released by the shadow creature from the back of the world. The hook could leave the back of the world, but the shadow creature couldn't.
Immediately after, the black hook shrank back with a whoosh, as if the shadow creature had given up.
However, Luo Zheng didn't let his guard down. The shadow creature seemed to be active in the "back of the Chaotic Astral Ocean." Although he didn't know what kind of malice these guys had towards him, staying here was definitely not a wise choice. Thus, he sped all the way to the Central Domain.
Sure enough, as Luo Zheng guessed, spatial cracks constantly appeared on the way, and the hook chased after Luo Zheng.
However, Luo Zheng had already fled far away from the moment he opened the spatial rift. Therefore, Luo Zheng didn't get hit by the hook even once during his mad dash.
This chase continued all the way to the edge of the Chaotic Astral Ocean, and only then did the shadow creature give up chasing him.
As for why the shadow creature gave up, Luo Zheng felt it was quite strange. It was reasonable to say that the shadow creature's range of movement was limitless. Even in the Central Domain, they could still chase after him in the shadows. So why did they suddenly stop here?
Of course, for Luo Zheng, he heaved a sigh of relief. What if these guys were still here? Even if Luo Zheng entered the Cloud Palace, they would still be able to hunt him down in the shadows. Then, he would never have a day of peace.
But why did these guys stop? Luo Zheng couldn't figure it out.
Could it be … there was something in the Central Domain that they feared?
This was simply impossible. Although there were many dangerous places in the Central Domain, after reaching the Life and Death Realm, one could enter and leave at will. For example, although the Cloud Dream Swamp was dangerous and mysterious to ordinary warriors, it wasn't scary to Luo Zheng.
On the way back to the Cloud Hall, Luo Zheng was still vigilant. He still had countless guesses in his heart. After thinking it over, Luo Zheng came up with a somewhat reasonable guess. Perhaps there was something powerful on the "back side" of the Central Domain! And the shadow creature was very afraid of those powerful things. Therefore, they didn't dare to invade the back side of the Central Domain!
But what was on the back side of the Central Domain?
Luo Zheng was also curious. With his current strength, he could also forcibly tear open space and enter.
But after thinking it over, he still gave up.
No matter what was on the back side of the Central Domain, it wasn't an existence he could provoke at his current stage. At least, that thing had existed in the Central Domain for so long, but it didn't seem to have appeared in the history of the Central Domain. Luo Zheng better not explore the back side of the Central Domain.
Luo Zheng naturally understood that his luck was very strong, but sometimes, he had to seek death. No matter how strong his luck was, it was useless!
After obtaining the Divine Essencefused Mountain, Luo Zheng returned to the Cloud Hall. In addition to cultivating the "Secret of Primal Chaos", he also had to study the Divine Essencefused Mountain. The simplest and most violent use of the Divine Essencefused Mountain was to smash people with it.
If an ordinary mountain was refined, it could be smashed to pieces by a Divine Sea Realm expert. However, the Divine Essencefused Mountain wasn't so easy to block. Of course, smashing people was only the simplest use. The Divine Essencefused Mountain could also extract the Divine Essencefused Light. The Divine Essencefused Light was the Divine Essencefused Light that evolved after the Power of the Five Elements had settled in the Divine Essencefused Mountain for countless years. It could be used for one's own use. According to the "Wonders of Nature", the Divine Essencefused Light could suppress all the Power of the Five Elements in the world.
After studying like this for two months, early one morning, Luo Zheng suddenly felt his soul tremble!
From the depths of his soul, there was a feeling of extreme joy. It was the joy of the world's will!
"Eh?"
Luo Zheng, who was still cultivating, had a strange expression on his face. He then closed his eyes and raised his eyebrows. "The Ascension Passage has been repaired!"
Luo Zheng didn't know who repaired the Ascension Passage. It could be the Demi-Immortal Clan or the Human Clan. According to the old man of the Demi-Immortal Clan, the Demi-Immortal Clan should have reached some kind of "agreement" with the Human Clan. At least, White Brow Daoist didn't find trouble with the Demi-Immortal Clan. As for who took the Evil Dragon away from the Heavenly Star Secret Territory, Luo Zheng didn't know.
He didn't expect the Ascension Passage to be so efficient. In such a short time, the Ascension Passage had been repaired …
However, in the Great Thousand World, besides Luo Zheng himself, all the creatures below the Divine Sea Realm were in a state of indifference.
The strength of Life and Death Realm experts wasn't enough to be rejected by the Great Thousand World. They couldn't ascend, so whether the Ascension Passage was repaired or not had nothing to do with them.
However, it was different for Divine Sea Realm experts!
Within the four Divine Kingdoms, dozens of Divine Sea Realm experts felt an invisible repulsive force being exerted on their bodies at this moment!
The repulsive force was trying to squeeze them out of this plane!
The moment they felt the repulsive force, the Divine Sea Realm experts were ecstatic. Even Battle Emperor Yu the Great, Tianfeng War Emperor, and the others were the same.
They dreamed day and night in the hope that one day, they could ascend to the Upper World. The repulsive force was great news for them.
In less than half an hour after the repulsive force appeared, a bright silver light burst out from the Black Iron Divine Kingdom and shot up into the sky!
"Damn it, who is this guy? Why are they so impatient?" Zhou Huang couldn't help but curse in the palace of the Black Iron Divine Kingdom.
It was true that the Divine Sea Realm experts in the four Divine Kingdoms had been holding back for countless years. Everyone was impatient, but they couldn't just ascend recklessly!
The four Divine Kingdoms had a good relationship with each other, but they maintained a balance of strength. If all the Divine Sea Realm experts in the Black Iron Divine Kingdom ascended and Zhou Huang was the only one left, how could this huge Divine Kingdom continue to function?
Therefore, Zhou Huang immediately summoned all the Divine Sea Realm experts in the Black Iron Divine Kingdom to the palace!
The problem was that those mighty warriors of the Spirit Sea Realm all wanted to ascend. Originally, they could ascend as long as they reached the Spirit Sea Realm. Most of them had reached the Spirit Sea Realm for many years. Zhou Huang couldn't stop them!

Chapter 978
Just as Zhou Huang announced the summons, two or three more silver lights shot into the sky. Only half of the Divine Sea Realm masters obeyed Zhou Huang's order and rushed towards the Black Iron Divine Kingdom's palace. These Divine Sea Realm masters had a good relationship with Zhou Huang. Some of them were even martial artists from the Zhou Family …
This situation didn't only happen in the Black Iron Divine Kingdom. Outside the Sky Feather Holy Sea, silver lights constantly shot into the sky. This became a spectacle that the citizens of the four Divine Kingdoms praised!
Some of the Divine Sea Realm masters weren't in such a hurry, but after handing over the affairs of their families and instructing their disciples, they also chose to ascend.
In fact, Zhou Huang, Tianfeng War Emperor, Sky Fortune Kingdom King, and Battle Emperor Yu the Great were also anxious. How could they not be anxious when they saw their subordinates scrambling to ascend? But they were kings after all. They weren't like the Divine Sea Realm masters who were all alone. At most, they could leave after telling the juniors of their families.
As kings, they had too many things to take care of before they could ascend.
Battle Emperor Yu the Great was the angriest of them all. There were close to 20 Divine Sea Realm masters in the Great Yu Divine Kingdom. In just half a day, almost all of them had ascended. Only one person was left behind, and that person was his brother from the Liu Family!
The moment the ascension passage was repaired, Battle Emperor Yu the Great had already announced that he was summoning all the Divine Sea Realm masters. But these guys who used to be very respectful to him didn't care about him at all! Battle Emperor Yu the Great originally wanted to threaten these guys. If anyone dared to ascend without permission, he would exterminate their families! But he didn't say this out loud. He was going to ascend too. If he ascended, so what if the Liu Family was exterminated?
This was also because Battle Emperor Yu the Great was too harsh on the other Divine Sea Realm cultivators in the Divine Nation. Now, everyone was like a bird that had just broken free from its shackles. They no longer had to endure Battle Emperor Yu the Great's humiliation. Why would they listen to him?
Relatively speaking, there were more Divine Sea Realm warriors left in the other three Divine Kingdoms. However, within a day, seventy percent of the Divine Sea Realm warriors had ascended. It was believed that soon, there would be a reshuffling of the forces in the Divine Kingdoms. After all, all the Divine Sea Realm warriors would ascend, which would weaken the four Divine Kingdoms. By then, it would be a competition between Life and Death Realm warriors.
…
Such a situation of fighting to be the first to ascend had never happened in the Sea God Continent.
The reason was simple. The number of Spirit Sea Realm warriors on the Seagod Continent was far less than on the Divine Kingdom Continent. Every race had at least two or three Spirit Sea Realm warriors. Also, the Divine Kingdom Continent was in a state of chaos. As Spirit Sea Realm warriors, they naturally had a strong sense of mission and crisis. The Divine Kingdom Continent was dominated by human beings, so there was no sense of crisis.
However, Situ Haotian and the others still discussed this issue for a long time.
Among them, Liu Yu was the most depressed.
Now that the Tower of Sin had been fully opened, the number of experts on the Sea God's Continent would experience an explosive breakthrough.
The humans who controlled the Tower of Sin and the Yao Night Clan would definitely become the rulers of the Sea God's Continent.
However, that wasn't what Liu Yu was depressed about. She didn't want to ascend ….
In the past, Liu Yu had a strong desire to ascend. She understood her own talent. Through the Tower of Sin, she also understood that if her talent was recognized by the Demi-Immortal Clan, even if she was only ranked in the second row, she would definitely be an outstanding existence in the higher realms. She would be able to shine even brighter for the Yao Night Clan and win even more glory for the three kings.
But now …. She didn't want to ascend.
The reason was simple. She was already involuntarily involved in the battle between the two kings.
Luo Zheng had shattered the statue of the King of Punishment twice, causing the latter to be in a violent rage. When Luo Zheng returned to the Sky Feather Holy Land last time, he had a direct confrontation with the King of Punishment. The King of Punishment had also ordered her to kill Luo Zheng and Xun.
However, Liu Yu didn't do as she was told. Logically speaking, she had already disobeyed the will of a king and deserved the death penalty.
If she ascended and returned to the Yao Night Clan, it was easy to imagine what would happen to her ….
But if she didn't ascend, was she going to remain in the Divine Sea realm for the rest of her life? Although she was still very young, only a little over two hundred years old, every cultivator had their own ambitions. How could she be content with staying in a corner and wasting her lifespan?
Thus, Liu Yu had been in a state of conflict ever since the ascension passageway was opened ….
… …
As for the Central Domain, no almighty Divine Sea realm cultivators had been born in the Central Domain ….
The matter of the Ascension Passageway's restoration caused a huge uproar in other places. Even on the undeveloped Desolate Continent, some living beings ascended. In the Boundless Sea, some powerful savage beasts ascended as well.
However, in the Central Domain, apart from Luo Zheng, no one else knew about this. The sun rose as usual. The commoners continued to live their ordinary lives, while the cultivators continued to cultivate without slacking off.
As for Luo Zheng, he had already begun his third Life Death Tribulation.
As long as the black eye didn't appear, the minor Heavenly Tribulation wasn't much of a threat to Luo Zheng. After taking the pill from the old man from the Demi-Immortal Clan, Luo Zheng's cultivation increased by several levels. According to the old man, the pill could only speed up his advancement to the Divine Sea realm, but for the current Luo Zheng, it was already very useful.
Time passed quickly. Apart from cultivating and accompanying the three women, Luo Zheng also cast his gaze on the four Divine Kingdoms. He found that most of the almighty Divine Sea realm cultivators in the four Divine Kingdoms had ascended. Only five or six relatively young almighty Divine Sea realm cultivators were still in the Divine Kingdoms.
What surprised Luo Zheng even more was that Huo Yuner had also ascended ….
This woman with the bloodline of the True Phoenix had also ascended, which really surprised Luo Zheng. However, after some investigation, Luo Zheng understood that she hadn't ascended.
When she left this world, Huo Yuner had just broken through to the Life and Death realm. However, the bloodline of the True Phoenix in her body had erupted, giving birth to a special flame. As for what this flame had, Luo Zheng didn't know. However, after she entered the flame, she disappeared from this plane. Luo Zheng guessed that she didn't go to the Human Realm. Most likely, she went to the Great Realm where the True Phoenix Clan resided.
Thinking of his promise to her that he would return to the Divine Kingdom Continent in the future, Luo Zheng felt a little regretful. However, since she had entered the Great Realm, there was a possibility that they would meet again in the future. However, the Hundred Thousand Great Realms were unimaginably vast. Luo Zheng also knew that this hope was very slim.
Time passed quickly. Luo Zheng's cultivation was also steadily improving.
In the past two years, he had checked the Soul Cultivation Wood several times. His soul was still slowly recovering under the nourishment of the Soul Cultivation Wood, but he still fell into a deep sleep and didn't wake up.
As for Feng Nianyun …
Under Qinglong's request, Luo Zheng also had her communicate with Qinglong several times. There was no way to undo the Soul Seal in the Great Realm in the Great Realm. If he wanted to undo it, he would have to return to the Great Realm.
If Luo Zheng ascended in the future, he couldn't bring Feng Nianyun with him.
In fact, there was a simple way to send Feng Nianyun back to the Great Realm. The Rising Dragon Platform could easily do it. Moreover, Feng Nianyun originally belonged to the True Dragon Clan. Naturally, the Rising Dragon Platform was the most suitable. Unfortunately, as soon as Luo Zheng suggested this, Feng Nianyun and Qinglong both refused.
It seemed that Qinglong wasn't the only one involved with the True Dragon Clan. Feng Nianyun also didn't want to return to the True Dragon Clan. As such, Luo Zheng didn't have a good way to help Feng Nianyun.
He could only place her body in the depths of the Cloud Hall and guard it closely. No one was allowed to approach it.
In the future, when he found a way to undo the Soul Seal in the Great Realm, he would return to the Great Realm to help Feng Nianyun undo the seal.
Luo Zheng's cultivation was still rising rapidly. The pills of the old man from the Demi-Immortal Clan continued to take effect. Soon, Luo Zheng ushered in the fourth Life and Death Tribulation. The interval between the fourth and third Life and Death Tribulations was only three months!
The interval between each Life and Death Tribulation was getting shorter and shorter.
For Luo Zheng, these were the most comfortable days. He even wanted to deliberately suppress the speed of his cultivation and take some time to accompany the three women. However, the rapid improvement of his cultivation made Luo Zheng feel somewhat helpless.
However, he had thought it through. Even if he entered the Divine Ocean Realm, he would suppress himself and not be in a hurry to ascend.
However, after the fifth Life and Death Tribulation, he strangely found that he was rejected by the Great Thousand World!
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 979
Generally speaking, only after breaking through to the Divine Sea Realm and opening up an inner world would the Great Thousand World sense it and repel it.
However, Luo Zheng was only at the fifth layer of Life and Death Realm, yet he could feel the repulsive force. This was a bit strange.
Moreover, Luo Zheng had already fused with the will of the world, yet he was still repelled by the Great Thousand World. Wasn't this the same as repelling himself?
Unfortunately, Luo Zheng couldn't question the Great Thousand World. Even if he did, he wouldn't get an answer. The Great Thousand World was the collection of all the creatures in the world, not a single will.
Even if he felt the repulsive force, Luo Zheng still tried his best to suppress it. Resisting this repulsive force even consumed Luo Zheng's strength!
"Something's wrong." Luo Zheng couldn't figure it out.
Through the will of the Great Thousand World, Luo Zheng also investigated the four Divine Kingdoms and Sea God Continent. Liu Yu and the others should also be repelled, but they seemed to be easily suppressing themselves in this plane. This force didn't cause them too much trouble.
However, ever since Luo Zheng's cultivation broke through to the fifth layer of Life and Death Realm, this invisible repulsive force became stronger and stronger with each passing day. It could even be said that it had multiplied!
On the first day, Luo Zheng was still very relaxed.
On the second day, the repulsive force was already pressuring Luo Zheng.
On the third day, the repulsive force was already affecting Luo Zheng. He had to divert his attention to suppress it, or else he wouldn't be able to concentrate on cultivation.
On the fourth day, Luo Zheng's face revealed a bitter expression! He had to divert a lot of his strength to suppress it!
At this time, Ning Yudie also noticed the strange look on Luo Zheng's face. She was also worried about her husband, so when she saw the worried look on Luo Zheng's face, she thought that Luo Zheng was not feeling well.
However, at this level of cultivation, it could be said that he was immune to all kinds of diseases. Those small problems simply didn't exist …
In order to dispel Ning Yudie's doubts, Luo Zheng forced himself to be calm and told Ning Yudie that it was nothing.
However, on the fifth day, Luo Zheng couldn't bear it anymore. He gathered the three women and told them the truth. He was afraid that he couldn't suppress the repulsive force and would be driven out of the Great Thousand World.
After the three women heard this, they were all shocked.
Although they knew that Luo Zheng would eventually ascend, Luo Zheng was still at the fifth layer of Life and Death Realm, they could accompany Luo Zheng for a period of time. How could he ascend now?
However, when they saw how strained Luo Zheng was, the three women had no choice but to believe him.
Ning Yudie was depressed. After accompanying her husband for a thousand days, there would eventually be a day when they would part. She didn't like it being so sudden …
Luo Zheng was even more depressed. He didn't know what the Great Thousand World meant. Originally, he had merged with the will of the Great Thousand World and was the spokesperson of this plane. Now, the Great Thousand World couldn't wait to kick him out. He couldn't figure it out. As for Qinglong, he was also puzzled. No matter how he looked at it, he couldn't explain this phenomenon in Luo Zheng's body.
In the end, Qinglong attributed the problem to the Primal Chaos Secret Technique that Luo Zheng cultivated. The problem could only be here because ascension was actually related to the Inner World. Ordinary people would only ascend after opening the Inner World, but the Inner World was formed by a sea of Vital Qi.
Although Luo Zheng didn't open the Inner World, it didn't contain Vital Qi, but Chaotic Qi!
Luo Zheng could only agree with this conjecture. However, the Primal Chaos Secret Technique that he cultivated had embarked on a completely different path from others. Perhaps even his master couldn't explain it. Only Qinglong's conjecture was the most reliable.
On the fifth day, Luo Zheng had to spare one-third of his strength to suppress himself!
On the sixth day, Luo Zheng had to use the power of the dragon scale. Only in this way could he prevent himself from being "ascended".
At this time, Luo Zheng knew that he probably couldn't drag it out until today.
In the past two days, Ning Yudie and Su Lingyun had completely stopped cultivating. After this separation, they didn't know when they would meet again. As for Xi Youqin, she had been sticking to Luo Zheng all day long. These two days, she had stuck to him even tighter. She also knew that after Luo Zheng left, she would also leave the Central Domain and enter the Indefinite Tower of the Extreme Purple Realm to cultivate.
Xi Youqin was struggling with another problem. The Upper World was so big that her brain couldn't even imagine it. Even if she succeeded in cultivating in the Extreme Purple Realm, how could she find Luo Zheng in the future?
The power of the dragon scale was not endless. Luo Zheng continued to use the power of the dragon scale to suppress himself. Even the dragon scales seemed to dim. Fortunately, Luo Zheng had a lot of dragon scales lit up in his body. After this batch of dragon scales dimmed, he could extract the power of another batch of dragon scales.
At noon, Xi Youqin hurriedly cooked a table full of dishes.
The family gathered in the Ice Palace of the Cloud Hall. The expressions on the faces of the three girls were different, but their eyes showed that they were reluctant to part with him.
Luo Zheng was thinking about things in his mind. After all, there were some details that he wanted to clarify. However, with Ning Yudie's current strength, there seemed to be no one in the Central Domain who could pose a threat to her. He was just afraid that she would accidentally enter the reverse side of the world in the future, so he would remind her of these things …
At night, Luo Zheng could no longer suppress the repulsive force.
The moment Luo Zheng withdrew the power of the dragon scale, a bright silver light enveloped Luo Zheng. In the light, Luo Zheng smiled faintly at Ning Yudie and the other two girls.
Ning Yudie bit her lips and wanted to say something, but the bright silver light was getting denser and denser. Luo Zheng's entire body was submerged in it, and his appearance could no longer be seen clearly. Then, he suddenly shot up into the sky!
Because it was nighttime, this brilliant ray of light was even more dazzling than a bolt of lightning. Everyone in an area of 500km around the city had witnessed this scene, and all sorts of rumors arose. However, they suddenly didn't know that the number one expert of the central territory had ascended to the higher dimensions amidst this ray of light.
The three girls stared blankly at the empty hall. Suddenly, they felt that something was missing in their hearts.
Among them, Xi Youqin was the calmest. She still believed that it wouldn't be long before she could meet Luo Zheng again!
At this moment, she looked at Ning Yudie's uncomfortable expression and suddenly said, "What were you planning to say to Luo Zheng just now?"
Ning Yudie pursed her lips and lowered her head to look at the floor. She said, "Nothing …."
When she saw Ning Yudie's expression, how could Xi Youqin believe Ning Yudie's words? However, since Ning Yudie didn't say anything, Xi Youqin couldn't force her.
Ning Yudie had just turned around when she suddenly clutched her chest. Her throat felt uncomfortable, and she couldn't help but retch.
Xi Youqin and Su Lingyun stared blankly at her. Suddenly, they understood something. Ning Yudie was actually pregnant.
In fact, Ning Yudie had realized that she was pregnant several days ago. However, she didn't have the time to tell Luo Zheng. In the past two days, she suddenly heard that Luo Zheng couldn't suppress himself and was about to be kicked out of this world. This made her even more anxious.
A cultivator's control over their body was far beyond that of ordinary people. In front of Luo Zheng, it was naturally easy for her to suppress her morning sickness. However, a living creature was forming in her stomach. This was a normal reaction. Suppressing it wasn't a good thing. It was only after Luo Zheng left that she started retching ….
Seeing Ning Yudie's uncomfortable expression, Xi Youqin muttered, "Why didn't you tell him?"
Ning Yudie shook her head. "Now isn't the time."
Xi Youqin smiled faintly. "It doesn't matter. When I meet Luo Zheng in the future, I'll tell him!"
"What do you mean?" The feeling of retching finally dissipated. When Ning Yudie heard this, she was also puzzled.
"Nothing." Xi Youqin smiled faintly. "I'm also leaving the Central Domain. Tomorrow, I'll go to the Void Spirit Sect to bid farewell to my great-grandfather and Xi Youjie!"
"You're going to the Purple Extreme Realm?" Ning Yudie asked with a frown.
Although Luo Zheng didn't say it, Ning Yudie knew that he wanted her to protect Xi Youqin and Su Lingyun. After all, she was the strongest now.
"Yes!" Xi Youqin nodded proudly. In the absence of Luo Zheng, Xi Youqin's temperament had returned to its original state.
Ning Yudie didn't expect this at all. She said almost reflexively, "I won't allow it."
However, how could Xi Youqin listen to Ning Yudie? She just snorted coldly. "You can't control me!"
Ning Yudie also knew that she couldn't interfere with Xi Youqin. In fact, on the day of her dual cultivation ceremony with Luo Zheng, she also saw the old man from the Celestial Clan talking a lot with Xi Youqin. Xi Youqin seemed to be exuberant, so it must be related to this matter. Looking at Xi Youqin's calm and collected appearance now, Ning Yudie was even more certain.
Therefore, Ning Yudie even had a hint of selfishness when she stopped Xi Youqin this time. It was probably because she couldn't ascend to the Upper Realm now because her cultivation wasn't enough. Naturally, she didn't want Xi Youqin to go to the Purple Extreme Realm. It was too shameless to save the country in such a roundabout way …
The two women were arguing, but they didn't notice Su Lingyun next to them.
The determination in Su Lingyun's heart was magnified countless times at this time. She knew that her talent was the weakest, so she had to work ten times harder than the two women to catch up to them.

Chapter 980
Enveloped by the bright silver light, Luo Zheng's speed grew faster and faster, and he flew higher and higher.
An invisible force pushed him away from this plane, shooting him toward the boundless sky.
Creatures that were too powerful would be rejected by the entire plane. The experts of the Celestial Clan should have used some kind of secret technique to avoid this repulsive force. It was just like how people who didn't know how to swim would find it difficult to overcome their own buoyancy and sink to the bottom of the water.
If Luo Zheng wanted to return to the Vast Thousand World in the future, he would probably have to master this secret technique.
Luo Zheng looked down at the dark and lofty continent below him. In the beginning, he could faintly see some lights coming from Cloudsea City, Apocalypse City, and a few other large cities. But as he flew up, the entire continent became smaller and smaller in his field of vision. After a while, Luo Zheng could already see the Divine Kingdom Continent and the Central Domain Continent.
"Whoosh … whoosh …"
The sound of wind mixed with the sound of metal colliding could be heard. It was the Astral Wind that was attacking him.
At this moment, the Astral Wind, which was invincible, was unable to cause any harm to Luo Zheng. The bright silver light formed a special layer of protective film. Ordinary attacks seemed to only be able to pass through Luo Zheng's body and were unable to cause any harm to him.
After passing through the Astral Wind layer, Luo Zheng continued to fly higher.
When Luo Zheng cultivated the Stellar Battle Body, he had already experienced this. But at that time, Luo Zheng relied on the gravity of the stars to guide his perception to soar. Now, Luo Zheng's physical body was roaming the sky! However, this wasn't Luo Zheng's own strength. Instead, he was relying on the repulsive force of the Vast Thousand World.
As Luo Zheng moved further and further away from the Vast Thousand World, his connection with the Vast Thousand World gradually weakened. At this moment, Luo Zheng suddenly thought of an important matter.
When he was in the Great Wide World, Luo Zheng could transfer his power to any place in the world at will. Now that he had left the Great Wide World, could he still do it?
With the miraculous ability to transfer power, Luo Zheng was almost invincible in this plane. No matter how powerful the power was, it was almost impossible to cause any substantial harm to Luo Zheng, unless the power was so strong that the entire Vast Thousand World couldn't bear it. In that case, the entire Vast Thousand World would be destroyed.
However, after leaving the Great Wide World, would this ability still be effective?
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng's heart stirred. He wanted to transfer his power, but … Luo Zheng's face revealed a trace of a bitter smile. The transfer of power had already lost its effect.
This important ability was lost after leaving the Great Wide World.
Although Luo Zheng felt a little regretful, his mentality soon became calm. After all, the transfer of power was not Luo Zheng's own ability, but a strength that could only be exerted with the help of the Vast Thousand World.
The cultivation of a cultivator emphasized more on his own strength. Relying on external forces could make him strong for a while, but in the end, he couldn't be proud for a lifetime. Relying too much on this ability would only make Luo Zheng unable to fully gain experience!
However, although the ability to transfer power could not be used, the memory of the Vast Thousand World still existed in Luo Zheng's mind. He still maintained a weak connection with the Vast Thousand World and could look up the past and present events of the entire Vast Thousand World.
Therefore, even after leaving this plane, Luo Zheng could still observe the movements of Ning Yudie and the others …
The speed of the physical body was far slower than the speed of perception, and it was also slower than the ascension channel opened by the Rising Dragon Platform. Luo Zheng was like a leaf drifting in the wind, drifting in the vast universe.
For a few days, Luo Zheng maintained a motionless posture.
Luo Zheng had seen the scenery in the universe dozens of times. This time, he closed his eyes and observed the movements in the Cloud Hall through the memory of the Vast Thousand World …
He saw Ning Yudie sitting in the ice palace in a daze, looking up at the sky in a slight daze. Seeing Xi Youqin like this, Luo Zheng felt a little distressed.
He also saw Su Lingyun with a serious expression on her face, cultivating meticulously …
However, where was Xi Youqin? Why was she missing?
He transferred his thoughts into that mass of memory, but he could not find any trace of her in the entire Vast Thousand World.
With Xi Youqin's cultivation, it was impossible for her to ascend. At present, there was only one explanation — she had already gone to the Extreme Purple Realm.
Why did she go to the Extreme Purple Realm?
Luo Zheng was also somewhat puzzled. Ever since the old man from the Demi-Immortal Clan came once, Xi Youqin had been evasive several times. She was hiding something from Luo Zheng.
After thinking for a long time, Luo Zheng still could not figure it out, so he could only let it be. This woman was extremely obedient in front of him, but her willfulness was almost innate. Whatever she wanted to do, Luo Zheng could not control it.
It took him a month to ascend to the Immortal Realm.
After a month, Luo Zheng's light suddenly scattered, and he rushed into a big dark hole at an extremely high speed. After passing through the big hole, a loud noise suddenly came from above.
"Boom!"
This violent noise was like thousands of thunderbolts, shaking Luo Zheng's ears, almost making Luo Zheng faint, and his whole mind was a mess.
When Luo Zheng gradually regained his consciousness, he found himself lying in a square well.
The clear water in this square well glowed with a light blue light, and the well water was so clear that one could see the bottom. Soaking in this light blue water gave Luo Zheng an extremely comfortable feeling.
In addition to Luo Zheng, there were several other people in this square well!
Like Luo Zheng, these people's faces were full of confusion, as if they did not know their situation.
Luo Zheng casually sized them up. These people were all in the Divine Sea Realm, but they seemed to have just stepped into the Divine Sea Realm. These people's strength was far inferior to Zhou Huang and the Tianfeng War Emperor in the Divine Kingdom Continent. Even the three Mansion Masters of the Profound Jade Martial House were far inferior.
These people should have ascended as well …
Luo Zheng guessed in his heart. Moreover, they were warriors who had just cultivated to the Divine Sea Realm and chose to ascend.
Because of the Ascension Passage in the Divine Kingdom Continent, many Divine Sea Realm warriors were unable to ascend. In the long years, their cultivation had risen to the middle stage of the Divine Sea Realm, and even the late stage of the Divine Sea Realm. These warriors who had just entered the Divine Sea Realm naturally could not be mentioned in the same breath.
"This, is this the Upper Realm …" A warrior looked at this square well for a long time and finally asked.
Another person nodded, "It should be! But what's up with this square well? "
The others shook their heads silently.
However, in the corner, a woman in plain clothes said, "This should be the Ascension Well."
"The Ascension Well?" The others asked at the same time.
The woman in plain clothes nodded, "I heard that some Holy Lands in the Upper Realm use the Ascension Well to guide the ascenders from the universe. I guess these square wells should be the Ascension Well, but I don't know which Holy Land we ascended to."
"Must it be the Holy Land of the Human Race? What if it's some other race's Holy Land? "A bearded man lightly said.
The woman in plain clothes continued to say, "This situation is relatively rare because there is a mysterious karmic connection between races. However … I heard that there are cases of people ascending to other races' Great Worlds. For instance, a human Holy Land has just been taken away by the Demon Race. If we ascend here, I'm afraid …"
"I'm afraid what?" A tall and thin warrior asked.
The woman in plain clothes faintly smiled, "If we fall into the hands of the Demon Race, we naturally won't be able to survive."
Hearing this, the others' complexions changed!
Those that could reach the Divine Sea Realm and ascend to the Upper Realm were all peak powerhouses in the Lower Realm they came from. The reason they chose to leave in such a hurry was because of their own ambitions, or perhaps it was driven by their own ambitions. They wanted to reach a higher level of cultivation and pursue the pinnacle of martial arts.
If they ascended to the Upper Realm and ended up in the wrong place, or even fell into the hands of the Demon Race or some other incomparably cruel race, then they wouldn't even have a place to cry.
To die before they even achieved victory, everyone was afraid of this situation.

Chapter 981
Only after breaking through to the Divine Sea Realm could one ascend. This had almost become the law of the universe.
Were the people in the Ascension Well all at the Divine Sea Realm? How could there be a Life and Death Realm kid in front of them?
Moreover, when the plain-clothed woman talked about ascending to other races, she quietly paid attention to Luo Zheng. She also noticed that this Life and Death Realm kid was the calmest. This calmness wasn't an act, but came from his heart. This was the characteristic of a Martial Heart that was extremely stable.
Indeed, they were all Divine Sea Realm warriors. Cultivating to this point, their Martial Hearts had been baptized by years of trials and tribulations. However, it was still difficult to be as calm as Luo Zheng.
It was also because of this that the plain-clothed woman paid more attention to Luo Zheng.
"Although the water in this well is comfortable, it's not a good idea to keep soaking in it! Let's go out first! "One of the warriors suggested.
This suggestion was immediately seconded by the others. One of them suddenly felt his body become light. His whole body floated above the well water, and he immediately rushed towards the well.
However, that person only rushed halfway before a muffled sound rang out. It was as if his head had hit an invisible wall, and with a plop, he fell back into the well.
"What's going on?"
"There seems to be a transparent cover above this well?"
The others asked one after another.
Luo Zheng indifferently glanced at the sky above the well. At the same time, he also jumped up and stretched his hand towards the void.
Sure enough, there was an invisible barrier above this well. If he wanted to break through this well, he had to open this barrier first.
Luo Zheng was extremely confident in his own strength, but he didn't do so. The key point was that Luo Zheng was unfamiliar with this place. It was better for him not to act rashly. Although he wasn't afraid of trouble, he didn't want to get into trouble for no reason.
"Plop!"
After sinking into the well water, Luo Zheng indifferently said, "There is indeed a barrier above. It should be man-made."
The few people in the well nodded. Everyone curiously sized up Luo Zheng. The more the bearded warrior looked, the more confused he became. He laughed and said, "How could you ascend when you're only at the Life and Death Realm? Is there a mistake? "
"Right! A Life and Death realm brat can actually ascend? I've never heard of such a thing!"
"Originally, I thought that since I had just opened my Deity Ocean, I would be at the bottom of the Upper Realm. I didn't expect that there would be a Life and Death Realm martial artist here. Hehe …"
There was a trace of disdain in the words of these people.
They were all Spirit Sea Realm warriors after all. How could they take Life and Death Realm warriors seriously?
Only the plain-clothed woman didn't look at Luo Zheng this way. In her opinion, ascending with the cultivation of Life and Death realm meant that he had broken the rules of ascension. Such a person was truly frightening. If her premonition was correct, this person's strength was absolutely not as simple as Life and Death realm!
Hearing their words, Luo Zheng's face was still indifferent. He stretched his body, trying to get as close to the well water as possible.
They didn't expect to soak in the well water for half a month. During this half a month, everyone could only stay in this small well, which was a bit boring. Moreover, the enchantment above the well could even isolate the world's origin energy. When they cultivated, they could only use the best grade true origin stones they carried with them to replenish their true essence.
Half a month later, everyone suddenly heard a series of footsteps.
Not long after, an armored warrior stood on the edge of the well, indifferently looking at the group of people below. At the same time, they heard the armored warrior say, "They should have been bathing for half a month. I don't know if this group of bumpkins from the Lower Realm have washed off the smell of dirt on their bodies!"
Hearing this, everyone in the well suddenly flew into a rage!
It was true that they came from the Lower Realm. However, as the top existences in the Lower Realm, they were usually aloof! Some of them were originally the kings of Divine Kingdoms. How could they endure such humiliation when they were the object of worship of countless creatures in a Divine Kingdom?
What's more, heroes didn't matter where they came from. How could warriors from the Lower Realm be so unbearable?
Therefore, the faces of those warriors were extremely gloomy. They angrily stared at the armored warrior above the well. However, when they thought that they couldn't do anything, they couldn't do anything.
Luo Zheng smiled slightly. His eyes were full of indifference. It seemed that this big world was similar to the Lower Realm. There was no lack of such people everywhere.
The armored warrior above the well felt the angry gazes below and laughed. "I know you are not convinced. However, to be honest, if you didn't bathe your bodies in the well of purification and jump out directly, I'm afraid you wouldn't be able to withstand the violent Yuan Qi of Heaven and Earth! Do you think our big world is the same as your small place? "
"Violent Yuan Qi of Heaven and Earth? What do you mean?" Everyone looked at each other.
The armored warrior laughed. "Anyway, you have been soaking in the well water for half a month. It seems that it's about time. I'll let you feel it!"
After saying this, the armored warrior undid the enchantment above the well!
The moment the armored warrior undid the enchantment, everyone felt a violent and chaotic power sweep over from above the well!
The expressions of the people in the well changed. When this violent power was released, it immediately gave them an invisible pressure, causing them to feel extremely uncomfortable.
"What's the matter with the Yuan Qi of Heaven and Earth?"
"It can even stimulate my Dantian …"
"My True Core Sea is stirring up turbulent waves!"
Even the plain-clothed woman, who knew a lot about the Upper Realm, didn't look good.
All of a sudden, these Spirit Sea Realm warriors found that their True Essence Sea was reacting with the violent natural essence. The seawater formed by the True Essence in their Inner Worlds was rolling up strange waves, as if their entire Inner Worlds were about to roll over. Of course, this feeling was uncomfortable.
Some martialists who couldn't bear it realized that the water in the well was the only thing that could protect them. Hence, they submerged their entire bodies into the well water. This was the only way to make them feel better.
"What a violent Yuan Qi of Heaven and Earth." Luo Zheng also felt the abnormality of the Yuan Qi of Heaven and Earth.
The Yuan Qi of Heaven and Earth was almost omnipresent in the universe. However, the Yuan Qi of Heaven and Earth in this big world was completely different from the Yuan Qi of Heaven and Earth in the Great Thousand World! If the Yuan Qi of Heaven and Earth in the Great Thousand World was described as a docile sheep, then the Yuan Qi of Heaven and Earth in this big world was a ferocious tiger or leopard!
However, no matter how violent the Yuan Qi of Heaven and Earth was, it didn't have much effect on Luo Zheng.
The reason was simple. He didn't have any True Core in his body. After practicing the Secret Skill of Primal Chaos, Luo Zheng's Dantian had undergone earth-shaking changes. Even if there was only Chaotic Qi in his Dantian, it wouldn't be affected by the violent Yuan Qi of Heaven and Earth.
Luo Zheng looked at these Divine Sea Realm experts with a strange expression. "Even if the Yuan Qi of Heaven and Earth is violent and hard to tame and absorb, these Divine Sea Realm warriors shouldn't have such a reaction, right?" Of course, he was asking Qinglong. If even the Divine Sea Realm experts couldn't stand the Yuan Qi of Heaven and Earth, then what about the other warriors in the big world?
The big world was rich in resources, but after all, not everyone was a Divine Sea Realm expert. There must be countless mortals, countless Primordial creatures, and countless Divine Reflection Realm warriors …
If even the Divine Sea Realm experts couldn't stand it, how could those Primordial creatures survive?
Qinglong smiled faintly in Luo Zheng's mind. "It's nothing strange. The reason is simple. It's like the fish that live in the deep sea. They can't survive in the shallow sea. If they are fished out of the deep sea by force, I'm afraid they will explode and die."
"I know. It's because of the water pressure." Luo Zheng nodded.
"Yes, the same problem exists in the Yuan Qi of Heaven and Earth. It's called the pressure of the Yuan Qi. Although they are Divine Sea Realm experts, they also need a period of adaptation. After this period of adaptation, they won't feel so uncomfortable," Qinglong answered.
"But when I went to the Zhenlong Field, I didn't feel the pressure of the Yuan Qi!" Luo Zheng asked again.
Luo Zheng had not only been to the Zhenlong Field, but also to the Extreme Purple Realm. Ning Yudie and Xi Youqin had also been there. At that time, Luo Zheng didn't feel anything strange.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 982
Qing Long explained to Luo Zheng's confusion. There were differences between big realms. The Zhenlong Realm and the Extreme Purple Realm were not complete big realms. The Yuan Qi of Heaven and Earth in these realms was almost the same as in the lower realms …
Luo Zheng nodded slightly. At this moment, the soldier saw the reactions of the Divine Sea Realm experts in the square wells and burst into laughter. "Look at you guys. Haha, every time I open a well, I see this scene. It's so funny!"
Although the Divine Sea Realm experts in the well water were angry, they could do nothing about it. The feeling of the Vital Spirit Sea rolling in their bodies was too uncomfortable. No one wanted to float up from the well water.
"It seems that there is no one worth seeing in this well. Take your time to get used to it!" After saying this, the soldier retreated.
Since the seal on the square wells had disappeared, there was no need for Luo Zheng to stay in the well water. Besides, he was not affected by the pressure of the Yuan Qi at all. He jumped up from the well and stood firmly on the edge of the well.
He glanced around and a look of surprise appeared on his face. He didn't expect there to be so many square wells around!
There were many square wells around Luo Zheng. Not far away, there were more than a dozen soldiers checking the wells one by one. "There are so many Ascension Wells. There must be hundreds of Divine Sea Realm experts in them. And they are only Divine Sea Realm experts who have ascended from the lower realms! "
Luo Zheng couldn't help but sigh in his heart.
However, he didn't consider the feelings of the soldiers when he jumped up.
Luo Zheng stood beside the square wells and immediately attracted the attention of the soldiers. Under their helmets, their eyes were focused on Luo Zheng. Their eyes were full of astonishment.
"Is there … a mistake?"
"Didn't the seal on the well just disappear? How can a guy adapt to the pressure of the Yuan Qi in such a short time?"
"Freak!"
Several soldiers stared at Luo Zheng in a daze.
"You've got the point wrong. This guy is not only a freak, he is a freak among freaks. This guy is only in the Life and Death Realm!" A soldier suddenly said.
Many soldiers just realized that Luo Zheng was only in the Life and Death Realm. They were completely stunned.
These soldiers were all in the Divine Sea Realm. Their task was to guide the ascenders from the lower realms. Over the years, they had guided many ascenders.
Most ascenders needed to stay in the well for ten days to half a month. The purpose was to let them gradually get used to the Yuan Qi of the World. The next step was to open the seal and let the Yuan Qi in the upper realms in order to let them get used to it!
If an ascender was suddenly thrown into this violent Yuan Qi, it would probably cause the entire True Yuan Sea to flow backward. The Dantian was the foundation of a martial artist. Every movement in the Dantian would have a great impact on the martial artist. Under the reverse flow of the True Yuan Sea, the lightest injury would be quite serious. In the worst case, one would suffer Qi Deviation or even directly explode. In other words, one would die from the explosion of the Dantian.
Therefore, after opening the seal, there was usually half a month to adapt.
Ascenders with stronger adaptability were able to adapt within ten days, while ascenders with excellent physiques were even able to adapt within seven days.
However, there were always some monstrous characters in this world who could adapt to the pressure of the Yuan Qi in about three days after opening the seal.
It was just that the number of such characters was extremely small. Often, one or two would only be seen in many years.
According to the older generation of martial artists who served as guides, there was once a person who ascended from the Lower Realm. It was said that he had adapted to the Essence Qi pressure within four hours, and that fellow was now the famous Empyrean Illusory Sea!
But now …
They seemed to have just opened the seal. The time given to this Life and Death Realm martial artist to adapt was less than half an hour.
How did this guy suddenly jump out? Moreover, he looked like he was fine!
What was even more frightening was that this guy seemed to be only at the Life and Death Realm. When would Life and Death Realm martial artists be able to ascend?
The minds of these soldiers were suddenly in a mess. They even suspected that something had happened in the universe. Did the rules of ascension change?
"You!" The soldier who had just left the square well turned his head and rushed towards Luo Zheng. However, when he arrived in front of Luo Zheng, his powerful aura rapidly weakened. He also couldn't think straight. However, at the last moment, he still managed to think straight …
He couldn't treat this guy as an ordinary ascender from the lower realms. This kind of character's growth speed was astonishing. If he made a name for himself in the future, not to mention becoming a Celestial or World Lord, even if he wanted to obtain an important position in the Holy Land, he wouldn't be an existence he could provoke.
He even regretted that when he had just opened the barrier, he shouldn't have ridiculed the cultivator in this square well. He had accidentally injured this fellow in front of him, so he only hoped that this fellow wouldn't take it to heart.
Therefore, when he rushed in front of Luo Zheng, a smile instantly appeared on his face. He said politely, "This friend's ability to adapt is not bad. Welcome to the upper realms and become a member of our Lingwu Holy Land!"
This guy's expression changed so quickly that even Luo Zheng secretly sighed. Although Luo Zheng hated this guy's words, he wouldn't really take this guy seriously. He just asked indifferently, "This is the Lingwu Holy Land of the human race?"
"Yes!" The soldier said proudly, "Lingwu Holy Land is one of the three great tenth-rank Holy Lands in the Spiritual Realm!"
Tenth-rank Holy Land …
Luo Zheng nodded. The four Divine Kingdoms could barely be called an eighth-rank Holy Land. A tenth-rank Holy Land was probably the pinnacle of Holy Lands. It was just that he didn't know how big the Lingwu Holy Land was and how many experts there were.
When the soldier saw that Luo Zheng didn't want to take it to heart, he took out a pair of black shackles and waved it in front of Luo Zheng. "Sorry, please put on these shackles …"
Seeing this pair of shackles, Luo Zheng frowned slightly. "What do you mean?"
Probably because they saw that Luo Zheng's expression wasn't good, two soldiers came over. One of them said in a businesslike manner, "Nothing. According to the rules of our Lingwu Holy Land, anyone who ascends must put on shackles and serve our Lingwu Holy Land. They can only leave after meeting certain conditions!"
"Is it the same for everyone?" Luo Zheng frowned even more.
According to Luo Zheng's plan, after ascending, he has two choices. The first is to become an independent warrior and gain experience in the universe. The second is to find out about his sister's whereabouts! "
After his strength increased, he would help Xun return to the Yao Ye Clan. It would be best if he could help Xun get rid of that bitch in the Yao Ye Clan. Then, he would help Qinglong build the Heavenly Divination Body …
Of course, each of these goals was more difficult than the last!
For example, Xun's strength was at the level of a Realm Lord. With Luo Zheng's current strength, it was unrealistic for him to face a Realm Lord. It would take him a long time to achieve this goal.
As for helping Qinglong build the Heavenly Divination Body, it wouldn't be easy either. Finding Luo Yan was the most difficult goal to achieve. At the same time, it was also a goal that had to be achieved. That young man's strength was still vivid in Luo Zheng's mind. He couldn't even compare to a millionth of that young man's strength. This gap was too wide.
Time was still pressing for Luo Zheng. He didn't feel the slightest bit relaxed.
He had just ascended, but he was about to be locked up like a prisoner and enslaved. It would be strange if Luo Zheng was in a good mood!
Seeing Luo Zheng's unhappy expression, the soldiers' auras also became cold. In their eyes, Luo Zheng might be an outstanding person among the ascenders, but he was still an ascender. Even his cultivation was only at the Life and Death Realm.
"These are the rules. Regardless of whether you follow the rules or not, you have to serve. You have no choice." One of the soldiers smiled faintly and said, "So don't make things difficult for us!"

Chapter 983
After Luo Zheng's hands were locked, he looked at the material of the shackles and shook his head slightly.
The resources in the Upper World were not as exaggerated as he thought. The shackles were just middle-grade sacred tools. It was wishful thinking for them to bind him. Although there were some mysterious divine patterns on the shackles, Luo Zheng was still confident that he could break them with his strength alone.
However, as these soldiers said, he didn't need to make things difficult for them. Not to mention that he was facing a group of Divine Sea Realm experts. With his current strength, he couldn't escape.
Since he had ascended to the Spiritual World, he needed to act according to the circumstances. Now, Luo Zheng had already passed the stage of being a hothead. He knew what it meant to be patient.
After putting on the shackles, Luo Zheng was led by a soldier to a pavilion not far away to wait.
Before the soldier left, Luo Zheng heard something in his Spirit Beast Pouch. Then, there was a "Gu Gu" sound.
The soldier looked at Luo Zheng and asked curiously, "You brought a Spirit Beast with you when you ascended? Is it a contract beast that you raised yourself? "
Hearing the soldier's question, Luo Zheng knew that something was wrong.
The Ancestral Dragon was something that the Demi-Immortal Clan wanted to capture. It was no small matter. If it was discovered, it would cause trouble. However, Luo Zheng's expression was still calm. He smiled and said, "Yes, a contract beast caught in the Lower World."
The soldier nodded faintly, then turned and left. In the eyes of warriors from the Upper World, what good things could there be in the Lower World? He was too lazy to check.
When the soldier left, Luo Zheng thought that it was a close call. He patted the Spirit Beast Pouch and said, "Little guy, don't make any noise. If you were taken away, I wouldn't be able to save you."
After these days of growth, the Ancestral Dragon's intelligence had reached the level of an eight or nine-year-old child. Naturally, it could understand Luo Zheng's words.
Before they ascended, Xi Youqin had made a lot of food and stored it in the Spirit Beast Pouch. It seemed that this little guy was almost done eating.
Actually, with the Ancestral Dragon's physique as a divine artifact, it didn't need to eat at all. It only needed to breathe in the Heaven and Earth Yuan Qi to grow. However, because Xi Youqin ate this fellow, it naturally became picky. This time, it probably couldn't hold back anymore.
Luo Zheng's threat was very effective. In the end, it left an impression on Luo Zheng. It was afraid of being separated from its "mother", so it curled up in the spirit beast bag and stayed motionless, no longer making any sound.
Half a month had passed as they waited ….
In the first seven days, not a single ascender was able to enter the pavilion. On the tenth day, the first cultivator to enter was the plain-clothed woman. The plain-clothed woman's hands were shackled, and when she looked at Luo Zheng, there was a look of surprise in her eyes.
She seemed to have learned about the rules of the Upper Realm through other means. She also knew that it wasn't easy to adapt to the Heaven and Earth Yuan Qi after leaving the Ascension Well.
Even so, it took her ten days before she dared to leave the Ascension Well. Compared to Luo Zheng, who had completely adapted to the Heaven and Earth Yuan Qi in the time it took for a stick of incense to burn, they couldn't be compared at all …
The plain-clothed woman sat next to Luo Zheng and asked directly, "How did you do it?"
Luo Zheng smiled faintly. "How did I do it?"
"There's only one explanation for adapting to the Heaven and Earth Yuan Qi in such a short time." The plain-clothed woman had another theory in her mind. "You … are not an Ascender from the Lower Realm. You're an Ascender from the Upper Realm who entered the Lower Realm and then returned to the Upper Realm through the Ascension Passage. Am I right?"
Hearing the plain-clothed woman's theory, Luo Zheng's face revealed a stunned expression. He then shook his head. "I'm not from the Upper Realm."
The plain-clothed woman pursed her lips. She knew that Luo Zheng wouldn't tell the truth, so she smiled calmly. "If you're really an Ascender, you'll definitely be put in an important position in the future!"
Luo Zheng's eyes were still calm as he looked at the Ascension Wells in the distance.
The plain-clothed woman saw that Luo Zheng didn't speak, so she continued, "Actually, one of my uncles is the City Lord of Dian Cang City in the Lingwu Holy Land. After I finish my service, I can seek refuge with him. If you have nowhere else to go, you can come with me."
Hearing the plain-clothed woman's words, Luo Zheng glanced at her indifferently. Previously, in the Ascension Well, this woman didn't even know if this was a world ruled by the Human Clan. It seemed like she was pretending not to know. The City Lord of Dian Cang City, Luo Zheng, didn't know how strong he was.
However, Luo Zheng hadn't decided on his own path yet. He was still leaning towards becoming an independent cultivator. As for joining the Lingwu Holy Land, he wasn't very interested, let alone following this woman to Dian Cang City.
He planned to leave the Lingwu Holy Land as soon as he finished his service.
However, Luo Zheng didn't expect that he had thought of things too simply.
The Psychic World was a big world that was relatively remote among the Human Clan. There were a total of three forces that ruled the Psychic World. They were the Lingwu Holy Land, the Tidal Holy Land, and the Psychic Holy Land.
In addition, there were countless Holy Lands and Holy Kingdoms in the big world.
Ninth Grade, Eighth Grade, Seventh Grade, Sixth Grade … There were all kinds of Holy Lands and Holy Kingdoms. As for Fifth Grade, Fourth Grade, Third Grade, and Second Grade Sects, they were as numerous as the hairs on a cow.
The Lingwu Holy Land and the Tidal Holy Land belonged to the weaker ranks among the Tenth Grade Holy Lands. As for the Psychic Holy Land, since it dared to use the word "psychic" in its name, and this world was called the Psychic World, it was naturally the core of the Psychic World!
Regardless of how big a big world was, just the area occupied by the Lingwu Holy Land was so big that Luo Zheng couldn't imagine it!
With Luo Zheng's current flying speed, it would take him at least three years to leave the Lingwu Holy Land.
This was also the reason why Gu Pei Er of the Demi-Immortal Clan said that the Holy Kingdom Continent wasn't as big as her back garden. The big world was so big that it was far beyond Luo Zheng's imagination.
As for the Dian Cang City that the plain-clothed woman mentioned, it was a relatively important city in the Lingwu Holy Land. Her uncle was also an Ascender from the Lower Realm like her. To be able to rise to the position of city lord, he was considered a top figure in the Lingwu Holy Land. Under such an invitation, she fancied Luo Zheng's talent. In the future, when she went to Dian Cang City, she would recommend him to her uncle.
However, she didn't expect Luo Zheng to be so arrogant, as if his ambition wasn't here. Naturally, she wouldn't mention it again.
After the plain-clothed woman left the Ascension Well, more and more warriors gradually adapted to the Yuan Qi of Heaven and Earth. After leaving one by one, they entered the pavilion with shackles.
These Divine Sea Realm warriors were all arrogant. How could they be willing to be shackled and controlled by others? It was inevitable that they would clash with the armored warriors. However, the armored warriors were merciless. They warned these guys that no matter how glorious they were in the Lower Realm, since they had chosen to ascend, they had to endure!
"In the Lower Realm, you may be able to decide the life and death of millions of people with a single word, and you are above countless people. However, in the Upper Realm, you are nothing!" One of the armored soldiers roared at the many Divine Sea Realm Almighties.
During this process, two Divine Sea Realm warriors even fought with the armored soldiers. The result was predictable. The price the two Divine Sea Realm warriors paid was stricter supervision. They were shackled with two shackles that were twice the size of normal shackles. What's more, the shackles could be classified as Divine Artifacts. As for the divine runes on the shackles, they were naturally more powerful. In addition, there were two huge black iron balls hanging below the shackles of the two Divine Sea Realm warriors.
They didn't know what kind of iron balls these were. Even the Divine Sea Realm warriors found it difficult to drag these iron balls. It was likely that it would be difficult for them to even fly while carrying these iron balls.
After another three days, all the Divine Sea Realm warriors left the Ascension Well. These Divine Sea Realm warriors stayed in the pavilion with Luo Zheng.
After the armored warriors did a headcount, they recorded the names of all the warriors. Then, they escorted them to a Transmission Array.
The big world was too big. Even Divine Ocean Realm or Extreme God Realm Almighties had to use the Teleportation Channel to travel. Otherwise, the distance between two big cities would be hundreds of millions of miles. It would take them at least a year and a half to get there …

Chapter 984
Before entering the teleportation formation, Luo Zheng had noticed that the divine markings on the teleportation formation were extremely special.
The teleportation formations in the Central Domain were all constructed from talismans and powered by True Origin Stones. The principle behind it was actually very simple. It was to use the talismans to expand a teleportation path, and then connect it with a teleportation formation in another place. Through the teleportation path, the distance between two places could be shortened. So, it would take an ordinary cultivator a few days to fly from Revelation City to Ocean of Clouds City, but it would only take less than the time for an incense stick to burn through the teleportation path.
However, the structure of the teleportation formation before him was completely different. Even though the divine markings were extremely unfamiliar to Luo Zheng, he was able to roughly analyze their uses.
Not only did the teleportation formation expand a teleportation path, but it also seemed to be connected to something. As for what it was, Luo Zheng really couldn't guess.
After the armored guard activated the teleportation formation, all the ascenders entered in a single file. Under the flickering white light, they entered the Ascension Path.
When they just entered the Ascension Path, there was nothing unusual. It looked almost the same as the teleportation paths in the lower realms. The entire teleportation path was azure blue.
Not long after, they passed through the azure blue teleportation path, and suddenly, an enormous teleportation path appeared before them!
"This teleportation path is actually so stable!" After seeing the teleportation path, Luo Zheng sighed with emotion.
The enormous teleportation path was light purple in color. Ordinary teleportation paths were only around 30 meters wide, but this enormous teleportation path was actually over 300 meters wide!
What surprised Luo Zheng the most was that this enormous teleportation path was far more stable than ordinary teleportation paths. The surrounding space seemed to have solidified, and it would probably not be easy for anyone to break open this teleportation path ….
However, as soon as they entered, they saw two cultivators flying over from the side of the teleportation path.
The two cultivators sized up the ascenders, and one of them chuckled and said, "The harvest this time is huge. I'm afraid there are over 1,000 ascenders?"
The armored guard who escorted Luo Zheng said, "What's the use of that? The exam is at hand. How can our Spirit Martial Holy Land count on these dirty ascenders from the lower realms?" As he spoke, the soldier took out thousands of top-grade True Origin Stones from his Sumeru Ring and said, "This time, the number of people using this transmission channel has reached a thousand. Can you give me a discount?"
That person counted the True Origin Stones and smiled faintly. He then picked out over a hundred True Origin Stones from the pile and returned them to the soldier. "There's no need to mention discounts. In any case, it's your Spirit Martial Holy Land that's paying for it …"
After the armored soldier received the returned True Origin Stones, he distributed a few to each of the other armored soldiers present. Clearly, he had pocketed them for himself.
After that, the soldier shouted at the ascenders, "Alright, everyone, follow behind me. Let's set off!"
When the ascenders heard the armored soldier's words, although they were dissatisfied, they did not act rashly in the end. They all followed behind the armored soldiers and continued to advance through the enormous teleportation channel.
"Where did this light purple portal come from? There's a fee? "Luo Zheng naturally wouldn't ask the armored soldiers, so he asked Azure Dragon.
Qinglong smiled faintly and replied, "This kind of large-scale teleportation channel is the Great World Channel! If you want to open a Great World Channel, you have to be at least a Realm Lord. "
"Great World Channel?"
"Yes, a Great World is too vast. For example, the small portal in the Central Domain can only be built for a distance of a hundred thousand miles at most. If it's too far, it's very difficult to stabilize," Qinglong said faintly.
Luo Zheng nodded slightly. Just like the super portal that Daoist Tian Miao built from the Central Domain to the Sea God Continent, because the distance was too far, the portal was very unstable. When Luo Zheng went from the Central Domain to the Sea God Continent, he encountered a violent spatial storm and almost lost his life. Even when he returned from the Sea God Continent, Luo Zheng's cultivation had improved steadily, but he still suffered heavy injuries.
The size and depth of a Great World were far beyond the imagination of the lower world. If a Divine Sea Realm cultivator wanted to cross a Great World, it would take hundreds of years, not one or two years!
However, there were countless capable people in the Upper World. Almost every Great World had opened a Great World Channel that could connect to each other. There were even some inter-realm channels, so the distance of transmission was even more ridiculous.
It took a lot of time and effort to build these portals, and it wasn't easy to maintain them. Therefore, to use these portals, one had to pay, and the price wasn't cheap. One person's head was one top-grade True Origin Stone, and the transmission was only a short distance, about two to three million miles. If it crossed an entire Great World, the cost of the portal would reach tens of thousands of top-grade True Origin Stones.
As for the cost of crossing an entire Great World, it would be one million top-grade True Origin Stones …
Luo Zheng was quite rich. When he was mining in the four Divine Kingdoms, he had exchanged a lot of top-grade True Origin Stones. However, now Luo Zheng realized that all his wealth added together couldn't even afford to cross an entire Great World Channel.
While sighing, Luo Zheng followed the crowd and flew through the giant portal.
An hour later, the soldiers in front suddenly stopped and left through a side tunnel. Everyone returned to the ordinary teleportation channel. Soon after, everyone left the teleportation channel and a black and steep mountain range appeared in front of them!
"This is …" Seeing the black mountain, an ascender asked.
Hearing his words, a soldier snorted and said, "This is the place where you serve. According to the Spiritual Martial Holy Land's rules, if you want to join the Spiritual Martial Holy Land, you have to dig out 100 Zhen Yuanyu here!"
"Zhen Yuanyu?"
"What's that?"
The soldier smiled faintly and said, "I know you don't know what Zhen Yuanyu is. The mountain range in front of you is a True Origin Stone mine. There are all kinds of True Origin Stones, including low-grade, high-grade, and top-grade. Of course, there are also Zhen Yuanyu. If you can't dig out any Zhen Yuanyu, it's fine. One Zhen Yuanyu can be exchanged for 10,000 top-grade True Origin Stones, so it's fine if you can dig out a million top-grade True Origin Stones!"
Hearing the soldier's explanation, the crowd immediately understood.
The best True Origin Stones in the Lower Realm were only top-grade. They didn't expect that there were purer True Origin Stones in the Upper Realm, which were Zhen Yuanyu.
However, after hearing that there were a million top-grade True Origin Stones, the crowd burst into an uproar. Wasn't this a little too much?
These Ascenders were all top-notch existences in the Lower Realm, and each of them possessed a considerable amount of wealth. However, when they ascended to the Upper Realm, they would probably only have a few tens of thousands of top-grade True Origin Stones with them. Some would probably only have a few hundred top-grade True Origin Stones with them.
Moreover, a million top-grade True Origin Stones was definitely not a small sum for any Divine Sea Realm martial artist.
The four Divine Kingdoms were relatively wealthy, but there were only 200,000 top-grade True Origin Stones in the treasury. Therefore, when they heard that they had to pay a million top-grade True Origin Stones to join the Spiritual Martial Holy Land, the Ascenders immediately burst into an uproar.
"A million …"
"How long are we going to dig?"
"We won't waste hundreds of years here, right?"
An Ascender said, "I don't want to join the Spiritual Martial Holy Land. I only want to be an independent martial artist!"
The soldier smiled and said, "You want to be an independent martial artist? Fine, then pay 200 Zhen Yuanyu! "
200 Zhen Yuanyu … That was two million top-grade True Origin Stones!
"This, this, this … Based on what!" The Ascender said angrily.
The soldier smiled faintly and said, "Based on nothing. Based on the fact that we are the Spiritual Martial Holy Land, based on the fact that you have ascended to the Spiritual Martial Holy Land!"
This tone was too familiar. The Divine Sea Realm martial artists suddenly fell silent. They had cultivated all the way to the Divine Sea Realm in the Lower Realm. How could they not understand this logic?
Under any circumstances, the question "why?" was very weak, and the answer was already clear. They relied on their own fists. Among the Spirit Sea Realm warriors present, which one of them could go against a Holy Land as big as the Upper Realm?
Seeing the depressed expressions of the Ascenders, the soldier said faintly, "Be mentally prepared. Hurry up and work. Our Spiritual Martial Holy Land is considered good. The other tenth-rank Holy Lands are even harsher!"
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 985
Everyone was silent for a while. Although they were all in the Divine Sea Realm, they had no choice but to lower their heads in the other party's territory.
However, at this moment, an ascender walked out and asked, "Can one million top-grade True Origin Stones exempt me from military service?"
The soldier replied, "It's not exempt from military service. The original meaning of military service is to let you dig for True Origin Stones! If you can pay enough True Origin Stones, you can leave at any time! "
Unexpectedly, as soon as the soldier finished speaking, the ascender took off a Sumeru Ring from his finger and gently tipped it over. A small mountain of True Origin Stones appeared in front of everyone's eyes!
These True Origin Stones were crystal clear and pure. The Heaven and Earth Origin Qi contained in them was also abnormally abundant. They were all top-grade True Origin Stones!
Although many ascenders were used to seeing big scenes, their hearts still thumped when they saw the small mountain of top-grade True Origin Stones in front of them.
"Here are one million top-grade True Origin Stones. Can you let me leave?" the ascender asked.
The soldier nodded, but then shook his head. "If you join the Spiritual Martial Holy Land, one million top-grade True Origin Stones will naturally be enough. However, if you want to become an independent warrior and leave the Spiritual Martial Holy Land or even the Spiritual World, you will need two million!"
The ascender frowned slightly and took out another Sumeru Ring. He gently waved it, and another small mountain of top-grade True Origin Stones appeared in front of everyone!
"Damn, this guy … is so rich!"
"Two million top-grade True Origin Stones in one go? Isn't this too exaggerated?"
"Some Great Thousand Worlds are indeed very rich. It is said that in the Great Thousand World next to mine, there is a huge True Origin Stone mine that runs through the entire Great Thousand World. The True Origin Stones there can't be compared because there are too many of them. They are worthless!"
With so many top grade True Essence Stones placed here, the soldier had nothing else to say. But, he never imagined that this ascender would quickly take out two mountains of True Essence Stones from his spatial ring and point at the other ascender behind him, saying, "Besides me, there is also my little brother. He also wants to go with me."
Seeing the four piles of top-grade True Origin Stones, the armored soldier picked one up and chuckled, "Of course there's no problem! This great world is vast, you can go wherever you want! "
The two ascenders nodded and left under the guidance of the other soldiers.
As the remaining people looked at the backs of the two ascenders, besides envy, they could only sigh in their hearts. Some ascenders weren't bad at all, but after ascending to the Higher Realms, who would bring so many top grade true essence stones with them for no reason?
