Luo Zheng also shook his head slightly. When he was in the four Divine Kingdoms, he had collected a lot of top-grade True Meta Stones, but he had left a considerable part of them in the Cloud Hall. When he ascended this time, he had brought nearly 200,000 top-grade True Meta Stones with him. Now, it seemed that the gap was still very large.
Luo Zheng had many valuable things on him, such as the Black Star Pickaxe. This supreme artifact was not cheap. Not to mention a million, even if it was a hundred million top-grade True Meta Stones, no one would be willing to sell it.
Luo Zheng naturally wouldn't sell it. Since he didn't have enough True Meta Stones, he would obediently mine …
The Zhen Yuanyu mentioned by the armored soldier just now had attracted Luo Zheng's interest. He only needed a hundred Zhen Yuanyu. Would he be able to dig up Zhen Yuanyu in this pitch-black mountain range in front of him?
The remaining people were led by the armored soldiers to a settlement. Around the settlement, there were several Ultimate Divine Stage experts standing guard.
In front of the settlement, there were many cultivators flying back with pickaxes. After they took out the True Meta Stones they had dug up and handed them over, they recorded the number of True Meta Stones and flew back again. Just like this, they went back and forth, digging for True Meta Stones in the mine …
After the ascenders entered the settlement, they received their respective pickaxes and nameplates according to the rules.
Although everyone was unwilling, they still had a certain fantasy in their hearts. They had the same idea as Luo Zheng. Since the Zhen Yuanyu only needed a hundred True Meta Stones, they would just dig up a hundred Zhen Yuanyu …
Coincidentally, there were more than a hundred people outside who returned to the settlement at the same time. These people took out the True Meta Stones they had dug up.
However, when Luo Zheng saw those True Meta Stones, he frowned slightly. Although there were many True Meta Stones, their quality wasn't high. They were mostly high-grade True Meta Stones and a small number of top-grade True Meta Stones. There were even a large number of middle-grade True Meta Stones. Where was the Zhen Yuanyu?
Luo Zheng walked forward and asked, "Friend, may I know why you want to dig up high-grade and middle-grade True Meta Stones? Isn't it too laborious to dig up these low-grade True Meta Stones? "
Everyone's goal was a hundred Zhen Yuanyu, which could be exchanged for one million top-grade True Meta Stones. If it was exchanged for high-grade True Meta Stones, it would be an astronomical figure. After all, one top-grade True Meta Stone could be exchanged for one hundred high-grade True Meta Stones. In other words, it was a hundred million high-grade True Meta Stones. In other words, it wasn't very useful to dig up these things …
The ascenders who came with Luo Zheng were also concerned about this problem. They all cast their gazes over.
Unexpectedly, the cultivator shook his head and revealed a wry smile. "If we don't dig up these things, what should we dig up?" he said as he sized up Luo Zheng. "You are the new ascenders, aren't you?"
Luo Zheng and the other ascenders nodded at the same time.
The cultivator sighed and said, "I guess you want to dig up that Zhen Yuanyu, right? Hehe … "
"What? Is it not possible? "Luo Zheng asked, puzzled.
"It's possible." The cultivator laughed. "When I first ascended, I also wanted to dig up Zhen Yuanyu so that I could leave this place as soon as possible. However, if you want to dig up Zhen Yuanyu, you have to be prepared to lose your life."
"What do you mean?" Luo Zheng asked immediately.
The cultivator pointed to the mountain range in the distance and said, "If you want to dig up Zhen Yuanyu, you have to sink into the depths of the mineral vein. There are ferocious beasts from level 11 to level 12 there. How can ordinary people dare to dig up Zhen Yuanyu? The Spirit Sea Realm cultivators cultivated by the Spirit Martial Holy Land are unwilling to take the risk to dig up Zhen Yuanyu. However, weak cultivators can't dig up Zhen Yuanyu at all. Naturally, they expect us to risk our lives for them … However, no one's life is for free. I would rather dig up ordinary True Meta Stones and waste my time here than dig up Zhen Yuanyu! "
Hearing the cultivator's words, many ascenders suddenly understood.
It turned out that Zhen Yuanyu was not so easy to dig up …
The Spirit Sea Realm cultivators who ascended might not be worth mentioning in the big world, but after all, they had ready-made strength.
Mining was originally done by some weak cultivators. It was the same in the Mortal World. Some Xiantian Realm cultivators and Divine Reflection Realm cultivators were the ones who dug up Zhen Yuanyu. The problem was that low-level cultivators couldn't dig up Zhen Yuanyu, and the native Spirit Sea Realm cultivators were unwilling to do it. Therefore, it was natural to use the ascenders to do it.
However, most of the ascenders cherished their lives. How could they be willing to risk their lives to dig up Zhen Yuanyu?
Anyway, the lifespan of a Spirit Sea Realm cultivator was long. At worst, they could spend 30 to 50 years or even 100 years to dig up Zhen Yuanyu. In the end, they could gather enough Zhen Yuanyu.
The group of ascenders who came with Luo Zheng also planned to dig up Zhen Yuanyu. However, when they heard this, they immediately dismissed the idea. It was safer to dig up Zhen Yuanyu.
The other ascenders also left one after another. The earlier they dug, the earlier they could leave. No one wanted to stay in this place forever …
As for the plain-clothed woman, she was holding a pickaxe in her hand. Her expression was not very good. She wanted to wait for her uncle to come and pick her up. However, she didn't expect that there would be no news from him until now. She had to dig up Zhen Yuanyu like the others. Naturally, she was very depressed.
Luo Zheng sized up the mountain range. After putting away his nameplate and pickaxe, he flew towards the mountain range.

Chapter 986
After ascending, he was treated as a miner. Of course, he was unhappy, but Luo Zheng didn't have any resentment.
If he had asked Yun Luo to extradite him or leave with the Sky Immortal Clan, he wouldn't have been treated like this.
However, both of them wanted him to ascend freely. Hence, the things that were in front of him were his paths. He had no other choice but to continue moving forward.
After Luo Zheng flew for a while, he found that the plain-clothed woman was following him.
Luo Zheng stopped, turned around, and asked, "Why are you following me?"
The plain-clothed woman smiled faintly, "Nothing! I just feel that if I dig with you, my luck will be better. "
"You didn't ask me if I agree or not," Luo Zheng said indifferently.
Luo Zheng's words made the plain-clothed woman slightly stunned.
Actually, for a Life and Death Realm warrior to talk to a Spirit Sea Realm warrior in such a way was a bit unbelievable in the eyes of others. Logically speaking, this Life and Death Realm warrior should be eager to climb up to other Spirit Sea Realm warriors. However, the situation was reversed right now.
The plain-clothed woman smiled, "So, do you agree?"
Luo Zheng shrugged, "As you wish."
"En! My name is Mu Mingxue. What about you? "The plain-clothed woman took the initiative to tell him her name.
"Luo Zheng."
After telling her his name, Luo Zheng didn't linger and flew toward the mountain range.
Mu Mingxue stared at Luo Zheng's back in a daze. A helpless smile appeared on her delicate face. A Life and Death Realm kid was so arrogant. This kid seemed to have some skills. No matter what, she instinctively felt that Luo Zheng was very special. If she followed this guy, her luck wouldn't be bad.
"Swoosh …"
Along the way, many cultivators entered the mountain range, and many cultivators returned from the mountain range with a bountiful harvest. Of course, there were also some cultivators who never returned, becoming lifeless corpses in the mine.
This mountain range had a total of hundreds of enormous mountains, each of which was covered with mines of various sizes. These mines were also excavated by the previous generations.
Previously, Luo Zheng had collected some information and had a rough idea of the distribution of True Essence Stones.
In the upper layer of this mountain range, there were large amounts of low-grade True Meta Stones. If placed in the Lower World or the Central Domain, it would definitely be considered a rich mine.
However, among the Divine Sea Realm powerhouses present, which one of them would place low-grade True Essence Stones in their eyes? Moreover, if low-grade true essence stones could be exchanged for 100 Zhen Yuanyu, then that should be one trillion … this number was simply too shocking. Low-grade true essence stones were easy to dig out, but who knew how long it would take to dig out such a large number?
In the middle layer of this vein, there were middle grade True Essence Stones, and in the lower layer, there were top grade True Essence Stones. At the bottom layer, one could excavate Zhen Yuanyu.
However, the danger of each layer was different. For the Divine Sea Realm powerhouses, the upper layer and the middle layer were almost safe, but the lower layer and the bottom layer were different.
After Luo Zheng randomly chose a mine, he flew down along the winding tunnel. The walls of the cave were full of excavation marks. This place must have been excavated countless times, so he naturally wouldn't waste his time here. As for Mu Mingxue, she followed closely behind Luo Zheng. She was getting more and more curious. Where was Luo Zheng going? Could it be that he wanted to go straight to the bottom layer?
In the time it took for a stick of incense to burn, Luo Zheng had entered the depths of the mine. He was several thousand feet away from the entrance. This was already the bottom layer of the mine. Logically speaking, Luo Zheng had a certain probability of excavating top grade True Essence Stones.
The surroundings were quiet. Occasionally, the sound of water dripping could be heard. In this dark mine, Luo Zheng didn't stop at all.
However, just as Luo Zheng was flying, several black shadows gently flapped their wings at the top of the cave. Then, they silently followed behind Luo Zheng. Under the cover of the darkness, they went straight for Luo Zheng.
It was unknown what cultivation method Mu Mingxue had cultivated, but her perception was extremely sharp. Those black shadows didn't emit any aura, but she still easily sensed them.
"Luo Zheng, be careful!"
Unexpectedly, just as she opened her mouth and was about to attack, Luo Zheng didn't even turn his head. A sharp sword suddenly appeared in his hand. As if it had eyes on its back, with a casual slash, the sharp sword disappeared from his hand. From the beginning to the end, Luo Zheng didn't stop at all.
"It's just some level 10 vicious beasts …" Luo Zheng said indifferently.
Mu Mingxue, who was following behind Luo Zheng, once again revealed a helpless smile. Level 10 vicious beasts were equivalent to the Life and Death Realm of humans. This Luo Zheng was only at the Life and Death Realm, yet he spoke like this. It was as if he didn't regard himself as a Life and Death Realm expert.
But then again, the sword attack Luo Zheng had just displayed had indeed surprised Mu Mingxue. She was born in a famous sect, and had seen many experts in the lower realms. However, she had never heard of someone who could slash out such a sharp and strange arc with a sword. This guy's strength was far from being as simple as it seemed from his cultivation base!
Mu Mingxue had already come to this conclusion in her heart.
As they continued forward, the surrounding environment also began to change. Originally, these caves were made of brown rock walls, but now, the surrounding rock walls began to become crystal clear. These rock walls were made of pure quartz.
There were also some other ores embedded in these quartz rocks. Some of these ores emitted a faint luster. Under the illumination of these lights, the environment below was no longer as oppressive.
"This place is quite beautiful …" Mu Mingxue looked around and said in a low voice.
"However, the top grade true origin stones have all been dug out." Luo Zheng pointed at a chiseled mark on the cave wall. The other ores didn't have much attraction to ascenders, but the top grade true origin stones were all dug out without a single exception …
Top grade true origin stones were formed by gathering heaven and earth origin energy. However, it took a long time for them to form. After so many years of excavation, the number of top grade true origin stones in this mine was probably decreasing.
At the very least, for someone like Luo Zheng, finding a top grade true origin stone and digging it out completely depended on luck.
Mu Mingxue suddenly looked around and found a top grade true origin stone not far away. "My luck isn't bad. I found one here!"
Luo Zheng smiled faintly. He didn't move and allowed Mu Mingxue to dig. Although it was said that digging out true origin stones was a small matter, Luo Zheng really didn't care about one or two pieces.
However, when Mu Mingxue walked over, her face suddenly revealed a surprised expression. "It's not just one piece. There's actually a cluster here. It seems to be a cluster of top grade true origin stones!"
When Luo Zheng walked closer to take a look, sure enough, from this tricky angle, along the translucent wall formed by quartz, there was a cluster of top grade true origin stones. This cluster of top grade true origin stones was probably hundreds of them.
"See, I told you. My hunch has always been very accurate. Following you will be very lucky," Mu Mingxue said with a smile. "We'll each take half!"
Luo Zheng nodded noncommittally.
In fact, Mu Mingxue's hunch was correct. Just like when he was in the Central Domain, she already had a conclusion about his luck.
For a warrior like Cui Xie, his luck was indeed very strong, but he would devour the luck of the people around him to strengthen himself. In other words, for people like Cui Xie, such as Si Miaoling, such as Wu Zhanhe … their luck was devoured by Cui Xie, and the final result was that they died at the hands of Luo Zheng.
However, Luo Zheng's own luck could spread to the people around him, such as Ning Yudie, Xi Youqin, and even the fat man from the World Business Alliance …
Mu Mingxue's hunch originated from this. She was very sensitive to other people's luck. However, this kind of unclear thing could only be described as a hunch.

Chapter 987
Although Luo Zheng didn't care about a few hundred top grade True Origin Stones, Mu Mingxue still divided them into five and gave them to Luo Zheng.
After collecting the top grade True Origin Stones, the two continued to descend. However, they didn't walk far before reaching the end of the tunnel.
This tunnel was originally excavated by the predecessors. After reaching the bottom of the mine, they didn't continue to dig any deeper.
Mu Mingxue looked around but didn't find any top grade True Origin Stones. Her beautiful eyes stared at Luo Zheng as if she wanted Luo Zheng to make the decision.
"What should we do? Should we change to another tunnel or dig horizontally? "Mu Mingxue asked.
Luo Zheng pointed downwards and said, "Downwards!"
"Downwards? You want to go to the bottom? "Mu Mingxue's eyes flashed.
If they dug horizontally, although it would be a little troublesome, they would still be able to collect a lot of top grade True Origin Stones. With their cultivation and strength, even if they casually dug at the bottom, it wouldn't be too dangerous even if the tunnel collapsed. However, if they continued to dig, they would be digging Zhen Yuanyu.
Didn't she hear it from others before? There were grade 11 and even grade 12 Vicious Beasts in the bottom. Grade 11 Vicious Beasts were still manageable as Mu Mingxue had the confidence to deal with them. However, grade 12 Vicious Beasts were existences at the Extreme Divine Stage. If they were unlucky and bumped into them, they would probably die!
This Life and Death Realm guy was extraordinarily confident!
Luo Zheng faintly smiled. He didn't need this girl to follow him. If she didn't want to, Luo Zheng naturally wouldn't force her. In fact, it was easier for him to dig alone. Having an extra person would always tie his hands and feet.
After taking two steps forward, he chose an ordinary spot and gently gestured with his fist. The power of two thousand dragon scales gathered on his right fist, and this fist heavily smashed towards the ground.
"Boom!"
With Luo Zheng as the center, countless cracks appeared around his fist. Those cracks quickly exploded under Luo Zheng's feet.
"Crackle, crackle …"
Seeing this fist, Mu Mingxue's brows suddenly twitched. This guy's strength was extraordinary. Moreover, looking at him, he didn't even use his full strength. Even so, his pure physical strength was probably much stronger than her, a Divine Sea Realm warrior. Was he really only at the Life and Death Realm?
The second fist …
"Boom!"
The ground that already had countless cracks suddenly shattered into countless tiny fragments, and a huge pit that was seven to eight feet deep appeared under Luo Zheng's feet!
Standing in the middle of the pit, Luo Zheng continued to smash downwards. He wanted to forcefully smash a path.....
Mu Mingxue's lips twitched as she watched this scene. She was a little hesitant. Should she leave this place and search for supreme-grade True Origin Stones, or should she follow Luo Zheng?
At this time, Mu Mingxue still chose to follow her intuition. She felt that following Luo Zheng would greatly shorten her time in the military service …
"Rumble, rumble, rumble …"
With a series of loud noises, Luo Zheng's position was constantly falling.
Seventy feet …
Ten feet …
Twenty feet …
Fifty feet …
One hundred feet …
Mu Mingxue naturally didn't rest. She was looking for top-grade True Origin Stones on the walls of the pit. She really found dozens of them.
Just like that, the huge pit extended all the way down for a full two thousand feet. Luo Zheng smashed his fist down again, directly smashing through the bottom! He directly fell into a cave.
Before Luo Zheng fell to the bottom, the chaotic energy in his body suddenly circulated. His figure steadily floated in the air. The debris that fell with Luo Zheng sprinkled down.
"It's through! Is this the bottom? "Mu Mingxue also followed Luo Zheng into the cave below.
The caves in front of them weren't man-made. They were naturally formed. This was the bottom of the mine …
They had to be even more careful at the bottom of the mine. The majority of ascenders didn't dare to enter here. There must be a reason for that.
They were already tens of thousands of feet away from the top of the mountain. For thousands of years, after the mine was formed, this place had never been exposed to sunlight. In the surrounding caves of various sizes, there were occasional gusts of cold wind blowing. The two of them also became cautious.
However, just as the two of them scanned their surroundings, a voice startled them.
"Still Water Celestial Venerable! You bastard, you only know how to hide. If you have the guts, come to Autumn Mountain Boundary and fight me to the death! Otherwise, I'll scold you like this once a month! "
This voice resounded clearly in the ears of Luo Zheng and Mu Mingxue. Both of them were stunned.
"That should be a Roaring Token …" Mu Mingxue immediately said. Although she was also an Ascender, she knew more about the Upper Realm than the average cultivator. Naturally, she felt that Luo Zheng didn't know anything about it, so she explained, "It is said that this Roaring Token is extremely expensive. A single Roaring Token is probably worth tens of millions of True Origin Jade. Even the Great Realm Masters of Great Realms wouldn't use it easily. The person who used this Roaring Token is probably a Heavenly Venerate powerhouse. His cursing words have probably spread throughout all the Great Realms.
One hundred Zhen Yuanyu could redeem an ascender's freedom. Tens of millions of Zhen Yuanyu was definitely an astronomical figure. It was a huge sum even for a tenth-rank Holy Land.
Mu Mingxue originally thought that Luo Zheng would be shocked. She didn't expect Luo Zheng to just nod.
She didn't know that Luo Zheng already bore a grudge against this guy who had just scolded him.
Luo Zheng had obtained a Roaring Token from the Palace Lord. Moreover, it was an unlimited Roaring Token!
Now that Luo Zheng was busy mining, he couldn't be bothered to pay attention to this guy. However, this guy had given him a big fright. When he had time, he would definitely use the Roaring Token to scold him. Even a Heavenly Venerable wouldn't be able to find him …
Fortunately, this Roaring Token was an extremely precious item to a Heavenly Venerable. After this sound reverberated throughout the universe, it didn't continue.
What Luo Zheng didn't know was that the martial artist who had just used the Roaring Token was the world-shaking Heavenly Venerable Ling Huo. The conflict between him and Heavenly Venerable Ling Huo had already reached the point where everyone knew about it. Every living being in the universe, from a toddler learning how to walk to an old man, knew that there was a life and death enmity between these two great figures.
This was the effect of the Roaring Token …
Heavenly Venerable Ling Huo would use the Roaring Token every month. Now that the number of Howling Celestial Tapirs was dwindling, the Roaring Tokens were becoming harder and harder to obtain. Even as a Heavenly Venerable, he had no choice but to use them sparingly.
Even if it was once a month, it was still very, very extravagant!
For example, a tenth-grade Holy Land like the Spiritual Item Holy Land managed nearly one-twentieth of the area of a Great Realm. Ten years of harvest could be exchanged for half a Roaring Token. What was even more frightening was that the Roaring Tokens were priceless!
But this time, Luo Zheng's interest was piqued when he heard the Celestial Venerate's roar. The Roaring Token in his hand seemed to be quite fun. More importantly, when he found an opportunity, he would use the Roaring Token to find Luo Yan …
Right now, it was better to find Zhen Yuanyu first!
Luo Zheng and Mu Mingxue traversed through these naturally formed caves at a low speed. This place was not like the upper level. If they were to alarm some level-12 vicious beasts, they could only flee.
After Luo Zheng passed through a cave that was as wide as a person, the front suddenly opened up. At the same time, Luo Zheng saw a behemoth crawling in the cave. This behemoth had nine heads, and at this moment, those heads were twisted together and lying on the ground, snoring loudly.
"It's a divine beast, a nine-headed snake, and an adult one!"
"Shh — a level-12 vicious beast, that's an existence comparable to a Divine Realm expert!" Mu Mingxue gestured in Luo Zheng's ear. "Let's go back!" Mu Mingxue looked very vigilant.
However, Luo Zheng's hand pointed in the distance, "Look at what that is!"
At the place where the nine-headed snake was sleeping, there was a pile of milky white gems. Even though they were 450 feet away, the two could feel the incomparably pure Qi of Heaven and Earth from it!
"Is that Zhen Yuanyu?" When Mu Mingxue saw the pile of milky white gems, her eyes suddenly jumped. She knew that this level-12 vicious beast's strength was not something she could provoke, but this pile of milky white gems had more than 30 of them!
If she got it, it would be equivalent to completing one-third of the mission. Even if she and Luo Zheng each took half, there would still be more than 10 gems!
Although she was afraid that she could not avoid it, such a great temptation was enough for her to consider the risk.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 988
In fact, both of them had never seen Zhen Yuanyu before.
The Lingwu Holy Place did not treat the ascenders well. They set up a barrier in the mine and threw the ascenders inside, leaving them alone. They were no different from prisoners in prison.
Want to go out? Just hand over 100 Zhen Yuanyu. It was no different from robbery.
The only advantage was that it would dispel the ascenders' beautiful vision of the Upper World and make them realize how powerless the Spirit Sea Realm was in the Upper World …
However, judging from the abundant True Core Energies released by those stones, they were most likely Zhen Yuanyu.
However, it would not be easy to take Zhen Yuanyu away from a Level 12 Ferocious Beast.
With Luo Zheng's current strength, he could fight against a Spirit Sea Realm expert. However, this Level 12 Ferocious Beast was equivalent to a Spirit Sea Realm expert. Moreover, Ferocious Beasts usually had a longer lifespan than humans. Not to mention, the Hydra was a Divine Beast. Luo Zheng had no chance of winning in a head-on battle!
As for Mu Mingxue, she had just broken through to the Spirit Sea Realm. Perhaps she thought she was stronger than Luo Zheng, but Luo Zheng was not optimistic about her strength at all.
The two of them observed for a while. They looked at the cluster of True Essence Jades and then looked at the sleeping Hydra. They felt very conflicted in their hearts.
They were reluctant to give up. However, taking these Zhen Yuanyu would be risking their lives!
However, at this moment, Mu Mingxue smiled. "I have learned a concealment magic weapon. It can hide more than 90% of one's breath. This Hydra is still sleeping. Let me go!"
Hearing Mu Mingxue's words, Luo Zheng did not say much. It would be better if they could take the Zhen Yuanyu away. The risk was high, but the reward was not small. Therefore, he nodded.
He then saw Mu Mingxue take out a light green gauze dress. The gauze dress was as thin as a cicada's wing. When Mu Mingxue casually put it on, Luo Zheng's face revealed a trace of surprise.
Indeed, as Mu Mingxue had said, when she was covered by the gauze, the majority of her aura was concealed. Even with Luo Zheng's incomparably sharp perception, if he did not pay attention, it would be difficult for him to detect her existence. Of course, that was when he used his perception to investigate. If he used his eyes, this green luster would be incomparably eye-catching.
However, the purpose of this kind of magic treasure was to hide one's aura. Not to mention that the nine-headed snake was still in a deep sleep. As long as the aura was covered, it was indeed very suitable for the current situation.
Luo Zheng thought to himself, "This magic treasure is probably at the level of a divine weapon."
Mu Mingxue winked at Luo Zheng. After nodding, she walked towards the nine-headed snake.
Although she tried her best to suppress her state of mind, Luo Zheng could still see that she was very nervous. Luo Zheng originally wanted her to give the gauze dress to him so that he could take the Zhen Yuanyu. However, after seeing her take a few steps, he gave up on this idea.
Mu Mingxue's nervousness was inevitable. She was born in a prestigious family. After ascending to the Immortal World, the situation was completely different from what she had imagined!
She originally thought that her own uncle would come and take her away directly. Then, she would join the Spirit Martial Holy Place and continue to cultivate quietly …
However, after ascending to the Immortal World, not only did she not see her uncle, but she also had to take this kind of risk in exchange for her freedom. However, in any case, she was still an almighty of the Divine Sea Realm. As a warrior, she should have the awareness that she should have. She could not be at a loss and cry like those delicate little girls when she encountered such trouble.
This nine-headed snake had been sleeping here for who knew how many years. Ferocious Beasts always slept for a long time. Some Ferocious Beasts usually slept for hundreds or even thousands of years before they advanced. Snakes had the habit of hibernating.
This thing would not wake up …
Mu Mingxue kept comforting herself in her heart. At the same time, she took light steps and approached the Zhen Yuanyu.
With her cultivation, she could almost control every meridian and muscle in her body in perfect harmony. Therefore, from beginning to end, she was like a nimble kitten as she walked towards the snake!
At this moment, in the universe, another voice spread out.
"Hehe, Celestial Lord Ling Huo, I am not hiding from you. After I finish cultivating this Return to Origin Doppelganger, you can obediently wait for your death in Autumn Mountain Boundary!"
This thunderous voice passed through 100,000 big boundaries and floated in the universe. No matter if it was in the depths of the earth or in the depths of the sea, this voice could still be clearly transmitted. Moreover, every person, every living thing, could hear this voice. Not a single corner was missed …
The Roaring Command was so domineering …
Luo Zheng could hear it. Mu Mingxue could hear it. Naturally, the sleeping nine-headed snake could also hear it.
"Huff …"
However, this nine-headed snake had been sleeping for who knew how many years, so it did not wake up. Only one of its heads rolled, spitting out its tongue and making a sound. Then, it fell back into a deep sleep.
Mu Mingxue, who was originally in a state of extreme nervousness, suddenly heard the sound in her ear. Her heartbeat sped up by more than two times. When she saw one of the nine-headed snake's heads roll, her body trembled. Her originally nimble steps were suddenly messed up. Her foot stepped on a palm-sized stone. As the stone rolled, it made a "peng peng peng" sound.
This sound echoed in the cave, clearly entering one's ears.
Mu Mingxue froze in place. Luo Zheng also did not move. At this moment, the two of them even stopped breathing.
"Huff huff …"
When two of the snake heads heard this sound, they slightly raised their heads, as if they were listening to the source of the sound. Fortunately, they did not open their eyes. Probably because they were extremely sleepy, they once again lowered their heads.
At this moment, Mu Mingxue felt that she was going to die. However, after seeing the nine-headed snake's reaction, she let out a deep breath. She turned her head and smiled at Luo Zheng. Then, she continued to walk towards the Zhen Yuanyu …
When Mu Mingxue successfully approached the Zhen Yuanyu, her eyes emitted a ray of light.
She casually glanced at it and calculated the number of Zhen Yuanyu. There were a total of thirty-nine of them! She did not know what these Zhen Yuanyu were. Obviously, they were a kind of ore, but they grew here like a plant. Each Zhen Yuanyu was like a "fruit" of this plant.
Mu Mingxue reached out to pluck the fruits one by one.
However, when she reached out to grab the Zhen Yuanyu and pulled, her expression changed. She actually could not pull it off!
Luo Zheng, who was observing from a distance, also frowned slightly. Mu Mingxue was an almighty Divine Sea Realm expert. Even though she was only a woman, her strength was not weak. Could she not pull it off?
Mu Mingxue gently exerted her strength. This time, she used thirty percent of her strength. However, the Zhen Yuanyu in her hand still did not move. The same was true for the milky white branch. It was as if it was cast from iron. It was fixed in place and did not even sway.
Even an iron tree could not withstand Mu Mingxue's pull. It seemed that it would not be easy to take away this Zhen Yuanyu.
Mu Mingxue once again took a deep breath. This time, she used sixty percent of her strength. However, the Zhen Yuanyu still did not move …
Ninety percent …
Mu Mingxue's face had already turned red. However, the Zhen Yuanyu was still firmly hanging on the branch.
At this moment, Mu Mingxue understood that with her strength, she would not be able to take it off.
She turned her head and looked at Luo Zheng helplessly. She could only give up for now and return to Luo Zheng's side.
"I can't take it off …" Mu Mingxue's face revealed a trace of anger. She was about to succeed, but she realized that she could not take away the Zhen Yuanyu. Naturally, she felt depressed.
This was all the Lingwu Holy Land's fault. Generally speaking, when the Lingwu Holy Land was short of Zhen Yuanyu, they would send some Divine Realm experts to the various mines in the Holy Land to dig for Zhen Yuanyu. Usually, because of the abundant reserves, these Divine Realm experts were too lazy to take action. Firstly, it was too time-consuming. Secondly, there was a certain amount of risk.
If they wanted to take the Zhen Yuanyu, they would usually be equipped with a tool for digging Zhen Yuanyu. That tool was at least a Divine Weapon!

Chapter 989
Luo Zheng had a panoramic view of this scene just now, so he had a rough idea of what was going on.
However, just because Mu Mingxue couldn't take it off didn't mean that Luo Zheng couldn't.
At this time, Mu Mingxue's face revealed a resentful expression. She immediately said, "Let's go. If we alarm that guy, we'll go for wool and come home shorn …"
Luo Zheng stretched out his hand towards Mu Mingxue. "Give it to me!"
"Give what?" Mu Mingxue asked curiously.
"Lend me your gauze dress," Luo Zheng replied.
"You want to take the Zhen Yuanyu?" Mu Mingxue finally reacted.
Luo Zheng nodded. However, Mu Mingxue's face was full of doubt. Her cultivation was one stage higher than Luo Zheng's, but even she didn't have a good method. Could it be that Luo Zheng had a way?
"Don't worry, I can take it off," Luo Zheng said with a faint smile.
Although Mu Mingxue didn't believe it in her heart, seeing Luo Zheng's deep affection, she inexplicably felt a trace of trust in him. She felt that this guy would definitely succeed if he set out.
Mu Mingxue was indeed unwilling to come to the lowest level without taking the Zhen Yuanyu away. Since Luo Zheng wanted to try, she had no reason to refuse. In the end, she took off her gauze dress and handed it to Luo Zheng.
"Thank you!"
After Luo Zheng took the gauze dress, he bent his waist and at the same time restrained his aura. With the gauze dress' effect of restraining aura, the aura emitted from Luo Zheng's body was almost indiscernible.
With a burst of quick steps, Luo Zheng unexpectedly rushed towards the Zhen Yuanyu at an extremely fast speed.
Seeing Luo Zheng so reckless, Mu Mingxue was startled and directly covered her mouth with her hand. When Luo Zheng safely approached the Zhen Yuanyu without making the slightest sound, Mu Mingxue breathed a sigh of relief. This guy was too confident …
After approaching the Zhen Yuanyu, Luo Zheng did the same thing as Mu Mingxue. He wanted to directly pull the Zhen Yuanyu down from above.
However, the Zhen Yuanyu didn't move.
Luo Zheng's face also revealed a strange expression. He immediately used the power of the dragon scales.
However, as Luo Zheng increased the power of the dragon scales to around 1000, the Zhen Yuanyu didn't move. At this time, Luo Zheng also stopped increasing his power.
This power could be called the "Strength to Pull Up Mountains". Even a mountain would be overturned by it. However, it still didn't move. This could only mean one thing. This kind of world spirit item must contain the law of cause and effect. In other words, this wasn't a problem that could be solved with strength.
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng slightly leaned his body to the side. With his back facing Mu Mingxue, he gently pressed his spatial ring and took out the Black Star Pickaxe. He didn't want others to know that he had a Black Star Pickaxe. Under this cover, Mu Mingxue wouldn't dare to release her perception. Presumably, she wouldn't be able to see it.
He slowly extended the pickaxe and gently pried one of the roots of the Zhen Yuanyu. A very subtle sound was transmitted.
"Pop!"
The Zhen Yuanyu steadily fell into his hand.
At this time, Luo Zheng slightly frowned. He patted his head. Why did he have to pry it out one by one? As a world spirit item, the Zhen Yuanyu was concentrated on the same root. Couldn't he just pry it out by the roots?
Therefore, this time, Luo Zheng directly extended the pickaxe to the bottom of the Zhen Yuanyu. With a gentle pry, the law of cause and effect of the Zhen Yuanyu came into play. He uprooted the entire Zhen Yuanyu.
Luo Zheng smiled faintly. He put the Black Star Pickaxe into the Sumeru Mustard. Holding the Zhen Yuanyu in his hand, he turned his head and waved it at Mu Mingxue.
As for Mu Mingxue, her pair of beautiful eyes were wide open. She stared blankly at the Zhen Yuanyu.
Just now, she had used all her strength but was unable to pull out the Zhen Yuanyu. Just like that, Luo Zheng easily took it in his hand. It seemed as if he didn't use much strength.
This guy …
At this moment, Mu Mingxue rejoiced in her heart. Her intuition was indeed as powerful as always. At the same time, Mu Mingxue was a little worried. This Luo Zheng wouldn't take it all for himself, right? However, Mu Mingxue quickly rejected this idea. She felt that her vision was very accurate. This guy wasn't a selfish cultivator.
Holding the Zhen Yuanyu in his hand, Luo Zheng slowly walked towards Mu Mingxue.
However, at this moment, the Hydra that had been sleeping suddenly sniffed. To be precise, all nine heads sniffed at the same time. They quickly realized that something was wrong.
The Hydra chose to sleep here because there was a Zhen Yuanyu here. As a Spirit Plant of Heaven and Earth, this Zhen Yuanyu had already absorbed enough Yuan Qi of Heaven and Earth. However, since this Zhen Yuanyu was already full, the excess Yuan Qi of Heaven and Earth would be spat out. This was also the reason why Luo Zheng and Mu Mingxue were attracted to the Zhen Yuanyu in an instant. It was because they felt that the Zhen Yuanyu was absorbing a large amount of Yuan Qi of Heaven and Earth.
Although the Hydra was sleeping, it quickly realized that something was wrong when the Yuan Qi of Heaven and Earth that was being absorbed by the Zhen Yuanyu suddenly disappeared.
"Heh …"
"Heh heh …"
The Hydra's nine heads woke up almost at the same time.
Previously, they couldn't detect Mu Mingxue because they didn't open their eyes. Now that they had woken up, they looked around and saw that a human not far away had taken the Zhen Yuanyu and was about to swagger away. How could the Hydra not be angry?
Because Luo Zheng's back was facing the Hydra, Mu Mingxue was the first to notice that the Hydra had woken up. She covered her mouth again and pointed behind Luo Zheng with a frightened look in her eyes.
Luo Zheng suddenly frowned. At this time, he also felt the movement behind him!
"Whoosh!"
He didn't even look back. Almost instinctively, he flew towards Mu Mingxue like an arrow.
At the same time, a roar came from behind him. One of the Hydra's heads opened its mouth and tried to swallow Luo Zheng. However, it was too late. The Hydra's head smashed the rock under Luo Zheng's feet into pieces. At the same time, the whole cave began to roar.
"Go! Why are you still in a daze? "
Luo Zheng stuffed the Zhen Yuanyu into his sumeru ring. When he reached Mu Mingxue's side, he pulled her up from the ground!
Mu Mingxue only regained her senses after being dragged by Luo Zheng for a while. She circulated her True Yuan and hurriedly dashed forward without a care in the world.
The Hydra's body began to twist. Although it was huge, it was very agile. Not to mention that it was a twelfth level vicious beast. Its speed was faster than Mu Mingxue and Luo Zheng. In the blink of an eye, the distance between them was shortened a lot …
"It's so fast!" Mu Mingxue crazily urged the True Yuan in her body. Behind her, there was a trail of sparkling and translucent snowflakes.
As the Hydra chased after them, there was a small Hydra's head in the middle of its nine heads. The Hydra's head unexpectedly spoke in human language, "You're just a Life and Death Realm human, yet you dare to steal my Zhen Yuanyu. Hehe … I advise you to obediently stop and let me devour you. My nine brothers have been hungry for a long time. Hehe!"
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. This Hydra seemed to have gained intelligence. Looking at the situation, its intelligence wasn't low.
Vicious beasts with intelligence were not uncommon. Not to mention, the Hydra was a Mythological Beast. Qinglong was also a Mythological Beast. His intelligence was much higher than that of many humans.
"Quick! Let's go through the cave in front! " Luo Zheng wasn't running around randomly. He was going straight through the path he came from. As for Mu Mingxue, she was at a loss. If she wasn't following Luo Zheng, she might have run away. The final result could be imagined. She would most likely alarm more and more vicious beasts, and eventually die without a burial place.
"Whoosh, whoosh …"
As the two sped forward, the Hydra was getting closer and closer to them.
"Is there any way to slow it down?" Luo Zheng asked.
As soon as his voice fell, Mu Mingxue turned around. A sparkling True Yuan ring spread out from her body, and immediately condensed into a fluttering snow ring.
"Falling Snow Domain!"
Generally, once a Domain was condensed, it would move along with its owner. However, after condensing it, Mu Mingxue directly threw the Domain in place, and followed closely behind Luo Zheng.

Chapter 990
Mu Mingxue's Snowfall Domain only slowed down the Hydra's movements by less than a breath's time. However, for the two of them, even a breath's time was extremely precious.
In fact, the danger in this mine couldn't be compared to the danger zones. If they dared to fight, the possibility of obtaining the Zhen Yuanyu was not small!
However, the ascenders had just ascended and didn't even have the time to properly see the world before losing their lives in this mine. No matter who it was, they wouldn't be willing to accept such a fate.
Therefore, the majority of the Divine Sea Realm experts would rather spend some time searching for top-grade True Origin Stones in the lower levels than searching for the Zhen Yuanyu.
Because Mu Mingxue had bought time for the two of them, they leaped all the way to the vertical hole that Luo Zheng had just smashed open. The two of them didn't stop at all and shot upwards.
How could the Hydra let the two of them go?
"Huff —"
All of the snake heads stood up. Its entire body surged rapidly as it climbed upwards.
Fortunately, the Hydra's body was huge, and the hole that Luo Zheng had smashed open was only two to three feet wide. However, the Hydra relied on its valiant strength to forcibly squeeze its way up!
"Crackle …"
The entire hole was forcibly crushed by its huge body and constantly collapsed on both sides. Even so, its speed wasn't affected in the slightest.
Mu Mingxue rushed upwards while releasing icicles one after another!
The attacks of the Divine Sea Realm experts weren't weak, but the problem was that the Hydra was even more powerful. Most of the icicles couldn't break its defense. Even if a small number of icicles pierced through the scales on its surface and caused a slight wound, the wound would heal in the blink of an eye!
Among the divine beasts, the Hydra's attack power was the weakest. However, this world had always been fair. Its attack power wasn't great, but its recovery ability was one of the best among the divine beasts. It was said that even if the Hydra's head was chopped off, it could still regrow it in a short time.
When Luo Zheng entered the Blue Clouds Road, he also obtained the Hydra's Blood Soul. It strengthened his Qi and blood power, allowing his body's recovery ability to rise to a new level.
Fortunately, the Hydra wasn't particularly good at attacking and chasing. If it were another kind of twelfth level vicious beast, Luo Zheng and Mu Mingxue wouldn't be able to escape …
Even the weakest divine beast was still a divine beast!
The Hydra seemed to be impatient in the midst of its relentless pursuit. The small head in the clump of snakes shrieked, "We're going to kill them, kill them! Poisonous Blood! "
"Huff —"
The Hydra's head, and the Hydra's's' mouths. '''s's' mouths condensed. a's's mouth.'s's's's's's.
Mu Mingxue's face turned ghastly pale at the sight of this. She and Luo Zheng were both ascending in this tunnel, and this tunnel was long and narrow. There was no place to dodge at all. They were in a hopeless situation!

Qinglong had already told Luo Zheng the value of the roaring token. A roaring token that could only be used once was already an extremely rare item. Even the Great Realm Lords might only have one or two. The value of this roaring token that could be used infinitely was immeasurable!
Therefore, he naturally had to be careful when using the roaring token.
After making all these preparations, Luo Zheng took out the roaring token. According to the method taught by the immortal in the book, he only needed to use his True Yuan to activate the roaring token.
Luo Zheng did not have True Yuan, but he had the Chaos Qi, which was even more powerful than True Yuan. This time, when the Chaos Qi was injected into the roaring token, it began to emit a faint golden light.
