"It's probably a coincidence." Mu Mingxue also felt that she was a bit crazy. No matter how amazing this Luo Zheng was, the roaring token was not something that he could have obtained.
At the same time, the vast majority of living beings in the universe who had gained sentience were puzzled.
Someone suddenly used the roaring token to throw out a sentence without rhyme or reason, and it seemed that he was looking for someone. However, this Luo Yan seemed to be a nameless person, so for some reason, he actually used the roaring token?
"Maybe he's a descendant of some Celestial Venerable. He has a lot of roaring tokens and is not afraid to waste them!"
"This is really burning money! Oh my God, a roaring token … ai! "
"Don't feel unbalanced. It's not like no one has done this before. When the eldest son of an Empyrean from the Realm of the Gods proposed to Empyrean Vast's daughter and confessed his feelings, he used a hundred roaring tokens!"
In the small cave, Luo Zheng didn't make a move. He was waiting.
The strength of that young man was unimaginably powerful. Just as Xun had said, he was an existence that surpassed an Empyrean. A mere roaring token should not be difficult for him. Since he had promised to protect Luo Yan, if Luo Yan heard his words, he would certainly respond with a roaring token.
After a long time, there was no sound in his ears. Luo Zheng's eyes gradually sank.
There were only two explanations for this situation. The first was that Luo Yan could not obtain the roaring token. The second was that Luo Yan was not within the scope of the universe.
If it was the first one, it meant that Luo Yan's situation was not as good as Luo Zheng had guessed. He might have been imprisoned, or something worse had happened. Of course, it was also possible that the young man was very stingy and was unwilling to respond to Luo Yan's roaring token …
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 994
The second possibility was that Luo Yan wasn't in the universe.
This was bad news for Luo Zheng …
The universe was so big that it would be harder for Luo Zheng to find his sister than to reach the heavens.
If Luo Yan was outside the universe, it would be even more difficult for Luo Zheng.
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng's face showed a trace of unwillingness, and then he activated the Roaring Token again.
"Luo Yan, where are you …"
As the Roaring Token vibrated slightly, Luo Zheng's second sentence spread out again, instantly resounding through the universe!
In the entire universe, countless races and living beings heard these words once again …
"Damn, who is this Luo Yan? Why is this woman so lucky? In order to find her, he burned two Roaring Tokens and didn't even blink an eye! "
"This is called being rich and willful!"
"But this sentence without rhyme or reason is quite depressing. Even if it's a missing person notice, it shouldn't be broadcasted like this. In any case, he should at least tell us his location or something. How can he find someone like this?"
What they didn't know was that Luo Zheng didn't have a powerful background, nor was he a descendant of a Celestial Master. With his current strength and status, he naturally had to be careful and wouldn't tell others his name and location. If he revealed too much information, Luo Zheng would simply be courting death!
After Luo Zheng's second message was transmitted, he waited for another incense stick's worth of time, but there was still no response.
He began to feel somewhat irritated.
Originally, after using the Roaring Token to find his sister's news, Luo Zheng would obtain enough Zhen Yuanyu to become an independent warrior. Then, he would find a way to travel through the universe to find Luo Yan.
However, this first step seemed to be impossible because Luo Yan didn't reply.
Very unwilling, Luo Zheng activated the Roaring Token again, and it was still the same sentence without rhyme or reason. "Luo Yan, where are you …"
Just as he finished speaking, he suddenly heard a voice. Someone had used the Roaring Token to reply!
"Hmph, so what if you've mastered the Returning to Origin Clone? I will wait for you in Autumn Mountain Jie. Let's see if you dare to come! "
When the voice suddenly sounded out, Luo Zheng was originally filled with anticipation, but in the end, it was the words of the Heavenly Sovereign Ling Huo. The originally frustrated Luo Zheng's expression became even more furious.
Luo Zheng was afraid that his voice couldn't reach Luo Yan's ears. If someone else interrupted him, how could he be in a good mood?
However, Luo Zheng couldn't be bothered to provoke Celestial Venerate Ling Huo. As soon as Celestial Venerate Ling Huo finished speaking, the other Celestial Venerate Still Water also used the Roar Command. "Hehe, just wait patiently. Wait for me to take your life!"
Luo Zheng was already very irritated. Now that the two Celestial Masters were quarreling with each other using the Roar Command, Luo Zheng was instantly annoyed. Impulsively, he activated the Roar Command.
"What are you guys arguing about? Can't it be a little quieter? "
Previously, Luo Zheng's three sentences were still in a gentle voice. Now, he suddenly exploded. All the living beings in the universe who had awakened were instantly stunned.
Vast Cloud Jie …
In the Vast Cloud Palace.
Countless Ascension Passageways were constantly flashing with milky white light.
These Ascension Passageways were not used for warriors from the Lower Realm to ascend. In fact, the one who created these Ascension Passageways was a woman in the Vast Cloud Palace.
This woman was currently sitting in a bathtub in the center of the Vast Cloud Palace. She was completely naked, but her body was shrouded in a cloud of mist. One could only vaguely see her beautiful figure and white jade-like skin. The only flaw was her back, which was covered with numerous scars …
Not long after, a Ascension Passageway flashed with silver white light. Then, a woman walked out of the Ascension Passageway. That woman looked exactly the same as the woman in the Vast Cloud Palace! Appearance, expression, and demeanor, there was no difference at all.
The woman who just walked out of the Ascension Tunnel took off all her clothes as she walked. She stepped into the Heavenly Palace naked. The moment she stepped into the Heavenly Palace, she turned into a red dot of light and was absorbed by the woman in the bathtub!
Women kept coming out of the Ascension Passageway and were continuously absorbed. All kinds of clothes were scattered around the Vast Cloud Palace, piling up into a mountain!
The woman in the bathtub was the woman Luo Zheng had seen in the Lower Realm, Yun Luo!
To be exact, Luo Zheng had seen her clone before.
Yun Luo cultivated a secret technique that split her soul into a million pieces and scattered them randomly in the Lower Realm to cultivate. This was the famous Million Clones Technique!
Through the continuous training of a million clones, Yun Luo's cultivation, experience, and comprehension had all increased tremendously!
She wanted to use this opportunity to break through to the Sector Lord level!
Right now, her Million Clones Technique had reached the final stage. She needed to absorb all her clones through the Ascension Passageway …
However, the entire absorption process was not completed in an instant. It would take about a month or so. After all, there were so many Greater Worlds, and the speed of ascension varied. Moreover, not all of the million doppelgangers survived. A small number of doppelgangers were killed by others, and some doppelgangers might have been imprisoned. She could not control all of her doppelgangers, and accidents were bound to happen.
Of course, Yun Luo couldn't go back to the Lower Realm to take revenge. She wasn't that bored.
Other than that, she also needed to retrieve the memories of her clones.
Most of the memories were meaningless to Yun Luo, but she was surprised to find a guy called Luo Zheng from two of her clones!
When she paid attention to the memories of the two clones, she quickly brought up all the things she had met with Luo Zheng …
"This little guy is very special. The Battle of the Worlds and the Fateless … These two destinies can't appear in the same person at the same time. They contradict each other, but they just happened to appear in this kid …"
Soaking in the emerald green bathtub, Yun Luo raised her head and pondered while tying her hair up.
Right at this moment, Luo Zheng's Roaring Token could be heard.
"Luo Yan, where are you …?"
When Yun Luo heard the voice, a puzzled expression emerged on her face.
She had just obtained the memories of Luo Zheng from her two clones …
One of the memories was when Luo Zheng was a Xiantian realm warrior, and the other was when Luo Zheng broke through to the Divine Core realm in the Evil Tower.
Therefore, when Luo Zheng's voice sounded out, Yun Luo immediately recognized his voice!
"His ascension speed is pretty fast." Yun Luo said with a faint smile on her face. "But, where did this guy get these Roaring Tokens?"
Even Yun Luo only had five Roaring Tokens in her possession.
With her half-step Realm Lord cultivation base, there weren't many people who had Roaring Tokens. Ordinary Realm Lords would only have one or two Roaring Tokens. Because of her special status, she had so many Roaring Tokens.
However, five Roaring Tokens were too rare, and she wouldn't use them unless it was an emergency.
However, in just a short moment, she heard Luo Zheng repeat the same sentence three times!
"He's wasting so much just to find his sister …" Yun Luo was speechless. Through Yun Luo's clone in White Emperor City, Yun Luo had learned that Luo Zheng had a sister called Luo Yan, and she seemed to be related to a Second Grade Sect in the Lower Realm.
Later on, she was kidnapped and brought to the Central Domain. As for how Luo Yan came to the Upper Realm, there was no clue in her clone's memory, and Yun Luo had no way of knowing.
She had just spread her clones like seeds, and she didn't know how to control the actions of her clones. Furthermore, there were over a million clones, and she couldn't control them all.
Next, the two Heavenly Lords used their Roaring Tokens to curse each other.
Heavenly Lord Ling Huo and Heavenly Lord Zhi Shui could be considered as a pair of old enemies. This cursing battle had lasted for many years, and no one knew how many Roaring Tokens had been wasted.
However, they were both Heavenly Lords, and they had the right to waste their tokens. But no one expected that Luo Zheng would be so wasteful …
Yun Luo walked around for a while, but she didn't find any signs of the Ascension Tunnel lighting up again. Most of her clones should still be in the process of ascension. So, she slid her body into the pool. During the process of absorbing her clones, her body was extremely weak, and she had to be completely soaked in this special liquid.

Chapter 995
In the universe, those who could use the Roaring Command were basically peak existences.
Very few people like Celestial Master Ling Huo and Celestial Master Zhi Shui, who always used the Roaring Command to curse at each other.
Both of them were extremely famous in the universe and were naturally very strong. Even many Celestial Masters were unwilling to provoke them.
It wasn't that they couldn't afford to provoke them, but Celestial Master Ling Huo and Celestial Master Zhi Shui were independent cultivators. Provoking them would cause endless trouble.
Therefore, when the two of them used the Roaring Command, at most, it was noisy and annoying. They just had to endure it.
Most people would pretend they didn't hear it. At most, they would curse them in private. Who would dare to use the Roaring Command to insult them?
But this Luo Zheng was very powerful!
He scolded the two of them as soon as he arrived.
Yun Luo coughed for a long time before spitting out the damned spirit liquid. She didn't know whether to laugh or cry.
"This kid is really fierce. I wonder where he ascended to?"
Yun Luo agreed with the way her clone in the Sin Tower did things. A Fateless person was very important. Don't twist his fate.
Although this kid was a Fateless person, if he really ascended to her domain, it would be fate. Thinking of this …
Yun Luo walked out of the bath. However, as soon as she left the mist in the bath, a faint green light appeared and enveloped her body. Soon, it formed a fitting green dress.
At this time, five or six transportation channels flickered with light. In an instant, several identical Yun Luos appeared.
Their expressions were wooden. They planned to take off their clothes and merge with Yun Luo's main body.
Yun Luo said indifferently, "Wait here. There's something I need to deal with."
The Yun Luos stood in place, stupefied. They didn't move at all, waiting for the main body to deal with them.
After Yun Luo left the Misty Cloud Palace, she issued an order to submit the list of all ascenders. She wanted to look through it.
The subordinate who received the order was rather puzzled. Why did Lord Yun Luo want to look through the list of ascenders? What was so good about them? However, since it was Yun Luo's personal order, he did not dare to neglect it and hurriedly issued the order.
Among the thousands of large realms, there were countless ascension channels. Collecting all the recent name lists through these large realms and presenting them to Yun Luo was a huge project.
When Luo Zheng cursed, all the Realm Lords, Empyreans, and countless other martial artists had the same reaction as Yun Luo!
At first, their faces were filled with astonishment. Then, they didn't know whether to laugh or cry …
Who was this guy? His guts were too fat, to even dare to scold an Empyrean!
Originally, when Luo Zheng was looking for Luo Yan, everyone was curious about his identity. This time, the entire Huan Yu exploded! Those were trillions of living beings!
As for the two Celestial Sovereigns, they were silent for a moment before Celestial Sovereign Still Water's furious roar resounded through the universe once again.
"Brat, do you know who I am?" Celestial Lord Still Water's temperament was unyielding. How could he tolerate others cursing him?
Everyone thought that this should be the end of it.
Offending a Celestial Venerable would result in suffering, so it was better not to go overboard. Many people admired Luo Zheng for scolding the two Celestial Venerables. After all, they had heard these two old monsters quarrel on and off over the years, and they were sick of it. Now that an unknown junior had jumped out and scolded them, they felt very relieved.
Moreover, this Roaring Token was a rare item. This brat probably obtained a few Roaring Tokens by chance. Once he used them up, they would be gone. It was impossible for him to reply to an Empyrean's words without limit.
However, everyone's guess was completely wrong.
"I know, aren't you Celestial Lord Still Water?" Luo Zheng activated the Roaring Token and sneered.
"Hmph, you know I'm Celestial Lord Still Water, yet you still dare to scold me? Do you want to die? "Celestial Lord Still Water said angrily.
Luo Zheng chuckled and activated the Roaring Token again. "Of course I don't want to die, but what can you do to me?"
The number of creatures in the universe was unimaginable. There were all kinds of races, all kinds of creatures …
Trying to find Luo Zheng in the hundreds of thousands of worlds was countless times more difficult than finding a needle in a haystack. Moreover, Celestial Lord Still Water did not know who Luo Zheng was. Even if he wanted to find him, he did not know where to start!
To put it bluntly, even if I scold you, you won't be able to find me. Other than feeling anxious, there was really no other way!
In the past, many people in the universe had done this kind of thing.
At that time, there were still many divine beasts like the Howling Celestial Tapir in the universe, and the Roaring Token was not so precious. Therefore, during that period of time, the entire universe was filled with a foul atmosphere.
Some Realm Lords were very dissatisfied with a certain Empyrean, so they gave thousands of Roaring Tokens to a subordinate to scold that Empyrean. They scolded him from day to night, and all kinds of curses continuously rang in the ears of the trillions of creatures in the universe. It did not stop day and night, and it almost drove them crazy.
Fortunately, these days did not last long. As the Howling Celestial Tapir became rarer and rarer, anyone who used the Roaring Token would have to think carefully. Even Empyreans did not have many Roaring Tokens, so they did not dare to use them carelessly.
Celestial Lord Ling Huo and Celestial Lord Still Water were originally enemies. However, because Luo Zheng scolded both of them, he also used the Roaring Token and said, "Indeed, we can't do anything to you. However, hmph, how many Roaring Tokens do you have to squander?"
In the cave, Luo Zheng's face finally revealed a smile. He had no intention of provoking the two Empyreans. He did this purely because he could not find Luo Yan and wanted to vent.
Therefore, Luo Zheng did something unimaginable.
"You want to know how many Roaring Tokens I have?"
"Then can you count them?"
…
When Luo Zheng said these two sentences, all the creatures in the universe were speechless. He could have said it in one sentence, but he had to say it in two sentences!
Even a Empyrean would not dare to squander Roaring Tokens like this!
But then, an inconceivable scene appeared.
Luo Zheng began counting.
"One."
"Two."
"Three."
"Four."
"Five …"
He would pause for a moment every time he counted a number. It was obvious that he used a Roaring Token for every number he counted.
Countless creatures in the universe were discussing this voice that was ringing in their ears …
"My God, how many Roaring Tokens does this kid have!"
"He's already counted to ten! These ten Roaring Tokens can basically buy an ordinary tenth-grade Sacred Land! "
"Who knows where this Empyrean successor came from. I've seen prodigals, but I've never seen such a prodigal!"
Such discussions rang out in almost every corner of the Hundred Thousand Great Realms.
The problem was that Luo Zheng was still counting …
"Twenty …"
"Twenty-one …"
"One hundred …"
At this time, Empyrean Still Water and Empyrean Ling Huo also fell silent.
The two of them had quite a number of Howling Tokens, but Celestial Master Still Water only had a little over a hundred of them. Although he had hundreds of Howling Sky Tapirs in captivity, most of them needed time to grow. Otherwise, the Howling Tokens they produced would not be able to resound throughout the universe!
As for Empyrean Ling Huo, he had even fewer Roaring Tokens, only a little more than eighty.
The problem was that these two Empyreans had more Roaring Tokens than other Empyreans. If they squandered them like Luo Zheng, they would also feel heartache!
The crux was where in the world did this kid get so many Roaring Tokens from?
If they traded these Roaring Tokens, they could exchange them for a huge amount of resources for their own cultivation! Why waste it like this …
Luo Zheng's voice continued to reverberate throughout the universe.
"One hundred and ten …"
"One hundred and twenty …"
"One hundred and thirty …"
From surprise to shock, to disbelief, then to disbelief, then to disbelief, and finally to numbness. This was the journey in the hearts of countless beings in the universe after hearing Luo Zheng's count.
This numbness lasted for a full four hours. Luo Zheng counted from one to more than ten thousand until another voice sounded in the universe …
"Hehe, what are you doing? Is counting fun?"
It was a woman's voice. It was very soft and ethereal, and when it reached one's ears, it made one feel numb.
When the Empyreans and Realm Lords in the universe heard this voice, their expressions changed drastically.

Chapter 996
Luo Zheng wasn't that bored, but it was his first time using the Roaring Token, so he was a little curious.
He didn't expect to hear a woman's voice towards the end of the count.
Luo Zheng was looking forward to hearing the voice of a Luo Yan. Unfortunately, this woman wasn't one. Hearing her question, Luo Zheng said angrily, "It's not fun."
"If it's not fun, why are you counting? Are you crazy?" the woman replied with a grin.
"It's really boring. Anyway, I'm going to cultivate." After fiddling with the Roaring Token for a long time, Luo Zheng was a little bored and was about to put it away.
Unexpectedly, the woman continued, "Don't cultivate. Do you want to listen to me sing?"
When her words resounded through the universe, dozens of voices suddenly sounded throughout the universe. Almost all the reclusive old monsters used the Roaring Tokens in their hands at this moment!
"Don't, please, don't sing!"
"We know your voice is nice, but you don't have to sing. We're really speechless!"
"Hey, hey, didn't you promise us that you won't sing in the future?"
The voices of many Celestial Venerables sounded at the same time.
Originally, the others didn't get involved in the quarrel between Luo Zheng, Celestial Venerable Ling Huo, and Celestial Venerable Still Water. After all, no one was willing to use the Roaring Token on unimportant matters.
However, when the mysterious woman's voice sounded, all the Celestial Venerables became nervous at the same time. When she was about to sing, all the Celestial Venerables were shocked and tried to stop her.
On the contrary, Luo Zheng was a little confused. Wasn't it just singing? It was better than listening to him count, right?
The mysterious woman said in an aggrieved voice, "I just want to sing. Is there a need to make you so nervous? Is it because my singing isn't nice? "
"No, no, no one in the universe has a better singing voice than yours. But you promised us that you wouldn't sing in the universe!"
"Yes, I also think your voice is nice, but please don't sing anymore …"
"Please don't sing, please don't sing!"
Hearing the pleading words of the Celestial Venerables, Luo Zheng asked curiously, "Why are they afraid of you singing? Is it because your singing is too bad? "
The woman giggled and said, "Yes, they don't like to hear me sing, so I have to sing …"
"It's really not that you find it unpleasant!" The roars of the several Empyreans were like free orders, constantly dissuading him.
The mysterious woman thought for a moment and said, "Alright, this time I won't release Devil Echo in my voice. Don't worry, it won't cause a beast tide!"
"Are you sure it won't cause a beast tide?" An Empyrean asked cautiously.
"Yes. Last time, it was because they were affected by the reverberation of the demonic voice that caused them to go berserk. This time, it won't happen again …" the mysterious woman continued.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng finally understood.
This was not the first time the mysterious woman's voice had appeared in the universe.
Just a few years ago, this mysterious woman's voice rang out for the first time. At that time, the first sentence she said was still fresh in the minds of the trillions of living beings in the universe because it was simply too profound.
At that time, the mysterious woman opened her mouth and asked, "Is there anyone here?"
This sentence was even more useless than the sentence Luo Zheng asked when he was looking for Luo Yan!
Of course there were people in the universe, but no one was bored enough to use Roaring Tokens to answer her question. Even the extremely wealthy Empyrean Still Water and Empyrean Ling Huo, although they had a large number of Roaring Tokens in their hands, they used them to curse at each other from a distance. They were disinclined to answer this mysterious woman's question.
Thus, after the mysterious woman asked several times in a row, she stopped asking and began to sing.
Her voice was originally very pleasant to listen to. If she sang, it would naturally be pretty good. But here was the problem.
Her voice seemed to contain some kind of special demonic nature! Even living beings with normal intelligence would be extremely agitated after hearing her voice and be unable to control themselves.
And when those Vicious Beasts with undeveloped intelligence heard her voice, it directly caused them to riot. Thus, her voice caused the largest Beast Tide in history!
Beast Tides weren't anything rare in the universe. Some great worlds would erupt with Beast Tides from time to time. For example, a certain race of Vicious Beasts would suddenly go crazy and gather hundreds of thousands or even millions of Vicious Beasts to attack a certain Holy Land at the same time …
Every race would more or less guard against the arrival of Beast Tides. And once a Beast Tide erupted in a great world, the races of the surrounding great worlds would lend a helping hand.
However, the Beast Tide triggered by this mysterious woman was no ordinary Beast Tide. It was a Beast Tide that erupted in the universe at the same time in a hundred thousand great worlds!
Generally speaking, Vicious Beasts with undeveloped intelligence wouldn't be particularly powerful. However, these Vicious Beasts were often terrifying in numbers. For example, Ant Tides, Scorpion Tides, Snake Tides, and so on. The ones to suffer were the weaker living beings.
However, there were still a considerable number of Vicious Beasts in the universe. Although they hadn't developed intelligence, their strength was abnormally powerful.
When these fellows were stirred up by the mysterious woman's voice, the situation immediately spiraled out of control.
That period of time could be called a dark day for many races in the universe. The hundred thousand great worlds suffered immeasurable losses. Many Holy Lands and Divine Kingdoms were destroyed. Although there were no Empyreans or World Lords that died, there were countless powerhouses below the World Lord level!
And the instigator of all of this was this mysterious woman. It was all because of one of her songs.
This was also why when this mysterious woman suddenly said she wanted to sing, the originally silent Empyreans all jumped out to stop her.
Up until now, where this woman came from and what her name was were all mysteries in the universe.
Some Empyreans had once tried to fish for information from her, but this mysterious woman refused to answer. Later, many Empyreans persuaded her not to sing in the future. Since then, this mysterious woman had never spoken.
At the very least, in these past few years, her voice had never resounded in the universe again.
But today, another fellow had appeared. Not only had he quarreled with the Empyreans, but he had also managed to make this mysterious woman speak. While the Empyreans were trembling with fear, they also resented Luo Zheng.
However, they didn't dare say anything. This mysterious woman's origins were unknown, so they could only persuade her like this. If she really opened her mouth to sing, it would be another disaster for the hundred thousand great worlds in the universe.
Fortunately, they heard this woman say she wouldn't use the Demonic Echo. If she promised not to trigger a Beast Tide, then singing a song wouldn't be too much of a problem …
In truth, Luo Zheng didn't know that all the Holy Lands and Divine Kingdoms in the hundred thousand great worlds had entered a state of alert!
Whether it was the Human Race, the Fiend Night Clan, or the Demon Race …
From the small third-grade and fourth-grade sects to the large ninth-grade and tenth-grade Holy Lands and Divine Kingdoms, all the factions issued orders to gather all their martial artists and deploy them to the various passes, main roads, and city walls …
Every martial artist's face flashed with nervousness. As for the leaders of the Holy Lands and the rulers of the Divine Kingdoms, their complexions were also grim!
Although this mysterious woman promised that her singing this time wouldn't trigger a Beast Tide, who could say for sure?
No one knew the woman's origins. Even if she triggered a Beast Tide again, there was almost nothing they could do to her!
They could only rely on themselves. No one was willing to see a devastating disaster brought about by a Beast Tide.
As for the Spiritual Martial Holy Land where Luo Zheng was, at this moment, they were also mobilizing a large number of experts and deploying them to various parts of the Spiritual Martial Holy Land. Of course, they didn't expect this group of ascenders to help, so these ascenders who were diligently mining in the mines didn't feel any sense of crisis. They, who had ascended from the lower realms, didn't know how terrifying this Beast Tide was.
After a while, the woman's ethereal and pleasant voice drifted through the universe …
"Awakening in the wind and leaves, the time and space under the shadow of the tree …"
"The notes at the fingertips, for whom are they played …"
"The whistling wheels …"
Her voice was as gentle as a nightingale. Coupled with this slow and elegant tone, it was indeed very pleasant to the ears.
However, except for Luo Zheng, most of the creatures in the universe weren't in the mood to appreciate her singing. It wasn't that they didn't know how to appreciate it, but they were now paying close attention to the great worlds they were in, afraid that there would be any trouble and trigger another Beast Tide!
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 997
After the song ended, the mysterious woman asked, "Is it nice?"
"It's nice, it's nice!"
"It's so nice. This song should only exist in the heavens. It's rare to hear it in the mortal world!"
"I've never heard such a nice song …"
Many Celestial Venerables also felt sorry for the Howling Tokens in their hands. After all, their Howling Tokens were not like Luo Zheng and this mysterious woman's. They could use them as they pleased. How wasteful was it to use the Howling Tokens to flatter others?
However, they also knew that they couldn't save their Howling Tokens. The mysterious woman didn't sound old, but in the hearts of many Celestial Venerables, she was like a great aunt. They were afraid that if they angered her and used the Demon Echo, they would be in big trouble.
Who knew that the mysterious woman didn't buy it. She just snorted and said, "I'm not asking you. I'm asking him!"
Everyone knew who she was asking. It had to be the mysterious young man who had been counting.
Seeing that the mysterious young man didn't speak, the other Celestial Venerables also hurriedly said, "Hey, boy, say something!"
Luo Zheng, who was in the cave, smiled faintly. He then calmly activated the Roaring Command in his hand and said, "Yes, it sounds good."
"Hehe." The mysterious woman smiled and said, "Then I'll sing it for you every day in the future, okay?"
In fact, as long as it didn't cause a beast tide, the Celestial Venerables wouldn't dislike this woman's singing. After all, her voice was very pleasant to the ears. However, everyone was worried and didn't want her to sing every day. But now, no one dared to jump out and stop her. They were afraid of angering her.
However, many Celestial Venerables still hoped that Luo Zheng would take the initiative to refuse …
However, Luo Zheng only responded, "Okay."
"Yes, that's right. I haven't asked your name yet. I'm afraid I won't know how to address you in the future." The mysterious woman's voice sounded again.
Hearing the mysterious woman ask Luo Zheng's name, many Celestial Venerables became interested.
If they knew the name of this mysterious young man, it would be much easier to find him. They were very interested in the mysterious woman, and they were also interested in Luo Zheng. These Celestial Venerables just wanted to figure out how these two people got their Roaring Tokens and why they never ran out.
Luo Zheng was not an idiot. How could he say his name in front of everyone in the universe? Earlier, he had scolded the two Celestial Lords. Moreover, he had a special roaring token in his hand. Wasn't he courting death by saying his name?
"It's not convenient for me to say this name," Luo Zheng responded.
"It's not convenient …" Hearing that Luo Zheng didn't want to tell her his name, the woman's voice seemed to be disappointed, but she immediately said, "It doesn't matter, as long as you can chat with me! I'm trapped here and can't go anywhere. It's really depressing! "
"If you have time, I'll be happy to keep you company," Luo Zheng said lightly.
In fact, Luo Zheng didn't have much interest in chatting with this mysterious woman. He was already too busy with his own affairs, so how could he have the time to chat with a woman of unknown origin?
Luo Zheng's answer was just perfunctory.
After putting away the Roaring Token, Luo Zheng continued to close his eyes and cultivate. Several hours had passed since he used the Roaring Token to cause such a ruckus in the universe.
The next day, when Luo Zheng returned to the settlement, he found that everyone was talking about the Roaring Token yesterday, including the soldiers on duty.
"Who do you think that mysterious young man is?"
"I think it's most likely that he's a descendant of a Celestial Lord!"
"Yes, I think so too. Only a descendant of a Celestial Lord would be so willful and use the Roaring Token …"
Hearing these conversations, a soldier sneered. "Don't use your ignorance to speculate. Let me tell you, let alone a descendant of a Celestial Lord, even a Celestial Lord wouldn't waste the Roaring Token like this! That mysterious young man's origin, hehe … isn't something you can speculate about! "
"Brother Xing, you said that the mysterious young man isn't a descendant of a Celestial Lord. Then who is he?" An ascendant asked the soldier.
The soldier spread out his hands and said, "You ask me, but who am I supposed to ask? I can only tell you that that guy is definitely not a descendant of a Celestial Lord. As for who he is … I'm afraid no one in the entire universe knows. "
This armored soldier called Big Brother Xing's words were too absolute.
For example, there were people who recognized Luo Zheng's voice, and there were more than one.
Just like Yun Luo and the people from the Demi-Immortal Clan …
However, the people from the Demi-Immortal Clan couldn't be sure that the mysterious young man was Luo Zheng. After all, there were too many living beings in the universe. Among the trillions of living beings, it wasn't difficult to find a cultivator with the same voice as Luo Zheng. Therefore, the people from the Demi-Immortal Clan could only guess.
As for Yun Luo, because she knew about Luo Yan and knew that Luo Zheng was looking for her sister, she was able to determine Luo Zheng's identity by comparing their voices.
Although the universe was vast, it couldn't be that there was a cultivator with the same voice as Luo Zheng, who was also looking for a cultivator called "Luo Yan," right? There was no such coincidence in the world.
According to yesterday's agreement, Mu Mingxue rested for a whole night before entering the settlement again.
Along the way, many Divine Sea Realm cultivators were sizing up Mu Mingxue. Their gazes gave her goosebumps all over her body. Some Divine Sea Realm cultivators even invited her to mine with them. Mu Mingxue refused all of these invitations without exception. She knew clearly that these guys were up to no good!
At this time, Mu Mingxue was still puzzled about the voice that had been ringing for a long time yesterday.
Yesterday, she listened carefully to every word. The mysterious young man's voice was too similar to Luo Zheng's! They were exactly the same!
However, Luo Zheng was just a cultivator who had just ascended. It was impossible for him to get a Roaring Token, not to mention that he had wasted so many Roaring Tokens …
This was Mu Mingxue's doubt.
When she saw Luo Zheng waiting in the settlement, she quickly walked over with a smile, "Let's go!"
Luo Zheng nodded. Since he had chosen to mine in the mine, Luo Zheng's target was the Zhen Yuanyu. The sooner he dug them out, the sooner he could regain his freedom.
However, on the way, Mu Mingxue kept asking Luo Zheng questions, which made Luo Zheng vigilant. This woman seemed to be suspecting him …
In fact, Luo Zheng could understand. Yesterday, she heard his voice in the Roaring Token. Today, she probably wanted to confirm it. However, since Luo Zheng didn't admit it, this matter couldn't be verified at all. As for Mu Mingxue's wild guesses, Luo Zheng wouldn't dispel her doubts.
However, in the process of rushing to the mine, the two didn't know that they were being watched.
The ascenders in the mineral veins were also grouped together.
All the ascenders could roughly be divided into three factions. Among them, the most powerful faction was led by a martial artist called Qiu Ren!
In fact, the bald cultivator who had been slapped to death by Luo Zheng yesterday was also Qiu Ren's subordinate. If it weren't for Qiu Ren's acquiescence, how could the bald cultivator dare to rob here?
Ascenders like Qiu Ren had already accumulated enough Zhen Yuanyu. Originally, they could have left the mine early. However, Qiu Ren wanted to accumulate enough Zhen Yuanyu here. After bribing the soldiers in the mine, he stayed in the mine to cultivate. He had already accumulated a lot of Zhen Yuanyu. When he left this place in the future, he would be able to buy a large amount of resources so that he could cultivate quickly …
Like Qiu Ren, the leaders of the other two forces also had the same plan. Therefore, the entire mine seemed to be in order. In fact, it was monopolized by these three forces. The other ascenders diligently mined the mine. Not only did they have to hand it over to the Lingwu Holy Land, they also had to give a portion of their labor to these three people!
Yesterday, the six Spirit Sea Realm cultivators fled in panic. They turned around and complained to Qiu Ren. Qiu Ren firmly controlled the mine in this area. How could he tolerate others challenging his authority?

Chapter 998
Regarding Luo Zheng's Life and Death cultivation base, Qiu Ren was also somewhat baffled.
Moreover, the bald cultivator's followers had also described Luo Zheng's fighting style to Qiu Ren. This guy seemed to have innate divine strength and had actually slapped the bald cultivator into the lode with a single palm.
This kind of strength was enough to arouse Qiu Ren's vigilance. Therefore, this time, he sent three people to follow Luo Zheng into the lode.
These three people were Qiu Ren's right-hand men. Although they were also ascenders, they were well-known figures among ascenders. Their strength was far more formidable than ordinary ascenders.
Qiu Ren had instructed these three to secretly kill Luo Zheng.
Although the Spirit Martial Holy Land didn't care much about fighting and killing in the lode, Qiu Ren had always been very careful. If he killed too many ascenders, the number of True Origin Stones he had to hand over would decrease. If the higher-ups blamed him, it would be difficult to explain to the guards in charge of managing the lode, and it would be even more difficult for Qiu Ren to explain.
Therefore, under normal circumstances, Qiu Ren wouldn't kill anyone. As long as one was willing to obediently hand over the True Origin Stones, he wouldn't make things difficult for them.
However, when it was time to act, Qiu Ren wouldn't hesitate. Since this Luo Zheng dared to kill his people, then he had to pay with his life. If he didn't do so, how could he, Qiu Ren, occupy this lode? How could he stand in front of so many subordinates?
Mu Mingxue was still immersed in doubts and speculations. She carefully thought about it. Yesterday, Luo Zheng seemed to have deliberately avoided her. In any case, during that time, she didn't know about Luo Zheng's whereabouts at all. He indeed had plenty of time …
Just as Mu Mingxue was making wild guesses, a True Origin Sound Transmission was transmitted to Mu Mingxue's ears.
"Someone is following us." This was Luo Zheng's voice.
Although the three people behind them had deliberately concealed their auras, how could they escape Luo Zheng's perception? Almost as soon as they left the settlement, Luo Zheng had already locked onto the three people's movements.
However, Luo Zheng hadn't confirmed it before. After all, these three people could also be mining. However, after Luo Zheng changed directions several times, they still firmly followed behind him. At the same time, he could feel a faint killing intent. Their goal couldn't be any simpler.
"Ah …"
After Luo Zheng's reminder, Mu Mingxue also immediately discovered the three people behind them.
In fact, these three people didn't have much ability to conceal themselves. Qiu Ren's order was to make Luo Zheng 'disappear' in the mineral vein. In their view, a Life and Death Realm martial artist and a female martial artist who had just ascended didn't need to be secretive at all. They were just fish on the chopping board.
"What should we do?" What could Mu Mingxue do? At this time, she could only ask asked asked Origin Sound Transmission Sound Transmission.
Luo Zheng said with an indifferent expression, "Let's talk about it after we enter the mine."
The two of them sped up and drilled into the mine. As for the three people behind them, they also sped up and followed closely behind.
Luo Zheng randomly chose a mine and entered it with Mu Mingxue.
The three people behind them laughed. One of them said, "This kid should be aware of our intentions. Even so, he still dares to enter the mine. It seems he is very confident."
"Confidence is a good thing, but blind confidence often leads to a quick death," another person responded.
"Leave that woman for me. There's no place to vent my anger in this damn place. It's not easy to find a beautiful woman. Don't kill her casually," the last person said with a grin.
After speaking, the three people turned into three escape lights and shot into the mine.
Just as the three people entered, they found Luo Zheng standing alone not far from the mine. He held a long sword in his hand and said with a smile on his face, "Hello."
The three people's expressions didn't change. The leader looked at Luo Zheng's posture and said coldly, "You know we want to kill you, but you still didn't run. Your courage is not bad."
"Unfortunately, you only have courage but no strength. In the end, you will only become a pile of bones," another person said.
Luo Zheng asked indifferently, "You came to kill me because of the bald cultivator yesterday?"
"We don't know. Big brother Qiu Ren told us to kill you. Naturally, you must die," one person replied.
"Qiu Ren?" Luo Zheng's eyebrows slightly raised. "I didn't provoke this person."
"You didn't provoke big brother Qiu Ren. That's not important. What's important is that you must die!" The three people acted very decisively. They didn't waste time talking nonsense with Luo Zheng. After speaking, the three people attacked Luo Zheng from three directions …
At the same time, a biting cold air swept over. It was Mu Mingxue's Snowfall Field that spread out and enveloped the three people.
Luo Zheng's face revealed a faint smile. He gently waved the long sword in his hand, which flashed a strange arc. He only sighed in his heart. This world was really no different from the lower realms. It was also a world where the strong preyed on the weak. It was also unreasonable. Suddenly, he felt a sense of weariness. If the peak of martial arts was also like this, then what was the point of pursuing this peak?
Although Luo Zheng was confused, the sword in his hand became even fiercer!
…
On the side of the settlement, some cultivators built some simple houses with stones.
Almighty Divine Sea Realm cultivators all had the power to move mountains and drain seas. It was too easy for them to cut some stones and build some simple houses.
And Qiu Ren lived here.
In fact, he originally planned to build a palace. When he was in the lower realms, he was a peerless hero. His Divine Kingdom almost completely ruled the great thousand worlds he lived in!
After getting used to living a flourishing life, he couldn't adapt after arriving in the upper realms.
However, the palace wasn't built in the end. The soldiers of the Spiritual Martial Holy Land didn't agree. They said that if the people above saw it, it would cause trouble.
After all, this was a place for ascenders to serve. If they wanted to serve, they had to act like they were serving. What was the meaning of building a palace here?
Therefore, Qiu Ren could only give up.
He also knew that his strength was nothing in the upper realms. But it didn't matter. He had accumulated tens of thousands of Zhen Yuanyu. This was a considerable amount of wealth. A part of it was used to bribe the soldiers. The other part could be taken away by himself. Using these Zhen Yuanyu to exchange for resources was enough to make his strength advance by leaps and bounds.
Qiu Ren looked forward to life in the upper realms. Unfortunately, he still needed to be patient now. He was patient now for the sake of being free in the future. Therefore, he was very patient. If he endured for a while more, he would be able to leave this damned place!
Even if he lived in this simple house, Qiu Ren still had his own fun.
For example, the three female ascenders in front of him were twisting their waists in front of him with all their might, throwing flirtatious glances at him to please him.
Although the female ascenders in this mine were excellent, there were still some. However, there weren't many people who were qualified to enjoy them. Of course, Qiu Ren was one of them.
These three women were also top existences in the lower realms. Perhaps they were the high and mighty Saintesses, or perhaps they were the Divine Queens of some Divine Kingdom … Unfortunately, after ascending, they could only cling to these men without any background!
Just as the three women were doing their best to twist and dance gracefully, Qiu Ren's expression suddenly changed. The metal wine cup in his hand was directly crushed into a ball by him.
"The slave seal disappeared?" Qiu Ren's expression darkened.
The slave seal that he planted on his subordinates had completely disappeared in an instant just now …
There was only one possibility. The three of them had been killed.
Those three were Qiu Ren's capable subordinates. He was very clear about their strength. Moreover, the slave seal had disappeared at almost the same time. This meant that the other party had used a thunderous method to kill them!
Just by relying on that Life and Death little guy and a female ascender at the Divine Ocean Realm?
This was impossible!
Qiu Ren almost subconsciously rejected the guess in his heart.
The only possibility was that those three were lured into the bottom layer of the mine by Luo Zheng and encountered some powerful ferocious beasts … However, those three were very cautious. They wouldn't have allowed Luo Zheng to enter the depths of the mine.
"Big Brother Qiu! What's wrong? You don't look good? "A female ascender's slender waist twisted and coiled around Qiu Ren's body.

Chapter 999
After killing those three people, Luo Zheng fell into deep thought.
It was as if he was possessed. Even Mu Mingxue, who was beside him, felt strange. It seemed that Luo Zheng was in a bad mood. She asked a few words of concern, but Luo Zheng seemed to be indifferent.
For many martial artists, the problem that Luo Zheng was thinking about was not a problem.
There were many directions in which a person could work hard, but they often lacked an ultimate goal.
Luo Zheng was indeed guided by Luo Yan. Perhaps this was fate's arrangement. Even now, Luo Yan was still like a compass, guiding him forward!
However, this was not Luo Zheng's ultimate goal. It was something that Luo Zheng had to accomplish, and the ultimate goal came from the deepest obsession in his heart.
Luo Zheng's obsession was indeed to pursue the pinnacle of martial arts. He wanted to see how far a martial artist could go. Could he control this world, or could he create a world?
When Luo Zheng climbed up to the Upper Bound, he found that the rules here were not much different from those in the Lower Bound …
A feeling of disgust arose in his heart, and this disgust accumulated and turned into confusion. If the scenery at the peak was the same, then it was better not to look!
In fact, Luo Zheng himself was not aware that he had also fallen into a spiritual dilemma.
The so-called spiritual dilemma was actually a kind of shackle on the heart of martial arts!
When Cui Xie kidnapped Luo Yan, it actually produced a spiritual dilemma, but it was produced by his opponent. To break this spiritual dilemma, Luo Zheng only needed to defeat Cui Xie, and his heart of martial arts would be unimpeded!
And now, this spiritual dilemma came from Luo Zheng himself. It was because he had doubts about the goal he had been pursuing all this time, and he felt tired of it!
This kind of mental dilemma was actually far more serious than Cui Xie's influence on Luo Zheng's martial heart. This was because Luo Zheng had set Cui Xie as his goal. As long as he continued to work hard to become stronger and then defeat this opponent, he would be able to break out of this situation.
Now, Luo Zheng's opponent was himself.
As the saying goes, the most powerful opponent is oneself. If Luo Zheng wanted to defeat himself and escape from this spiritual dilemma, it would not be an easy task!
Along the way, Luo Zheng searched for True Origin Stones in the mineral vein. He did not choose to enter the lowest level, so he was not in high spirits.
Therefore, this day of mining only ended hastily, and he only dug out some top-grade True Origin Stones.
When Mu Mingxue saw Luo Zheng like this, it was not good for her to ask anything. However, after seeing Luo Zheng's strength, she also knew that she was lucky to be able to obtain Luo Zheng's protection before relying on her uncle. If it was not for Luo Zheng, she was afraid that she could only be like the other female ascenders, relying on an even more powerful ascender …
After returning to the settlement, Luo Zheng casually found a place and sat down cross-legged, but he did not cultivate.
He was still racking his brains to think about this problem.
Many people would encounter this problem. A person who was striving hard would suddenly lose the motivation to struggle one day, and would ask himself what the meaning of life was.
If he could not find an answer, it was very likely that he would not be able to recover from this setback, and his entire temperament would change … But once he found the answer, his spirit would undergo a transformation, and his entire person would rise again!
Now, Luo Zheng was at a fork in the road.
While Luo Zheng was pondering, Qiu Ren, who was wearing a black shawl, swaggered through the settlement, and walked directly towards Luo Zheng.
Some of the newly ascended warriors did not know about Qiu Ren's power, but the majority of the surrounding ascenders were clear. Although this person was also an ascender, they absolutely should not provoke him. This person was even more difficult to deal with than the armored warriors of the Spirit Martial Holy Land.
These armored warriors were restricted by the rules of the Spirit Martial Holy Land, but Qiu Ren was not restricted by any rules. Although he was only an ascender, he had the power to decide life and death in this mine!
Luo Zheng sat quietly in the same spot with his eyes closed. His mind was running at full speed.
Even Qinglong was aware of the change in Luo Zheng's mentality.
But it didn't say anything. It just carefully watched Luo Zheng's every move. It also guessed that Luo Zheng was in a spiritual dilemma, and this kind of spiritual dilemma was very troublesome. The more confident Luo Zheng was, the stronger his obsession was, and the more serious this dilemma was!
Others could not help Luo Zheng. Qinglong's simple preaching was meaningless. Luo Zheng could only convince himself and defeat himself. This was a battle between himself and himself. No one could participate unless someone could change Luo Zheng's mind. But in that case, Luo Zheng's soul and memory would be reconstructed, and he would not be the current Luo Zheng.
At this moment, Qiu Ren walked in front of Luo Zheng.
Feeling a warrior of the Spirit Sea Realm in front of him, Luo Zheng opened his eyes and looked at Qiu Ren indifferently. Although he did not know Qiu Ren, Luo Zheng did not need to think to guess the identity of this person. The one who sent people to kill him during the day must be this guy in front of him.
"Where are they?" Qiu Ren stared at Luo Zheng and asked coldly.
"Who?" Luo Zheng asked back.
Qiu Ren sneered, "Three people. Who do you think they are? Playing dumb? "
"Oh … I already killed them," Luo Zheng answered indifferently.
"You?" Qiu Ren frowned.
Luo Zheng let out a long sigh and then said, "Yes, with me …"
Hearing the conversation between Luo Zheng and Qiu Ren, the ascenders who were cultivating or resting around them were stunned.
It seemed that there was a conflict between this Life and Death Realm youth and Qiu Ren?
And this Life and Death Realm youth killed the three people around Qiu Ren …
Was this guy so strong?
Wasn't provoking Qiu Ren the same as seeking his own death?
"You are seeking your own death." Although Qiu Ren already knew the result, after confirming it again, the coldness on his face became more and more intense.
"Don't you have to find your own path …" Luo Zheng suddenly raised his head and looked at the starry sky. It was a cloudy night and the night sky was misty.
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Qiu Ren's face showed a confused expression. "What do you mean?!"
"I want to know the meaning of the end of this path …" Luo Zheng continued.
This time, not only Qiu Ren, but the other warriors also had strange expressions on their faces. Was this guy crazy? Or was he scared out of his wits by Qiu Ren?
Looking at Luo Zheng's sluggish face, Qiu Ren laughed instead. "It's useless to pretend to be crazy. You have two choices in front of you. You can either plant a slave imprint in you and become my slave and listen to my orders, or you can go to the mine tomorrow and die!"
Luo Zheng indifferently looked at Qiu Ren, his eyes incomparably clear. Suddenly, he slapped his head and said, "I know why I'm tired. I've heard your words countless times!" As he spoke, he even laughed. This smile gave people a very pure feeling.
This time, it was Qiu Ren's turn to be speechless. He even suspected that Luo Zheng wasn't pretending to be crazy, but was really stupid. Could it be that he was scared silly?
"Even if I know why I'm so disgusted, I still can't find the meaning …" Luo Zheng frowned and stared at Qiu Ren.
If it weren't for Qiu Ren not wanting to make a move in front of so many people and the soldiers of the Spirit Martial Holy Land, he would have already killed this strange guy.
"This Life and Death Realm kid seems to be seriously ill …"
"He must be scared silly and doesn't dare to face Qiu Ren. But this trick is useless! With Qiu Ren's character, he won't let go of anyone who offends him. Begging for mercy is useless, and pretending to be stupid is even more useless! "
"Why do I feel like he's nothing? This guy's words sound a little strange, but he seems to be thinking about something."
Although the ascenders didn't dare to come over, they didn't disperse. They also knew that Qiu Ren wouldn't kill people here, so they didn't have to worry about being implicated. Of course, when they discussed, they also lowered their voices to avoid Qiu Ren hearing them.
"What do you think? What's the meaning of pursuing power? "Luo Zheng suddenly stared at Qiu Ren and asked.
Qiu Ren's eyelids twitched, and his big fists were already clenched tightly. He was a little unable to tolerate this guy. He originally wanted to put psychological pressure on Luo Zheng and make him obediently become his slave. He didn't expect that this guy didn't put him in his eyes at all. Instead, he popped out a string of irrelevant words!
He really wanted to make a move here …
But in the end, he suppressed the anger in his heart and didn't say a word. It was enough to think of a way to deal with this idiot tomorrow. Thinking of this, he turned around and left.
However, Luo Zheng still didn't let Qiu Ren go. Instead, his figure flashed and blocked in front of Qiu Ren. He stared at Qiu Ren and asked, "What do you think? What's the meaning of pursuing power? "
He wanted to leave, but this kid actually blocked his way. Qiu Ren felt like he was about to collapse …
As for the surrounding ascenders, they also stared at this scene with wide eyes. One of them whispered, "He's probably really scared out of his wits by Qiu Ren. Otherwise, how could he have the guts to block Qiu Ren's path …"

＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1000
If Qiu Ren knew that Luo Zheng was acting like an idiot, he wouldn't have come tonight even if he was beaten to death!
His purpose for looking for Luo Zheng today was very simple. Either he planted a slave imprint on Luo Zheng and become his slave, or he died. Qiu Ren would never indiscriminately kill useful people. This was also one of his skills in this lode.
Who would have thought that this guy wouldn't feel the danger at all? Instead, he was like an idiot and didn't answer his question.
If it wasn't for the fact that he had some reservations and didn't want to fight here, Luo Zheng would be a dead man by now.
"I'm warning you for the last time. Get out of my way." Qiu Ren's face was as cold as ice in the winter. Being teased by a Life and Death little guy in front of so many people was indeed a very shameful thing.
Luo Zheng didn't have the slightest fear. He still stared at Qiu Ren and asked, "Tell me, what is the purpose of becoming strong?"
Qiu Ren's eyes flashed with killing intent. He reached out and grabbed Luo Zheng's shoulder. Since this guy didn't want to get out of the way, he would let him suffer first.
His eagle claw-like hand directly grabbed Luo Zheng's shoulder. The True Yuan in his body began to circulate. He didn't make a sound, but in fact, he had already used eighty percent of his strength!
How could the other martial artists not understand? Qiu Ren had most likely used his hidden strength. In the next breath, Luo Zheng's bones would probably shatter.
Who would have thought that Luo Zheng would still be motionless and repeat the same sentence …
At this moment, Qiu Ren displayed his strength. They saw Luo Zheng's shoulder lightly shake, and a powerful force that Qiu Ren couldn't resist rolled out.
At this moment, Qiu Ren's expression changed drastically. Under the sweeping force, Qiu Ren's entire body began to rapidly retreat. His feet plowed out two grooves in the ground. After he retreated several dozen feet, his entire body somersaulted, and he once again smashed his fist into the ground behind him. Only then did he come to a stop!
Qiu Ren's punch was actually releasing the force of Luo Zheng's shoulder gently shaking. If he didn't do so, his bones wouldn't be able to withstand such a powerful force. The final result was that all the bones in his body would probably break!
"Boom!"
The punch landed on the ground behind Qiu Ren, causing the ground to sink slightly. Then, the surface of the ground rippled like water, forming a crater that was hundreds of meters wide. Many ascenders who were meditating in meditation were swept into the crater, and they all flew up into the air.
These ascenders' cultivations were disturbed, and all of them had angry looks on their faces. But when they saw Qiu Ren, how could they dare to flare up? He was just curious about who made Qiu Ren suffer a loss.
As for those ascenders who were watching the show, they were all dumbstruck after witnessing the entire process.
These ascenders could easily create a crater that was hundreds of meters deep with a single punch.
The problem was that they clearly saw that Luo Zheng didn't even throw a punch. He only moved his shoulder slightly!
What's more, after the swing, the force was transmitted to Qiu Ren's body. This was already past the first level of dissipation! While Qiu Ren was constantly retreating, this was the second layer of energy dissipation. When Qiu Ren was unable to completely dissipate this energy, he could only choose to strike this energy into the ground. This was the third layer of energy dissipation!
In other words, Luo Zheng's power was still able to cause such destruction even after being weakened three times.
What was even more frightening was that he only moved his shoulder slightly.
Just how terrifying was Luo Zheng's power?
As for Qiu Ren, his eyes revealed an expression of disbelief. Of course, he couldn't understand how Luo Zheng could erupt with such power. But now, he finally understood why the three people he sent out were instantly killed by Luo Zheng, and why the three slave seals disappeared at almost the same time.
This brat's power was immeasurable!
"Whoosh!"
Luo Zheng was like a ghost. He passed through space and instantly appeared in front of Qiu Ren.
Qiu Ren no longer looked down on Luo Zheng. He realized that even if he went all out, it wouldn't be easy to take down Luo Zheng. Therefore, when Luo Zheng teleported over, the true essence in his body began to surge crazily. He was already on full alert as he faced Luo Zheng, ready to attack at any time.
If he attacked Luo Zheng here, it might cause some unnecessary trouble, but no trouble was more important than his own life.
Who knew that Luo Zheng would move in front of Qiu Ren and say the same thing, "Tell me …"
At this time, the expression on Qiu Ren's face was incomparably wonderful. Fools weren't scary, but fools were scary. What the hell was this guy trying to do?
It seemed that if he didn't answer Luo Zheng's question, he wouldn't be able to leave today. Therefore, this fierce and ruthless person who was like an iron tower finally resisted the urge to attack and seriously considered Luo Zheng's question.
"Becoming stronger means that you can live longer!" Qiu Ren helplessly replied. Anyway, he had already lost all his face today, so it didn't matter if he lost a little more.
"What is the purpose of living longer?" Luo Zheng asked again.
"The purpose of living longer is to become stronger!" Qiu Ren rolled his eyes and continued to answer.
"Then what is the purpose of becoming stronger?" Luo Zheng continued to ask.
Qiu Ren's mouth was wide open as he stared at Luo Zheng. He was really on the verge of collapse. Was this person crazy?
As for the other ascenders, they were already forcefully holding back their breaths to prevent themselves from laughing out loud … This was Qiu Ren. If it were someone else, they would have already started to laugh out loud.
Qiu Ren, who was on the verge of collapse, finally roared, "Becoming stronger is for better enjoyment, to be able to dominate this world as one pleases! To be able to enjoy the wealth of the entire world, to be able to dictate the life and death of all living things! "
Hearing Qiu Ren's roar, Luo Zheng's eyes suddenly lit up, and he once again sank into contemplation.
"Dictate everything …" Luo Zheng muttered as he pondered.
He was indeed tired, but this didn't mean that Luo Zheng had lost his martial heart. He was just tired of this kind of killing, tired of this law of the jungle …
"If I can dictate everything, I will definitely change this law, change this universe." The light in Luo Zheng's eyes became brighter and brighter, like a blazing star!
"With my current strength, I don't have the qualifications to be tired. I still have too many important things to accomplish …"
"It's useless to be tired. I can only work hard to become stronger, and then change …"
After ascending to the Upper Realm, perhaps because he wasn't used to it, or perhaps because he was lonely, it had a subtle impact on Luo Zheng's state of mind. In the end, it caused Luo Zheng to fall into a spiritual dilemma!
However, Luo Zheng's self-recovery ability was also extremely strong. He almost relied on his instincts to grasp Qiu Ren and carry out a "deduction".
Of course, the answer that Qiu Ren was forced to come up with out of helplessness wasn't correct, or it could be said that it wasn't important.
Luo Zheng only relied on Qiu Ren's words to let himself discern the correct direction!
To a martial artist, a spiritual dilemma was an extremely dangerous matter. Many martial artists were unable to find the meaning of their lives because they encountered a spiritual dilemma. There were those who lived in seclusion in the mountains and forests, and there were also those who went insane. There were even those who saw through the meaning of life and committed suicide …
Luo Zheng, on the other hand, quickly walked out of this predicament through deduction.
After breaking free from the spiritual dilemma, Luo Zheng's goal became clearer. His martial arts heart also completed a transformation and sublimation in this "deduction" …
Qiu Ren's face almost began to flash with an aggrieved expression. He had provoked this idiot. It was his bad luck!
However, at this moment, Luo Zheng's gaze had already returned to its ethereal state. Instead, he smiled slightly at Qiu Ren. "Thank you!"
Qiu Ren frowned. He didn't want to say another word to Luo Zheng. He shook the cloak behind him and quickly rushed to the other side of the settlement!
This kid didn't go crazy. He was just pretending to be stupid to tease me. He couldn't take this lying down no matter what. He thought in his heart. Just as he returned to his residence, he saw two armored soldiers standing at his door.
Seeing the two armored soldiers from the Lingwu Holy Land, Qiu Ren's face immediately filled with a smile. He relied on these two big brothers to act like a tyrant in this mineral vein.
"Elder Huang, Elder Chen, why are the two of you here? Don't you have enough Zhen Yuanyu to spend? "Qiu Ren laughed mischievously. His expression was very polite.
After he took control of this mineral vein, he bribed a lot of Zhen Yuanyu to these two people. They could be considered very familiar with each other. The reason why Qiu Ren could sit firmly in the mineral vein was also because of these two people's care.
